sm
vyavahāraparikarakāṇdam
loke sarvamanuṣyāṇāṃ vivāde luptadharmake |
nirṇayaḥ kriyate ‘smābhir yuktismṛtyanurodhataḥ ||
sakalavivādanirṇayārthaṃ smṛtyarthanirṇayaḥ kriyate | tatra nāradaḥ |
manuḥ prajāptir yasmin kāle rājyam abūbhyjat |
dharmaikātānāḥ puruṣās tadāsan satyavādinaḥ ||
tadā na vyavahāro ‘bhūn na dveṣo nāpi matsaraḥ |
naṣṭe dharme manuṣyeṣu vyavahāraḥ pravartitaḥ ||
draṣṭā ca tasya nyāyasya rājā dhārayitā kṛtaḥ |
“dharmaikatānāḥ” dharmaikavistārāḥ | sarveṣāṃ dharmaprādhānyāt vyavahārahetubhūtadveṣamatsarabījā dharmābhān nāsti vyavahāraḥ | idānīṃ kaliyuge kāladoṣān manuṣyāṇām adharmaprādhānyāt, tannibandhanānyāyanivṛttyarthaṃ manvādibhiḥ śiṣṭaiḥ vyavahārākhyo nyāyaḥ “pravartitaḥ” darśitaḥ | tasya nyāyasya draṣṭā anyāyapravṛttānāṃ ca daṇḍasya “dhārayitā” manvādibhī rājā kṛtaḥ | atra manuḥ |
vyavahārān didṛkṣus tu brāhmaṇaiḥ saha pārthivaḥ |
mantrajñair mantribhiś caiva vinītaḥ praviśet sabhām ||
tatrāsīnaḥ sthito vāpi pāṇim udyamya dakṣiṇam |
vinītaveśābharaṇaḥ kuryāt kāryāṇi kāryiṇām ||
pratyahaṃ deśadṛṣṭaiś ca śāstradṛṣṭaiś ca hetubhiḥ |
aṣṭādaśasu mārgeṣu vyavahārān pṛthak pṛthak ||
dharmāsanam adhiṣṭhāya saṃvītāṅgaḥ samāhitaḥ |
praṇamya lokapālebhyaḥ kāryadarśanam ārabhet ||
yathā nayaty asṛkpātair mṛgasya mṛgayuḥ padam |
nayet tathānumānena dharmasya nṛpatiḥ padam ||
“āsīnaḥ sthito vā” paśyet na śayānaḥ | “pāṇim udyamya dakṣiṇam” iti uparivastrasyopavītavat dhāraṇam ucyate | “hetubhiḥ” pramāṇaiḥ deśācāraprāptaiḥ śāstraprāptaiś ca aṣṭādaśasu śoṣitalepair mṛgapadānumānavat sūkṣmair hetubhiḥ vyavahāranirṇayaḥ kartavyaḥ | atra yājñavalkyaḥ |
vyavahārān nṛpaḥ paśyed dvivadbhir brāhmaṇaiḥ saha |
dharmaśāstrānusāreṇa krodhalobhavivarjitaḥ ||
śrutādhyayanasaṃpannā dharmajñāḥ satyavādinaḥ |
rājñā sabhāsadaḥ kāryā ripau mitre ca ye samāḥ ||
pūrvaślokair brāhmaṇaiḥ saha trayo brāhmaṇāḥ sabhāsadaḥ kāryāḥ | tathā ca kātyāyanena sabhāsado bhedena darśitāḥ |
vinītaveṣo nṛpatiḥ sabhāṃ gatvā samāhitaḥ |
āsīnaḥ prāṅmukho bhūtvā paśyet kāryāṇi kāryiṇām ||
sa tu sabhyaiḥ sthirair yuktaḥ prajñāmūlair dvijottamaiḥ |
dharmaśāstrārthakuśalair arthaśāstraviśāradaiḥ ||
saprāḍvivākaḥ sāmātyaḥ sabrāhmaṇapurohitaḥ |
sasabhyaḥ prekṣako rājā svarge tiṣṭhati dharmataḥ ||
śrutismṛtiviruddhaṃ ca bhūtānām ahitaṃ ca yat |
na tat pravartayed rājā pravṛttaṃ ca nivartayet ||
“saprāḍvivākaḥ sāmātyaḥ” ity asmin śloke sabhāsadaḥ bhedena darśitāḥ | vyavahāradarśaṇakālo ‘pi tenaivoktaḥ |
divasasyāṣṭamaṃ bhāgaṃ muktvā kālatrayaṃ tu yat |
sa kālo vyavahārāṇāṃ śāstradṛṣṭaḥ paraḥ smṛtaḥ ||
divasam aṣṭadhā kṛtvā prathamam agnihotrārthaṃ brāhmaṇatṛptyarthaṃ ca muktvā, anantaraṃ bhāgatrayaṃ vyavahārakāla iti | tathā ca manuḥ |
hutvāgnīn brāhmaṇān arcya praviśet suśubhāṃ sabhām |
tatra sabhāsthānam āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
durgamadhye gṛhaṃ kuryāj jalavṛkṣānvitaṃ pṛthak |
prāgdiśi prāṅmukhīṃ tasya kalyāṇīṃ kalpayet sabhām ||
mālyadhūpāsanopetāṃ bījaraktasamanvitām |
pratimālekhyadevaiś ca yuktām agnyambunā tathā ||
ādityacandrādidevatāyuktāṃ kalpayed iti | “yuktām” lokapālayuktaḥ ca kalpayed iti || atrānukalpam āha manuḥ |
yadā svayaṃ na kuryāt tu nṛpatiḥ karyadarsanam |
tadā niyuñjyād vidvāṃsaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ kāryadarśane ||
so ‘sya kāryāṇi saṃpaśyet sabhyair eva tribhir vṛtaḥ |
sabhām eva praviśyāgryām āsīnaḥ sthita eva vā ||
tasyaiva prāḍvivāka iti saṃjñā | tathā ca bṛhaspatiḥ |
vivāde pṛcchati praśnaṃ pratipraśnaṃ tathaiva ca |
priyapūrvaṃ prāg vadati prāḍvivākas tataḥ smṛtaḥ ||
tathā ca vyāsaḥ |
vivādānugataṃ pṛṣṭvā sasabhyas taṃ prayatnataḥ |
vicārayati yenāsau prāḍvivākas tataḥ smṛtaḥ ||
dāntaḥ kulīno madhyastho rāgadveṣavivarjitaḥ |
so ‘sya kāryāṇi saṃpaśyet sabhyair eva tribhir vṛtaḥ ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
apaśyatā kāryavaśād vyavahāraṃ nṛpeṇa tu |
sabhyaiḥ saha niyoktavyaḥ brāhmaṇaḥ sarvadharmavit ||
vyāsaḥ |
jātimātropajīvī vā kāmaṃ syād brāhmaṇabruvaḥ |
dharmapravaktā nṛpater na tu varṇās trayo ‘pare ||
avidvatkṣatriyavaiśyaviṣayam idam | tathaiva kātyāyanaḥ |
tatra vipro na vidvān syāt kṣatriyaṃ tatra yojayet |
vaiśyaṃ vā dharmaśāstrajñaṃ śūdraṃ yatnena varjayet ||
vidvatkṣatriyavaiśyābhāve manur āha |
jātimātropajīvī vā kāmaṃ syād brāhmaṇabruvaḥ |
dharmapravaktā nṛpateḥ na tu śūdraḥ kathaṃcana ||
amātyamukhyaṃ dharmajñaṃ prājñaṃ dāntaṃ kulodbhavam |
sthāpayed āsane tasmin khinnakārye kṣaṇe nṛṇām ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
dāntaṃ kulīnaṃ madhyastham anudvegakaraṃ sthiram |
paratra bhīruṃ dharmiṣṭham udyuktaṃ krodhavarjitam ||
atra hārītaḥ |
yathā śalyaṃ bhiṣak kāyād uddhared yantṛyuktibhiḥ |
prāḍvivākas tataḥ śalyam uddhared vyavahārataḥ ||
[sabhyalakṣaṇam āha] kātyāyanaḥ |
alubdhā dhanavantaś ca dharmajñāḥ satyavādinaḥ |
dharmaśāstrapravīṇāś ca sabhyaiḥ karyā nṛpair dvijāḥ ||
tatra sabhālakṣaṇam āha manuḥ |
yasmin deśe niṣīdanti viprā vedavidas trayaḥ |
rājñaś ca prākṛto vidvān sā yajñasadṛśī sabhā ||
prākṛto vidvān prāḍvivāka ity arthaḥ |
pūrvāmukhas tūpaviśed rājā sabhyā udaṅmukhāḥ |
gaṇakaḥ paścimāsyas tu lekhako dakṣiṇāmukhaḥ ||
vasiṣṭhaḥ |
yat puṇyam uddhṛte vipre mriyamāṇe jalāśaye |
tat puṇayaṃ saṃśayārūḍye (ḍhe) vyavahāre samuddhṛte ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
lokavedāṅgadharmajñāḥ sapta pañca trayo ‘pi vā |
yatopaviṣṭā viprāgryāḥ sā yajñasadṛśī sabhā ||
satyaṃ devā samāsena manuṣyās (?) tv anṛtaṃ viduḥ |
ihaiva tasya devatvaṃ yasya satye sthitā matiḥ ||
cāturvidhyaṃ sabhāyām āha |
pratiṣṭhitā calā caiva mudritā śāstritā tathā |
caturvidhā sabhā proktā sabhyāś caiva tathāvidhāḥ ||
teṣāṃ vyākhyānam āha |
pratiṣṭhitā pure grāme calā nāmāpratiṣṭhitā |
mudritādhyakṣasaṃyuktā rājayuktā ca śāstritā ||
nṛpo ‘dhikṛtasabhyāś ca smṛtir gaṇakalekhakau |
hemāgnyambu svapuruṣāḥ sādhanāṅgāni vai daśa ||
teṣāṃ mūrdhā nṛpo ‘ṅgānāṃ mukhaṃ cādhikṛtaḥ kṛtam [kṛtaḥ] |
bāhū sabhyāḥ smṛtir hastau jaṅghe gaṇakalekhakau ||
hemāgnyambu dṛśau tatra pādau svapuruṣas tadā |
daśānām api caiteṣāṃ karma proktaṃ pṛthak pṛthak ||
vaktādhyakṣo nṛpaḥ śāstā sabhyāḥ kāryaparīkṣaṅāḥ |
smṛtir vinirṇayaṃ brūte jayaṃ dānaṃ damaṃ tathā ||
śapathārther (?) hiraṇyāgnī tṛṣitakṣubdhayor jalam |
gaṇako gaṇayed arthaṃ likhen nyāyaṃ ca lekhakaḥ ||
pratyarthisabhyāānayanaṃ sākṣiṇāṃ ca svapūruṣaḥ |
kuryād anargalo rakṣed arthiprathyarthinau sadā ||
etad daśāṅgakaraṇaṃ yasyām adhyāsya pārthivaḥ |
nyāyān paśyet kṛtamatiḥ sā sabhādhvarasannibhā ||
gaṇakalekhakayor lakṣaṇaṃ sa evāha |
paśvājyaṛtvigādīnāṃ saṃyogāj jāyate ‘dhvaraḥ |
yathā saṃbadhyate tena vyavahāras tathocyate ||
yajñe saṃpūjyate viṣṇuḥ vyavahāre mahīpatiḥ |
yajamāno jayī tatra jitaḥ paśur udāhṛtaḥ ||
pūrvapakṣottarāv ājyaṃ prajñā ca havir ucyate |
trayī śāstrāṇi sabhyās tu ṛtvijo dakṣiṇā damaḥ ||
lobhadveṣādikaṃ tyaktvā yaḥ kuryāt kāryanirṇayam |
śāstroditena vidhinā tasya yajñaphalaṃ labhet (bhavet) ||
satyaṃ devāḥ samāsena manuṣyās tv anṛtaṃ viduḥ |
ihaiva tasya devatvaṃ yasya satye sthitā matiḥ ||
tatra bṛhaspatiḥ |
śabdābhidhānatattvajñau gaṇanākuśalau śucī |
nānālipijñau kartavyau rājñā gaṇakalekhakau ||
idam aparaṃ kartavyam ity āha kātyāyanaḥ |
kulaśīlavayovṛttavittavadbhir amatsaraiḥ |
vaṇigbhiḥ syāt katipayaiḥ mūlabhūtair adhiṣṭhitam ||
dharmāsanaṃ kuryād iti śeṣaḥ | atra nāradaḥ |
kulāni śreṇayaś caiva gaṇāś cādhikṛto nṛpaḥ |
pratiṣṭhāvyavahārāṇāṃ gurv eṣām uttarottaram ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
nṛpādhikṛtapūgāś ca śreṇayo ‘tha kulāni ca |
pūrvaṃ pūrvaṃ guru jñeyaṃ vyavahāravidhau nṛṇām ||
kīṇāśaḥ kārukaḥ śilpī kusīdiśreṇinartakāḥ |
liṅginas tartakāḥ kuryuḥ svena dharmeṇa nirṇayam ||
ye tv araṇyacarās teṣāṃ āraṇyaiḥ karaṇaṃ bhavet |
senāyāṃ sainikānāṃ tu sārtheṣu vaṇijāṃ tathā ||
tapasvināṃ tu kāryāṇi traividyair eva kārayet |
māyāyogavidaś caiva gaṇāś cādhikṛtā nṛpāḥ ||
svasamayasiddhena dharmeṇa hetunety arthaḥ | tatra vyāsaḥ |
kāryeṣv adhikṛtā rājñāṃ grāmaśreṇigaṇāḥ kulam |
gurusvāmī kuṭumbī ca pitā jyeṣṭhaḥ pitāmahaḥ ||
vivādān api paśyeyuḥ svādhīne viṣaye nṛṇām |
atha naigamān āha kātyāyanaḥ |
nānāpaurasamūhas tu naigamākhyaḥ prakīrtitaḥ |
nānāyudhadharā vrātāḥ samavetāḥ prakīrtitāḥ ||
samūho vaṇigādīnāṃ pūgaḥ sa parikīrtitaḥ |
brāhmaṇānāṃ samūhas tu gaṇa ity ucyate budhaiḥ ||
yaḥ saugatārhatādīnāṃ samūhaḥ saṅgha ucyate |
catuṣpadā gavādīnāṃ samūho vraja ucyate ||
asacchāstrādhigantṝṇāṃ samūhaḥ puñja ucyate |
caṇḍālaśvapacādīnāṃ samūho gulma ucyate ||
kāruśilpiprabhṛtīnāṃ nivahaḥ śreṇir ucyate |
pūgaśreṇigaṇādīnāṃ bhaveyuḥ kāryacintakāḥ ||
śucayo vedadharmajñā dakṣā dāntāḥ kulodbhavāḥ |
sarvakāryapravīṇāś cālubdhā vṛddhā mahattarāḥ ||
kartavyaṃ vacanaṃ teṣāṃ samūhahitavādinām |
pūganaigamapāṣaṇḍasaṅghānām apy ayaṃ vidhiḥ ||
pāṣaṇḍalakṣaṇam abhihitaṃ ṣaṭtriṃśanmate |
prāmāṇyam eva ye vede na vadanti kudṛṣṭayaḥ |
teṣāṃ baudhārhatādīnāṃ pāṣaṇḍākhyā prakīrtyate ||
pravrajya vasitā ye tu pāṣāṇḍās te prakīrtitāḥ |
pauruṣeyatayā vedaṃ prāṃāṇyaṃ pravadanti ye |
teṣāṃ vaiśeṣikādīnāṃ naigamākhyā prakīrtyate ||
kātyāyenenānyathā naigamākhyā pradarśitā |
nānāpaurasamājas tu naigamākhyaḥ prakīrtitaḥ | iti |
tathā svāyambhuvāgame ṣaṭ samayā uktāḥ |
bauddhaṃ caivārhataṃ caiva śaivaṃ pāśupataṃ tathā |
kāpālaṃ pāñcarātraṃ ca ṣaḍ ete samayāḥ smṛtāḥ ||
tatra vyāsaḥ |
vaṇikśilpiprabhṛtiṣu kṛṣiraṅgopajīviṣu |
aśakyo nirṇayo jy anyais tajjñair eva tu kārayet ||
etad uktaṃ bhavati | sarveṣu samayeṣu śāstroktamārgeṇa svasamayena vā, nirṇito vyavahāraḥ siddhyatīti | tatrottarottaraḥ pūrvapūrvasmād guruḥ | ata ānṛpātyunar (?) darśanaṃ yujyate | nṛpeṇa dṛṣṭe vyavahāre tata uttarasyābhāvāt punaḥ prasaṅgo nāsti | tathā ca pitāmahaḥ |
kulādibhiḥ kṛtaṃ kāryaṃ vicāryaṃ tad yathottaram |
asaṃtuṣṭasya kartavyaṃ yāvad rājñā kṛtaṃ bhavet ||
tatra gautamaḥ: “tatra vyavahāro vedo dharmaśāstrāṇy aṅgāni upavedāḥ purāṇam | deśajātikuladharmāś ca āmnāyair aviruddhāḥ pramāṇam | karṣakavaṇikpaśupālakusīdakāravaḥ sve sve varge | tebhyo yathādhikāram arthān pratyavahṛtya dharmavyavasthā” | smṛtyantare |
jātijānapadān dharmān śreṇidharmāṃś ca śāśvatān |
samīkṣya kuladharmāṃś ca svadharmaṃ pratipālayet ||
sadbhir ācaritaṃ yat syāt dhārmikaiś ca dvijātibhiḥ |
tad deśakulajātīnām aviruddhaṃ prakalpayet ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
gotrasthitis tu yā yeṣāṃ kramāyātā ca dharmataḥ |
kuladharmaṃ tu taṃ prāhuḥ pālayet taṃ tathaiva tu ||
yasya deśasya yo dharmaḥ pravṛttaḥ sārvakālikaḥ |
śrutismṛtyavirodhena deśadṛṣṭaḥ sa ucyate ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
udūhyate dākṣiṇātyair mātulasya sutā dvijaiḥ |
madhyadeśe karmakarāḥ śilpinaś ca gavāśinaḥ ||
matsyādāś ca narāḥ pūrve vyabhicāraratāḥ striyaḥ |
uttare madyapā nāryaḥ spṛśyāḥ nṝṇāṃ rajasvalāḥ ||
khaṣajāḥ pratigṛhṇanti bhrātṛbhāryām abhartṛkām |
anena karmaṇā naite prāyaścittadamārhakāḥ ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
prātilomye prasūtānāṃ tathā durganivāsinām |
varṇānāṃ niyataṃ dharmaṃ nyāyopetaṃ na cālayet ||
atha kāryanirṇaye aniyojyān āha vyāsaḥ |
dvijān vihāya yaḥ paśyet kāryāṇi vṛṣalaiḥ saha |
tasya prakṣubhyate rāṣṭraṃ balaṃ kośaś ca naśyati ||
deśācārānabhijñā ye nastikāḥ śāstravarjitāḥ |
unmattāḥ kruddhabālāś ca na niyojyā vinirṇaye ||
dharmo viddhas tv adharmeṇa sabhāṃ yatropatiṣṭhati |
śalyaṃ vāsya na kṛntanti viddhās tatra sabhāsadaḥ ||
sabhā vā na praveṣṭavyā vaktavyaṃ vā samañjasam |
abruvan vibruvan vāpi naro bhavati kilbiṣī ||
eka eva suhṛd dharmo nidhane ‘py anuyāti yaḥ |
śarīreṇa samaṃ nāśaṃ sarvam anyad dhi gacchati ||
pādo dharmasya [‘dharmasya?] kartāraṃ pādaḥ sākṣiṇam ṛcchati |
pādaḥ sabhāsadaḥ sarvān pādo rājānam ṛcchati ||
manuḥ |
rājā bhavaty anenās tu mucyante ca sabhāsadaḥ |
eno gacchati kartāraṃ nindārho yatra nindyate ||
nyāyaśāstram atikramya sabhyair yatra tu niścitaḥ |
tatra dharmo hy adharmeṇa hato hanti na saṃśayaḥ ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
adharmataḥ pravṛttaṃ tu nopekṣeran sabhāsadaḥ |
upekṣamāṇāḥ sa nṛpāḥ narakaṃ yānty adhomukhāḥ ||
nyāyamārgād apetaṃ tu jñātvā cittaṃ mahīpateḥ |
vaktavyaṃ tat priyaṃ tatra na sabhyaiḥ kilbiṣī bhavet ||
atra vyavahāranirṇaye kramam āha nāradaḥ |
tatra dharmāsanaṃ prāpya rājā nigatamatsaraḥ |
samaḥ syāt sarvabhūteṣu bibhrad vaivasvataṃ vratam ||
dharmaśāstraṃ puraskṛtya prāḍvivākamate sthitaḥ |
samāhitamatiḥ paśyet vyavahārān anukramāt ||
dharmaśāstrārthaśāstrābhyām avirodhena pārthivaḥ |
samīkṣyamāṇo nipuṇaṃ vyavahāragatiṃ nayet ||
yathā mṛgasya viddhasya vyādho mṛgapadaṃ nayet |
kakṣe śoṇitalepena tathā dharmapadaṃ nayet ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
nyāyamārgād apetaṃ tu jñātvā cittaṃ mahīpateḥ |
vaktavyaṃ tat priyaṃ tatra na sabhyas tatra kilbiṣī ||
adharmataḥ pravṛttaṃ tu nopekṣeran sabhāsadaḥ |
upekṣamāṇāḥ satataṃ narakaṃ yānty adhomukhāḥ ||
prāḍvivākasadasyānām upajīvyamatāni tu |
tadyuktiyogād yo ‘rtheṣu nirṇaye na sa daṇḍabhāk ||
asabhyalakṣaṇam āha nāradaḥ |
rāgād ajñānato vāpi lobhād vā yo ‘nyathā vadet |
sabhyo ‘sabhyaḥ sa vijñeyaḥ taṃ pāpaṃ vinayed bhṛśam ||
yatra sabhyāḥ sabhāṃ prāpya tūṣṇīṃ dhyāyanta āsate |
yathāprāptaṃ ca na brūyuḥ sarve te ‘nṛtavādinaḥ ||
asatyadaṇḍam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
rāgāl lobhād bhayād vāpi smṛtyapetādikāriṇaḥ |
sabhyāḥ pṛthak pṛthag daṇḍyā vivādād dviguṇaṃ damam ||
yādṛśāḥ punaḥ sabhāsadaḥ kartavyāḥ | tān darśayati nāradaḥ |
rājā tu dhārmikān sabhyān niyuñjyāt suparīkṣitān |
vyavahāradhuraṃ voḍhuṃ ye śaktāḥ sadgavā iva ||
dharmaśāstrārthakuśalāḥ kulīnāḥ satyavādinaḥ |
samāḥ śatrau ca mitre ca nṛpateḥ syuḥ sabhāsadaḥ ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
ekaṃ śāstram adhīyāno na vidyāt kāryanirṇayam |
tasmād brahmāgamaḥ kāryo vivādeṣūttamo nṛpaiḥ ||
nāradaḥ |
nāniyuktena vaktavyaṃ vyavahāreṣu kiṃcana |
niyuktena tu vaktavyam apakṣapatitaṃ vacaḥ ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
anirdiṣṭāś ca ye kuryuḥ vyavahāravinirṇayam |
rājavṛtte pravṛttā ye teṣāṃ daṇḍaṃ prakalpayet ||
nāradaḥ |
niyukto vāniyukto vā śāstrajño vaktum arhati |
devīṃ vācaṃ sa vadati yaḥ śāstram upajīvati ||
anyatra |
na sā sabhā yatra na santi vṛddhāḥ
na te vṛddhā ye na vadanti dharmam |
nāsau dharmo yatra na satyam asti
na tat satyaṃ yac chalenānuviddham ||
atha prāḍvivākatatsabhyānāṃ ca daṇḍam āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
anyāyavādinaḥ sabhyās tathaivotkocajīvinaḥ |
viśvāsavañcakāś caiva nirvāsyāḥ sarva eva te ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
anirṇīte ‘pi yady arthe saṃbhāṣeta raho ‘rthinā |
prāḍvivāko ‘tha daṇḍyaḥ syāt sabhyāś caiva na saṃśayaḥ ||
tatra rāgadveṣaviyuktaiḥ nirūpaṇakuśalaiḥ, brāhmaṇair mantrajñair mantribhiḥ, samṛddhair vaṇigbhiḥ, purohitena sabhyaiḥ sabhāyāṃ parivṛto rājā prāḍvivāko vāpi vyavahāradarśanam ārabheteti |
iti śrīmadvaradarājīye vyavahāranirṇaye vyavahāraparikarakāṇḍaṃ samāptam
2. āvedanakāṇḍam
atha vyavahāralakṣaṇam āha kātyāyanaḥ |
prayatnasādhye vicchinne dharmākhye nyāyavistare |
sādhyamūlas tu yo vādo vyavahāraḥ sa ucyate ||
tatra vyavahāraśabdanirvacanaṃ kātyāyana āha |
vinānārthe ‘vasandehe haraṇaṃ hāra ucyate |
nānāsandehaharaṇāt vyavahāra iti smṛtaḥ ||
nāradaḥ |
āgamaḥ prathamaṃ kāryo vyavahārapadaṃ tataḥ |
vicāro nirṇayaś ceti darśanaṃ syāc caturvidham ||
āgamo ‘rthivacanaśravaṇam | tataḥ pratijñottarapramāṇānāṃ vicāraḥ | tataḥ pramāṇato jayāvadhāraṇam iti kramaḥ |
āvedanakāṇḍam
tatra vyavahāramātṛkāyām āvedanaṃ yājñavalkya āha |
smṛtyācāravyapetena mārgeṇādharṣitaḥ paraiḥ |
āvedayati ced rājñe vyavahārapadaṃ hi tat ||
anyāyamārgeṇa paraiḥ ādharṣito vimarditaḥ, sabhāṃ prāptaḥ, rājñā prāḍvivākena vā pṛṣṭaḥ, kāryārthī yat āvedayati tad evāsya vyavahārasyāspadam | āvedayati cet iti svayam evāgatasya kāryadarśanaṃ kartavyam | na punar daṇḍadeśabandhādilobhena rājñā prāḍvivākena vā kāryam utpādya pravartyam ity uktaṃ bhavati | tathā ca manuḥ |
notpādayet svayaṃ kāryaṃ rājā nāpy asya pūruṣaḥ |
na ca prāpitam anyena grased arthaṃ kathaṃcana ||
aprāpitam anāveditam | graset svīkuryāt | āvedanakāle yad āveditaṃ nyūnam adhikaṃ tad bhūmau phalakādau vā lekhanīyam | kātyāyanaḥ |
rāgādinā yad ekena kopitaḥ karaṇe vadet |
tad om iti likhet pūrvaṃ vādinaṃ phalakādiṣu ||
karaṇe nirūpaṇasthāne | tathā ca bṛhaspatiḥ |
pūrvapakṣaṃ svabhāvoktaṃ prāḍvivāko hi lekhayet |
pāṇḍulekhyena phalake tataḥ patre viśodhitam ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
adhikaṃ chedayed arthān nyūnāṃśca paripūrayet |
bhūmau niveśayet tāvat yāvat so ‘rtho na niścitaḥ ||
āvedanaprakāram āha prajāpatiḥ |
niravadyaṃ sapratijñaṃ pramāṇāgamasaṃyutam |
alpākṣaraṃ prabhūtārthaṃ niḥsaṃdigdhaṃ nirākulam ||
āvedayed vivādārthī pareṇānyāyapīḍitaḥ |
cirakālotsṛṣṭeṣv āvedanaprakāraṃ pitāmaha āha |
kṣamākāraṇam ādau tu sopapattivadan naraḥ |
āvedayed vivādārthī satyaṃ jalpavivarjitam ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
saśastro ‘nuttarīyaś ca muktakakṣyaḥ sahāsanaḥ |
vāmahastena vā vādaṃ vadan daṇḍam avāpnuyāt ||
āvedanānantarakālam abhiyogāt prāk sabhyair vinā saṃdhau kṛte, abhiyogānurūpeṇa daṇḍam āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
āvedya tu gṛhīte ‘rthe praśamaṃ yānti ye mithaḥ |
abhiyogānurūpeṇa teṣāṃ daṇḍaṃ prakalpayet ||
tatrāvedanāntaraṃ tasyārthasya nyāyatve vyavahāradarśanaṃ kāryam | anyāyyaṃ cet parityājyam eva | tatra prajāpatiḥ |
patitādikṛtaś caiva yaś ca na prakṛtiṃ gataḥ |
asvatantrakṛtaś caiva pūrvapakṣo na siddhyati ||
ṛśyaśṛṅga-yājñavalkyau |
mattonmattārtavyasanibālabhītādiprayojitaḥ |
asaṃbaddhakṛtaś caiva vyavahāro na siddhyati ||
nāradaḥ |
guruśiṣyau pitāputrau dampatī svāmibhṛtyakau |
eteṣāṃ samavetānāṃ vyavahāro na siddhyati ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
viruddhaṃ cāviruddhaṃ ca dvāv apy arthau niveditau |
ekasmin yatra dṛśyete taṃ pakṣaṃ dūratas tyajet ||
anādeyavādasya lakṣaṇam āha nāradaḥ |
ekasya bahubhiḥ sārdhaṃ strīṇāṃ preṣyajanasya ca |
anādeyo bhaved vādo dharmavidbhir udāhṛtaḥ ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
rājñā visarjito yas tu yaś ca pauravirodhakṛt |
rāṣṭrasya vā samas tasya prakṛtīnāṃ tathaiva ca ||
atho janapadagrāmamahājanavirodhakṛt |
anādeyās tu te sarve vyavahārāḥ prakīrtitāḥ ||
punaḥ nāradaḥ |
yad bālaḥ kurute kāryam asvatantraḥ tathaiva ca |
akṛtaṃ tad iha prāhuḥ śāstraiḥ śāstravido janāḥ ||
ekasya bahubhiḥ iti bhinnahetur vivādo nirākriyate | asādhyapakṣam āha kātyāyanaḥ |
aprasiddhaṃ nirābādhaṃ nirarthaṃ niṣprayojanam |
asādhyaṃ vā viruddhaṃ vā pakṣaṃ rājā vivarjayet ||
svayam eva vyākhyānam āha |
na kenacit kṛto yas tu so ‘prasiddha udāhṛtaḥ |
anyārthaś cārthahīnaś ca nirābādhaḥ prakīrtitaḥ ||
alpāparādhaś cālpārtho nirarthaka iti smṛtaḥ |
kāryābādhavihīnas tu vijñeyo niṣprayojanaḥ ||
asaṃbhāvyam asiddhaṃ tat pakṣam āhur manīṣiṇaḥ |
yasminn āvedite pakṣe prāḍvivāke ‘tha rājani ||
pure grāme virodhaḥ syāt viruddhaḥ so ‘bhidhīyate |
udāharaṇam – ‘na kenacid’ kṛto madīyam idaṃ vastram anena bhakṣitam iti | ‘anyārthaś ca’ anena devadattenāpakṛtam iti | ‘arthahīnaḥ’ madīyo dīpo devadattagṛhaṃ prakāśayati | ‘alpāparādhaḥ’ aham anena kṣaṇaṃ sasmitaṃ nirīkṣitaḥ | ‘alpārthaḥ’ madīyavrīhibījam anenāpahṛtam iti | ‘kāryābādhavihīno’ madīyayajñāvabhṛte ‘nena snātam | ‘asaṃbhāvyam’ khapuṣpam anenāpahṛtam iti | evaṃ bhūtārthapratipādakavākyam asādhyam, sādhanāsaṃbhavād anādeyam iti | yasminn artha ukte rājñi prāḍvivāke vā virodhaḥ, ‘ātmārthe pṛthivīṃ tyajet’ iti nyāyāt so ‘pi tyājyaḥ | tathā purarāṣṭragrāmavirodhaṃ ‘grāmasyārthe kulaṃ tyajet’ iti nyāyāt so ‘pi tyājyaḥ | evam uktadoṣarahite āvedane kṛte evaṃ kartam iti kātyāyana āha |
kāle kāryārthinaṃ pṛcchet praṇataṃ purataḥ sthitam |
kiṃ kāryaṃ kā ca te pīḍā mā bhaiṣīr brūhi mānava ||
kena kasmin kadā kasmāt pṛcched evaṃ samāgatam |
evaṃ pṛṣṭaḥ sa yad brūyāt tat sabhyair brāhmaṇaiḥ saha ||
vimṛśya kāryaṃ nyāyyaṃ ced āhvānārtham ataḥ param |
mudrāṃ dadyāt tathā patraṃ puruṣaṃ vā samādiśet ||
āhūtas tv avamanyeta yaḥ śakto rājaśāsanam |
abhiyogānurūpeṇa tasya daṇḍaṃ prakalpayet ||
hīne karmaṇi pañcāśan madhyame dviśatāvaraḥ |
gurukārye ca daṇḍyaḥ syān nityaṃ pañcaśatāvaraḥ ||
evaṃ vyākhyātṛbhir uktam idam upalakṣaṇam, daṇḍapaṇatāratamyasya | tatra sakṛdanāgamane tṛtīyāṃśo daṇḍaḥ | dviranāgamane ‘rdham | triranāgamane pūrṇam | evaṃ daṇḍam ādāya triṃśadrātrād ūrdhvaṃ tripakṣād dvādaśabandhārthaṃ gṛhītvā hāninimittajayapatraṃ dadyāt | evaṃ kṛte yadi prativādy āgacchati, punar api vyavahāradarśanaṃ kartavyam iti | tatra trairātre sūtre viśeṣaḥ | trirātrāgamane caturo caṇḍyaḥ | palāyane caivam, parājaye caturaḥ kārṣāpaṇān ity alpadravyāvedanaviṣayam | evaṃvidhe hāni (?) jayapatraviṣaye yāvat kāryasiddhis tāvantaṃ kālaṃ dūtāya vetanam āhvātā dadyād ity āha |
ekāhaṃ dvyahaṃ tryahaṃvā deśakālādyapekṣayā |
dūtāya sādhite kārye netā bhaktaṃ prakalpayet ||
netā āhvātā | tatra mudrābir āhūto yadi nāgacchet tadā yāvad vyavahāranirṇayam āsedhayed ity āha nāradaḥ |
vaktavye ‘rthe na tiṣṭhantam utkrāmantaṃ ca tadvacaḥ |
āsedhayed vivādārthī yāvad āhvānadarśanam ||
‘vaktavye ‘rthe’ saṃdigdhe ‘rthe nirṇayārthaṃ na pravartamānaṃ na kevalaṃ na pravartate | ‘utkrāmantaṃ ca tadvacaḥ’ mā gā iti vāryamāṇo na tiṣṭhet, taṃ ‘āsedhayet’ | maryādāyām ‘yāvat’ ity avadhiparicchedaḥ | āhūyate ‘sminn iti ‘āhvānam’ vyavahāraḥ | ato yāvad vyavahāradarśanam āsedhaḥ kartavyaḥ | tatrāsedhaś caturvidha ity āha |
sthānāsedhaḥ kālakṛtaḥ pravāsāt karmaṇas tathā |
caturvidhaḥ syād āsedhaḥ tam āsiddho na laṅghayet ||
nāsmāt sthānāt calitavyam iti sthānāsedhaḥ | kālāsedhas tu dvitrirahobhis tvaṃ na karoṣi cet tatas tvayā iyān daṇḍo rājñe dātavya iti | ‘pravāsāt’ grāmāntaram anyatra vā gacchasi pūrvavad daṇḍya iti | ‘karmaṇaḥ’ yady adatvā gṛhanirmāṇādikaṃ karoṣi pūrvavad daṇḍya iti | evaṃ caturvidham āsedham āsiddho na laṅghayed iti | tathā –
kṣetrārāmagṛhādīni dhanadhānyādikaṃ tathā |
anyāyavādināṃ caitāny āsedhyāni vādinā ||
etāni kṣetrādīni vivādāspadāny evāsedhavyāni, nānyāni | tac ca kṣetrādiṣv api dravyotpacyupāyabhūtabījāvāpādayo niṣeddhavyāḥ, āseddhuḥ parājaye bhogadaṇḍayor dātavyatvaśravaṇāt |
mithyāsedhe punar dāpyo bhogadaṇḍaṃ ca tatsamam |
āseddhā tu svam āsedhaṃ svayam evotsṛjed yadi ||
iti |
na tasyātikramād doṣo na ca daṇḍaṃ prakalpayet |
rājño nivedanād ūrdhvam āseddhā notsṛjet svayam ||
utsṛjaṃś ca damaṃ dāpya āsiddhaś ca na laṅghalet |
āsedhane prāg āvedanāt mocane āseddhā na daṇḍyaḥ | ūrdhvaṃ tūtsṛjan daṇḍya eva | vivādaparājayaprāptaṃ damaśabdenocyate | athānāsedhyān āha nāradaḥ |
nadīsantārakāntāradurdeśopaplavādiṣu |
āsiddhas taṃ parāsedham utkrāman nāparādhnuyāt ||
niveṣṭukāmo rogārto yiyakṣur vyasane sthitaḥ |
abhiyuktas tathānyena rājakāryodyatas tathā ||
niveṣṭukāmaḥ vivāhābhimukhaḥ | kālaviśeṣād anāseddhyān āha |
gavāṃ pracāre gopālāḥ sasyārambhe kṛṣīvalāḥ |
śilpinaś cāpi tatkāla āyudhīyaś ca vigrahe ||
aprāptavyavahārāś ca dūto dānonmukho vratī |
viṣamasthāś ca nāseddhyā na cainān āhvayen nṛpaḥ ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
vṛkṣaparvatam ārūḍhā hastyaśvarathanausthitāḥ |
vimānasthāś ca te sarve nāseddhyāḥ kāryasādhakaiḥ ||
yas tv indriyanirodhena vyāhārocchvasanādibhiḥ |
āsedhayed anāsedhyān sa daṇḍyo na tv atikramī ||
evam āvaśyake śarīravyāpāre niṣedhaṃ kurvan daṇḍyaḥ, na ca pūrvāparādhakṛd ity arthaḥ | smṛtyantare |
akalyabālasthaviraviṣamasthakriyākulān |
kāryātipātivyasaninṛpakāryodyatāṃs tathā ||
mattonmattapramattārtabhṛtyān naivāhvayen nṛpaḥ |
na hīnapakṣāṃ yuvatiṃ kule jātāṃ prasūtikām ||
sarvavarṇottamāṃ kanyāṃ na jñātiprabhukāṃ striyam |
jñātiprabhukāḥ jñātiparatantrāḥ | tathoktam |
patipakṣaḥ prabhuḥ strīṇāṃ pitṛpakṣas tadatyaye |
viniyogātmarakṣāsu haraṇe ca s īśvaraḥ || iti |
kvacid viṣaye pūrvāpavādam āha |
tadadhīnakuṭumbinyaḥ svairiṇyo gaṇikāś ca yāḥ |
niṣkulā yāś ca patitās tāsām āhvānam arhati ||
rājñeti śeṣaḥ | tatrākalyabālasthavirastrīṇāṃ svarūpeṇa vyavahārānadhikāriṇāṃ putrādikam anyaṃ vā suhṛdaṃ samāśritya vyavahāraḥ pravartitavyaḥ | tasminn arthe viśeṣam āha hārītaḥ |
yo na bhrātā na ca pitā na putro na niyogakṛt |
parārthyavādī daṇḍyaḥ syāt vyavahāreṣu vibruvan ||
teṣāṃ jayaparājayau mūlapuruṣasyaiva jayaparājayāv iti nāradaḥ |
arthinā saṃniyukto vā pratyarthiprahito ‘pi vā |
yo yasyārthe vivadate tayor jayaparājayau ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
apragalbhajaḍonmattastrībālarogiṇām |
pūrvottaraṃ vaded yaḥ sa niyuktaś cen na doṣabhāk ||
brahmahatyāsurāpānasteyagurvaṅganāgame |
anyeṣv asabhyavādeṣu prativādī na dīyate ||
anyatra |
manuṣyamāreṇe steye paradārābhimarśane |
abhakṣyabhakṣaṇe caiva kanyāharaṇadūṣaṇe ||
pāruṣye kūṭakaraṇe nṛpadrohe tathaiva ca |
prativādī na dāpyaḥ syāt kartā tu vivadet svayam || iti |
mahāpātakādyabhiyoge akalyādīnāṃ sarveṣāṃ svarūpeṇaivāhvānaṃ kartavyam |
yasyābhiyogaṃ kurute satyenāśaṅkayā tathā |
tam evānāyayed rājā mudrayā puruṣeṇa vā ||
nāradaḥ |
deśaṃ kālaṃ ca vijñāya kāryāṇāṃ ca balābalam |
akalyādīn api śanaiḥ yānair āhvāyayen nṛpaḥ ||
devabrāhaṇarājadrohādāv akalyabālasthavirādīnām api svarūpeṇāhvānaṃ yānādibhiḥ kartavyam ity arthaḥ | evaṃ prativādisaṃnidhāne saty eva kartavyam iti yājñavalkyaḥ |
ubhayor pratibhūr grāhyaḥ samartha kāryanirṇaye |
kātyāyanaḥ |
atha cet pratibhūr nāsti vādayogyasya vādinaḥ |
sa rakṣito dinasyānte dadyād dūtāya vetanam ||
kāryanirṇaye samartho darśabandhadaṇḍādiyogyaḥ | atra kātyāyanaḥ |
dvijātiḥ pratibhūhīno rakṣyaḥ syād bāhyacāribhiḥ |
śūdrādīn pratibhūhīnān bandhayen nigaḍena tu ||
atikrame ‘payāne vā daṇḍayet taṃ paṇāṣṭakam |
nityakarmoparodhas tu na kāryaḥ sarvavarṇinām ||
iti śrīmadvaradarājīye vyavahāranirṇaye
āvedanakāṇḍaṃ samāptam
3. vyavahāramātṛkākāṇḍam
vyavahāramātṛkāyām abhiyogasvarūpam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
pratyarthino ‘grato lekhyaṃ yathāveditam arthinā |
samāmāsatadardhāharnāmajātyādicihnitam ||
arthaḥ prayojanam | sisādhayiṣitaṃ yasyāsti so ‘rthī | tasya pratipakṣatayā vyavasthitaḥ pratyarthī | tasya agrataḥ saṃnidhau lekhyaṃ lekhanīyam | arthinā yathāveditaṃ pūrvaṃ saṃbandhapraveśanakāle yathālekhitaṃ tathā lekhyam | anyathā cet “anyavādī kriyādveṣī” iti parājayaprasaṅgāt | nanu yathāveditasya pūrvam eva saṃbandhapraveśanakāle likhitatvāt punar lekhanam anarthakam, ity atrāha “samāmāsa” iti. nānārthakaṃ punar lekhanaṃ pūrvaṃ vādinā kālādiviśeṣaṇavidhuraṃ sādhyamātram āveditam | udāharaṇam tu – amuko hiraṇyādikaṃ matto gṛhītvā idādīṃ prārthyamāno ‘pi na dadātīti | tad adhunā pratyarthino ‘grata samāmāsādikālaviśeṣacihnitaṃ śuddhe (śuddhaṃ?) patre punar lekhanīyam iti viśeṣaḥ | ādigrahaṇena dravyasaṃkhyāpramāṇādīnāṃ grahaṇam | tathā coktaṃ bṛhaspatinā |
deśakālavihīnaś ca dravyasaṃkhyāvivarjitaḥ |
sādhyapramāṇahīnaś ca pakṣo ‘nādeya iṣyate ||
tathā ca prajāpatiḥ |
pratyarthino ‘grataḥ sthitvā vādipakṣaṃ pravartayet |
niravadyaṃ sapratijñaṃ pramāṇāgamasaṃyutam ||
dravyasaṃkhyodayaṃ pīḍāṃ kṣamāliṅgaṃ ca lekhayet |
atra viśeṣam āha kātyāyanaḥ |
deśaḥ kālas tathā sthānaṃ saṃniveśas tathaiva ca |
jātisaṃjñādhivāsaś ca pramāṇaṃ kṣetranāma ca ||
pitṛpaitāmahaṃ caiva pūrvarājānukīrtanam |
sthāvareṣu vivādeṣu daśaitāni niveśayet ||
tad ayam arthaḥ — deśo madhyadeśādiḥ | sthānaṃ vārāṇasyādi | saṃniveśaḥ tatraiva pūrvādidigvibhāgaparicchannaḥ samyaṅniveśito gṛhakṣetrādiḥ | jātiḥ arthipratyarthinoḥ brāhmaṇatvādiḥ | saṃjñā devadattādiḥ | adhivāsaḥ samīpadeśaḥ | pramāṇaṃ nivartanādiparimāṇam | kṣetranāma śālikṣetraṃ kramukakṣetraṃ kṛṣṇabhūmiḥ pāṇḍubhūmir iti | pituḥ pitāmahasya ca nāmārthipratyarthinoḥ | pūrveṣāṃ trayāṇāṃ rājñāṃ nāmakīrtanaṃ ceti | samāmāsādīnāṃ yasmin vyavahāre yāvad upayujyate tatra tāval lekhanīyam iti tātparyārthaḥ | tathā ca kātyāyanaḥ |
valāṃ pradeśaṃ viṣayaṃ sthānaṃ jātyākṛtī vayaḥ |
sādhyapramāṇaṃ draṣṭavyaṃ saṃkhyā nāma tathātmanaḥ ||
kṣamāliṅgāni vākyāni pakṣaṃ saṃkīrtya kalpayet ||
nānyad iti vyavasthā | yasya viśeṣaṇasya yatra dṛṣṭa upayogaḥ tadviśeṣaṇaṃ tatra vivakṣaṇīyam, nānyad iti vyavasthā, tatra,
ādhau pratigrahe krīte pūrvā tu balavattarā |
iti vacanāt, tatraiva kālaviśeṣaṇasyopayogaḥ | deśādeḥ sthāvaravyavahāra evopayogaḥ | sāmantair niyantavyatvāt | tena sādhyasiddhim icchatāmukenāmuke kāle ‘mukaṃ dravyam iyatsaṃkhyākkam amukayā vṛddhyā matto gṛhītaṃ prārthanākāle dāsyāmīti | prārthitam adhunā na prayacchatīty abhiyogo vaktavyaḥ | anyathā kenāpi kim api gṛhītam ity ucyamāne na kaścin nyāyaḥ pravartate | anekeṣāṃ yugapat kāryasaṃnipāte kramam āha prajāpatiḥ |
arthānarthāv ubhau buddhvā dharmādharmau ca kevalau |
varṇakrameṇa sarvāṇi paśyet kāryāṇi kāryiṇām ||
atra viśeṣam āha nāradaḥ |
yasya vāpy adhikā pīḍā kāryaṃ vāpy adhikaṃ bhavet |
tasyārthavādo dātavyo nayaḥ pūrvaṃ nivedayet ||
arthavādo ‘bhiyogavāda ity arthaḥ | bṛhaspatiḥ |
ahaṃpūrvikayā tāvad arthipratyarthinor yadā |
vādo varṇānupūrvyeṇa grāhyaḥ pīḍām avekṣya vā ||
smṛtyantare |
yad yad agurutaraṃ kāryaṃ na tat pūrvaṃ viśodhayet |
varṇakrameṇa vā paśyet guṇayogena vā punaḥ ||
devarājakṛto doṣaḥ tasmin kāle yadā bhavet |
avadhityāgamātreṇa na bhavet sa parājitaḥ ||
abhiyoktur asamarthatve tasya śikṣārthaṃ kāladānaṃ kartavyam iti kātyāyanaḥ |
abhiyoktāpragalbhatvāt vaktuṃ notsahate yadi |
tasya kālaḥ pradātavyaḥ kāryaśaktyanurodhataḥ ||
śvo lekhanaṃ vā labhate tryahaṃ saptāham eva vā |
matir utpadyate yāvat vivāde vaktum icchataḥ ||
tathā ca smṛtyantare |
bhūmau hi lekhayet tāvat yāvad artho na niścitaḥ |
ūnādhikaṃ tu saṃśodhya paścāt patre niveśayet ||
etad uktaṃ bhavati | āvedanakāle rāgādinā nyūnādhikaṃ vā āveditaṃ phalakādau likhitam, tathāpi bhāṣākāle krodhādyupaśamanāt prativādisaṃnidhau vivakṣitam upādāya heyaṃ tyaktvā śuddhaṃ patre lekhayed iti | nanūktasyānyathākaraṇe avyavasthā syāt | nāvyavasthā, nāradena vyavasthā uktatvāt | tathā hi |
bhāṣāyā uttaraṃ yāvat pratyarthī na niveśayet |
arthī tu lekhayet tāvad yāvad vastu vivakṣitam ||
tathā,
śodhayet pūrvavādaṃ tu yāvan nottaradarśanam |
avaṣṭabdhasyottareṇa nivṛttaṃ śodhanaṃ bhavet ||
uttaroktau tu “duṣṭasya pakṣasya nivṛttir eva śodhanam” iti vacanād uttarottarakālam uktasya anyathā karaṇaṃ na saṃbhavatīti nāvyavasthāprasaṅgaḥ | tasya duṣṭasya pakṣasya śodhanam akṛtvottaroktau,
rāgāl lobhād bhayād vāpi smṛtyapetādikāriṇaḥ |
ity uktarītyā sabhyāḥ pṛthak pṛthak daṇḍyāḥ | vyavahāradarśanaṃ punaḥ pratijñāntarapūrvakaṃ kartavyam eva |
anekapadasaṃkīrṇaḥ pūrvapakṣo na siddhyati |
iti yadīdaṃ vacanaṃ tat sādhanabhedapratipattiviṣayam | udāharaṇam – kasmiṃścit kāle suvarṇam anenāpahṛtaṃ śapathena sādhayāmi | kadācid rajatam apy apahṛtaṃ sākṣibhiḥ sādhayāmi | kadācid vartulavṛddhyarthaṃ matto gṛhītaṃ yācyamānaṃ na dadāti, tal lekhyena sādhayāmi | ity evam ukte sādhanabhedāt krameṇa niraṇayo varṇanīyaḥ, na yugapat | tathātve ‘ṣṭādaśapadānāṃ saṃkaraḥ syāt | ato yathābhiyoktṛpuruṣabhede kriyābhedaḥ | evaṃ hetubhede ‘pi kriyābhedaḥ | ato bhinnasādhanānekapadasaṃkīrṇaḥ pūrvapakṣo yugapan na siddhyatīti kriyābhedāt kālabhedena vyavahāro yujyate | yatra tv anekapadānām apy ekasādhanena sādhyatā tatra sādhyabhedād anekapratijñā yuktety āha |
bahupratijñaṃ yat kāryaṃ vyavahāre suniścitam |
kāmaṃ tad api gṛhṇīyāt rājā tattvabubhutsayā ||
suniścitam ekena sādhanena sādhayituṃ yogyatayā niścitam ity arthaḥ | tatrābhiyogo dviprakāra ity āha nāradaḥ |
abhiyogas tu vijñeyaḥ śaṅkā tattvābhiyogataḥ |
ubhayatra kāraṇam āha |
śaṅkāsatāṃ tu saṃsargāt tattvaṃ hoḍhādidarśanāt |
duṣṭaiḥ saṃsargāt śaṅkā bhavati | hoḍhā loptṛcihnam iti naṣṭaikadeśadravyam iti yāvat | apahṛtadravyaikadeśasya darśanān niścaya iti | ubhayatrodāharaṇaṃ tu – coraiḥ saṃsargān madīyaṃ dravyam anenāpahṛtam iti śaṅkā bhavatīti śaṅkābhiyogaḥ | sa caikarūpa eva | naṣṭadravyasaikadeśadarśanāt tattvābhiyogaḥ, niścayābhiyoga ity arthaḥ | sa ca vidhipratiṣedharūpeṇa dvividha ity āha kātyāyanaḥ |
nyāyyaṃ vā necchate kartum anyāyyaṃ vā karoty ayam |
na lekhayati yas tv evaṃ tasya pakṣo na siddhyati || iti |
“nyāyyaṃ na karoti” “anyayyaṃ vā karoti” iti tattvābhiyogo dvividhaḥ | udāharaṇaṃ tu – kāle dāsyāmīty uktvā vṛddhyarthaṃ matto hiraṇyādikaṃ gṛhītvā kāle prāpte yācyamāno na dadāti, nyāyaṃ na karotīty abhiyogaḥ | madīyaṃ hiraṇyādikam ayaṃ balās aparatīti anyāyaṃ karotīti, anyāyābhiyogaḥ | asyaiva dvividhasya abhiyogasya ṛṇādisādhyabhedenāṣṭādaśadhā bhedam āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
dvipado vyavahāraḥ syād dhanahiṃsāsamudbhavaḥ |
dvisaptako ‘rthamūlas tu hiṃsāmūlaś caturvidhaḥ ||
tathā ca kātyāyanaḥ |
dve pade (bhede) sādhyabhedāt tu padāṣṭādaśatāṃ gate |
aṣṭādaśakriyābhedāt bhinnāny atra sahasraśaḥ ||
vādiprativādipuruṣāṇāṃ kriyābhedāt sahasraśo bahuśa ity arthaḥ | atha tāny aṣṭādaśapadāny āha nāradaḥ |
ṛṇādānaṃ hy upanidhiḥ saṃbhūyotthānam eva ca |
dattasya punar ādānam aśuśrūṣābhyupetya ca ||
vetanasyānapākarma tathaivāsvāmivikrayaḥ |
vikrīyāsaṃpradānaṃ ca krītvānuśaya eva ca ||
samayasyānapākarma vivādaḥ kṣetrajas tathā |
strīpuṃsayoś ca saṃbandho dāyabhāgo ‘tha sāhasam ||
vākpāruṣyaṃ tathaivoktaṃ daṇḍapāruṣyam eva ca |
dyūtaṃ prakīrṇakaṃ caivety aṣṭādaśa padaṃ smṛtam ||
evaṃ pūrvapakṣe ‘bhihite uttaraṃ vyavahāramātṛkāyāṃ yājñavalkya āha |
śrutārthasyottaraṃ lekhyaṃ pūrvāvedakasaṃnidhau |
śrutaḥ anubhūtaḥ pratyakṣeṇaiva yena bhāṣite ‘rthaḥna paravacanāt tasya uttaraṃ lekhanīyam, pūrvāvedakasyārthinaḥ saṃnidhāne | kātyāyanaḥ |
yadā tv evaṃvidhaḥ pakṣaḥ kalpitaḥ pūrvavādinā |
dadyāt tatpakṣasaṃbandhaṃ prativādī tadottaram ||
pakṣasaṃbandham uttarakālaṃ kṛtsnapakṣārthanirākaraṇasamartham uttaraṃ dadyād iti nāradaḥ |
pakṣasya vyāpakaṃ sāram asaṃdigdham anākulam |
avyākhyāgamyam ity evam uttaraṃ tadvido viduḥ ||
śālīnatvād bhayād vāpi pratyarthismṛtivibhramāt |
kālaṃ prārthayate yatra tatremaṃ labdhum arhati ||
ekāhaṃ vā tryahaṃ vāpi pañca saptāham eva vā |
māsaṃ māsatrayaṃ varṣaṃ labhate śaktyapekṣayā ||
samātīte dinaṃ deyaṃ trirātraṃ tu ṣaḍabdake |
dvyabde trirātram eva syāt saptāhaṃ dvādaśābdike ||
pañcāhaṃ navavarṣe syāt daśāhaṃ viṃśatau bhavet |
māsārdhaṃ pañcaviṃśatyā māsaṃ triṃśatsamāsv api ||
tripakṣaṃ parato deyaṃ vivādaṃ vadatāṃ nṛṇām |
kālaṃ śaktiṃ viditvā tu kāryāṇāṃ ca balābalam ||
alpaṃ vā bahu vā kālaṃ dadyāt pratyarthine prabhuḥ |
kiṃ bahunā |
sadyaḥ kṛtyeṣu kāryeṣu sadya eva vivādayet |
kāle ‘tīteṣu vā kālaṃ dadyāt pratyarthine nṛpaḥ ||
atra prajāpatiḥ |
dinam ekam atha dve vā trīṇi vā pañca sapta vā |
kālas tv ṛṇādau dāpyaḥ syāt tripakṣād api (vadhikaḥ) smṛtaḥ ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
saṃvatsaraṃ vāmanako jaḍo vyādhiprapīḍitaḥ |
digantarād gacchati ced yas tu svakṛtinirṇayaḥ ||
yadā punaḥ kāle datte devakṛtaṃ rājakṛtaṃ vā vilambanaṃ bhavati, tadānyaḥ kālo deyaḥ |
datte ‘pi kāle deyaḥ syāt punaḥ kāryasya gauravāt |
hārītaḥ |
kṛte kārye vivāde tu sadyo ‘nantaram uttaram |
aparatrāgate kāle kālaḥ kāle ‘pi dīyate ||
atha sadyo vivādam āha bṛahaspatiḥ |
dhenāv anaḍuhi kṣetre strīṣu prajanane tathā |
nyāse yācitake datte tathaiva krayavikraye ||
kanyāyā dūṣaṇe steye kalahe sāhaseṣu ca |
upadhau kūṭasākṣye ca sadya eva vivādayet ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
sāhasasteyapāruṣyago’bhiśāpātyaye striyāḥ |
vivādayet sadya eva kālo ‘nyatrecchayā smṛtaḥ ||
atrottaraṃ kātyāyanaḥ |
mithyāsaṃpatipattyā vā pratyavaskandanena vā |
prāṅnyāyavidhisiddhyā vāpy uttaraṃ syāc caturvidham ||
udāharaṇaṃ tu — matto hiraṇyādikaṃ gṛhītam ity abhiyoge kṛte, uttaravādinā na gṛhītam ity ekam | satyaṃ gṛhītaṃ dāsyāmīty aparam | gṛhītvā dattam ity anyat | asmin vivāde mayā pūrvam eva vijita iti | evaṃ caturvidham uttaraṃ saṃbhavati | na punaḥ gṛhītvā na prayacchāmīty uttaraṃ saṃbhavati | tatra na gṛhītam ity uttare ity uttare pūrvavādinā grahaṇaṃ sādhyam | satyaṃ gṛhītaṃ dāsyāmīty uttare na kriyāyāḥ prayojanam iti, saṃpratipatteḥ | gṛhītvā dattam ity uttare grahaṇasyābhyupagatatvād uttaravādinā pratidānaṃ sādhyam | asmin vivāde mayā pūrvam eva jita ity ukte, jayo jayapatreṇa vyavahāradarśibhir vā bhāvayitavyaḥ | tathā ca vṛddhavasiṣṭhaḥ |
satyānṛte kāraṇaṃ ca prāṅnyāyaś cottaraṃ pṛthak |
sādhyasya satyavacanaṃ pratipattir udāhṛtā ||
kāraṇaṃ syād avaskando mithyā syāt satyanihṇutiḥ |
ācāreṇāvasanno ‘pi punar lekhayate yadi |
so ‘bhidheyo jitaḥ pūrvaṃ prāṅnyāyas tu sa ucyate ||
tatrottaroktikramaṃ kātyāyana āha |
tathye tathyaṃ prayuñjīta mithyā mithyā (?) ca lekhayet |
kāraṇaṃ kāraṇopete prāṅnyāye tu jayaṃ tathā || iti |
tatra sādhanaprakāram āha hārītaḥ |
prāṅnyāye kāraṇoktau ca pratyarthī nirdiśet kriyām |
mithyokau pūrvavādī tu pratipattau na sā bhavet ||
tathā ca nāradaḥ |
mithyā kriyā pūrvavāde kāraṇe prativādini |
prāṅnyāyavidhisiddhau tu jayapatrakriyā bhavet ||
tatra mithyācāturvidhye hārītaḥ |
mithyaitan nābhijānāmi tadā tatra na saṃnidhiḥ |
ajātaś cāsmi tatkāla iti mithyā caturvidhā ||
sarvathā yathā kathaṃcid abhiyogābhāve mithyā bhavatīty aikārthyam eveti kātyāyanaḥ |
śrutvā bhāṣārtham anyas tu yadā taṃ pratiṣedhati |
arthataḥ śabdato vāpi mithyā taj jñeyam uttaram ||
anyair apy evam eva saṃkṣipya mithyālakṣaṇam uktam |
abhiyukto ‘bhiyogasya yadi kuryād apahnavam |
mithyā tat tu vijānīyād uttaraṃ vyavahārataḥ ||
manuḥ |
arthinā likhito yo ‘rthaḥ pratyarthī yadi taṃ tathā |
prapadya kāraṇaṃ brūyāt pratyavaskandanaṃ hi tat ||
etad evādhāryam iti kaiścid ucyate | vṛddhakātyāyanaḥ |
vyapetā tu kriyā yatra hy abhyuktena kīrtitā |
ādhāryaṃ tad vijānīyāt pūrvapakṣasya nānyathā ||
vyapetā tu kriyā yatra yadā nyagbhāva eva vā |
hārītaḥ |
ācāreṇāvasanno ‘pi punar lekhayate yadi |
so ‘bhidheyo jitaḥ pūrvaṃ prāṅnyāyas tu sa ucyate ||
tatra kāraṇottaraṃ dvividham, śuddhaṃ mithyāsahacaritaṃ ca | tatra śuddhaysodāharaṇam – suvarṇaśataṃ madīyam anena gṛhītam ity ukte satyaṃ gṛhītam, tat pratidattam iti | etad eva pratyavaskandanam ity ucyate | asādhyatvenānyagbhāvāt | asādhyatvaṃ cābhyupagamāt | tathā yair eva viśeṣaṇair yukto vādinārtho likhitaḥ, tair eva yuktam abhyupagamya prativādī yadi kāraṇam āha tad ādāryam ity ucyate | udāharaṇaṃ tu – rūpakaśatam anena gṛhītam, na dattaṃ ca, tad dhiraṇyādikrayārtham ity ādhārya gṛhītaṃ na dattam ity asyārthasyābhyupagamād evāsādhyatvena nyagbhāvāt | tatra mithyāsahacaritasyodāharaṇam – yathā śṛṅgiṇī kṛṣṇā madīyā gaur asmadgṛhe jātā, amukasmin kāle naṣṭā, sā caitasya gṛhe dṛṣṭā ity ukte, uttaravādī tv āha – mithyaitat, sa mama gṛhe jātaiva, tvatpradarśitakālāt prāg api madgṛhe sthitaivety ukte na śūddhamithyā, kāraṇopanyāsāt | nāpi śuddhakāraṇam, pakṣādyupagamābhāvāt, “vādinā likhito yo ‘rthaḥ” iti manunā śuddhakāraṇalakṣaṇābhidhānāt | na cānuttaram, vyāpakatvena pakṣanirākaraṇasamarthatvāt | tad etan mithyāsahacaritaṃ kāraṇam |
prāṅnyāyas tu trividhaḥ | aindrasthāne jitasya prāṅnyāyavidhisiddhau jayapatreṇāhaṃ bhāvayāmīty ekaḥ, kulavyavahāre ca jayapatrābhāvāt, “kulyāḥ kulavivādeṣu vijñeyās te ‘pi sākṣiṇaḥ” iti vacanāt | tair eva vyavahāradarśibhiḥ bhāvayāmīty dvitīyaḥ,
rājñā dharmāsanasthena yac chrutaṃ tattvadarśinā |
nānyaḥ sākṣī bhavet tatra muktvā rājānam īśvaram ||
iti vacanāt, sākṣyabhāvāt | rājñā bhāvayāmīti tṛtīyaḥ |
tatra “śrutārthasyottaraṃ lekhyam” ity ekavacanaśravaṇāt, bahūnāṃ hetūnāṃ saṃkaro nirastaḥ | udāharaṇam – madīyaṃ hiraṇyaṃ rūpakaṃ ratnāni vāsāṃsi cānena gṛhītānīty ukte, satyaṃ hiraṇyaṃ gṛhītam, dāsyāmi, rūpakaṃ ca pradattam, vāsāṃsi na gṛhītāni | hiraṇyādiratnāni gṛhītāni krayārtham ity ukta, avaśyaṃ mithyākāraṇayor ubhayatra kriyā bhavati, tac ca na saṃbhavati, ekasmin vivāde ubhayoḥ sādhyābhāvāt | etat sarvaṃ kātyāyana āha |
pakṣaikadeśe yat satyam ekadeśe ca kāraṇam |
mithyā caivaikadeśe ca saṃkarāt tad anuttaram ||
ity uktvā, tatraiva tenaivoktam |
na caikasmin vivāde tu kriyā syāḍ vādinor dvayoḥ |
na cārthasiddhir ubhayor na caikatra kriyādvayam ||
tatra saṃkarokāv evaṃ kartavyam iti hārītaḥ |
mithyottaraṃ kāraṇaṃ ca syātām ekatra ced ubhe |
satyaṃ vāpi sahānyena tatra grāhyaṃ kim uttaram ||
ity uktvoktam |
yat prabhūtārthaviṣayaṃ yatra vā syāt kriyāphalam |
uttaraṃ tatra vijñeyam asaṃkīrṇam ato ‘nyathā ||
evam uttaraṃ tatrāsaṃkīrṇaṃ bhavati | ato ‘nyathā saṃkīrṇaṃ vādabhedāpādakaṃ bhavatīty arthaḥ | udāharaṇam – asau mattaḥ suvarṇaśatam ekaṃ ca rajataṃ gṛhītvā yācyamāno na prayacchatīty abhiyoge, suvarṇaśataṃ na gṛhītam, rajatam ekaṃ pratidattam ity abhihite na vādabhedaḥ | mithyāṃśe pūrvavādinaḥ kriyā prāptā prabhūtaviṣayeti, pūrvavādy eva tatsādhanād eva rajatapratidānābhāvam api sādhayed iti na vādabhedaprasaṅgaḥ | kriyāphalasyodāharaṇam – matto ‘sāv ekaṃ suvarṇaṃ gṛhītvā yācyamāno na kevalaṃ na dadāti, kiṃ tu macchirasi tāḍitavān ity abhiyoge, na śirasi tāḍitam, suvarṇaṃ ca pratidattam ity uttare ‘bhihite ‘pi, na vādabhedaḥ | pūrvavādinaḥ kriyāyā daṇḍagurutvāpādakatvāt, pūrvavādy eva śirasi tāḍanaṃ suvarṇapratidānābhāvaṃ ca sādhayet | udāharaṇaṃ tu – asau mattaḥ suvarṇadvayaṃ gṛhītvā yācyamāno na prayacchati ity abhiyoge, ekaṃ na gṛhītam ekaṃ gṛhītam, tac ca pratidattaṃ cety uttare, daṇḍārthayoḥ sāmyāt vādabhedaḥ | yatra punar ekasminn eva sādhye, mithyākāraṇe bhavataḥ | udāharaṇam – śṛṅgiṇī gaur madīyā amukasmin māsi naṣṭā, sāsya gṛhe dṛśyamānā prārthyamāna na dīyata ity abhiyoge, uttaravādī tu, na tvadīyā sā gaur iti praiṣidhya, madīyaiva sā ity ātmasaṃbandhaṃ pratijñāya kāraṇam abhihitavān, sā gaur mamaiva, tvaduktakālāt prāg api madgṛhe sthiteti | evaṃ mithyākāraṇasaṃkare uttare, uttaram eva kāraṇaṃ grāhyam,
mithyākāraṇayor vāpi grāhyaṃ kāraṇam uttaram |
iti vacanāt | na ca “abhāvavādini kriyākāraṇe prativādini” iti vacanāt | nanu “mithyā kriyā pūrvavāde” itīdam api vacanam eva | satyam, tacchuddhaṃ mithyābhiprāyeṇa | nanu “kāraṇe prativādini” iti kim iti śuddhakāraṇābhiprāyeṇa na bhavati | maivam | “prativādini” ity anena saṃkarottare mithyānirākaraṇamātram atrocyate | “mithyākriyā pūrvavāde” ity anena yasyāsādhyam asti asau sādhayed ity ucyate | kāraṇottare punar uttaravādina eva sādhyam, “kāraṇāt pūrvapakṣo ‘pi hy uttaratvaṃ prapadyate” iti vacanāt | tena “mithyottare pūrvavādini” ityanena “kāraṇottare uttaravādini” ity asyārthasya siddhatvāt, “kāraṇe prativādini” ity anena mithyānirākaraṇam evocyata iti suṣṭūktam | atha uttarābhāsānāṃ lakṣaṇam āha kātyāyanaḥ |
saṃdigham anyat prakṛtād atyalpam atibhūri ca |
pakṣaikadeśavyāpyaṃ yat tac ca naivottare bhavet ||
tathā ca hārītaḥ |
vyatyastapadam avyāpi nigūḍhārtham athākulam |
vyākhyāgamyam asāraṃ ca nottaraṃ śasyate budhaiḥ ||
udāharaṇāni —
saṃdigdham yathā – suvarṇaśatam anena gṛhītam ity ukte, satyaṃ gṛhītam, suvarṇaśataṃ māṣaśataṃ veti prakṛtād anyathā suvarṇasya śatābhiyoge vastraśataṃ dhārayāmīti | atyalpaṃ yathā – suvarṇaśatābhiyoge pañcāśataṃ dhārayāmīti | atibhūri yathā – śatābhiyoge sahasraṃ dhārayāmīti | pakṣaikadeśavyāpi yathā – hiraṇyavastrābhiyoge hiraṇyaṃ dhārayāmīti | vyastapadaṃ yathā – ṛṇādānābhiyoge padāntareṇottaram | yathā suvarṇaśatābhiyoge anenāhaṃ tāḍita iti | avyāpi yathā – deśasthānādiviśeṣaṇāvyāpīty arthaḥ | yathā madhyadeśe vārāṇasyām uttarasyāṃ diśi śālikṣetram apahṛtam ity ukte kṣetraṃ nāpahṛtam iti | nigūḍhārthaṃ yathā – suvarṇaśatābhiyoge kim aham eva dhārayāmīti | arthī prāḍvivākaḥ sabhyo vānyo vānyasmai dhārayati, sūcayatīti nigūḍhārtham | ākulaṃ pūrvāparaviruddhaṃ yathā – śatābhiyoge satyaṃ gṛhītaṃ na dhārayāmīti | vyākhyāgamyaṃ yathā – duḥśliṣṭavibhaktasamāsādhyāhārābhidhānena vyākhyāgamyam | adeśabhāṣābhidhānena vā yathā – suvarṇaśataviṣaye pitrarṇābhiyoge gṛhītaṃ vacanāt suvarṇānāṃ pitur jānāmīti | atra gṛhītaśatasya pitur vacanāt, suvarṇānāṃ śataṃ gṛhītam iti na jānāmīti vyākhyāgamyatvam | asāraṃ yathā – tac ca viruddhaṃ suvarṇaśataṃ vṛddhyā gṛhītam, vṛddhir eva dattā na mūlyam ity abhiyoge, satyaṃ vṛddhir dattā na mūlyaṃ gṛhītam iti | kātyāyanaḥ |
unmattamattanirdhūtamahāpātakadūṣitāḥ |
jaḍāndhavṛddhā strībālā vijñeyās tu niruttarāḥ ||
atra bṛhaspatiḥ |
pūrvapakṣe yathārthaṃ tu na dadyād uttaraṃ tu yaḥ |
pratyarthī dāpanīyaḥ syāt sāmādibhir upakramaiḥ ||
sāmādilakṣaṇaṃ tathā |
priyapūrvaṃ vacaḥ sāma bhedas tu bhayadarśanam |
arthāpakarṣaṇaṃ daṇḍas tāḍanaṃ bandhanaṃ tathā ||
upāyaiś codyamānas tu na dadyād uttaraṃ tu yaḥ |
atikrāntaṃ saptarātre jito ‘sau daṇḍam arhati ||
mohād vā yadi vā śāṭhyād yan noktaṃ pūrvavādinā |
uttarāntargataṃ vāpi tad grāhyam ubhayor api ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
svabhāvāl likhite vākye prārabdhe kāryanirṇaye |
anuktaṃ tatra yo brūyāt tadarthāt sa tu hīyate ||
idaṃ parastrībhūmyṛṇādānavyatiriktaviṣayam, tatra arthahānyabhāvāt | sūtrasthānīyāṃ vyavahāramātṛkāṃ nigamayati yājñavalkyaḥ |
tato ‘rthī lekhayet sadyaḥ pratijñātārthasādhanam |
tatsiddhau siddhim āpnoti viparītam ato ‘nyathā ||
catuṣpād vyavahāro ‘yaṃ vivādeṣūpadarśitaḥ |
tataḥ tasmād uttarakārād anantaram, arthī lekhayet sādhyavān | sadyaḥ eva nābhiyogottarapratīkṣā kartavyā | sādhyate ‘neneti sādhanaṃ pramāṇam | tatas tasya pratijñātārthasiddhau jayapatralakṣaṇaprāptiḥ siddhiśabdenocyate | viparītaṃ parājayaḥ | anyathā tasyāsiddhau | ataḥ sadya evārthī pratijñātārthasya sādhanaṃ lekhayed iti | atrārthigrahaṇena yasya sādhyam asti tasya pratigrahaḥ | tathā ca sati prāṅnyāye kāraṇoktau pratyarthī vārthī jātaḥ tasyaiva sādhyayogena pūrvavāditvāt | tathā ca kātyāyanaḥ |
kāraṇāt pūrvapakṣo ‘pi hy uttaratvaṃ prapadyate |
ataḥ kriyā sadā prokto pūrvapakṣaprasādhinī ||
sādhyavādinaḥ kriyety arthaḥ | bṛhaspatiḥ |
prāṅnyāye kāraṇoktau ca pratyarthī nirdiśet kriyām |
mithyoktau pūrvavādī tu pratipattau na sā bhavet ||
tatra kāraṇasya sādhyatvāt tannirdeśasya pratijñātvam asti | ataḥ prativādino ‘pi tasmin arthe sādhanaṃ yujyata eva | tathā coktaṃ nāradena |
sāras tu vyavahārāṇāṃ pratijñā samudāhṛtā |
taddhānau hīyate vādī taraṃs tām uttaro bhavet ||
evamuktanyāyenāyaṃ catuṣpād vyavahāro vivādeṣu vakṣyamāṇeṣūpadarśitaḥ | yat pūrvaṃ “vyavahārān nṛpaḥ paśyed vidvadbhiḥ” ity uktam, saṃpratipattau tu sādhananirapakṣatvād vivādaḥ | tathā ca bṛhaspatiḥ |
mithyoktau tu catuṣpāt syāt pratyavaskandane tathā |
prāṅnyāye ca sa vijñeyo dvipāt saṃpratipattiṣu ||
atra kātyāyanaḥ |
pūrvapakṣo ‘py uttaraś ca pratyākalitam eva ca |
kriyāpādaś ca vijñeyaḥ catuṣpāt samudāhṛtaḥ ||
tatra vyavahāramātṛkāyāṃ yājñavalkyena “pratyarthino ‘grato lekhyam” iti pratijñoktā | “śrutārthasyottaraṃ lekhyam” ity uttaram | “tato ‘rthī lekhayet” iti pratyākalitam | arthipratyarthinoḥ pratisādhanam asya vā bhavatīti vimarśaḥ pratyākalitam ucyate | “pratijñātārthasādhanam” iti kriyā | “tatsiddhau siddhim āpnoti” iti tadvyavahāraphalam | kecit pratyākalitaṃ vihāya jayaparājayāv evaikapāda iti manyante | tad ayuktam, jayāder vyavahāraphalatvāt saṃpratipattāv api vidyamānatvāt tripātprasaṅgaḥ | nanu nāradenānyathā catuṣpāttvam uktam |
dharmaś ca vyavahāraś ca caritraṃ rājaśāsanam |
catuṣpād vyavahāro ‘yam uttaraḥ pūrvabādhakaḥ || iti |
maivam | “catuṣpād vyavahāraḥ” catuṣprakāro vyavahāra ity arthaḥ | ata eva “uttaraḥ pūrvabādhakaḥ” ity uktam | atha kātyāyanaḥ saṃdhim āha |
pūrvottare tu likhite prakrānte kāryanirṇaye |
dvayoḥ saṃtaptayoḥ saṃdhiḥ syād ayaḥkhaṇḍayor iva ||
sākṣisabhyavikalpaś ca bhaved yatrobhayor api |
ḍolāyamāne kārye ca tatra saṃdhiḥ pravartate ||
pramāṇasamatā yatra bhedaḥ śāstracaritayoḥ |
tatra rājājñayā saṃdhir ubhayor api śasyate ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
yatra sāśayiko dharmo vyavahāraś ca pārthive |
saṃdhis tatra tu kartavyo ‘yasoḥ saṃtaptayor yathā ||
samaḥ saṃdhis tadā kāryo viṣamas tu nivartate ||
vyavahāramātṛkām uktvā vibhajya padāny āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
abhiyogam anistīrya nainaṃ pratyabhiyojayet |
abhiyuktaṃ na cānyena noktaṃ viprakṛtiṃ nayet ||
abhiyukto ‘bhiyogam anistīryābhiyoktāraṃ nābhiyojayet | anyenābhiyuktaṃ vā nābhiyuñjīta, yugapadvyavahāradvayāsaṃbhavāt | tathā āvedanakāle yad uktaṃ tad viprakṛtim anyathābhāvaṃ bhāṣākāle na nayet, anyavādinaḥ parājayaprasaṅgāt | tatra nāradaḥ |
pūrvavādaṃ parityajya yo ‘nyam ālambate naraḥ |
vādasaṃkramaṇāj jñeyo hīnavādī sa vai naraḥ ||
abhiyogam anistīryāpi pratyabhiyogam āha |
kuryāt pratyabhiyogo tu kalahe sāhaseṣu ca |
“kalahe sāhaseṣu ca” avaśyam abhiyogam anistīryaiva pratyabhiyogo dātavyaḥ | aham anena pūrvam āhataḥ śapto veti | yasmāt prathamapravṛttasya gurutaro doṣaḥ | tathā ca nāradaḥ |
pūrvam ākṣārayed yas tu niyataṃ syāt sa doṣabhāk |
paścād yaḥ so ‘py asatkārī pūrve tu vinayo guruḥ ||
tatra pratyabhiyogānuguṇo arthivādo dātavya ity āha nāradaḥ |
yasya vāpy adhikā pīḍā kāryaṃ vapy adhikaṃ bhavet |
tasyārthivādo (?) dātavyo na yaḥ pūrvaṃ nivedayet ||
tena pratyabhiyogakartur eva arthivādaḥ abhiyogavādo dātavyaḥ, na pūrvābhiyoktuḥ, ādharyapratyavaskandhavac ca tasyādharyam uttaraṃ pratijñā ceti vaktavyam | uktārthena tu sottaratvena sādhyatvam | pratijñārthasyaiva sādhyatvāt pratyabhiyogena kartur evārthivādo dātavyaḥ | abhiyogavādo na pūrvābhiyoktuḥ | ādharyapratyavaskandanavac ca tasyādharyam uttaraṃ pratijñā ceti vaktavyam | uttaratvena sādhyatā, pratijñātārthasyaiva sādhyatvāt | tathā ca kātyāyanaḥ |
vādinā yad abhipretaṃ svayaṃ sādhayituṃ sphuṭam |
tatsādhyaṃ sādhanaṃ yena tatsādhyaṃ sādhyate ‘khilam ||
atha pratijñā | atha pratijñātaṃ pratijñādvayam ekasmin vāde yuktam ucyate | etad ubharūpam uttaraṃ pratijñā ceti | etad bṛhaspatinā spaṣṭam uktam |
anuktvā kāraṇaṃ yatra pakṣaṃ vādī prapadyate |
pratipattis tu sā jñeyā kāraṇaṃ tūttaraṃ pṛthak ||
yadi kāraṇanirapekṣam abhyupagacchati tadā saṃpratipattiḥ | kāraṇe punar abhihite uttaram anyad ubhayarūpaṃ kāraṇaṃ pratijñā ceti kāraṇasya sādhyatvāt pratijñātatvam aviruddham | pūrvavipratijñātārthasyābhyupagame sādhanaṃ sādhyatvāpādakatvāt hetutvam api yuktam | tena pratyabhiyoge ādharye pratyavaskandane ca samāno nyāyaḥ | yājñavalkyaḥ |
nihnave bhāvito dadyād dhanaṃ rājñe ca tatsamam |
mithyābhiyogī dviguṇam abhiyogād dhanaṃ vahet ||
yad anena likhitaṃ mithyeti nihnave lekhyādibhiḥ pūrvavādinā bhāvito dadyāt | dhanaṃ tatsamaṃ daṇḍarūpeṇa rājñe ‘pi | atha bhāvayituṃ na śaknoti, tadā mithyābhiyogī dviguṇaṃ daṇḍaṃ rājñe dadyād iti | yājñavalkyaḥ |
nihnute likhitaṃ naikam ekadeśavibhāvitaḥ |
dāpyaḥ sarvaṃ nṛpeṇārthaṃ na grāhyas tv aniveditaḥ ||
naikam anekaṃ suvarṇarajatavastrādikam, likhitam abhiyuktam, pratyarthī yadi sarvam eva nihnute avajājīte, tadārthinā ekadeśabhūtahiraṇyādikam anyad vā sākṣyādibhiḥ pratyarthī bhāvito ‘ṅgīkāritas tadā sarvaṃ pūrvalikhitam arthine nṛpeṇa dāpyaḥ | pūrvaṃ bhāṣākāle arthinā aniveditaḥ paścāt pūrvaṃ mayā vismṛta iti bhāṣākāle nivedyamāno ‘rtho na grāhyaḥ | tathā ca nāradaḥ |
anekārthābhiyuktena sarvārthavyapalāpinā |
vibhāvitaikadeśena deyaṃ yad abhiyujyate ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
sarvāpalāpaṃ yaḥ kṛtvā tatho ‘lpam api saṃvadet |
sarvam eva tu dāpyaḥ syād abhiyukto bṛhaspatiḥ ||
yat tu kātyāyanavacanam,
anekārthābhiyoge tu yāvat saṃsādhayed dhanī |
sākṣibhis tāvad evāsau labhate sāshitaṃ dhanam ||
iti, tat putrādideyapitrādyṛṇaviṣayam,
putrapautrair ṛṇaṃ deyaṃ nihnave sākṣibhāvitam |
iti vacanāt | yad apīdaṃ kātyāyanavacanam,
ṛṇādiṣu vivādeṣu sthiraprāyeṣu niścitam |
ūne vāpy adhike vārthe prokte sādhyaṃ na siddhyati ||
iti, tat sarvārthasādhanatayopanyastaiḥ sākṣyādibhir ekadeśābhidhāne adhikābhidhāne vā sādhyaṃ na siddhyatīti niścitam iti saṃbandhaḥ | sāhasādau tu, sakalasādhyasādhanatayoddiṣṭaiḥ sākṣibhir ekadeśe ‘pi sādhite kṛtsnaṃ sādhyaṃ siddhaṃ bhavaty eveti | kātyāyanaḥ |
sādhyārtho ‘ṃśe ‘pi gadite sākṣibhiḥ sakalaṃ bhavet |
strīsaṅge sāhase caurye yat sādhyaṃ parikīrtitam ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
sarveṣv arthavivādeṣu balavaty uttarā kriyā |
ādhau pratigrahe krīte pūrvā tu balavattarā ||
kriyata iti kriyā kāryam | ṛṇādiṣu “sarveṣv arthavivādeśu” uttaraṃ balavat | udāharaṇaṃ tu – kaścic chataṃ matto gṛhītam iti grahaṇaṃ sādhayati, anyas tu pratipradānaṃ sādhayati, uttaraṃ pratipādanaṃ balavad iti tatsādhanāt tadvādī jayī bhavati | pūrvakārye siddhe ‘pi tadvādī parājito bhavati | ādhyādikṣetreṣu ekam eva kṣetram ekasyādhiṃ kṛtvā punar apy anyasya karoti cet, pūrvasyaiva tad bhavati nottarasyeti |
sākṣiṣūbhayataḥ satsu sākṣiṇaḥ pūrvavādinaḥ |
pūrvapakṣe ‘dharībhūte bhavanty uttaravādinaḥ ||
tatraikasmāt puruṣāt etat kṣetraṃ pratigraheṇa labdham ity eko vakti | anyo ‘pi tathaiva | ubhayatra sākṣisadbhāve ‘pi tatra pūrvavādinaḥ sākṣiṇo bhavanti | tatra pūrvapakṣo yady adharīkṛtaḥ satyam etasyaiva pūrvaṃ dattam, kiṃ tu etasyaiva hastāt krayeṇa rājñā gṛhītvā mahyaṃ dattam ity ukte, bhavanty uttaravādino ‘pi sākṣiṇaḥ |
atha vādiprativādinor duṣṭalakṣaṇāny ucyante | tatra nāradaḥ |
ākārair iṅgitair gatyā ceṣṭayā bhāṣaṇena ca |
netravaktravikāraiś ca gṛhyate ‘ntargataṃ manaḥ ||
kampaḥ svedo ‘tha vaivarṇyam oṣṭhaśoṣābhimarśane |
bhūlekhanaṃ sthānahāniḥ tiryag ūrdhvaṃ nirīkṣaṇam ||
svarabhedaś ca duṣṭasya cihnāny āhur manīṣiṇaḥ ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
deśād deśāntaraṃ yāti srakkṇī parileḍhi ca |
lalāṭaṃ svidyate cāsya mukhaṃ vaivarṇyam eti ca ||
pariśuṣyatskhaladvākyo viruddhaṃ bahu bhāṣate |
vākcakṣuḥ pūjayati no tathoṣṭhau nirbhujaty api ||
svabhāvād vikṛtiṃ gacchet manovākkāyakarmabhiḥ |
abhiyoge tathā sākṣye duṣṭaḥ sa parikīrtitaḥ ||
āsannaparājayamanaskasya balād etāni cihnāni bhavantīty abhiprāyaḥ | nāradaḥ |
sāpadeśaṃ haran kālam abruvaṃś cāmi (?) saṃsadi |
uktvā vāco vibruvaṃś ca hīyamānasya lakṣaṇam ||
manuḥ |
adeśyaṃ yaś ca diśati nirdiśyāpahnute ca yaḥ |
yaś cādharottarān arthān vihīnān nāvabudhyate ||
apadiśyāpadeśyaṃ ca punar yas tv apadhāvati |
samyakpraṇihitaṃ cārthaṃ pṛṣṭaḥ san nābhinandati ||
brūhīty uktaś ca na brūyāt uktaṃ ca na vibhāvayet |
na ca pūrvāparaṃ vidyāt tasmād arthāt sa hīyate ||
santi jñātāra ity uktvā diśety ukto diśen na yaḥ |
dharmasthaḥ kāraṇair etaiḥ hīnaṃ tam api nirdiśet ||
abhiyoktā na cet brūyād vadhyo daṇḍyaś ca dharmataḥ |
na cet tripakṣāt tad brūyāt dharmaṃ prati parājitaḥ ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
sākṣiṇas tu samuddiśya yas tu tān na vibhāvayet |
triṃśadrātrāt tripakṣād vā tasya hāniḥ prajāyate ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
sārabhūtaṃ padaṃ muktvā hy asārāṇi bahūny api |
saṃsādhayet kriyāyāṃ tu tāṃ jahyāt sāravarjitām ||
nāradaḥ |
nirṇīte vyavahāre tu pramāṇaṃ niṣphalaṃ bhavet |
likhitaṃ sākṣiṇo vāpi pūrvam āveditaṃ na cet ||
yathā pakveṣu dhānyeṣu niṣphalāḥ prāvṛṣo guṇāḥ |
nirṇītavyavahārāṇāṃ pramāṇam aphalaṃ tathā ||
[iti vādiprativādinor duṣṭalakṣaṇāi]
atha kriyāpādaḥ
atra bṛhaspatiḥ |
dviprakārā kriyā proktā mānuṣī daivikī tathā |
ekaikānekadhā bhinnā ṛṣibhis tattvavedibhiḥ ||
sākṣilekhyānumānaṃ ca mānuṣī trividhā smṛtā |
dhaṭādhyā dharmajāntā ca daivī navavidhā kriyā ||
sākṣī dvādaśabhedaś ca likhitaṃ daśadhā smṛtam |
anumānaṃ tu dvividhaṃ daivikī navadhā kriyā ||
anumānaṃ tu dividham ity arthāpattyanumānayor grahaṇam | tathā hi – bhuktiḥ kaiścid viśeṣaṇair viśiṣṭā svatvahetuḥ | sā tu krayasākṣilekhyādikam avyabhicārād anumāpayati | anupapadyamānā vā kalpayatīty anumānaṃ dvividham iti bhukter evoktā | tatra kātyāyanaḥ |
sākṣiṇo likhitaṃ bhuktiḥ pramāṇaṃ trividhaṃ viduḥ |
liṅgoddeśas tu yuktis tu dviyā hy agniviṣādayaḥ ||
ṛṇe lekhyaṃ sākṣiṇo vā yuktileśādayo ‘pi vā |
daivikī vā kriyā proktā prajānāṃ hitakāmyayā ||
“daivikī vā” iti na pūrvais tulyavikalpaḥ | kiṃ tu pūrvapramāṇābhāva eva | tathā ca yājñavalkyaḥ |
pramāṇaṃ likhitaṃ bhuktiḥ sākṣiṇaś ceti kīrtitam |
eṣām anyatamābhāve divyānyatamam ucyate ||
tathā ca nārada-bṛhaspatī |
divā kṛte kāryavidhau grāmeṣu nagareṣu ca |
saṃbhave sākṣiṇāṃ caiva na divyā bhavati kriyā ||
atra kātyāyanaḥ |
kriyā na daivikī proktā vidyamāneṣu sākṣiṣu |
lekhye ca sati vādeṣu na syād divyaṃ na sākṣiṇaḥ ||
tathā ca vyāsaḥ |
saṃbhave sākṣiṇāṃ prājño daivikīṃ varjayet kriyām |
saṃbhave tu prayuñjāno daivikīṃ hīyate dhruvam ||
tatra viśeṣam āha kātyāyanaḥ |
yady eko mānuṣīṃ brūyād anyo brūyāt tu daivikīm |
mānuṣīṃ tatra gṛhṇīyān na tu daivīṃ kriyāṃ nṛpaḥ ||
dharmādhikāriṇāṃ prāptayor vādiprativādinor eko mānuṣīṃ kriyām avalambate, aparas tu daivīm | tatra mānuṣy eva grāhyeti niyamyate | tathā |
yady ekadeśavyāptāpi kriyā vidyeta mānuṣī |
sā grāhyā na tu pūrṇāpi daivikī vadatāṃ nṛṇām ||
yatrāpi kṛtsnena daivasaṃbhavaḥ, ekadeśe mānuṣasaṃbhavaḥ, tatrāpi na daivam āśrayaṇīyam | udāharaṇaṃ tu – rūpakaśatam vṛddhyā matto gṛhītvā yacyamāno na prayacchatīty abhiyogāpahnave uttaravādinā kṛte, grahaṇe sākṣiṇaḥ santi, na saṃkhyāyām, vṛddhiviśeṣe vā, tatra na divyena vṛddhisaṃkhyayoḥ sādhanam, nāpi nihnavavādinaḥ kṛtsne divyaniveśaḥ, ekadeśavibhāvitanyāyenāpi svarūpe sākṣibhir bhāvine saṃkhyāvṛddhiviśeṣasiddheḥ na divyasyāvakāśaḥ | yat tu,
gūḍhasāhasikānāṃ tu prāptaṃ divyaiḥ parīkṣaṇam |
yuktileśeṅgitākāravākcakṣuśceṣṭitair nṛṇām ||
iti, tad api mānuṣāsaṃbhave niyamaparam | tasmāt sarvatra mānuṣāsaṃbhave divyena nirṇaya iti |
lekhyasākṣibhuktidivyādīnāṃ vyavasthāpakāni vacanāny ucyante | tatra bṛhaspatiḥ |
vākpāruṣye ca bhūmau ca divyaṃ tu parivarjayet |
vikrayādānasaṃbandhe krītvā dhanam icchanti ||
dyūte samāhvaye caiva vivāde samupasthite |
sākṣiṇaḥ sādhanaṃ proktaṃ na divyaṃ na ca lekhyakam ||
pūgaśreṇigaṇādīnāṃ yā sthitiḥ parikīrtitā |
tasyās tu sādhanaṃ lekhyaṃ na divyaṃ na ca sākṣiṇaḥ ||
dvāramārgakriyābhogajalavāhādike tathā |
bhuktir eva tu gurvī syān na lekhyaṃ na ca sākṣiṇaḥ ||
atra vyāsaḥ |
rahaḥkṛtaṃ prakāśaṃ ca dvividhaṃ kāryam ucyate |
prakāśaṃ sākṣibhir bhāvyaṃ daivikena rahaḥkṛtam || iti |
smṛtyantare |
vākpāruṣye mahīvāde niṣiddhā daivikī kriyā |
pradātavyā prayatnena sāhaseṣu caturṣv api || iti |
manuṣyamāraṇacauryaparadāragamanavāgdaṇḍapāruṣyāṇīti caturvidhāni sāhasāni,
mānuṣyamāraṇaṃ cauryaṃ paradārābhimarśanam |
pāruṣyam ubhayaṃ ceti sāhasaṃ syāc caturvidham ||
iti kātyāyanavacanād iti | tathā |
yadā sākṣī na vidyeta vivāde vadatāṃ nṛṇām |
tadā divyaiḥ parīkṣeta śapathaiś ca pṛthagvidhaiḥ ||
strīṇāṃ śīlābhiyoge ca steyasāhasayor api |
evaṃvidho vidhir dṛṣṭaḥ sarvārthāpahnaveṣu ca ||
gūḍhasāhasikānāṃ tu prāptaṃ divyaiḥ parīkṣaṇam |
yuktileśeṅgitākāravākcakṣuśceṣṭitair nṛṇām ||
tathānyatra |
maṇimuktāpravālānāṃ kūṭakṛn nyāsahārakaḥ |
hiṃsako ‘nyāṅganāsevī parīkṣyaḥ śapathaiḥ sadā ||
mahāpāpābhiyogeṣu nikṣepaharaṇe tathā |
divyaiḥ kāryaṃ parīkṣeta rājā satsv api sākṣiṣu ||
samatvaṃ sākṣiṇāṃ yatra divyais tatra viśodhayet |
prāṇāntikavivādeṣu vidyamāneṣu sākṣiṣu ||
divyam ālambate vādī pṛcchet tatra na sākṣiṇaḥ |
likhite sākṣivāde ca saṃdeho jāyate yadā ||
anumāne ca saṃbhrānte tadā divyaṃ na yojayet |
iti bṛhaspativacanāt |
likhite śithilībhūte sākṣiṇāṃ ca parāhatau |
yuktyabhāve ca divyaṃ syāt kāraṇatrayam asti cet ||
iti kātyāyanavacanāc ca |
eṣām anyatamābhāve divyānyatamam ucyate |
iti yājñavalkyavacanāc ca | likhitabhuktisākṣilekhyeṣv asatsv eva dviyaṃ deyam | anyathā, nirālambanavādaprasaṅgād iti smṛteḥ, dhāreśvara-udyotana-kṛṣṇādayo manyante, aprakāśavivādeṣu divyābhāvaprasaṅgāt | tathā hi,
likhitādikriyā vā syāc chapathaṃ divyam eva vā |
sarveṣu ca vivādeṣu yojayed divyam eva vā ||
iti prājāpatyavacanavirodhāt |
eṣām anyatamābhāve divyānyatamam ucyate |
iti yājñavalkyavacanavirodhāc ca | ataḥ sarvārthavivādeṣu sākṣyādīnāṃ saṃbhave ‘pi tair nirṇayābhāve teṣām asaṃbhave ca divyaṃ yuktam eva | nanu kathaṃ sarvatra divyaprāptiḥ,
sthāvareṣu vivādeṣu divyāni parivarjayet |
iti, tathā,
pāruṣye ca mahīvāde niṣiddhā daivikī kriyā |
iti, tathā,
dyūte samāhvaye caiva vivāde samupasthite |
sākṣiṇaḥ sādhanaṃ proktaṃ na divyaṃ na ca lekhyakam ||
iti, tathā,
pūgaśreṇigaṇādīnāṃ yā sthitiḥ parikīrtitā |
tasyās tu sādhanaṃ lekhyaṃ na divyaṃ na ca sākṣiṇaḥ ||
ity evamādibhir vacanair vivādaviśeṣeṣu pramāṇaviśeṣaniyamāt | ucyate — evamādivacanāni vivādaviśeṣeṣu lekhyādipramāṇāviśeṣaprābalyaparāṇi, na punar divyādipramāṇāntaraniṣedhaparāṇi | tathātve sarvatra pramāṇānāṃ prāpakavacanavirodhāt | ataḥ pramāṇāntarasaṃbhave mahīvāde na divyam | tathā dyūte samāhvaye caiva sākṣisaṃbhave na divyam, nāpi lekhyam | tathā ṛṇādivādeṣu lekhyasaṃbhave “na divyaṃ na ca sākṣiṇaḥ” iti niyamaḥ | vivādāntareṣu parājitasya daṇḍadvaiguṇyādipaṇaṃ kṛtvā pramāṇantarāvalambanaṃ yuktam | mahīvādādiṣu parājitasya daṇḍadvaiguṇyakalpanayāpi na divyādikalpanam ity avirodhaḥ ||
iti śrīmadvaradarājīye vyavahāranirṇaye
vyavahāramātṛkākāṇḍaṃ samāptam
4. mānuṣapramāṇakāṇḍam
atha pramāṇāny ucyante
tatra mānuṣaṃ pramāṇaṃ trividhaṃ lekhyabhuktisākṣirūpeṇa,
sākṣilekhyānumānaṃ ca mānuṣī trividhā smṛtā |
iti bṛhaspativacanāt | tathā –
ṣāṇmāsike ‘pi samaye bhrāntiḥ saṃjāyate yataḥ |
yatrākṣarāṇi spaṣṭāni patrārūḍhāny ataḥ purā ||
deśācārayutaṃ varṣamāsapakṣāhvṛddhimat |
ṛṇisākṣilekhakānāṃ hastāṅkaṃ lekhyam ucyate ||
tatra lekhyam – niravdyakartṛlekhakasākṣihastalipyakṣarānumitaiḥ śabdaiḥ vākartham avagamayatīti śābdaṃ pramāṇam | tatrānyahastalikhitasya sākṣibahutvāpekṣā lekhakāpekṣā, kartrapekṣā ca, likhitasāmagrīśāstraniyamasiddhā | svahastalikhitasya lekhakasākṣyanapekṣā ca śāstraniyamasiddhā | śākṣiṇaś ca śāstroktaniyamā svapratyakṣādipramāṇamūlaiḥ śabdair artham avagamayantīti śābdeam eva pramāṇam | śāstreṇa tryavarāḥ sādhava ityādiniyamaś ca jñānaprakāradarśanaparaḥ |
atha bhuktir ucyate – bhuktiś ca pañcavidhaviśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭā | svatvahetubhūtakrayādyanumānaliṅgākalpakaṃ vā tataś ca yuktileśādivad bhuktir anumānam arthāpattir vā, pramāṇam ity abhiprāyaḥ | tatra viśeṣam āha nāradaḥ |
likhitaṃ balavan nityaṃ jīvantas tv eva sākṣiṇaḥ |
kālātiharaṇād bhuktir iti śāstreṣu niścayaḥ ||
niravadyasya lekhyasya sarvadā balavattvam | jīvadavasthāyām eva sākṣiṇo balavattvam | cirakāle ‘tīte bhukteś ca balavattvam | tatra prajāpatiḥ |
rājakīyaṃ jānapadaṃ dvividhaṃ likhitaṃ smṛtam |
śāsanaṃ rājadattādau cīrakaṃ syād ṛṇādiṣi ||
rājñā datte kṛtaṃ lekhyaṃ rājaśāsanam ucyate ||
ṛṇādiṣu vivādeṣu jānapadaṃ lekhyaṃ cīrakam ity ucyate | tatra rājaśāsanasya lakṣaṇam āha |
rājñā svahastacihnena saṃdhivigrahalekhakaiḥ |
likhitaṃ rājanāmāṅkaṃ mudritaṃ rājamudrayā ||
samāmāsatadardhāhonāmajātyādicihinitam |
spaṣṭavarṇakramopetam aviluptakramākṣaram ||
rājaśāsanam etat syād uttamaṃ sarvalekhyataḥ |
tatra vyāsaḥ |
rājñā tu svayam ādiṣṭaḥ saṃdhivigrahalekhakaḥ |
tāmrapaṭṭe paṭe vāpi pralikhed rājaśāsanam ||
cīrakaṃ nāma likhitaṃ pradhānaiḥ pauralekhakaiḥ |
arthipratyarthimadhyasthasamāmāsādisaṃyutam ||
rājasthāne niyuktakāyasthādikṛtaṃ tadadhyakṣakaracihnitam rājahastamudrayā mudritaṃ rājalekhyam ity ucyate | sthānalekhyaṃ tu grāmajapadādiṣv adhikṛtakāyasthakṛtaṃ tadadhyakṣakaracihnitaṃ cīritam ity ucyate | atra viśeṣam āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
rājalekhyaṃ sthānakṛtaṃ svahastalikhitaṃ tathā |
lekhyaṃ tu trividhaṃ proktaṃ bhinnaṃ tad daśadhā punaḥ ||
dānalekhyaṃ bhāgalekhyaṃ sīmālekhyaṃ tathaiva ca |
krayalekhyaṃ dāsalekhyam ādhilekhyaṃ tataḥ param ||
saṃviduddāmalikhyaṃ ca jayapatrakam eva ca |
saṃdhipatraṃ tathaivaitat kriyābhedād anekadhā ||
bhūmiṃ datvā tu yat patraṃ kuryāc candrārkakālikam ||
ity uddeśeḥ |
atha lakṣaṇam |
anācchedyam anāhāryaṃ dānalekhyaṃ tu tat smṛtam |
vibhāgapatram ity etad bhāgānāṃ nirṇayaṃ kṛtam ||
sīmāvivāde nirṇīte sīmāpatram iti smṛtam |
gṛhakṣetrādikaṃ krītvā tulyamūlākṣarāṇvitam ||
patraṃ kārayate yat tu krayalekhyaṃ tad ucyate |
ādhyartham ādhilekhyaṃ syād dāsārthaṃ dāsapatrakam ||
samīhitārthasiddhyarthaṃ grāmaśreṇigaṇādibhiḥ |
śāstrāvirodhi dharmārthe kṛtaṃ saṃvittipatrkam ||
dhanaṃ gṛhītvā vṛddhyarthaṃ svayaṃ kuryāc ca kārayet |
uddāmapatraṃ tat proktam ṛṇalekhyaṃ manīṣibhiḥ ||
jayapatralakṣaṇam āha kātyāyanaḥ |
yathopanyastasādhyārthasaṃyuktaṃ sottarakriyam |
sāvadhāraṇakaṃ caiva mudritaṃ rājamudrayā ||
sabhāsanyastacihnaṃ ca vādipratyarthisaṃyutam |
prāḍvivākādihastāṅkaṃ jayapatrakam iṣyate ||
saṃdhisthalekhyalakṣaṇam āha vṛddhavasiṣṭhaḥ |
arthipratyarthivākyāni pratijñā sākṣivāk tathā |
nirṇayaś ca tathā tasya yathā cāvadhṛtaṃ svayam ||
etad yathākṣaraṃ lekhyaṃ yathāpūrvaṃ niveśitam |
caturṣv api vivādeṣu jayine jayapatrakam ||
kulādibhir nirṇaye jayapatrābhāvān nirṇayapatraṃ tatra kāryam, prattapatram iti yāvat | tathā ca kātyāyanaḥ |
deśācāra(ā)viruddhaṃ (?) yad vyaktādhikṛtalakṣaṇam |
tat pramāṇaṃ smṛtaṃ lekhyam aviluptakramākṣaram ||
sabhāsadaś ca ye tatra smṛtiśāstravidas tathā |
yathālekhyavidas tadvat svahastaṃ tatra dāpayet ||
abhiyoktābhiyuktānāṃ vacanaṃ prāṅniveśayet |
sabhyānāṃ prāḍvivākasya kulānāṃ vā tataḥ param ||
niścayaṃ smṛtiśāstrāṇāṃ mataṃ tatraiva lekhayet |
saṃdhau kṛte tu yat patraṃ saṃdhilekhyaṃ tad ucyate ||
atha karalekhyam
tatra kātyāyanaḥ |
deśācārasthitiyutaṃ karalekhyādikaṃ smṛtam |
samāmāsādihīnaṃ tu pramāṇaṃ tad bhavet punaḥ ||
atra viśeṣam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
yaḥ kaścid artho niṣṇātaḥ svarucyā tu parasparam |
lekhyaṃ tu sākṣimat kāryaṃ tasmin dhanikapūrvakam ||
samāmāsatadardhāhonāmajātisvagotrakam |
sabrahmacārikātmīyapitṛnāmādicihnitam ||
samāpte ‘rthe ṛṇī nāma svahastena niveśayet |
mataṃ me ‘mukaputrasya yad atropari lekhitam ||
ubhayābhyarthitenaitan mayā hy amukasūnunā |
likhitaṃ hy amukeneti lekhakas tv antato likhet ||
vināpi sākṣibhir lekhyaṃ svahastalikhitaṃ tu yat |
tat pramāṇaṃ smṛtaṃ sarvaṃ balopadhikṛtād ṛte ||
ṛṇaṃ lekhyakṛtaṃ deyaṃ puruṣais tribhir eva tu |
tathā |
sākṣiṇaś ca svahastena pitṛnāmādipūrvakam |
atrāham amukaḥ sākṣī likheyur iti te samāḥ ||
alipijña ṛṇī yaḥ syāl lekhayet svamataṃ tu saḥ |
sākṣī vā sākṣiṇānyena sarvasākṣisamīmataḥ (?) ||
samāpte lekhye ṛṇī svahastena svanāmaliptāṃ lipiṃ kuryāt | tadanantaraṃ lekhakaḥ, tadanantaraṃ sākṣiṇaḥ | alipijñānām eteṣāṃ svamatam anyena lekhanīyam | tatra svahastalikhitaṃ lekhyaṃ sākṣibhir vināpi pramāṇam | alipijñenānyena likhitaṃ sākṣimad eva pramāṇam |
sthāvare prakriyādhāne vibhāge dāya eva ca |
pratigrahe ca krīte ca nālekhyā siddhyati kriyā ||
iti yad idaṃ kātyāyanīyavacanam, tal lekhyaprādhānyaparam, na tv anyanivṛttiparam, tathātve vacanāntaravirodhāt | atrāyam arthaḥ | ukteṣu lekhyaviśeṣaṇeṣu yasya yatra dṛṣṭārthatvaṃ tatra tad ādaraṇīyam | netarat, adṛṣṭārthatvaprasaṅgāt |
atha punarlekhyam
atra yājñavalkyaḥ |
deśāntarasthe durlekhye naṣṭonmṛṣṭe hṛte tathā |
bhinne dagdhe tathā chinne lekhyam anyat tu kārayet ||
pūrvakṛte lekhye vyavahārākṣame anyat lekhyaṃ kārayed ity arthaḥ | vyāsaḥ |
aprakāśād dhi svakṛtāt sākṣimallekhyakānvitam |
svahastacihnitaṃ lekhyaṃ śreṣṭham anyena lekhitam ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
darśitaṃ pratikālaṃ yac chrāvitaṃ smāritaṃ tathā |
lekhyaṃ siddhyati sarvatra mṛteṣv api hi sākṣiṣu ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
na jātu hīyate lekhyaṃ sākṣibhiḥ śapathena vā |
adarśanād aśravaṇād dhāniṃ prāpnoty upekṣayā ||
lekhyaṃ triṃśatsamātītam adṛṣṭāśrāvitaṃ ca yat |
na lekhyaṃ siddhim āpnoti tiṣṭhatsv api hi sākṣiṣu ||
lekhyasya asiddhahetūn āha vyāsaḥ |
aśrutārtham adṛṣṭārthaṃ vyavahārātham āgatam |
na lekhyaṃ siddhim āpnoti jīvatsv api hi sākṣiṣu ||
anyatrāpi |
ādhātari tirobhūtam āvibhūtaṃ mṛte sati |
anāttavittaṃ tal lekhyaṃ pratidattam athāpi vā ||
vyāsaḥ |
svahastaikakṛtāj jānapadaṃ tasmāt tu śāsanam |
pramāṇataram iṣṭaṃ hi vyavahārārtham āgatam ||
tathānyatra |
deśādhyakṣādinā lekhyaṃ yatra jānapadaṃ kṛtam |
samakālaṃ paścimaṃ vā tasmāj jānapadaṃ śubham ||
lekhakasākṣiṇām atyaye tallikhitaiḥ itarākṣaraiḥ saṃśayanirāsam āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
atha pañcatvam āpanno lekhakaḥ sākṣibhiḥ saha |
tat svahastādibhis teṣāṃ viśudhyeta na saṃśayaḥ ||
lekhye saṃśayam āpanne sākṣilekhakakartṛbhiḥ |
duṣṭeṣu teṣu taddhastakṛtapūrvākṣarādibhiḥ ||
yathālābhopapannais tair nirṇayaṃ kārayen nṛpaḥ ||
nāradaḥ |
aśrutārtham adṛṣṭārthaṃ vyavahārārtham āgatam |
na lekhyaṃ siddhim āpnoti jīvatsv api hi sākṣiṣu ||
ṛṇino hastasaṃdehe jīvato vā mṛtasya vā |
tat svahastakṛtair anyaiḥ patrais tallekhyanirṇayaḥ ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
saṃdigdhalekhyaśuddhiḥ syāt svahastalikhitādibhiḥ |
yuktiprāptikriyācihnasaṃbandhāgamahetubhiḥ ||
saṃdigdhe lekhye anyas taddhatalikhitaiḥ śuddhiḥ | ādiśabdāt sākṣilekhakahastalikhitāntarasaṃvādāc chuddhiḥ | yuktvā prāptiḥ yuktiprāptiḥ | deśakālapuruṣāṇāṃ dravyeṇa saha saṃbandhaprāptiḥ | asmin deśe asmin kāle asya puruṣasya idaṃ dravyaṃ ghaṭata iti yuktiprāptiḥ | kriyā tatkriyā tatsākṣyupanyāsaḥ | cihnam asādhāraṇasvasvīkārād iti | saṃbandhaḥ arthipratyarthinoḥ pūrvam api parasparaviśvāsena dānagrahaṇādisaṃbandhaḥ | āgamaḥ asyaitāvato ‘rthasya saṃbhāvitaḥ prāptyupāyaḥ | eta eva hetavaḥ | ebhir lekhyaśuddhir iti | lekhyaparīkṣām āha kātyāyanaḥ |
dṛṣṭe patre sphuṭaṃ doṣaṃ noktavān ṛṇiko yadi |
tato viṃśativarṣātikrāntaṃ patraṃ sthiraṃ bhavet ||
śaktasya saṃnidhāv artho yasya lekhyena bhujyate |
varṣāṇi viṃśatir yāvat tat patraṃ doṣavarjitam ||
strībālārtālipijñānāṃ vañcayanti svabāndhavāḥ |
lekhyaṃ kṛtvā svanāmāṅkaṃ jñeyaṃ yuktyāgamādibhiḥ ||
vyāsaḥ |
yatrānyasya kṛtaṃ lekhyam anyahaste pradṛśyate |
avaśyaṃ tatra kartavyaṃ patrasyāgamanaṃ tataḥ ||
prajāpatiḥ |
yannāmagotrais tulyaṃ tu lekhyakaṃ hi kvacid bhavet |
agṛhīte dhane tatra kāryo divyena nirṇayaḥ ||
nāradaḥ |
darpaṇasthaṃ yathābimbam asat sad iva dṛśyate |
tathā lekhyasya bimbāni kurvanti kuśalā narāḥ ||
lekhyākāreṇa likhitaṃ sākṣyākāreṇa sākṣiṇaḥ |
asatyāḥ satyasaṃkāśāḥ satyāś cāsatyadarśinaḥ ||
dṛśyante vividhā bhāvās tasmād yuktaṃ parīkṣaṇam |
bṛhaspatiḥ |
puruṣāḥ santi lobhād ye kāryaṃ prabrūyur anyathā |
santi cānye durātmānaḥ kūṭalekhyakṛto narāḥ |
ataḥ parīkṣyam ubhayam etad rājñā viśeṣataḥ ||
vyāsaḥ |
lekhya ālekhyavat kecil likhanti kuśalā narāḥ |
tasmān na lekhyasāmarthyāt siddhir aikāntikī matā ||
jaḍamūkāndhabālastrīgaṇavṛddhārtabhīrubhiḥ |
kātyāyanaḥ |
sākṣidoṣād bhaved duṣṭaṃ patraṃ vā lekhakaḥ svayam |
dhanikasyopadhādoṣāt tathā ca ṛṇikasya vā ||
jaḍamūkāndhabālājñakruddharogārthabhīrubhiḥ |
asvatantropadhāduṣṭaiḥ kṛtaṃ lekhyaṃ na siddhyati ||
samāmāsādihīnaṃ ca deśācāravirodhi ca |
luptakramavarṇaṃ ca śṛṅgahīnaṃ ca duṣyati ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
dūṣito garhitaḥ sākṣī yatraiko viniveśitaḥ |
kūṭalekhyaṃ tu tat prāhuḥ lekhako vāpi tadvidhaḥ ||
sthānabhraṣṭāś ca malināḥ saṃdigdhā lakṣaṇacyutāḥ |
yatraivaṃ susthitā varṇā lekhyaṃ duṣṭaṃ tu tad bhavet ||
yad ujvalaṃ cirakṛtaṃ malinaṃ svalpakālikam |
bhagnonmṛṣṭākṣarayutaṃ lekhyaṃ kūṭatvam āpnuyāt ||
ekam eva bhavel lekhyam ekasyārthasya siddhaye |
anekeṣu tu lekhyeṣu doṣam utpadayed api ||
deśācāraviruddhaṃ yat saṃdigdhaṃ kramavarjitam |
kṛtam asvāminā yac ca sādhyahīnaṃ ca duṣyati ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
sākṣilekhakakartāraḥ kūṭatāṃ yānti te yathā |
tathā doṣāḥ prayoktāvyā duṣṭalekhyaṃ ca duṣyati ||
duṣṭair duṣṭaṃ bhavel lekhyaṃ śuddhaiḥ śuddhaṃ vinirdiśet |
tat patram upadhāduṣṭaiḥ sākṣilekhakakārakaiḥ ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
mumūrṣuśiśubhītārtastrīmattavyasanāturaiḥ |
niśopadhibalātkārakṛtaṃ lekhyaṃ na sidhyati ||
vyāsaḥ |
dāsāsvatantrabālaiś ca strīkṛtaṃ caiva yad bhavet |
pramāṇaṃ naiva tal lekhyam iti śāstravido viduḥ ||
manuḥ |
yasmin yasmin vivādeṣu kūṭasākṣyaṃ kṛtaṃ bhavet |
tat tat kāryaṃ nivarteta kṛtaṃ cāpy akṛtaṃ bhavet ||
yogādhamanavikrītaṃ yogadānapratigraham |
yatra cāpy upadhiṃ paśyet tat sarvaṃ vinivartayet ||
balād dattaṃ balād bhuktaṃ balāc cāpi lekhitam |
sarvān balakṛtān arthān akṛtān manur abravīt ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
balopadhivinirvṛttān vyavahārān nivartayet |
strīnaktam antarāgāraṃ bahiḥśatrukṛtaṃ tathā ||
nāradaḥ |
strīṣu rātrau bahirgrāmād antarveśmanyarātriṣu |
vyavahāraḥ kṛto ‘py eṣu punaḥkartavyatām iyāt ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
lekhyadoṣās tu ye kecit sākṣiṇo vāpi ye smṛtāḥ |
vādakāleṣu vaktavyāḥ paścāduktīṃs tu varjayet ||
prajāpatiḥ |
yannāmagotrais tulyaṃ hi lekhyaṃ kartuḥ kvacid bhavet |
agṛhīte dhane tatra kāryo divyena nirṇayaḥ ||
tathā ca prajāpatiḥ |
lekhye kūṭatvam āpanne saṃdigdhe kāryanirṇaye |
asat kṛtya ca tal lekhyam arthe divyena nirṇayaḥ ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
na mayaitat kṛtaṃ patraṃ kūṭam etena kāritam |
adharīkṛtya tat patram arthe divyena nirṇayaḥ ||
aduṣṭalekhakartṛlekhakasākṣiyuktaṃ lekhyam arthasyātyantasādhakam iti | anyan na sādhakam iti |
iti lekhyaprakaraṇam
atha sākṣiṇaḥ
tatra manuḥ |
pṛṣṭopavyayamānas tu kṛtāvastho dhanaiṣiṇā |
tryavaraiḥ sākṣibhir bhāvyo nṛpabrāhmaṇasaṃnidhau ||
apavyayamāno vivadamānaḥ | kṛtāvasthaḥ kṛtāvasthānaḥ, yathāsau nādhigacchati tathā rājñā kṛta ity ārthaḥ | tathā |
yādṛśā dhanibhiḥ kāryā vyavahāreṣu sākṣiṇaḥ |
tādṛśān saṃpravakṣyāmi yathāvācyam ṛtaṃ ca taiḥ ||
gṛhiṇaḥ putriṇo maulā kṣatraviṭchūdrayonayaḥ |
arthyuktāḥ sākṣyam arhanti na ye kecid anāpadi ||
maulāḥ kulīnāḥ grāmaśreṣṭhāḥ pradhānapuruṣāḥ vā |
āptāḥ sarveṣu varṇeṣu kāryākāryeṣu sākṣiṇaḥ |
sarvadharmavido ‘lubdhān viparītāṃs tu varjayet ||
nāradaḥ |
saṃdigdheṣu ca kāryeṣu dvayor vivadamānayoḥ |
dṛṣṭaśrutānubhūtatvāt sākṣibhyo vyaktidarśanam ||
samakṣadarśanāt sākṣī vijñeyaḥ śrotracakṣuṣoḥ |
cakṣuṣā śrotreṇa vā saha manovyāpāro yasyāsau sākṣī | “sākṣād draṣṭari saṃjñāyām” iti pāṇinismaraṇāt, sākṣād draṣṭā sākṣī | atra yājñavalkyaḥ |
tapasvino dānaśīlāḥ kulīnāḥ satyavādinaḥ |
dharmapradhānā ṛjavaḥ putravanto dhanānvitāḥ ||
tryavarāḥ sākṣiṇo jñeyāḥ śrautasmārtakriyāparāḥ |
yathājāti yathāvarṇaṃ sarve sarvāsu vā smṛtāḥ ||
anyatra nāradaḥ |
kulīnā ṛjavaḥ śuddhā janmataḥ karmato ‘rthataḥ |
tryavarāḥ sākṣiṇo jñeyāḥ śucayaś ca subuddhayaḥ ||
brāhmaṇāḥ kṣatriyā vaiśyā śūdrā ye cāpy aninditāḥ |
prativarṇaṃ bhaveyus te sarve sarveṣu vā smṛtāḥ ||
śreṇiṣu śreṇipuruṣāḥ sveṣu varṇeṣū varṇiṇaḥ |
bahirvāsiṣu bāhyāḥ syuḥ striyaḥ strīṣu ca sākṣiṇaḥ ||
punaḥ nāradaḥ |
ekādaśavidhaḥ sākṣī śāstre dṛṣṭo manīṣibhiḥ |
kṛtaḥ pañcavidhas teṣāṃ ṣaḍvidho ‘kṛta ucyate ||
lekhitaḥ smāritaś caiva yadṛcchābhijña eva ca |
gūḍhaś cottarasākṣī ca kṛtaḥ pañcavidhaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
akṛtān āha nāradaḥ |
anye punar anirdiṣṭāḥ sākṣiṇaḥ samudāhṛtāḥ |
grāmaś ca prāḍvivākaś ca rājā ca vyavahāriṇām ||
kārye ‘py abhyantare yaḥ syāt arthinā prahitaś ca yaḥ |
kulyāḥ kulavivādeṣu bhaveyus te ‘pi sākṣiṇaḥ ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
likhito lekhito gūḍhaḥ smāritaḥ kulyadūtakau |
yādṛcchikaś cottaraś ca kārymadhyagatas tathā ||
nṛpo ‘dhyakṣas tathā grāmaḥ sākṣī dvādaśadhā smṛtaḥ |
anena tu sākṣiṇāṃ kṛtau tal lekhyena vinirṇayaḥ |
prāḍvivākaprasiddhyarthaṃ madhyasthān pūjayet tataḥ ||
prabhedam eṣāṃ vakṣyāmi yathāvad anupūrvaśaḥ |
jātināmābhilikhitaṃ yena svaṃ pitryam eva ca ||
nivāsaś ca sa vijñeyaḥ sākṣī likhitasaṃjñakaḥ |
saṃdhikriyāṃ kriyābhedais tasya kṛtvā ṛṇādike ||
pratyakṣaṃ likhite yaś ca lekhitaḥ sa udāhṛtaḥ |
kuḍyavyavahito yas tu śrāvyate ṛṇabhāṣitam ||
vinihnute yathābhūtaṃ gūḍhasākṣī sa kīrtitaḥ |
vyāsaḥ |
arthinaḥ svārthasiddhyarthaṃ pratyarthivacanaṃ sphuṭam |
yaḥ śrāvyate sthito gūḍho gūḍhasākṣī sa ucyate ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
āhūya yaḥ kṛtaḥ sākṣī ṛṇavyāsakriyādike |
smāryate ca muhuḥ kāryaṃ smāritaḥ so ‘bhidhīyate ||
vibhāgadāne vipaṇe jñātir yatropadiśyate |
dvayoḥ samāno dharmajñaḥ kulyaḥ sa parikīrtitaḥ ||
arthipratyarthivacanaṃ śṛṇuyāt preṣitas tu yaḥ |
ubhayoḥ saṃmataḥ sādhuḥ dūtakaḥ sa udāhṛtaḥ ||
kriyamāṇe tu kartavye yaḥ kaścit svayam āgataḥ |
atra sākṣī tv asmākam uktau yādṛcchikas tu saḥ ||
yatra sākṣī diśaṃ gacchan mumūrṣur vā yathāśrutam |
anyaṃ saṃśrāvayet tat tu vidyād uttarasākṣiṇam ||
ubhābhyāṃ yasya viśvastaṃ kāryaṃ cāpi niveditam |
gūḍhasākṣī sa vijñeyaḥ kāryamadhyagatas tathā ||
arthipratyarthinor vākyaṃ yac chrutaṃ bhūbhṛtā svayam |
sa eva tatra sākṣī syād visaṃvāde dvayor api ||
nanu “na sākṣī nṛpatiḥ kāryaḥ” iti rājñaḥ sākṣitvaniṣadhāt taska kathaṃ sākṣitvam iti cet, ucyate | vyavahāradarśanakāle ‘vagateṣv artheṣu tasyeha sākṣitvam ucyate, kālāntarāpekṣayā niṣedhaḥ | tathā ca vyāsaḥ |
rājñā dharmāsanasthena yac chrutaṃ tattvam icchatā |
nānyaḥ sākṣī bhavet tatra muktvā rājānam īśvaram || iti |
bṛhaspatiḥ |
nirṇīte vyavahāre tu punarnyāyo yadā bhavet |
adhyakṣaḥ sabhyasahitaḥ sākṣī syāt tatra nānyathā ||
rājamūlaḥ saṃdhimūlaḥ dvividho nirṇayo mataḥ |
ādye daśāṅgakathanād dvitīye ca yathāvidhi ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
lekhakaḥ prāḍvivākaś ca sabhyāś caivānupūrvaśaḥ |
nṛpe paśyati tat kāryaṃ sākṣiṇaḥ samudāhṛtāḥ ||
jayapatranāśe idaṃ vacanam | sadbhāve tad eva pramāṇam | bṛhaspatiḥ |
muṣitaṃ ghātitaṃ yatra sīmāyāś ca samantataḥ |
akṛte ‘pi bhavet sākṣī grāmas tatra na saṃśayaḥ ||
manuḥ |
yatrānibaddho vīkṣeta śṛṇuyād vāpi kathaṃcana |
pṛṣṭas tatra sakṛd brūyād yathādṛṣṭaṃ yathāśrutam ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
aśakya āgamo yatra videśe prativādinā |
traividyaprahitaṃ tatra lekhyaṃ sākṣyaṃ pradāpayet ||
sākṣiṇaś ca svarūpeṇāgamanāśaktau vidvatsaṃnidhau tatkṛtena lekhyena sākṣyaṃ bhāvayet | iti |
atha sākṣisaṃkhyā
bṛhaspatiḥ |
nava saptāpi vā pañca catvāras traya eva vā |
ubhau vā śrotriyau grāhyau naikaṃ pṛcchet kadācana ||
likhitau dvau tathā gūḍhau tricatuḥpañca lekhitāḥ |
yadṛcchāsmāritāḥ kulyās tathā cottarasākṣiṇaḥ ||
dūtako dhaṭikāgrāhī kāryamadhyagatas tathā |
eka eva pramāṇaṃ syān nṛpo ‘dhyakṣas tathaiva ca ||
dhaṭikāgrāhī gaṇakaḥ | adhyakṣaḥ prāḍvivākādiḥ | yājñavalkyaḥ |
ubhayānumataḥ sākṣī bhavaty eko ‘pi dharmavit |
anyatra |
abhyantaras tu nikṣepe sākṣyam eko ‘pi vocyate |
āṣṭamāt vatsarāt siddhiḥ smāritasyeha sākṣiṇaḥ ||
ā pañcamāt tathā siddhiḥ yadṛcchopagatasya ca |
ā tṛtīyāt tathā siddhiḥ varṣāt gūḍhasya sākṣiṇaḥ ||
ā vatsarāt tathā siddhiḥ bhavaty uttarasākṣiṇaḥ |
atha vā kālaniyamo na dṛṣṭaḥ sākṣiṇāṃ prati ||
smṛtyapekṣaṃ hi sākṣitvam āhuḥ śrāstravido janāḥ |
yasya nopahatā buddhiḥ dṛṣṭiśrotre ca nityaśaḥ |
sudīrgheṇāpi kālena sākṣī sākṣitvam arhati ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
dāsacāraṇamallānāṃ hastyaśvarathajīvinām |
pūgaśreṇigaṇādīnāṃ sākṣyaṃ sveṣu vidhīyate ||
samyak kriyāparijñāne deyaḥ kālas tu sākṣiṇām |
asaṃdigdhaṃ yatra sākṣyaṃ tatra sadyo vinirṇayaḥ ||
saṃdigdhaṃ yatra sākṣyaṃ tu tatra divyena nirṇayaḥ |
sarvatra svajanasya svāmyaṃ svataḥprāptir ucyate | manuḥ |
anubhāvī tu yaḥ kaścit kuryāt sākṣyaṃ vivādinām |
antarveśmany araṇye vā śarīrasyaiva cātyaye ||
asaṃbhave sarveṣāṃ sarvatra sākṣitvaṃ yuktam eva | bṛhaspatiḥ |
ubhayānumataḥ sākṣī caiko ‘pi ca yathoditaḥ |
vaden na kālaniyamaḥ tatrānyat kāryam iṣyate ||
pañcāśadvatsarād arvāk na kālaniyamaḥ smṛtaḥ |
ūrdhvaṃ tu kālaniyamaḥ syād evety abravīn manuḥ ||
athāsākṣiṇaḥ
atrāha nāradaḥ |
asākṣy api hi śāstreṣu dṛṣṭaḥ pañcavidho budhaiḥ |
pāñcavidhyam āha |
vacanāt doṣato bhedāt svayam uktair mṛtāntarāt ||
tatra vacanāt iti padaṃ vivṛṇoti |
śrotriyās tāpasā vṛddhā ye ca pravrajitā narāḥ |
asākṣiṇas te vacanān nātra hetur udāhṛtaḥ ||
doṣataḥ iti padaṃ vyācaṣṭe |
stenāḥ sāhasikāś caṇḍāḥ kitavā vadhakās tathā |
asākṣiṇas te duṣṭatvāt teṣu satyaṃ na vidyate ||
rājñā parigṛhīteṣu sākṣiṣv ekārthanirṇaye |
vacanaṃ yatra bhidyeta te syur bhedād asākṣiṇaḥ ||
svayam uktaiḥ iti padaṃ vyācaṣṭe |
svayam uktaḥ sa nirdiṣṭaḥ svayam evaitya yo vadet |
sūcīty uktaḥ sa śāstreṣu na sa sākṣitvam arhati ||
sūcī sūcaka iti śāstreṣūktaḥ | mṛtāntarāt iti padaṃ vyācaṣṭe |
yo ‘rthaḥ śrāvayitavyaḥ syāt tasminn asati cārthini |
kva tad vadatu sākṣitvam ity asākṣī mṛtāntaraḥ ||
“yo ‘rthaḥ śrāvayitavyaḥ syāt” pūrvapakṣiṇā prativādinā vā bhavet, asati tasminn arthe ‘nivedita ity arthaḥ | arthini cāsati tasmin mṛte kasminn arthe kīdṛśe vārthe vadati sākṣitvam, sākṣitvenānirdiṣṭaḥ | etad uktaṃ bhavati | dhanini prete anāvedite ca vyavahāre, anāhūtaḥ san, svayam evaitya mṛtaputraṃ nirdiśyāmuṣyāsau dhārayati, ahaṃ sākṣīti yo bravīti, asau mṛtāntaranāmā asākṣī, mumūrṣuṇā putraṃ prati śrāvite tasminn arthe ‘so sākṣīty evaṃ śrāvitaḥ putraṃ prati sākṣī bhavaty eva | atra bṛhaspatiḥ |
santo ‘pi na pramāṇaṃ syuḥ mṛte dhanini sākṣiṇaḥ |
putre tu śrāvitā ye syuḥ svayam āsannamṛtyunā |
te sākṣiṇo bhavanty eva mṛtāntaratayāpi ca ||
atra manuḥ |
nārthasaṃbandhino nāptā na sahāyā na vairiṇaḥ |
na dṛṣṭadoṣāḥ kartavyā na vyādhyārtā na dūṣitāḥ ||
na sākṣī nṛpatiḥ kāryo na kārukakuśīlavau |
na śrotriyo na liṅgastho na saṅgebhyo vinirgataḥ ||
nādhyadhīno na vaktavyo na dasyur na vikarmakṛt |
na vṛddho na śuśur naiko nāntyo na vikalendriyaḥ ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
strībālavṛddhakitavamattonmattābhiśastakāḥ |
raṅgāvatārī pāṣaṇḍī kūṭakṛd vikalendriyaḥ ||
patitāptārthasaṃbandhasahāyariputaskarāḥ |
sāhasī dṛṣṭadośaś ca nirdhūtaś cety asākṣiṇaḥ ||
prajāpatiḥ |
dāsanaikṛtikā śūdranāstikagrāmayājakāḥ |
mahāpathikasāmudravaṇigviṣadavahnidāḥ ||
yugmaikācārā dūtāś ca hīnāṅgā bakavṛttayaḥ |
hīnāś ca vrātyadārāgnityāgino ‘yājyayājakāḥ ||
bhūtāviṣṭanṛpadviṣṭavarṣanakṣatrasūcakāḥ |
ekasthālī sahāyārī jñātisaṃbandhibāndhavāḥ ||
viṣajīvī bhiṣagjīvī parapreṣyo niyojitaḥ |
ādhyadhīnanisṛṣṭārthaśūdrā putropapādakāḥ ||
bhinnavṛttasamāvṛttamāhāyajñapravartakāḥ |
klāntaśrānto ‘ghaśaṃsī ca śyāvadat kunakhas tathā ||
ātmavikretṛmitradhrukśaṭhaśauṇḍīkapauṣṭikāḥ |
aindrajālikalubdhograśreṇīgaṇavirodhinaḥ ||
tailakṛc citrakṛn madguḥ kuhako rājapūruṣaḥ |
nagnāṅgaḥ pratyavasito baiḍālavratahaitukāḥ ||
kaivartabakavṛttārtalokāyatikasaugatāḥ |
mauhūrtikaḥ śākuniko dharmāt svāt pracyutas tathā ||
manuḥ |
manuṣyapaśumāṃsāsthipaśukṣīrāmbusarpiṣām |
vikretā brāhmaṇaś caiva dvijo vārdhuṣikaś ca yaḥ ||
pitrā vivadamānaś ca kulikaḥ sūcakas tathā |
śrutismṛtyuditaṃ karma tyajann anyat karoti vā ||
bahunā kim ihoktena sākṣyaṃ teṣu na vidyate |
uktānāṃ sākṣiṇām asaṃbhave niṣiddheṣv api pratiprasavam āha manuḥ |
striyāpy asaṃbhave kāryaṃ bālena sthavireṇa vā |
śiṣyeṇa bandhunā vāpi dāsena bhṛtakena vā ||
atra viśeṣam āha nāradaḥ |
asākṣiṇo ye nirdiṣṭā dāsanaikṛtikādayaḥ |
kāryagauravam āsādya bhaveyus te ‘pi sākṣiṇaḥ ||
teṣām api na bālaḥ san naiko na strī na kūṭakṛt |
n bāndhavo na cārātiḥ brūyus te sākṣyam anyathā ||
bālo ‘jñānād asatyāt strī pāpābhyāsāc ca kūṭakṛt |
vibrūyuḥ bāndhavāḥ snehāt vairaniryātanād ariḥ ||
eko lubdhas tv asākṣī syāt bahvyaḥ śudhyo ‘pi na striyaḥ |
strībuddher asthiratvāc ca doṣaiś cānye ‘pi ye vṛtāḥ ||
anye ‘pi doṣair vṛtāḥ doṣayuktā na sākṣiṇa ity arthaḥ | atra yājñavalkyaḥ |
sarvaḥ sākṣī saṃgrahaṇe cauryapāruṣyasāhase |
atra uśanāḥ |
dāso ‘ndho badhiraḥ kuṣṭī strībālasthavirādayaḥ |
ete ‘py anabhisaṃbandhāḥ sāhase sākṣiṇo matāḥ ||
sarvatrādṛṣṭadoṣāṇāṃ pratiprasavo na stryādīnāṃ taskarādīnāṃ teṣāṃ pāpābhyāsavaśāt satyābhāvāc ca | yatra punar duṣṭā eva draṣṭāro na sādhavaḥ tatra tān vihāya divyaiḥ parīkṣaṇaṃ kartavyam |
sāhaseṣu ca sarveṣu steye saṃgrahaṇeṣu ca |
vāgdaṇḍayoś ca pāruṣye na parīkṣeta sākṣiṇaḥ ||
iti yad idaṃ nāradavacanam, tathā –
steye ca sāhase caiva pāruṣye saṅgame striyāḥ |
garādīnāṃ prayoge ca na doṣaḥ sākṣiṣu smṛtaḥ ||
iti yac vyāghrapādavacanam, tad ubhayam abhirūpaparīkṣānirākaraṇārtham, na punaḥ steyādidoṣanirākaraṇārtham | sākṣiṇāṃ parīkṣā kartavyā na vā ity atra avaśyaṃ kartavyaiva iti kātyāyanaḥ |
ṛṇādiṣu parīkṣeta sākṣiṇaḥ sthirakarmasu |
sāhasātyāyake caiva parikṣā kutracit smṛtā ||
atha sākṣiparīkṣā
atra kātyāyanaḥ |
upasthitān parīkṣeta sākṣiṇo nṛpatiḥ svayam |
athaite sākṣiṇaḥ sarve sādhavo ‘rhanti sākṣitām ||
vṛddhakātyāyanaḥ |
sākṣiṇo ‘rthī samuddiṣṭān satsu doṣeṣu dūṣayet |
aduṣṭān dūṣayan vādī tatsamaṃ daṇḍam arhati ||
sabhāsadāṃ prasiddhaṃ yat lokasiddham athāpi vā |
sākṣiṇāṃ dūṣaṇaṃ grāhyam asādhyaṃ nānyad iṣyate ||
