saṃsadi prativādinā sākṣidūṣaṇe kṛte sākṣiṇaḥ praṣṭavyāḥ, “yuṣmāsv abhihito doṣaḥ kiṃ satyaṃ na vā” iti | te tu yadi dūṣaṇam abhyupagacchanti tadā na sākṣiṇaḥ | atha nāṅgīkurvanti, tadā dūṣaṇavādinā dūṣaṇakriyābhāvyā | atha bhāvayituṃ na śaknoti, tadā dūṣaṇavādi tadanusāreṇa daṇḍyaḥ | yadi bhāvayati tadā na te sākṣiṇaḥ | yadā punar uddiṣṭāḥ sākṣiṇaḥ sarva eva duṣṭā bhavanti, tathārthinaḥ parājayaḥ, viparyayasya niścitatvāt | atha vā sākṣiṇāṃ doṣe ‘pi sabhyānāṃ sādhyārthasaṃdehaḥ, tadā vādaśeṣaḥ sādhanāntareṇa parvartayitavyaḥ | yadi sādhanānantaraṃ pūrvaṃ nirdiṣṭam, atha anirdiṣṭam, tadā vādasamāptiḥ, “pūrvam āveditaṃ na cet” iti vacanāt | na caitat prastutavyavahārāt vyavahārāntaraṃ tasminn eva vyavahāre pramāṇasādhanadūṣaṇābhidhānād ity etat sarvam kātyāyana āha |
sākṣidoṣāḥ prayoktavyāḥ saṃsadi prativādinā |
patre ‘bhilikhitān sarvān vācyaṃ pratyuttaraṃ tataḥ ||
pratipattau na sākṣitvam arhanti tu kadācana |
ato ‘nyathā bhāvanīyāḥ kriyayā prativādinā ||
abhāvayan damaṃ dāpyaḥ pratyarthī sākṣiṇaḥ sphuṭam |
bhāvitāḥ sākṣiṇaḥ sarve sākṣidharmanirākṛtāḥ ||
pratyarthino ‘rthino vāpi sākṣidūṣaṇasādhane |
prastutārthopayogena vyavahārāntaraṃ na ca ||
jitaḥ sa vinayaṃ dāpyaḥ śāstradṛṣṭena karmaṇā |
yadi vādī nirākāṅkṣaḥ sākṣiṣv eva vyavasthitaḥ ||
nirākāṅkṣaḥ sādhanāntaranirapekṣaḥ, “sākṣiṣv eva vyavasthitaḥ” tadā parājito daṇḍya iti | sākṣivāde na kālaharaṇaṃ kartavyam ity āha kātyāyanaḥ |
na kālaharaṇaṃ kāryaṃ rājñā sākṣiprabhāṣaṇe |
mahān doṣo bhavet kālāt dharmavyāvṛttilakṣaṇaḥ ||
atha sākṣivacanaśravaṇaṃ kartavyam ity āha manuḥ |
devabrāhmaṇasāṃnidhye sākṣyaṃ pṛcched ṛtaṃ dvijān |
udaṅmukhān prāṅmukhān vā pūrvāhṇe prayatāñ chucīn ||
atra nāradaḥ |
sākṣiṇaḥ śrāvayed vādī prativādisamīpagān |
samastān viditācārān vijñātārthān pṛthak pṛthak ||
atra manuḥ |
sabhāntaḥ sākṣiṇaḥ sarvān arthipratyarthisaṃnidhau |
prāḍvivāko niyuñjīta vidhinānena sāntvayan ||
yad dvayor anayor vettha kārye ‘smiṃś ceṣṭitaṃ mithaḥ |
tad brūta sarvaṃ satyena yuṣmākaṃ hy atra sākṣitā ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
arthipratyarthisāṃnidhye sādhyatvasya ca saṃnidhau |
pratyakṣaṃ codayet sākṣyaṃ na parokṣaṃ kathaṃcana ||
arthasyopari vartavyaṃ tayor api vinā kvacit |
cutuṣpadeṣv ayaṃ dharmo dvipadaḥ sthāvareṣu ca ||
taulyagāṇimameyānām abhāve ‘pi hi vādayet |
kriyākāleṣu sarveṣu sākṣitvaṃ na tv ato ‘nyathā ||
atra śaṅkhaḥ |
prāḍvivāko nṛpo vāpi samavetān pṛthak pṛthak |
śapathapūrvaṃ śrāvayed iti śeṣaḥ | atra pitāmahaḥ |
saṃnidhau caṇḍikāyāś ca vādidvayasamīpataḥ |
raktacandanadigdhāṅgān pṛcchec chapathapūrvakam ||
atra gautamaḥ: “śapathenaike satyaṃ karma | tad devarājabrāhmaṇasaṃsadi syāt abrāhmaṇānām | gurau dvisaptāhe sākṣiṇo ‘narthadarśinaḥ” | atra manuḥ |
yasya dṛśyeta saptāhād ukatavākyasya sākṣiṇaḥ |
rogāgnijñātimaraṇam ṛṇaṃ dāpyo damaṃ ca saḥ ||
atra kaṇvaḥ |
saptāhābhyantare yasya jñātibandhusuhṛnmṛtiḥ |
arthanāśas tathā rogo dhanaṃ dāpyo damaṃ ca saḥ ||
gurukārye dvisaptāhe sākṣiṇo ‘narthadarśanam |
āhūto yadi nāgacchet sākṣī rogavivarjitaḥ ||
ṛṇaṃ damaṃ ca dāpyaḥ syāt tripakṣāt paratas tu saḥ |
atra śapathapūrvaṃ vacanaprakāram āha kātyāyanaḥ |
vihāyopānaduṣṇīṣaṃ dakṣiṇaṃ pāṇim uddharet |
hiraṇyagobījadarbhān samālabhya mataṃ vadet ||
atra śaṅkha-likhitau: “sākṣiṇaḥ suvarṇarajataratnagodhānyasūryāgnigajaskandhāśvarathosth-śastrādibhiḥ tathā putrapautraiḥ śāpayet yathāvarṇaṃ parigrahaviśeṣaḥ syāt, devabrāhmaṇasvāminām agre” | kātyāyanaḥ |
sabhāntasthais tu vaktavyaṃ sākṣyaṃ nānyatra sākṣibhiḥ |
sarvasākṣye ‘py ayaṃ dharmo nānyatra sthāvareṣu tu ||
vācayet prāṇināṃ sākṣyaṃ vācayec chivasaṃnidhau |
tadabhāve tu viprasya nānyathaiva pradāpayet ||
tathā sākṣiṇām api vadatāṃ niyamo nāsti “nāsamavetāḥ apṛṣṭāḥ prabrūyuḥ” iti gautamasmaraṇāt | tatra manuḥ |
brūhīti brāhmaṇaṃ pṛcchet satyaṃ brūhīti pārthivam |
gobījakāñcanair vaiśyaṃ śūdram ebhis tu pātakaiḥ ||
vakṣyamāṇaiḥ ebhiḥ pātakaiḥ ity arthaḥ | tāny āha nāradaḥ |
brahmaghnasya ca ye lokā ye ca strībālaghātinaḥ |
mitradruhaḥ kṛaghnasya ye te syuḥ bruvato mṛṣā ||
janmaprabhṛti yat kiṃcit puṇyaṃ bhadra tvayā kṛtam |
tat te sarvaṃ śuno gacchet yadi brūyās tvam anyathā ||
eko ‘ham asmīty ātmānaṃ svayaṃ kalyāṇa manyase |
nityaṃ sthitas te hṛdy eṣa puṇyapāpekṣitā muniḥ ||
yamo vaivasvato rājā yas tvaiṣa hṛdi sthitaḥ |
tena ced avivādas te mā gaṅgāṃ mā kurūn gamaḥ ||
nagno muṇḍaḥ kapālena bhikṣārthaṃ kṣutpipāsitaḥ |
andhaḥ śatrugṛhaṃ gacchet yaḥ sākṣyam anṛtaṃ vadet ||
avākdhirās tamasy andhe kilbiṣī narakaṃ vrajet |
yaḥ praśnaṃ vitathaṃ brūyāt pṛṣṭaḥ san dharmaniścaye ||
andho matsyān ivāśnāti sa naraḥ saha kaṇṭakaiḥ |
yo bhāṣate ‘rthavaikalyam apratyakṣaṃ samāgataḥ ||
brūh pṛcched ity asya kvacid viṣaye apavādam āha |
gorakṣakān vāṇijakān tathā kārukuśīlavān |
preṣyān vārdhuṣikāṃś caiva viprāñ chūdravad ācaret ||
atra manu-nāradau |
yāvato bāndhavān yasmin hanti sākṣye ‘nṛtaṃ vadan |
tāvataḥ saṃkhyayā tasmin śṛṇu saumyānupūrvaśaḥ ||
pañca paśvanṛte hanti daśa hanti gavānṛte |
śatam aśvānṛte hanti sahasraṃ puruṣānṛte ||
hanti jātān ajātāṃś ca hiraṇyārthe ‘nṛtaṃ vadan |
sarvaṃ bhūmyanṛte hanti mā sma bhūmyanṛtaṃ vadeḥ ||
apsu bhūmivad ity āhuḥ strīṇāṃ bhoge ca maithune |
abjeṣu caiva ratneṣu sarveṣv aśvamayeṣu ca ||
paśucat kṣaudraghṛtayoḥ yāneṣu ca tathāśvavat |
govad rajatavastreṣu dhānye brahmaṇi caiva hi ||
etān doṣān avekṣya tvaṃ sarvān anṛtabhāṣaṇe |
yathāśrutaṃ yathādṛṣṭaṃ satyam evāñjasā vada ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
nāpṛṣṭair abhiyuktair vā samaṃ satyaṃ prayatnataḥ |
vaktavyaṃ sākṣibhiḥ sākṣyaṃ vivādasthānam āgataiḥ ||
svabhāvoktaṃ vacas teṣāṃ grāhyaṃ yad doṣavarjitam |
ukte tu sākṣiṇo rājñā praṣṭavyā na punaḥ punaḥ ||
ukte sākṣivākye yadi saṃdehaḥ syāt tadā punaḥ punaḥ praṣṭavyāḥ | asaṃdehe punaḥ svabhāvoktam eva grāhyam | na punaḥ punaḥ praṣṭavyā iti,
sākṣye sabhyāvasannānāṃ jñeyaḥ paunarbhavo vidhiḥ |
iti vacanāt | yājñavalkyaḥ |
yasyocuḥ sākṣiṇaḥ satyāṃ pratijñāṃ vijayī bhavet |
anyathāvādino yasya dhruvaṃ tasya parājayaḥ ||
tathā ca bṛhaspatiḥ |
deśakālavayodravyajātisaṃkhyāpramāṇataḥ |
anyūnaṃ cen nigaditaṃ siddhaṃ sādhyaṃ vinirdiśet ||
nirdiṣṭeṣv arthavādeṣu sākṣī cet sākṣya āgate |
na brūyād akṣarasamaṃ na tan nigaditaṃ bhavet ||
arthinoktavākyānuguṇaṃ yadi sākṣivākyaṃ na bhavati, tadā na sādhyasiddhir ity arthaḥ |
deśakālavayodravyapramāṇākṛtijātiṣu |
yatra vipratipattiḥ syāt sākṣyaṃ tad api nānyathā ||
nyūnam abhyadhikaṃ vārthaṃ prabrūyur yatra sākṣiṇaḥ |
tad apy anuktaṃ vijñeyam eṣa sākṣividhiḥ smṛtaḥ ||
atra pūrvavādinā kathitaiḥ pūrvoktalakṣaṇaiḥ sākṣibhiḥ sādhite ‘rthe, pratijñātārthavaiparītyenābhihite, yady anye sākṣiṇaḥ pratijñātārthānuguṇyena sākṣitvaṃ brūyuḥ, tadā teṣāṃ guṇavattamatve saṃkhyādhikye vā sati pūrvasākṣiṇaḥ kūṭā bhavantīti yājñavalkya āha |
ukte ‘pi sākṣibhiḥ sākṣye yady anye guṇavattarāḥ |
dviguṇā vānyathā brūyuḥ kūṭāḥ syuḥ pūrvasākṣiṇaḥ ||
atra viśeṣaṃ sa evāha |
varṇināṃ hi vadho yatra tatra sākṣy anṛtaṃ vadet |
tatpāvanāya nirvāpyaś caruḥ sārasvato dvijaiḥ ||
tathā ca manuḥ |
śūdraviṭkṣatraviprāṇāṃ yatroktena bhaved vadhaḥ |
tatra vaktavyam anṛtaṃ tad dhi tatra viśiṣyate ||
vāgdevatyaiś ca carubhiḥ yajeraṃs te sarasvatīm |
anṛtasyainasas tasya kurvāṇā niṣkṛtiṃ parām ||
kūṣmāṇḍair vāpi juhuyād ghṛtam agnau yathāvidhi |
ud ity ṛcā vā vāruṇyā tṛcenābdaivatena vā ||
atha kūṭasākṣiṇāṃ daṇḍāḥ
atha kūṭasākṣiṇo vadham āha manuḥ |
lobhān mohād bhayān maitrāt kāmāt krodhāt tathaiva ca |
ajñānād bālabhāvāc ca sākṣyaṃ vitatham ucyate ||
eṣām anyatame sthāne yaḥ sākṣyam anṛtaṃ vadet |
tasya daṇḍaviśeṣaṃ tu pravakṣyāmy anupūrvaśaḥ ||
lobhāt sahasraṃ daṇḍas tu mohāt pūrvaṃ tu sāhasam |
bhāyād dvau madhyamau daṇḍau maitrāt pūrvaṃ caturguṇam ||
kāmād daśaguṇaṃ pūrvāt krodhāt tattriguṇaṃ param |
ajñānād dve śate pūrṇe bāliśyāc chatam eva tu ||
etān āhuḥ kūṭasākṣye proktān daṇḍān manīṣiṇaḥ |
dharmasyāvyabhicārārtham adharmaniyamāya ca ||
kūṭau dākṣyaṃ tu kurvāṇān trīn varṇān dhārmiko nṛpaḥ |
pravāsayed daṇḍayitvā brāhmaṇaṃ tu vivāsayet ||
yājñavlkyaḥ |
pṛthakpṛthak daṇḍanīyāḥ kūṭakṛt sākṣiṇas tathā |
vivādāt dviguṇaṃ daṇḍaṃ vivāsyo brāhmaṇaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
yaḥ sākṣyaṃ śrāvito ‘nyebhyo nihnute tat tamovṛtaḥ |
sa dāpyo ‘ṣṭaguṇaṃ daṇḍaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ tu vivāsayet ||
abruvan hi naraḥ sākṣyam ṛṇaṃ sadaśabandhakam |
rājñā sarvaṃ pradāpyaḥ syāc catvāriṃśattame ‘hani ||
na dadāti hi yaḥ sākṣyaṃ jānann api narādhamaḥ |
sa kūṭasākṣiṇāṃ pāpaiḥ tulyo daṇḍena caiva hi ¦¦
kātyāyanaḥ |
yena sākṣyasya lobhena nirdiṣṭāḥ kūṭasākṣiṇaḥ |
gṛhītvā tasya sarvasya kuryān nirviṣayaṃ tataḥ \\
kūṭasākṣiṇā sarvasvāpahāraḥ kartavya ity arthaḥ |
atha sākṣibalābalam
atra manu-viṣṇū |
bahutvaṃ parigṛhṇīyāt sākṣidvaidhe narādhipaḥ |
sameṣu tu guṇotkṛṣṭān guṇidvaidhe dvijottamān ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
sākṣidvaidhe tu bahavaḥ grāhyāḥ sāmye guṇānvitāḥ |
guṇidvaidhe kriyāyuktāḥ tatsāmye matimattarāḥ ||
ūnādhikaṃ tu yatra syāt sākṣyaṃtatra vivarjayet |
sākṣī tatra na daṇḍyaḥ syād abruvan daṇḍam arhati ||
sādhyārthāṃśe nigadite sākṣibhiḥ sakalaṃ bhavet |
strīsaṅge sāhase caurye yat sādhyaṃ parikalpyate ||
anyatra |
sākṣiṇāṃ likhitānāṃ ca nidiṣṭānāṃ ca vādinā |
eko yady anyathā brūyāt sarva eva na sākṣiṇaḥ ||
atra bṛhaspatiḥ |
gṛhakṣetravivādeṣu sāmantebhyo vinirṇayaḥ |
nagaragrāmaṇībhyo vā ye ca vṛddhatarā matāḥ ||
āgamaṃ ca pramāṇaṃ ca bhogaṃ kālaṃ ca nāma ca |
bhūbhāgalakṣaṇaṃ caiva ye vidus te ‘pi sākṣiṇaḥ ||
asākṣike cirakṛte pṛcched uttarasākṣiṇaḥ |
śapathān vā prayuñjīta yuktileśam athāpi vā ||
atra nāradaḥ |
codanāpratighāte tu yuktileśais tam anviyāt |
deśakālārthasaṃbandhaparimāṇākrayādibhiḥ ||
ulkāhasto ‘gnido jñeyaḥ śastraprāṇis tu ghātakaḥ |
keśākeśī gṛhītas tu yugapat pāradārikaḥ ||
kuddālapāṇir vijñeyaḥ setubhettāpagādiṣu |
tathā kuṭhārapāṇis tu tarucchettā prakīrtitaḥ ||
atra prajāpatiḥ |
anvayavyatirekābhyāṃ yasya yad gamakaṃ bhavet |
sātra yuktis tasya bhavet tayā sarvaṃ viśodhayet ||
likhitādikam api yathoktalakṣaṇānanugame yuktitvena svīkartavyam iti | kātyāyanḥ |
samatvaṃ sākṣiṇāṃ yatra divyais tatra viśodhayet |
prāṇāntikavivādeṣu vidyamāneṣu sākṣiṣu ||
divyam ālambate yatra na pṛcchet tatra sākṣiṇaḥ |
bhāvitaiḥ sākṣivāde tu paritoṣam anāgate ||
abhiyoktari divyādīn pratyarthini ca dṛśyate ||
abhiyoktāram āgacchet vyavahārapadaṃ punaḥ |
pratyarthinas tu tat sarvaṃ sākṣyeṇaiva vinirṇayḥ ||
atha bhuktir ucyate
tatra bṛhaspatiḥ |
etad vidhānam ākhyātaṃ sākṣiṇāṃ likhitasya ca |
sāṃprataṃ sthāvare prāpte bhukteś ca vidhir ucyate ||
pitryalabdhakriyādhānarikthaśauryapravedanāt |
prāpte saptavidhe bhoge sāgamaḥ siddhim āpnuyāt ||
tathā |
kramāgataḥ śāsanikaḥ krayādhānāgamāgataḥ |
evaṃvidhas tu yo bhogaḥ sa tu siddhim avāpnuyāt ||
tathā |
saṃvibhāgakrayaprāptaṃ pitrā labdhaṃ tu rājataḥ |
sthāvaraṃ siddhim āpnoti bhuktyā hānim upekṣayā ||
ādhyāsanāt samārabhya bhuktir yasyāvighātinī |
triṃśadvarṣāṇy avicchinnā tasya tāṃ na vicālayet ||
samā viṃśatyavadhiketi pāṭhāntaram |
pitrā bhuktaṃ tu yad dravyaṃ bhuktyācāreṇa dharmataḥ |
tasmin prete ‘pi tatprāptaṃ bhuktyā prāptaṃ tu tasya tat ||
tribhir eva tu yā bhuktā puruṣair bhūr yathāvidhi |
lekhyābhāve tu tāṃ tatra caturthaḥ samavāpnuyāt ||
caturthasya bhuktir eva, nāgamāpekṣety arthaḥ | tathā ca nāradaḥ |
ādau tu kāraṇaṃ dānaṃ madhye bhuktis tu sāgamā |
antato bhuktir evaikā santatā yā cirantanī ||
ādau prathamapuruṣabhogākhyaviṃśatyabde | madhe dvitīre tṛtīye ca viṃśatidvaye sāgamabhuktiḥ | tataś caturthe ṣaṣṭir varṣāvicchinnā bhuktir eva kāraṇam iti | atra kātyāyanaḥ |
bhuktir balavatī śāstre santatā yā cirantanī |
vicchinnāpi tu sā jñeyā yā tu pūrvaprasādhitā ||
ṣaṣṭir varṣabhukter ūrdhvaṃ vicchede na doṣaḥ | kātyāyanaḥ |
smārte kāle kriyābhūmeḥ sāgamā bhuktir iṣyate |
asmārte ‘nugamābhāvāt kramāt tripuruṣāgatā ||
smārtakālaḥ smaraṇayogyaḥ kālaḥ ṣaṣṭivarṣākhyo navativarṣākhyo vā ekasya puruṣasya sphuṭataraḥ smṛtisaṃbhavaḥ kālaḥ | atra kātyāyanaḥ |
mukhyā paitāmahī bhuktiḥ paitṛkī cāpi saṃmatā |
vayopetair avicchinnā sthirā ṣaṣṭyabdikī matā ||
vatsarāṇāṃ tu navatir atha vā syāt tripūruṣī |
ataḥ paraṃ pramāṇaṃ syāt bhuktir evāgamād ṛte ||
navatiḥ ity etad alpavicchedaviṣayaṃ hīnavarṇabhoktṛviṣayaṃ vā | vyāsaḥ |
varṣāṇi viṃśatir bhuktā svāmināvyāhatā satī |
bhuktiḥ sā pauruṣī bhūmeḥ dviguṇā tu dvipauruṣī ||
tripauruṣī ca triguṇā na tato ‘nveṣya āgamaḥ |
atra viśeṣam āhatuḥ bṛhaspati-kātyāyanau |
sanābhibhir bāndhavaiś ca yat svajanais tathā |
bhogāt tasya na siddhiḥ syāt bhogam anyeṣu kalpayet ||
anyatra bṛhaspatiḥ |
bhuktis tripauruṣī siddhyet pareṣāṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ |
anivṛtte sapiṇḍatve skulyānāṃ na siddhyati ||
smṛtyantare |
asvāminā tu yad bhuktaṃ gṛhakṣetrāpaṇādikam |
suhṛttvena sakulyasya na tad bhogena hīyate ||
pūrvasvāmino na hīyata ity arthaḥ | tena sapiṇḍasamānodakapratyāsannabandhuvyatirikteṣu bhogaḥ kalpyata iti | atra viśeṣam āha vyāsaḥ |
āgamaḥ kāladairghyaṃ ca vicchedo ‘paravojjñanam |
pratyarthisaṃnidhānaṃ ca pañcāṅgo bhoga iṣyate ||
(svojñanam) aparavojñanam ākrośābhāvaḥ | bṛhaspatiḥ |
sāgamo dīrghakālaś ca vicchedo ‘paravoñjñitaḥ (?) |
pratyarthisaṃnidhānaṃ ca paribhogo ‘pi pañcadhā ||
yady ekaśāsane grāmakṣetrārāmāś ca lekhitāḥ |
ekadeśopabhoge ‘pi sarve bhuktā bhavanti te ||
anumānād varaḥ sākṣī sākṣibhyo likhitaṃ guru |
avyāhatā tripuruṣī bhuktir ebhyo garīyasī ||
anumānaṃ sada eva sākṣī cāmaraṇād bhavet |
avyāhataṃ lekhyabhogaṃ pramāṇaṃ tu tripūruṣam ||
vyāsaḥ |
pitā pitāmaho yasya jīvet tu prapitāmahaḥ |
trayāṇāṃ jīvatāṃ bhogo vijñeyas tv ekapūruṣaḥ ||
pitāmahena yad bhuktaṃ tat putreṇa vinā ca tam |
tau vinā tasya putreṇa tasya bhokgas tripūruṣaḥ ||
tathānyata – agnimitraḥ |
tribhir eva tu yā bhuktā puruṣair bhūr yathāvidhi |
lekhyābhāve ‘pi tāṃ tatra caturthaḥ samavāpnuyāt ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
yathā kṣīraṃ tu bhavati dhadhi kālavaśānvitam |
dānahetus tathā kālāt bhogas tripuruṣāgatāḥ ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
āgamo ‘bhyadhiko bhogāt vinā pūrvakramāgatāt |
āgame ‘pi balaṃ naiva bhuktiḥ stokāpi yatra no ||
āgamaḥ krayādisvatvahetuḥ | tasya lekhyaṃ sākṣiṇaś ca | sa āgamo bhogād adhikaḥ, balavān | pūrvatripuruṣakramāgatāṃ bhuktiṃ vinā sarvapramāṇād balavatī | āgame ‘pi stokabhuktyabhāve balaṃ nāsti | tathā ca nāradaḥ |
vidyamāne ‘pi likhitaṃ jīvatsv api ca sākṣiṣu |
viśeṣataḥ sthāvarāṇāṃ yan na bhuktaṃ na tat sthiram ||
durbalam ity arthaḥ |
bhujyamānaḥ parair arthān yaḥ svān mohād upekṣate |
samakṣaṃ jīvato ‘py asya tān bhuktiḥ kurute vaśe ||
vyāsaḥ |
upekṣitā yathā dhenuḥ kālāt pālena naśyati |
paśyato ‘nyais tathā bhuktā bhūmiḥ kālena hīyate ||
ṣaṣṭir varṣākhyakālena pañcāṅgabhogena hīyata ity arthaḥ | nanu,
āgame ‘pi balaṃ naiva bhuktiḥ stokāpi yatra no |
iti yājñavalkyavacanenāgamasya bhuktivaśena prāmāṇyam uktam |
āgamena viśuddhena bhogo yāti pramāṇatām |
aviśuddhāgamo bhogaḥ prāmāṇyaṃ naiva gacchati ||
iti āgamavaśena bhukteḥ prāmāṇyam ity anyonyāśrayaḥ syāt | maivam | tad bhinnapuruṣaviṣayam ity avirodhaḥ | ādyaviṃśatāv āgamaprābalyaṃ bhogasya tadānguṇyāt | dvitīye bhogāgamayoḥ sāmyam | tṛtīye bhukteḥ prābalyam | caturthe puruṣe pañcāṅgabhoga eva pramāṇam | nāgamāpeketi siddham | atra pañcādhyāyyāṃ bṛhaspatiḥ |
trivarṣaṃ bhujyate yena samakṣaṃ bhūr avāritā |
tasya sā nāpahartavyā kṣamāliṅgaṃ na ced vadet ||
catuṣpād dhanadhānyādi varṣād dhānim avāpnjyāt | iti |
etad vacanaṃ bhukter ādarārthaṃ na yathāśrutārthaṃ bahupratipakṣatvād iti vyākhyeyam | atra gautamaḥ: “paśubhūmistrīṇām anatibhogaḥ” iti paśvādīnāṃ trayāṇām atibhogo nāprekṣitaḥ | ākrośarahitena alpabhogenāpi svatvaṃ bhavati | kim ity anantaragrahe duhyamānāṃ pracurakṣīrāṃ dhenum upekṣate bahuphalaṃ vārāmādikaṃ kiṃ vā pareṇa bhujyamānām abhirūpāṃ dāsīm iti | yad apīdaṃ prājāpatyavacanam,
ādānakālād ārabhya bhuktir yasyāvighātinī |
samā viṃśatyavadhikā tasya tāṃ na vicālayet ||
yad apīdaṃ nāradīyavacanam,
pratyakṣaparibhogāc ca svāmino dvidaśāḥ samāḥ |
ādhyādīny api jīryeyuḥ strīnarendradhanād ṛte ||
yad apīdaṃ yājñavalkyavacanam,
paśyato ‘bruvato bhūmeḥ hānir viṃśativārṣikī |
pareṇa bhujyamānāyā dhanasya daśavārṣikī ||
iti, evam anyāny api yāni vacanāni viṃśativarṣapañcāṅgabhogamātrāt svatvanivṛttiparāṇi, tāni yathāśrutārthaparāṇi | vacanāntareṇa ṣaṣṭivarṣanavativarṣasmārtakālabhogasyaiva svatvahetutvenāvagamāt | tathā,
bhogaṃ kevalato yas tu kīrtayen nāgamaṃ kvacit |
bhogachalāpadeśena vijñeyaḥ sa tu taskaraḥ ||
iti vyāsavacanāt, tathā,
anāgamaṃ tu yo bhuṅkte bahūny abdaśatāny api |
coradaṇḍena taṃ pāpaṃ daṇḍayet pṛthivīpatiḥ ||
iti nāradīyavacanāc ca | āgamarahitāc ca kevalabhogāt svatvanivṛttyavagamāt sāgamasya ṣaṣṭivarṣādibhogasya svatvahetutvam iti sthitam |
nanu,
anyāyenāpi yad bhuktaṃ pitrā pūrvatarais tribhiḥ |
na tac chakyam apākartuṃ kramāt tripuruṣāgamam ||
iti nāradavacanāt, tathā,
yad vināgamam atyantaṃ bhuktaṃ pūrvais tribhir bhavet |
na tac chakyam apākartuṃ kramāt tripuruṣāgatam ||
iti vyāsavacanāc cānāgamasyāpi tripuruṣasyāpi tripuruṣabhogasya svatvahetutvaṃ gamyate |
maivam, sāgamasyaiva bhogasya svatvahetutvapratipādakānekavacanadarśanāt, vacanadvayam idaṃ na yathāśrutārtham | kiṃ tu kiṃ punar anyāyenānyabhuktasya ṣaṣṭivarṣād ūrdhvaṃ sthairyaparam iti vyākhyeyam | kātyāyanaḥ |
varṣāṇi viṃśatir yasya bhūmir bhuktā parair iha |
sati rājñi samarthasya tasya seha na siddhyati ||
ityevamādibhir vacanair etāvantaṃ kālam upekṣamāṇasyānyatra svadravyārthaṃ prāṇaparityāgenāpi abhiniviśamānasya pareṇa bhujyamānam ātmīyaṃ cet kim ity upekṣata iti vikrayādivad upekṣayā dairghyaliṅgenāsya svatvanivṛttiḥ, parasya svatvaprāptir anumīyata iti dhāreśvara-asahāya-śrīkarādayo manyante |
tathā virakālopekṣayā kvacit svarūpahāniḥ saṃbhāvyeta | atas tūṣṇīṃ na sthātavyam iti vyavahāropadeśamātraṃ kriyata iti | viṃśativarṣā bhuktir bhogahānikarī, na svarūpahānikarī, tathātve sakalavacanavirodhād iti viśvarūpa-bhaṭṭakumāra-brahmavit-pravarādayo manyante |
tathā kvacid bhogahāner apy apavādam āha yājñvalkyaḥ |
ādhisīmopanikṣepajaḍabāladhanair vinā |
tathopanidhirājastrīśrotriyāṇāṃ dhanair api ||
atra manur api āha |
ādhiś copanidhiś cobhau na kālātyayam arhataḥ |
apahāryau bhavetāṃ tau dīrghakālam api sthitau ||
cirakāle ‘pi na tayor bhogāt svatvam iti sthitḥ ||
atra viśeṣam āha uśanāḥ |
daśavarṣād bhogyaṃ bhoktuḥ svaṃ bhavati na bhūr iti |
nāradaḥ |
yat kiṃcid daśavarṣāṇi saṃnidhau prekṣate dhanī |
bhujyamānaṃ parais tūṣṇīṃ na sa tal labdhum arhati ||
upekṣāṃ kurvatas tasya tūṣṇīṃbhūtasya tiṣṭhataḥ |
kāle ‘tipanne pūrvokte vyavahāro na siddhyati ||
ajaḍaś ced apaugaṇḍo viṣaye cāsya bhujyate |
bhagnaṃ tad vyavahāreṇa bhoktā tad dhanam arhati |
tad bhoktā vyavahāreṇārhati na bhūtapūrvagatyety arthaḥ | bṛhaspatiḥ |
āhartā śodhayed bhogam āgamaṃ caiva saṃsadi |
tatsuto bhuktim evaikāṃ pautrādis tu na kiṃcana ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
āhartā bhuktiyukto ‘pi lekhyadoṣān viśodhayet |
tatsuto bhuktidośāṃs tu lekhyadoṣāṃās tu nāpnuyāt ||
tad ayam arthaḥ | pitrā ekena, pitṛpitāmahābhyāṃ dvābhyām, pitṛpitāmahaprapitāmahaiḥ tribhir vā aśītivarṣaṃ ṣaṣṭiṃ vā navatiṃ vā pañṣāṅgabhoge viparītāgamāprasiddhau ca bhukteḥ prāmāṇyam iti | atra kātyāyanaḥ |
svadravyaṃ dīyamānaṃ tu tatsvāmī na nirodhayet |
rikthibhir vā  parair vātha dattaṃ tenaiva tad bhavet ||
smṛtyantare |
rikthibhir vā parair dravyaṃ samakṣaṃ yasya dīyate |
anyasya bhuñjataḥ paścāt na sa tal labdhum arhati ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
paśyann anyasya dadataḥ kṣitiṃ yo na nivārayet |
svāmī satāpi sekhyena na sa tāṃ labdhum arhati ||
atra bṛhaspatiḥ |
bhūmer abhuktir lekhyasya yathākālam adarśanam |
sākṣyasyāsmaraṇaṃ caiva svārthahānikarāṇi tu ||
tasmād yatnena kartavyaṃ pramāṇaparipālanam |
tena kāryāṇi siddhyanti sthāvarāṇi carāni ca ||
vyāsaḥ |
pramāṇahetucaritaiḥ śapathena nṛpājñayā |
vādisaṃpratipattyā vā nirṇayo ‘ṣṭavidhaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
likhitaṃ sākṣiṇo bhuktiḥ pramāṇaṃ trividhaṃ smṛtam |
anumānaṃ vidur hetus tarkaś ceti manīṣiṇaḥ ||
deśasthitiḥ pūrvakṛtā caritaṃ samudāhṛtam |
arthānurūpāḥ śapathāḥ smṛtāḥ satyadhaṭādayaḥ ||
teṣām abhāve rājājñāṃ nirṇayaṃ tu vidur budhāḥ |
pramāṇaṃ traividhyād aṣṭavidhatvam | bṛhaspatiḥ |
pramāṇaniścayo yas tu vyavahāraḥ sa ucyate |
anumānena nirṇītaḥ caritram iti kathyate ||
kevalaṃ śāstram āśritya na kartavyo ‘rthanirṇayaḥ |
yuktihīne vicāre tu dharmahāniḥ prajāyate ||
dharmeṇa vyavahāreṇa cāritreṇa nṛpājñayā |
catuṣprakāro ‘bhihitaḥ saṃdighdhe ‘rthe vinirṇayaḥ ||
sarvavivādeṣu lekhyasākṣyādidharmaśāstroktapramāṇānām abhāve dharmānusāreṇa nirṇayaḥ kartavyaḥ | teṣu likhitādiṣu satsu tair eva nirṇayaḥ kartavyaḥ | deśacāritravyavahārayor virodhe vyavahāro vādanīyaḥ | rājājñāvirodhe deśacaritravyavahārāv api bādhanīyāv iti |
iti śrīmadvaradarājīye vyavahāranirṇaye vyavahāramātṛkāyā
mānuṣapramāṇakāṇḍaṃ samāptam
5. divyapramāṇanirṇayakāṇḍam
atha divyāny ucyante
atra pitāmahaḥ |
dhaṭo ‘gnir udakaṃ caiva viṣaṃ kośaś ca pañcamaḥ |
ṣaṣṭhaś ca taṇḍulaḥ proktaḥ saptamaṃ taptamāṣakaḥ ||
aṣṭamaṃ phālam ity uktaṃ navamaṃ dharmasaṃbhavam |
divyāny etāni sarvāṇi gurukāryeṣu yojayet ||
yasmād devaiḥ prayuktāni puṣkarārthaṃ mahātmabhiḥ |
parasparaviśuddhyarthaṃ tasmād divyāni nāmataḥ ||
aparādhānurūpeṇa sādhvasādhuvivakṣayā |
deśakālārthasaṃkhyābhiḥ pradātavyāni nānyathā ||
ṛṇādikeṣu kāryeṣu savivāde parasparam |
dravyasaṃkhyānvitā deyā puruṣāpekṣayā tathā ||
tatra śaṅkhaḥ: “tatra divyaṃ nāma tulādhāraṇaṃ viṣāśanam apāṃ praveśo lohadhāraṇam iṣṭāpūrtāpatyapradānam anyāṃś ca śapathān kārayet” | atra viṣṇuḥ:
atha samayakriyā | rājadrohapātakeṣu yathākāmam, nikṣepasvarṇasteyasāhaseṣu arthapramāṇāt sarveṣv arthajāteṣu mūlyaṃ kanakaṃ kalpayet | tatra kṛṣṇalone śūdraṃ dūrvākaraṃ śāpayet | dvikṛṣṇalone tilakaram, trikṛṣṇalone rajatakaram, catuḥkṛṣṇalone suvarṇakaram, pañcakṛṣṇalone sīroddhṛtamahīkaraṃ suvarṇārdhone kośo deyaḥ śūdrasya | tataḥ paraṃ yathārhaṃ daṇḍāḥ – dhaṭāgnyambuviṣāṇām anyatamaṃ kośavarjaṃ brāhmaṇasya | āgāmisamayanibandhakriyāsu kośaḥ | dviguṇe ‘rthe yathābhihitā samayakriyā vaiśyasya | triguṇe ‘rthe rājanyasya, caturguṇae ‘rthe brāhmaṇasya | prāgdṛṣṭadoṣaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ divyānām anyatamenaiva svalpe ‘rthe ‘pi kārayet | suviditasaccaritraṃ na mahaty arthe ‘pīti ||
atha divyakālaḥ
atra prajāpatiḥ |
dhaṭaḥ sarvartukaḥ prokto vāte vāti ca varjayet |
agneḥ śiśirahemantau varṣāś caiva prakīrtitāḥ ||
grīṣme toyaṃ samākhyātaṃ hemante śiśire viṣam |
avyavasthitamaryādaṃ viṣaṃ prāvṛṣi varjayet ||
caitro mārgaśiraś caiva vaiśākhaś ca tathaiva ca |
ete sādhāraṇā māsā divyānām avirodhinaḥ ||
atra viśeṣam āha pitāmahaḥ |
pūrvāhṇe ‘gniparīkṣā syāt pūrvāhṇe ca dhaṭo bhavet |
madhyāhne tu jalaṃ deyaṃ dharmatattvam abhīpsatā ||
divasasya tu pūrvāhṇe kośadivyaṃ vidhīyate |
rātrau tu paścime yāme viṣaṃ deyaṃ prayatnataḥ ||
anuktakālaviśeṣāṇāṃ divyānāṃ pūrvāhṇa eva kālaḥ,
pūrvāhṇe sarvadivyānāṃ pradānaṃ parikīrtitam |
iti nāradavacanāt |
atha kartṛniyamaḥ
brāhmaṇasya dhaṭaḥ proktaḥ kṣatriyasya hutāśanam |
vaiśyasya salilaṃ proktaṃ viṣaṃ śūdrasya nirmitam ||
toyam agnir viṣaṃ caiva dātavyaṃ balināṃ nṛṇām |
bālavṛddhastriyaś caiva parīkṣeta dhaṭena tu ||
kuṣṭhināṃ varjayed agniṃ salilaṃ śvāsakāsinām |
pittaśleṣmavatāṃ nityām viṣaṃ tu parivarjayet ||
atra yājñavalkyaḥ |
tulāgnyāpo viṣaṃ kośo divyānīha viśuddhaye |
mahābhiyogeṣv etāni śīrṣakastha ‘bhiyoktari ||
pitāmahaḥ |
avaṣṭambhābhiyuktānāṃ dhaṭādīni vinirdiśet |
taṇḍulāś caiva kośaś ca śaṅkāsv etau na saṃśayaḥ ||
tatra cauryaśaṅkāyāṃ taptamāṣaḥ |
cauryaśaṅkābhiyuktānāṃ taptamāṣo vidhīyate |
vinirdiśed iti vacanāt |
cirsthāyivihīnāni divyāni parivarjayet |
dhaṭādīni viṣāntāni kośas tv evāśirā mataḥ ||
rājabhiḥ śaṅkitānāṃ ca nirdiṣṭānāṃ ca dasyubhiḥ |
ātmaśuddhiparāṇāṃ ca divyaṃ deyaṃ śiro vinā ||
aśirāṃsy api divyāni bhūbhṛd bhṛtyeṣu dāpayet |
atra nāradaḥ |
śiro ‘vasthāyini jane abhiyoktary avasthite |
divyapradānam ucitam anyatra nṛpaśāsanāt ||
atra pitāmahaḥ |
tryahoṣitāya vidhivad ekarātroṣitāya vā |
nityaṃ deyāni divyāni śucaye cārdravāsase ||
tryahoṣitatvam atyantamalinapuruṣābhiprāyam | atra yājñavalkyaḥ |
sacelasnātam āhūya sūryodaya upoṣitam |
kārayet sarvadivyāni nṛpabrāhmaṇasaṃnidhau ||
yad idaṃ vacanam,
strīṇāṃ ca na bhaved divyaṃ yadi dharmam apekṣate |
ity āṅgirasaṃ tat puruṣasya saṃbhave, na strīṇām ity abhiprāyeṇa | ata eva pitāmahaḥ |
bālavṛddhastriyaś caiva parīkṣeta dhaṭena tu | iti |
tathā nārado ‘pi divyāny uktvā āha |
strīṇāṃ śīlābhiyoge ca steyasāhasayor api |
evaṃvidho vidhir dṛṣṭaḥ sarvārthāpahnaveṣu ca || iti |
yājñavalkyaḥ |
tulā strībālavṛddhāndhapaṅgubrāhmaṇarogiṇām |
agnir jalaṃ vā śūdrasya yavāḥ sapta viṣasya vā ||
atra hārītaḥ |
rājanye ‘gniṃ dhaṭaṃ vipre vaiśye toyaṃ niyojayet |
sarveṣu sarvaṃ divyaṃ vā viṣavarjaṃ dvijottame ||
na viṣaṃ brāhmaṇe dadyād viṣaṃ varṇāntare smṛtam |
kośataṇḍuladharmāṃs tu dharmasaṃbhavam eva ca ||
putradārādiśapathān sarvakālaṃ prayojayet |
nārtānāṃ toyaśūddhiḥ syāt na viṣaṃ pittarogiṇām ||
śvitryandhakunakhādīnāṃ nāgnikarma vidhīyate |
pitāmahaḥ |
strībālavṛddharogārtanirutsāhāni durbalā |
na majjanīyās toyeṣu svapaprāṇā hi te smṛtāḥ ||
na lohaśipinām agniṃ salilaṃ nāmbusevinām |
mantrayogavidāṃ caiva viṣaṃ dadyāt tu na kvacit ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
divyaṃ tu varjayen nityam ārtānāṃ tu garair nṛṇām |
taṇḍulair nābhiyuñjīta vratināṃ mukharogiṇām ||
mahābhiyoge nirdharme kṛtaghne klībakutsite |
nāstike dṛṣṭadoṣe ca kośapānaṃ vivarjayet ||
pitāmaḥ |
mātāpitṛdvijagurubālastrīrājaghātinām |
mahāpātakayuktānāṃ nāstikānāṃ viśeṣataḥ ||
liṅgināṃ kṣapaṇādīnāṃ mantratantrakriyāvidām |
varṇasaṃkarajātānāṃ pāpābhyāsapravartinām ||
eteṣv evābhiyogeṣu nindyeṣv anyeṣu caiva hi |
divyaṃ prakalpayen naiva rājā dharmaparāyaṇaḥ ||
mahāpātakaśaṅkāyāṃ paraśus taptamāṣakaḥ |
daśaniṣke tadarthe ca tadardhe ca tathā smṛtaḥ ||
pañcaniṣke tadardhe ca tadardhe taptamāṣakaḥ |
iti pāṭhāntaram | atra hārītaḥ |
visrambhe saṃdhikārye ca vivāde rikthināṃ tathā |
kriyāsamūha ṛtvikṣu kośam eva pradāpayet ||
ubhayānumate deyaṃ sarvaṃ divyaṃ prayatnataḥ |
pitāmahaḥ |
lokāpavādaduṣṭānāṃ divyaṃ deyaṃ śiro vinā |
indrasthāne ‘bhiśaptānāṃ mahāpātakināṃ nṛṇām ||
rājadrohapravṛttānāṃ rājadvāre pradāpayet |
prātilomyaprasūtānāṃ divyaṃ deyaṃ catuṣpathe ||
ato ‘nyeṣu ca kāryeṣu sabhāmadhye prayojayet |
yathoktavidhinā deyaṃ divyaṃ divyaviśāradaiḥ ||
ayathoktaprayuktaṃ tu na satyaṃ sādhyasādhane |
adeśakāladattāni bahirvedikṛtāni ca ||
vyabhicāraṃ sadāmṛni (?) kurvantīha na saṃśayaḥ |
[iti divyānāṃ kāladeśapātrakartṛbhedanirṇayaḥ]
atha dhaṭavidhir ucyate
atra prajāpatiḥ |
divyeṣu sarvakāryāṇi prāḍvivākaḥ pradāpayet |
adhvareṣu yathādhvaryuḥ sopavāso nṛpājñayā ||
chittvā yājñīyavṛkṣaṃ tu yūpavan mantrapūrvakam |
praṇamya lokapālebhyaḥ tulā kāryā manīṣibhiḥ ||
ṛjvī dhaṭatulā kāryā khādirī taindukī tathā |
khādiraṃ kārayet tatra nirvraṇaṃ śuklavarjitam ||
śiṃśapā tadalābhe tu sālaṃ vā koṭaraṃ vinā |
evaṃvidhāni kāṣṭhāni dhaṭārthaṃ parikalpayet ||
mantraḥ saumyo vānaspatyaḥ chedane japya eva ca |
caturaśrā tulā kāryā pādau cāpi tathāvidhau ||
anantaraṃ tu tayor hastau bhaved adhyardham eva vā |
toraṇe ca tathā kārye pārśvayor ubhayor api ||
dhaṭād uccatare syātāṃ nityaṃ daśabhir aṅgulaiḥ |
avalambau ca kartavyau toraṇābhyām adhomukhau ||
mṛṇmayau sūtrasaṃbaddhau dhaṭamastakacumbinau |
prāṅmukho niścalaḥ kāryaḥ śucau deśe dhaṭaḥ sadā ||
indrasthāne sabhāyāṃ vā rājadvāre catuṣpathe |
śikyadvayaṃ samāsajya pārśvayor ubhayor api ||
prāṅmukhān kārayed darbhān śikyayor iti niścayaḥ |
ekasmiṃs tulayet kartṝn anyasmin mṛttikāṃ śubhām ||
iṣṭakābhasmapāṣāṇakapālāsthivivarjitām |
parīkṣakā niyoktavyās tulāmānaviśāradāḥ ||
vaṇijo hemakārāś ca kāṃsyakārās tathaiva ca |
kāryaḥ parīkṣakair nityam avalambasamo dhaṭaḥ ||
udakaṃ ca pradāpyaṃ syāt dhaṭasyopari paṇḍitaiḥ |
yasmin na plavate toyaṃ sa vijñeyaḥ samo dhaṭaḥ ||
dhaṭaṃ tu kārayen nityaṃ patākādhvajaśobhitam |
tulayitvā naraṃ pūrvaṃ tatas tam avatārayet ||
evam uktavidhinā tulāṃ kṛtvā prāḍvivākaḥ evaṃ kuryād ity āha pitāmahaḥ |
prāñjaliḥ prāṅmukho bhūtvā prāḍvivākas tato vadet |
ehy ehi bhagavan dharma asmin divye samāviśa ||
sahito lokapālaiś ca vasvādityamarudgaṇaiḥ |
āvāhya tu dhaṭe dharmaṃ paścād aṅgādi vinyaset ||
indraṃ pūrve tu digbhāge preteśaṃ dakṣiṇe tathā |
varuṇaṃ paścime bhāge kuberaṃ tūttare tathā ||
agnyādilokapālāṃś ca koṇabhāgeṣu vinyaset |
indraḥ pīto yamaḥ śyāmo varuṇaḥ sphāṭikaprabhaḥ ||
kuberas tu suvarṇābho hy agniś cāpi suvarṇabhaḥ |
tathaiva nirṛtiḥ śyāmo vāyur dhūmraḥ praśasyate ||
īśānas tu bhaved rakta evaṃ dhyāyet kramād imān |
indrasya dakṣiṇe pārśve vasūn ārādhayed budhaḥ ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
dharodghruvas tathā soma āpaś caivānilo ‘nalaḥ |
deveśeśānayor madhye hy ādityānāṃ tato ‘yanam ||
dhātāryamā ca mitraś ca varuṇo ‘ṃśo bhagas tathā |
indro vivasvān pūṣā ca parjanyo daśamaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
tatas tvaṣṭā tato viṣṇur ajaghanyo jaghanyajaḥ |
ity ete dvādaśādityā nāmabhiḥ parikīrtitāḥ ||
agneḥ paścimabhāge tu rudrāṇām ayanaṃ viduḥ |
vīrabhadraś ca sāṃbhuś ca girīśaś ca mahāyaśāḥ ||
ajekapād ahirbudhnyaḥ pinākī cāparājitaḥ |
bhavanātheśvaraś [bhuvanādhīśvarāś] caiva kapālī ca viśāṃpatiḥ ||
sthāṇur bhagaś ca bhagavān rudrāś caikādaśa smṛtāḥ |
preteśarakṣasor madhye mātṛsthānaṃ prakalpayet ||
brāhmī māheśvarī caiva kaumārī vaiṣṇavī tathā |
vārāhī caiva māhendrī cāmuṇḍā gaṇasaṃyutā ||
nirṛter uttare bhāge gaṇeśāyatanaṃ viduḥ |
varuṇasyottare bhāge marutāṃ sthānam ucyate ||
śvasanaḥ sparśano vāyur anilo mārutas tathā |
prāṇaḥ prāṇeśajīvau ca maruto ‘ṣṭau prakīrtitāḥ ||
smṛtyantare |
āvaho vivahaś caiva udvahaḥ saṃvahas tathā |
nyāvaho nivahaś caiva pravaho jīvahas tathā ||
nāmabhedena marutāṃ vikalpaḥ sa udāhṛtaḥ |
dhaṭasyottarabhāge tu durgām āvāhayed budhaḥ ||
etāsāṃ devatānāṃ tu svanāmnā pūjanaṃ viduḥ |
dhūpāvasānaṃ dharmāya datvā cārghyādikaṃ kramāt ||
dhaṭasya paścād aṅgānāṃ bhūṣaṇāntaṃ prakalpayet |
gandhapuṣpādīni ca dhaṭapūjāyāṃ raktāni kāryāṇi, nāradavacanāt:
raktair gandhaiś ca puṣpaiś ca dadhyapūpākṣatādibhiḥ |
arcayet tu dhaṭaṃ pūrvaṃ tataḥ śiṣṭāṃs tu pūjayet || iti |
indrādīnāṃ raktair anyair vā pūjanam | etac ca sarvaṃ prāḍvivākaḥ kuryād ity āha prajāpatiḥ |
prāḍvivākas tato vipro vedavedāṅgapāragaḥ |
śrutavṛttopasaṃpannaḥ śānticitto vimatsaraḥ ||
satyasaṃdhaḥ śucir dakṣaḥ sarvaprāṇihite rataḥ |
upoṣitaḥ śuddhavāsāḥ kṛtadantānudhāvanaḥ ||
sarvāsāṃ devatānāṃ ca pūjāṃ kuryād yathāvidhi |
caturdikṣu tathā homaḥ kartavyo vedapāragaiḥ ||
ājyena haviṣā caiva samidbhir homasādhanaiḥ |
sāvitryā praṇavenātha svāhāntenaiva hāvayet ||
dharmārtham abhiyuktaḥ syāt likhitvā tantupatrake |
mantreṇānena sahitaṃ tat kāryaṃ tu śirogatam ||
ayaṃ mantraḥ |
ādityacandrāv anilo ‘nalaś ca dyaur bhūmir āpo hṛdayaṃ yamaś ca |
ahaś ca rātriś ca ubhe ca saṃdhye dharmaś ca jājāti narasya vṛttam || iti |
anena mantreṇa sahitam abhiyuktārthaṃ patre likhitvā tat patraṃ śirasi dhāryam | etac ca dharmāvāhanādi śirasi patrārohaṇāntaṃ divyamātṛkākhyaṃ sarvadivyasādhāraṇam uktam | tathā ca prajāpatiḥ |
imaṃ mantravidhiṃ kṛtsnaṃ sarvaṃ divyeṣu yojayet |
āvāhanaṃ ca devānāṃ tathaiva parikalpayet |
anantaraṃ prāḍvivāko dhaṭam āmantrayet |
tvaṃ dhaṭa brahmaṇā sṛṣṭaḥ parīkṣārthaṃ durātmanām |
dhakārād dharmamūrtis tvaṃ ṭakārāt kuṭilaṃ naram ||
dhṛto bhāvayase yasmāt dhaṭs tenābhidhīyase ||
ity anena mantreṇa tulām abhimantrayet | yājñavalkya āha |
tulādhāraṇavidvadbhir abhiyuktas tulāddhṛtaḥ |
pratimānasamībhūto rekhāṃ kṛtvāvatāritaḥ ||
tvaṃ tule satyadhāmāsi purā devair vinirmitā |
tat satyaṃ vada kalyāṇi saṃśayān māṃ vimocaya ||
yady asmi pāpakṛn mātaḥ tato māṃ tvam adho naya |
śuddhaś ced gamayordhvaṃ māṃ tvaṃ tulety abhimantrayet ||
atra nāradaḥ |
śikyadvayaṃ samāsādya dhaṭakarkaṭayor dṛḍham |
ekatra śikye puruṣam anyatra tulayec chilām ||
dhārayed uttare pārśve puruṣaṃ dakṣiṇe śilām |
pīṭhikāṃ kārayet tasmin iṣṭakāpāṃsuloṣṭakaiḥ ||
pratimāne mṛcchilayor vikalpaḥ | evaṃ śiro’vasthitajitapatrakramam anantaraṃ dhaṭam āropayati | āropitaṃ ca vināḍīpañcakaṃ yāvat tāvat tathaivāvasthāpayet,
vināḍyaḥ pañca vijñeyāḥ parīkṣyāḥ kālakovidaiḥ |
iti smṛteḥ | daśagurvakṣayoccāraṇakālaḥ prāṇaḥ | ṣaṭprāṇo vināḍiḥ |
daśagurvakṣaraḥ prāṇaḥ ṣaṭprāṇāḥ syur vināḍikā |
tāsāṃ ṣaṣṭyā tu ghaṭikā tāsāṃ ṣaṣṭyā tv ahaḥ smṛtam || iti |
śuddhyaśuddhinirṇayakāraṇam āha prajāpatiḥ |
tulito yadi vardheta sa śuddhaḥ syān na saṃśayaḥ |
samo vā hīyamāno vā na viśuddho bhaven naraḥ ||
tathā anyatra |
kakṣyācchede tulābhaṅge dhaṭakarkaṭayos tathā |
rajjuchede ‘kṣabhaṅge ca tathaivāśuddhim ādiśet || iti |
yadā tu dṛśyamāne kāraṇe eṣāṃ bhaṅgaḥ nādṛṣṭavaśāt, tadā samādhānaṃ kṛtvā punar āropaṇaṃ kartavyam,
śikyādichedabhaṅge ca punar āropayen naram |
iti smṛtyantaravacanāt | same punaḥprayoga iti bṛhaspatir āha |
dhaṭe niyuktas tulito hīnaś ced dhānim āpnuyāt |
tatsamas tu punas taulyo vardhito vijayī bhavet ||
śikyachede ‘kṣabhaṅge vā dadyāc chuddhiṃ punar nṛpaḥ ||
atra prajāpatiḥ |
sākṣiṇo brāhmaṇāḥ śreṣṭāḥ yathādṛṣṭārthavādinaḥ |
jñāninaḥ śucayo ‘lubdhā niyoktavyā nṛpeṇa tu ||
teṣāṃ vacanato gamyaḥ śuddhyaśuddhivinirṇayaḥ |
ṛtvikpurohitācāryān dakṣiṇābhiś ca toṣayet ||
śaṃsantu brāhmaṇāḥ sarve śuddhyaśuddhī nṛpe tadā |
sadbhiḥ parivṛto rājā śuddham āhūya pūjayet ||
śuddhivyañjanahetvarthaṃ patraṃ dadyāt svacihnitam |
aśuddhaṃ daṇḍayitvā tu svarāṣṭrād vipravāsayet ||
evaṃ kārayitā rājā bhuktvā bhogān manogatān |
mahatīṃ kīrtim āsādya brahmabhūyāya kalpate ||
iti dhaṭavidhiḥ
athāgnividhiḥ
atha prajāpatiḥ |
agner vidhiṃ pravakṣyāmi yathāśāstreṣu coditam |
kāreyan maṇḍalāny aṣṭau purastān navamaṃ tathā ||
āgneyaṃ maṇḍalaṃ tv ādyaṃ dvitīyaṃ vāruṇaṃ smṛtam |
tṛtīyaṃ vāyudaivatyaṃ caturthaṃ yamadaivatam ||
pañcamaṃ tv indradaivatyaṃ maṇḍalaṃ tūcyate budhaiḥ |
kauberam ucyate ṣaṣṭhaṃ saptamaṃ somadaivatam ||
ādityam aṣṭamaṃ caiva navamaṃ sarvadaivatam |
etāni maṇḍalāny aṣṭāv iti śāstravido viduḥ ||
aṣṭabhir maṇḍalaiḥ proktam aṅgulīnāṃ śatadvayam |
caturviṃśatsamadhikaṃ bhūmes tu parikalpanā ||
ṣoḍaśāṅgulisaṃjñeyaṃ maṇḍalaṃ tāvad antaram |
aṅgulipramāṇaṃ ca,
tiryagyavodarā hy aṣṭāv ūrdhvā vā vrīhayas trayaḥ |
pramāṇam aṅgulasyoktaṃ vitastir dvādaśākgulā ||
hato vitastidvitayaṃ daṇḍo hastacatuṣṭayam |
tatsahasradvayaṃ krośo yojanaṃ taccatuṣṭayam || iti |
smṛtyantare |
dvātriṃśadaṅgulaṃ prāhuḥ maṇḍalaṃ tāvadantaram |
aṣṭabhir maṇḍalair evam aṅgulīnāṃ śatadvayam ||
ṣaṭpañcāśatsamadhikaṃ bhūmes tu parikalpanā |
kartuḥ padaḥ samaṃ kāryaṃ maṇḍalaṃ tu pramāṇataḥ ||
padaḥ samam iti kalpāntaram | tatra navamamaṇḍalaparimitāṅgulapramāṇakaṃ vihāyāṣṭabhir maṇḍalaiḥ aṣṭabhiś cāntarālaiḥ pratyekaṃ ṣoḍaśāṅgulipramāṇena aṅgulīnāṃ ṣaṭpañcāśadadhikaṃ (?) śatadvayaṃ saṃpadyate | tatrāpi gamyāni saptaiva maṇḍalāni | yasmāt prathame tiṣṭhati navame kṣipati | tatra sacelasnātam āhūya ityādi divyamātṛkoktasādhāraṇadharmeṣv anuṣṭhiteṣu tulāvidhānoktadharmāvāhanādi-śiraḥpatrāropaṇānte ca vidhyante | saty agnividhau viśeṣam āha prajāpatiḥ |
paścime maṇḍale tiṣṭhan prāṅmukhaḥ prāñjaliḥ śuciḥ |
lakṣayeyuḥ kṣatādīni hastayor adhikāriṇaḥ ||
karābhyāṃ yenāsau vimṛditavrīhihastasya karau lakṣayitvā tilakālakavaṇakiṇādisthāneṣv alaktakarasādināṅkayitvā yathāha nāradaḥ |
hastakṣateṣu sarveṣu kuryād dhaṃsapadāni tu |
iti | anantaraṃ saptāśvatthasya parṇāni hastayor añjalīkṛtayor nyaset,
patrair añjalim āpūrya āśvatthaiḥ saptabhiḥ samaiḥ |
iti smaraṇāt | tāni ca hastasahitāni sūtreṇa tāvad veṣṭayet | yāvanty aśvatthaparṇāni saptakṛtvo veṣṭayed ity arthaḥ | sūtrāṇi ca saptaśuklāni bhavanti |
veṣṭayīta sitair hastau saptabhiḥ sūtratantubhiḥ |
iti nāradavacanāt | tathā sapta śamīpatrāṇi saptaiva dūrvāpatrāṇi cākṣatāś ca dadhyaktān akṣatāṃś cāśvatthapatrāṇām upari vinyaset,
sapta pippalapatrāṇi śamīpatrāṇy athākṣatān |
dūrvāyāḥ sapta patrāṇi dadhyaktāś cākṣatān nyaset ||
iti smaraṇāt, tathā kusumāni ca vinyaset,
sapta pippalapatrāṇi akṣatān sumanodadhi |
hastayor nikṣipet tatra sūtreṇāveṣṭanaṃ tathā ||
iti pitāmahavacanāt | sumanasaḥ puṣpāṇi | yad api smaraṇam,
ayas taptaṃ tu pāṇibhyām arkapatrais tu saptabhiḥ |
antarhitaṃ haran śuddhas tv adagdhaḥ saptame pade ||
iti tad aśvatthapatrābhāve ‘rkapatraviṣayaṃ veditavyam | atra yājñavalkyaḥ |
karau vimṛditavrīheḥ lakṣayitvā tato nyaset |
saptāśvatthasya patrāṇi tāvat sūtreṇa veṣṭayet ||
yasya karābhyāṃ vrīhayo vimarditāḥ, tasya karau lakṣayitvā hastagatatilakālavraṇakiṇādi parigaṇayya, anantaraṃ saptāśvatthasya patrāṇi añjalīkṛtayor hastayor nyaset | tāny aśvatthapatrāṇi saptakṛtvaḥ śuklais tantubhiḥ veṣṭayet ,
veṣṭayeran sitair hastau saptabhiḥ sūtratantubhiḥ |
iti nāradavacanāt | aśvatthapatrāṇām upari saptaśamīpatrāṇi dūrvāpatrāṇi ca sapta dadhyaktān akṣatāṃś ca vinyaset | tathā ca pitāmahaḥ |
sapta pippalapatrāṇi śamīpatrāṇy athākṣatān |
dūrvāyāḥ sapta patrāṇi dadhyaktān akṣatāṃs tathā ||
vinyased iti śeṣaḥ | tathā kusumādi ca vinyaset |
saptapippalapatrāṇi akṣatān kusumaṃ dadhi |
hastayor nikṣipet tatra sūtreṇāveṣṭayet tathā || iti |
yad apīdaṃ smaraṇam,
ayas taptaṃ tu pāṇibhyām arkapatrais tu saptabhiḥ |
antarhitarajaḥ śuddhas tv adagdhaṃ saptame pade ||
iti, tad aśvatthapatrābhāve draṣṭavyam | atra prajāpatiḥ |
agrahīnaṃ samaṃ kṛtvā tv aṣṭāṅgulam ayomayam |
piṇḍaṃ tu dāpayed agnau pañcāśatpalikaṃ smṛtam ||
atra vyāsaḥ |
prāṅmukhas tu tatas tiṣṭhet prasāritakarāṅguliḥ |
ārdravāsāḥ śuciś caiva śirasy āropya patrakam ||
jatyaiva lohakāro yaḥ kuśalaś cāgnikarmaṇi |
dṛṣṭaprayogaś cānyatra tena yo ‘gnau tu dāpayet ||
agnivarṇamayaḥ piṇḍaṃ sasphuliṅgaṃ surakṣitam |
pañcāśatphalikaṃ bhūyaḥ kārayitvā śucir dvijaḥ ||
tṛtīyatāpe tapyantaṃ brūyāt satyapuraskṛtam |
śṛṇvimaṃ mānavaṃ dharmaṃ lokapālair adhiṣṭhitam ||
tvam agne sarvadevānāṃ pavitraṃ paramaṃ mukham |
tvam agne sarvabhūtānāṃ hṛdistho vetsi ceṣṭitam ||
satyānṛte ca vijñāya tvattaḥ samupagṛhyate |
vedādibhir idaṃ proktaṃ nānyathā kartum arhasi ||
sa eṣa tvāṃ kārayati satyenānena mānavaḥ |
satyavākyasya cāsya tvaṃ śīto bhava hutāśana ||
mṛṣāvākyasya pāpasya daha hastau ca māyinaḥ |
amum arthaṃ ca patrastham abhiyuktaṃ yathārthataḥ ||
saṃśrāvya mūrdhni tasyaiva yasya deyo yathākramam |
ity uktvā hastayos tasya pradadyāt piṇḍapāvakam ||
saṃprataptāyasaṃ bhugnaṃ visphuliṅgārcir uddhṛtam |
dṛḍhaṃ gṛhītvā saṃdaṃśaiḥ vidyujjvālāsamaprabham ||
hastābhyāṃ taṃ samādāya prāḍvivākasamīritaḥ |
sthitvaikasmiṃs tato ‘nyāni vrajet sapta tv ajihamagaḥ ||
asaṃbhrāntaḥ śanair gacched akruddhaḥ so ‘nalaṃ prati |
na pātayet tām aprāpya yā bhūmiḥ parikalpitā ||
na maṇḍalam atikrāmet nāntarā sthāpayet padam |
maṇḍalaṃ nāṣṭamaṃ gatvā tato ‘gniṃ visṛjen naraḥ ||
yas tv antarā pātayati dagdhaś ca na vibhāvyate |
punas taṃ hārayed agniṃ sthitir eṣā dṛḍhīkṛtā ||
prathamaṃ maṇḍalasthitasya kartur agnyabhimantraṇam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
tvam agne sarvabhūtānām antaś carasi pāvaka |
sākṣivat puṇyapāpebhyo brūhi satyaṃ kave mama ||
satyaṃ brūhi darśayety arthaḥ | tatra prathamaṃ sutaptaṃ piṇḍaṃ lohaśuddhyartham udake nikṣipya, punaḥ saṃtāpyodake nikṣipya, tṛtīyatāpe saṃdaṃśena gṛhītvā, purata ānīte kartā tvam agne sarvabhūtānām ity abhimantraṇaṃ brūyāt | asminn avasare prāḍvivāko maṇḍalānāṃ dakṣiṇapradeśe laukikam agnim upasamādhāya agnaye pāvakāya svāhā iti aṣṭottaraśatam ājyena juhuyāt,
śāntyarthaṃ juhuyād agnau ghṛtaṃ cāṣṭottaraṃ śatam |
iti smṛtyantare darśanāt | aṣṭame maṇḍale sthitvā, navame ‘gnityāge kṛte, evaṃ kuryād ity āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
muktvāgniṃ mṛditavrīhir adagdhaḥ śuddhim āpnuyāt |
atra kātyāyanaḥ |
praskhalaty abhiśastaś cet sthānād anyatra dahyate |
adagdhaṃ te vidur devāḥ tasya bhūyo ‘pi dāpayet ||
sthānād anyatra hastābhyām anyatrety arthaḥ | atra prajāpatiḥ |
antarā patite piṇḍe saṃdehe vā punar haret |
hṛte tu mardayet paścāt vrīhīn apy atha vā yavān ||
yadi gacchataḥ antarāṣṭamamaṇḍalād arvāg eva piṇḍaḥ patati, vrīhiyavādimardane kṛte ‘pi dagdhādagdhatvena vā saṃśayo bhavati, tadā punar haret | dvitīye prayoge ‘pi saṃdehe hānir eva, punaḥ punar hared iti vacanābhāvāt | tatra śuddhasya rājñā śuddhipatraṃ deyam | aśuddhasya pūrvavad daṇḍādānapūrvakaṃ tyāga eva |
[ity agnividhiḥ]
athodakavidhir ucyate
atra prajāpatiḥ |
toyasyātha pravakṣyāmi vidhiṃ dharmyaṃ purātanam |
śubhāśubhaparīkṣārthaṃ brahmaṇābhihitaṃ svayam ||
śarān saṃpūjayed bhaktyā vaiṇavaṃ ca dhanus tathā |
arcayet puṣpadīpaiś ca tataḥ karma samārabhet ||
tatra kātyāyanaḥ |
śarān anāyasair agraiḥ prakurvīta viśuddhaye |
veṇukāṇḍamayāṃś caiva kṣeptā ca sudṛḍhaṃ kṣipet ||
atra nāradaḥ |
krūraṃ dhanuḥ saptaśataṃ madhyamaṃ ṣaṭchataṃ smṛtam |
mandaṃ pañcaśataṃ jñeyaṃ eṣa jñeyo dhanurvidhiḥ ||
nātikrūreṇa dhanuṣā kṣepayitvā śaratrayam |
pānīye majjanaṃ kāryaṃ śaṅkā yatra prajāyate ||
brāhmaṇaḥ kṣatriyo vaiśyo rāgadveṣavivarjitaḥ |
nābhimātre jale sthitvā puruṣaṃ stambayed balī ||
tasyorū pratisaṃgrāhya nimajjed abhiśāpavān |
atra prajāpatiḥ |
śaratrayaṃ vaiṇavaṃ ca dhanuṣy api ca vaiṇave |
tatraikādaśāṅgulyadhikaṃ hastacatuṣṭayam uttamasya dhanuṣaḥ pramāṇam, madhyamasya daśāṅgulādhikam, adhamasya navāṅgulādhikam, śarāś cānāyasāgrā vaiṇavāḥ kāryāḥ | atra prajāpatiḥ |
kṣeptā ca kṣatriyaḥ kāryaḥ tadvṛttir brāhmaṇo ‘pi vā |
akrūrahṛdayaḥ śāntaḥ sopavāsaḥ kṣiped iṣūn ||
madhyamena tu cāpena prakṣipet tu śaratrayam |
hastānāṃ tu śate sārdhe lakṣyaṃ kṛtvā vicakṣaṇaḥ ||
tadgocarāṇāṃ hi budhaḥ śarāṇāṃ śastracodanāt |
madhyamas tu śaro grāhyaḥ sarpaṇaṃ parivarjayet ||
sarpan sarpan śaro yāyāt dūrād dūrataraṃ bahiḥ ||
atra vyākhyātṛbhir evam uktam | lakṣyavidhānāt patanaṃ grāhyam iti ca vidhānāl lakṣyād arvāk patane śaram ādāya tal lakṣyaṃ gatvā vegavatā puruṣeṇāgantavyam | lakṣyam atikramya patane sati, śaram ādāya patanadeśe sthitvā, tata ārabhya vegavatā puruṣeṇāgantavyam | lakṣyapatanayor ubhayor dṛṣṭārthatvāyeti |
iṣūn na prakṣipet prājño mārute cātivāyati |
viṣame bhūpradeśe vā vṛkṣasthāṇusamākule ||
devahradeṣu yat toyaṃ nāsmin kuryād viśodhanam |
āhāryaṃ varjayet toyaṃ śrīghragāsu nadīṣu ca ||
āhāryaṃ ghaṭādibhir uddhṛtam |
sthiratoye nimajjeta gandhavarṇarasānvite |
tṛṇaśaivālarahite jalūkāmatsyavarjite ||
nābhimātre nimajjeta na cāgādhe na cālpake |
āviśen majjane nityam ūrmipaṅkavivarjite ||
sthāpayet prathamaṃ toye stambhavat puruṣaṃ nṛpaḥ |
āśritya yaṃ nimajjanti mānavāḥ śuddhikāṅkṣiṇaḥ ||
atra smṛtyantarād viśeṣaḥ |
udake prāṅmukhas tiṣṭhed dharmasthūṇāṃ pragṛhya ca | iti |
nābhipramāṇodakastho yājñiyavṛkṣodbhavāṃ dharmasthūṇām avaṣṭabhya prāṅmukhas tiṣṭhed ity arthaḥ |
puruṣasya ca kartuś ca dharmadṛṣṭena hetunā |
kṣiptāyāṃ tu tṛtīyāyāṃ samaṃ gamanamajjanam ||
āgataṃ prāṅmukhaṃ kṛtvā toyamadhye tu kāriṇam |
tatas tv āvāhared devān salilaṃ cānumantrayet ||
smṛtyantare | toraṇam api nimajjanasamīpasthāne same deśe śodhyapramāṇatocchritaṃ kāryam | tatra triṣu śareṣu mukteṣu eko vegāt puruṣo madhyamaśarasthānaṃ gatvā tam ādāya tatraiva tiṣṭhati | anyo ‘pi puruṣo vegāc charamokṣasthāne toraṇamūle tiṣṭhati | etāv apy evaṃ lakṣaṇāv iti nāradaḥ |
pañcāśato dhāvakānāṃ yau syātām adhikau jave |
tau tatra viniyoktavyau śarānayanakāraṇāt ||
tatra prathamaṃ varuaṇapūjā kartavyā, nāradavacanāt |
gandhamālyaiḥ surabhibhiḥ madhukṣīraghṛtādibhiḥ |
pūjām ādau prakurvīta varuṇāya samāhitaḥ ||
evaṃ varuṇapuñāṃ prathamaṃ kṛtvā, dharmāvāhanādisakaladevatāpujāhomaṃ samantrakaṃ śiraḥpatrāropaṇādiṣu kṛteṣu prāḍvivākenodakābhimantraṇaṃ kartavyam iti yājñavalkyaḥ |
toyaṃ tvaṃ prāṇināṃ prāṇaḥ sṛṣṭer ādyaṃ tu nirmitam |
śuddhaṃ ca kāraṇaṃ proktaṃ dravyāṇāṃ dehināṃ tathā ||
atas tvaṃ darśayātmānaṃ śubhāśubhaparīkṣaṇe |
atra viṣṇuḥ |
tvam ambhaḥ sarvabhūtānām antaś carasi sākṣivat |
tvam evāmbho vijānīṣe na vidur yāni mānavāḥ ||
vyavahārābhiśasto ‘yaṃ mānuṣas tvayi majjati |
tad enaṃ saṃśayād asmād dharmatas trātum arhasi ||
evaṃ prāḍvivākena jalābhimarśane kṛte, śodhya evaṃ kuryād ity āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
satyena mābhirakṣasva varuṇety abhiśāpya kam |
nābhidadhnodakasthasya gṛhītvorū jalaṃ viśet ||
kam udakam | abhiśāpya abhimantrya | evaṃ toraṇamūle śarapatanayoḥ sthitayoḥ tṛtīyasyāṃ karatālikāyāṃ śodhyo nimajjati | tatsamakālam eva toraṇamūle sthito dūrataraṃ madhyamaśarapātasthānaṃ gacchati | anyo ‘pi śaragrāhī ca tasmin prāpte, drutataraṃ toraṇamūlaṃ prāpyāntarjalagataṃ yadi na magnaṃ paśyati, tadā śuddho bhavati | etad eva spaṣṭīkṛtaṃ pitāmahena |
gacchet toraṇamūlāt tu lakṣasthānaṃ javī naraḥ |
tasmin gate dvitīyo ‘pi vegād ādāya sāyakam ||
gacchet toraṇamūlaṃ tu yataḥ sa puruṣo gataḥ |
āgatas tu śaragrāhī na paśyati tadā jale ||
antarjalagate samyak tadā śuddhiṃ vinirdiśet |
anyathā na viśuddhaḥ syād ekāṅgasyāpi darśane ||
sthānād vānyatra gamanād yasmin pūrvaṃ niveśitaḥ | iti |
pūrvaṃ niveśitasthānād anyatra gamane ‘py aśuddhir draṣṭavyā | ekāṅgasyāpi darśana iti karṇādyabhiprāyeṇa,
śiromātraṃ tu dṛśyeta na karṇau nāpi nāsike |
apsu praveśane yasya śuddhaṃ tam api nirdiśet ||
iti viśeṣābhidhānāt |
nimajyo ‘plavane yas tu dṛṣṭaś cet prāṇinā naraḥ |
punas tatra nimajjet sa daśacihnavibhāvitaḥ ||
atra śuddhasya śuddhipatraṃ rājñā dātavyam | viparītaṃ daṇḍayitvā vivāsayet |
[iti udakavidhiḥ]
atha viṣavidhir ucyate
tatra prajāpatiḥ |
viṣasyātha pravakṣyāmi vidhiṃ bhakṣaṇacoditam |
abhiyuktāya dātavyaṃ yavamātraṃ pradīyate ||
yavāḥ sapta pradātavyā atha vā ṣaṭ ca saṃkhyayā |
śṛṅgiṇo vatsanābhasya haimajasya viṣasya vā ||
vārijāni ca jīrṇāni kṛtrimāṇi tathaiva ca |
bhūmijāni ca sarvāṇi viṣāṇi parivarjayet ||
oṣadhīś caiva ratnāni mantrayogād viṣāpahāt |
kartuḥ śarīrasaṃsthāś ca parīkṣeta mahīpatiḥ ||
pūrvāhṇe śītale deśe viṣaṃ deyaṃ ghṛtaplutam |
nāradaḥ |
varṣāsu ṣaḍyavā mātrā grīṣme pañcayavāḥ smṛtāḥ |
hemantake saptayavāḥ śarady alpā tato ‘pi hi ||
dadyād dhi sopavāsāya devabrāhmaṇasaṃnidhau |
dhūpopahāramantraiś ca pūjayitvā maheśvaram ||
dvijānāṃ saṃnidhāv eva dakṣiṇābhimukhe sthite |
udaṅmukhaḥ prāṅmukho vā dadyād vipraḥ samāhitaḥ ||
tatra prāḍvivākaḥ sopavāso mahādevaṃ saṃpūjya tatpurataḥ śailajaṃ śṛṅgaprabhavaṃ viṣaṃ sthāpayitvā dharmādipūjāṃ kṛtvā śodhyaśirasi patraṃ nidhāya viṣam abhimantrayate |
tvaṃ viṣa brahmaṇā sṛṣṭaḥ parīkṣārthaṃ durātmanām |
pāpānāṃ darśayātmānaṃ śuddhānām amṛtaṃ bhava ||
mṛtyumūrte viṣa tvaṃ hi brahmaṇā parinirmitam |
trāyasvainaṃ naraṃ pāpāt satyaṃ ced amṛtaṃ bhava ||
evam abhimantrya dakṣiṇābhimukhāya udaṅmukho vā samāhito viṣaṃ dadyāt | śodhyaḥ, evam abhimantrya bhakṣayed ity āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
tvaṃ viṣa brahmaṇaḥ putra satyadharme vyavasthita |
trāyasvāsmād abhīśāpāt satyena bhava me ‘mṛtam ||
evam uktvā viṣaṃ śārṅgaṃ bhakṣayed dhimaśailajam |
yasya vegair vinā jīryet śuddhiṃ tasya vinirdiśet ||
viṣavego nāma dhātor dhātvantratvaprāptiḥ “dhātor dhātvantaraprāptiḥ viṣavegaḥ” iti bṛhaspativacanāt | dhātor dhātavaś ca tvagasṛṅmāṃsamedo’sthimajjāśuklādīni saptadhā | evaṃ ca viṣavegāḥ sapta bhavanti | tair vinā yasya bhakṣitaṃ jīrṇaṃ bhavati sa śuddhaḥ | tathoktam |
bhakṣite tu yadā sadyo mūrchāchardivivarjitaḥ |
nirvikāro dinasyānte śuddhaṃ tam api nirdiśet || iti |
evaṃ śuddhasya śuddhipatraṃ rājñā dātavyam | itarasya tu daṇḍādikaṃ kartavyam iti |
[iti viṣavidhiḥ]
atha kośavidhir ucyate
atra prajāpatiḥ |
kośasyātaḥ pravakṣyāmi vidhiṃ dharmyaṃ purātanam |
kāraṇeṣu ca dātavyo yeṣu yeṣu ca tāñ chṛṇu ||
visrambhe sarvaśaṅkāyāṃ saṃdhikārye tathaiva ca |
eṣu kośaḥ pradātavyo nityaṃ cātmaviśuddhaye ||
madyapastrīvyasanināṃ kitavānāṃ tathaiva ca |
nāśtikadvijamukhyānāṃ kośapānaṃ vivarjayet ||
tathā ca nāradaḥ |
mahāparādhe nirdharme kṛtaghne klībakutsite |
nāstikavrātyadāśeṣu kośapānaṃ vivarjayet ||
pūrvāhṇe sopavāsasya snātasyārdrapaṭasya ca |
āstikasyāvyasaninaḥ kośapānaṃ vidhīyate ||
icchataḥ śraddhadhānasya devabrāhmaṇasaṃnidhau |
yadbhaktaḥ so ‘bhiyuktaḥ syāt taddevasya tu pāyayet ||
atra pitāmahaḥ |
bhakto yo yasya devasya pāyayet tasya taj jalam |
samabhāve tu devanānām ādityasya tu pāyayet ||
durgāyāḥ pāyayec corān ye ca śastropajīvinaḥ |
bhāskarasya tato ‘nyas tu brāhmaṇāṃs tu na pāyayet ||
durgāyāḥ snāpayec chūlam ādityasya tu maṇḍalam |
itareṣāṃ tu devānāṃ snāpayitvāyudhāni tu ||
prāṅmukhaṃ kāriṇaṃ kṛtvā pāyayet prasṛtitrayam |
atra viṣṇuḥ: “ugrān devān samabhyarcya tatsnānādikam āhṛtya prasṛtitrayaṃ pibet idaṃ mayā na kṛtam iti vyāharan devatābhimukhaḥ” iti | atra kātyāyanaḥ |
svalpe ‘parādhe devānāṃ snāpayitvāyudhāni tu |
pāyyaḥ vikāre vāśuddho niyamyaḥ śucir anyathā ||
atra nāradaḥ |
saptāhābhyantare yasya dvisaptāhena vā punaḥ |
vaikṛtaṃ yasya dṛśyeta saiva tasya vibhāvanā ||
rogāgnijñātimaraṇam arthabhrāṃśo dhanakṣayaḥ |
pratyātmikaṃ bhaved yasya vidyāt tasya na śuddhyatām ||
ūrdhvaṃ yasya dvispatāhāt vaikṛtaṃ sumahad bhavet |
nābhiyojyaḥ sa viduṣā kṛtakālasyātikramāt ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
arvāk caturdaśād ahno yasya no rājadaivikam |
vyasanaṃ jāyate ghoraṃ sa śuddhaḥ syān na saṃśayaḥ ||
alpasya dehinām aparihāryatvāt mahad bhayam ity uktam | etan mahābhiyogaviṣayaṃ “mahābhiyogeṣv etāni” iti prastutyābhidhānāt | avadhyantarāṇi pitāmahoktāny alpadhanaviṣayāṇi “kośam alpe ‘pi dāpayet” iti vacanāt |
trirātrāt saptarātrād vā dvādaśāhād dvisaptakāt |
vaikṛtaṃ yatra dṛśyeta pāpakṛt sa udāhṛtaḥ ||
iti mahābhiyogoktadravyād arvācīnaṃ dravyaṃ tridhā vibhajya trirātrādipakṣatrayaṃ vyavasthāpanīyam | tatra dharmāvāhanādisakaladevatāpūjāhomantrapratijñāpatraśiro-niveśanānteṣu kṛtyeṣu | yājñavalkyaḥ |
devān ugrān samabhyarcya tatsnānodakam āharet |
saṃśrāvya pāyayet tasmāt jalāt tu prasṛtitrayam ||
ugradevatābhiṣekajalaṃ prāḍvivāko jalapratyayokena “toya tvaṃ prāṇinā prāṇa” ity etena mantreṇābhimantrya, anantaraṃ śodhyena ca “satyena mābhirakṣa tvaṃ varuṇa” ity anena mantreṇābhimantritaṃ prasṛtitrayaṃ pāyayed iti | saṃśrāvya ity ubhayābhimantraṇam ucyate | śātātapaḥ |
saptāhābhyantare yasy dvisaptāhāntare ‘pi vā |
rogo vahnir jṇātimṛtis (?) tathā rājikadaivikam ||
jāyate vogra ātaṅkaḥ paśudhānyadhanakṣayaḥ |
yasyaivamādiko ‘narthas tam aśuddhaṃ vinirdiśet ||
smṛtyantare |
tripakṣe vā trispatāhe saptāhābhyantare ‘pi vā |
rogo ‘gnir jñātimaraṇaṃ paśudhānyadhanakṣayaḥ ||
evamādīni dṛśyante yasy ghorāṇi sarvaśaḥ |
taṃ pāpaṃ vinayed rājā tasya sārāparādhataḥ ||
svalpe ‘parādhe saptāhaṃ madhyame dvisaptāhaṃ mahāparādhe trisaptāhaṃ pakṣatrayaṃ veti vikalpaḥ | evaṃ kṛte pūrvoktakāleṣu vikārābhāve śuddho bhavati tasya śuddhipatraṃ deyam | itarasya daṇḍādikaṃ kartavyam iti |
[iti kośavidhiḥ]
atha taṇḍulavidhir ucyate
pitāmahaḥ |
taṇḍulānāṃ pravakṣyāmi vidhiṃ bhakṣaṇacoditam |
caurye tu taṇḍulā deyā nānyatreti viniścayaḥ ||
taṇḍulān kārayec chuklān śālīyān yasya kasyacit |
mṛṇmaye bhājane kṛtvā ādityasyāgrataḥ śuciḥ ||
snānodakena saṃsicya rātrau tatraiva vāsyet |
prabhāte kāriṇo deyā ādityābhimukhasya tu ||
prāṅmukhopoṣitaṃ snātaṃ śiroropitapatrakam |
taṇḍulān bhakṣayitvā tu pātre niṣṭhīvayet tataḥ ||
bhūrjasyaiva tu patre vā tv alābhe pippalasya vā |
śoṇitaṃ dṛśyate yasya hanū gaṇḍaś ca sīdati ||
gatraṃ ca kampate yasya tam aśuddhaṃ vinirdiśet |
atra kātyāyanaḥ |
devatāsnānapānīyasnigdhataṇḍulabhakṣaṇe |
śuddhān niṣṭhīvanāc chuddho niymyo ‘śucir anyathā ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
sopavāsaḥ sūryadine taṇḍulān bhakṣayec chuciḥ |
śuddhaḥ sa śuddhaniṣṭhīve raktamiśre ca doṣabhāk ||
atrāpi sarvadivyasādhāraṇadharmāvāhanādihomaśiraḥpatrāropaṇāntaṃ kṛtvā upoṣitasyārdrapaṭasya taṇḍulabhakṣaṇe kṛte, niṣṭhīvane ca kṛte, śuklaniṣṭhīvane śuddhaḥ syāt | tasya śuddhipatraṃ dātavyam | itarasya daṇḍādikam iti |
[iti taṇḍulavidhiḥ\
atha taptamāṣavidhir ucyate
atra pitāmahaḥ |
taptamāṣasya vakṣyāmi vidhim uddharaṇe śubham |
kārayed āyasaṃ pātraṃ tāmraṃ vā ṣoḍaśāṅgulam ||
caturaṅgulakhātaṃ tu mṛṇmayaṃ vātha maṇḍalam |
pūrayed ghṛtatailābhyāṃ viṃśatyā vā palais tu tat ||
sutapte nikṣipet tatra suvarṇasya tu māṣakam |
aṅguṣṭhāṅguliyogena taptamāṣakam uddharet ||
karāgraṃ tu dhuned yasya visphoṭo vā na jāyate |
śuddho bhavati dharmeṇa pitāmahavaco yathā ||
uddharet iti vacanāt, pātrād uddharaṇamātraṃ kartavyam | na bahiḥ prakṣepaṇam ādaraṇīyam | pakṣāntaram āha |
sauvarṇe rājate tāmre āyase mṛṇmaye ‘pi vā |
gavyaṃ ghṛtam upādāya tad agnau tāpayec chuciḥ ||
sauvarṇīṃ rājatīṃ tāmrīm āyasīṃ vā suśodhitām |
salilena sakṛd dhautāṃ prakṣipet tatra mudrikām ||
ghṛte vīcītaraṅgāḍhye santāpasparśagocare |
parīkṣetārdraparṇena carutkāraṃ saghoṣakam ||
tataś cānena mantreṇa sakṛt tad abhimantrayet |
paraṃ pavitram amṛtaṃ ghṛtaṃ tvaṃ yajñakarmaṇi ||
daha pāvaka pāpaṃ tvaṃ himaśītaḥ śucau bhava |
upoṣitaṃ sakṛt snātam ārdravāsasamāgatam ||
grāhayen mudrikāṃ tāṃ tu ghṛtamadhyagatāṃ tathā |
prādeśiṇīṃ ca tasyātha parīkṣeyuḥ parīkṣakāḥ ||
yasya visphoṭako na syāt śuddho ‘sāv anyathāśuciḥ |
“tvam agne sarvabhūtānām” it śodhyasyābhimantraṇamantraḥ | atrāpi dharmāvāhanādi sarvam anusaṃdhātavyam | śuddhasya śuddhipatraṃ dātavyam | athāśuddhasya daṇḍādikaṃ kartavyam iti |
[iti taptamāṣavidhiḥ]
atha phālavidhir ucyate
bṛhasptatiḥ |
āyasaṃ dvādaśapalaṃ ghaṭitaṃ phālam ucyate |
aṣṭāṅgulaṃ bhaved dīrghaṃ caturaṅgulavismṛtam ||
agnivarṇaṃ tu tac coro jihvayā lelihet sakṛt |
adagdhaś cec chuddhim iyād anyathā sa tu hīyate ||
brāhmaṇacaurye taṇḍulā eva niyatāḥ | varṇāntarasya phālam | atrāpi dharmāvāhanādi sarvaṃ kartavyam iti ||
[iti varadarājīye vyavahāranirṇaye smṛtisaṃgrahe phālavidhiḥ]
atha dharmavidhir ucyate
atra bṛhaspatiḥ |
vastradvaye lekhanīyau dharmādharmau sitāsitau |
jīvadānādikair mantraiḥ gāyatryādyaiś ca sāmabhiḥ ||
āmantrya pūjayed gandhaiḥ kusumaiś ca sitāsitaiḥ |
abhyukṣya pañcagavyena mṛtpiṇḍāntaritau tataḥ ||
samau kṛtvā nave kumbhe sthāpyau cānupalakṣitau ||
dharmāvāhanādi kṛtvā,
yadi pāpavimukho ‘haṃ dharmas tvaṃ yātu me kare |
iti abhiśasto ‘numantrayate |
tataḥ kumbhāt piṇḍam ekaṃ pragṛhṇītāvilambitaḥ |
dharme gṛhīte śuddhaḥ syāt saṃpūjyaḥ sa parīkṣakaiḥ ||
adharme saṃgṛhīte tu daṇḍyo nirvāsya eva vā |
evam ukteṣu navasu divyeṣu tulāparaśūdakaviṣāṇi catvāri divyāni mahābhiyogaviṣayāṇi, taṇḍulaaptamāṣaphāladharmākhyāni catvāri alpābhiyogaviṣayāṇi | kośas tūbhayaviṣayaḥ | atra yājñavalkyaḥ |
tulāgnyāpo viṣaṃ kośo divyānīha viśuddhaye |
mahābhiyogeṣv etāni śīrṣakasthe ‘bhiyoktari ||
etāni tulādīni “abhiyoktari śīrṣakasthe” | “śīrṣakam” śiraḥ, vyavahārasya caturthaḥ pādo jayaparājayalakṣaṇaḥ, tatra tiṣṭhatīti śīrṣakasthaḥ, tatprayuktadaṇḍaḥ daśadaṇḍabhāg ity arthaḥ |
rucyā vānyataraḥ kuryād aparo vartayec chiraḥ |
vināpi śīrṣakaṃ kuryāt rājadrohe ‘tha pātake ||
nātra mānuṣapramāṇavadbhāvavādina eva divyaṃ nābhāvavādina iti niyamaḥ | “rucyā vānyataraḥ kuryāt” iti rājadrohaśaṅkāyāṃ brahmahatyādipātakaśaṅkāyāṃ mahācauryaśaṅkāyāṃ ca vinā śīrṣakāt kuryād iti | uktaṃ ca pitāmahena |
atha ced ātmaśūddhyarthaṃ divyaṃ prakramate naraḥ |
aśiras tatra dātavyam iti śāstraviniścayaḥ ||
rājabhiḥ śaṅkitānāṃ ca nirdiṣṭānāṃ ca dasyubhiḥ |
ātmaśuddhiparāṇāṃ ca divyaṃ deyaṃ śiro vinā || iti |
“kośam alpe ‘pi dāpayet” iti vacanāt alpābhiyoge ‘pi kośo deyaḥ | taṇḍulāḥ punaś cauryaśaṅkāyām eva,
caurye tu taṇḍulā deyā nānyatreti viniścayaḥ |
iti pitāmahavacanāt | taptamāṣe ‘pi,
cauryaśaṅkābhiyuktānāṃ taptamāṣo vidhīyate |
iti vacanāt, cauryaśaṅkābhiyogeṣv eveti kecit | anyatrāpīti ity anye | anye punaḥ śapathāḥ alpadravyaviṣayāḥ | tāṃś ca vakṣyāmaḥ | yady api śapathānām api divyaśabdavācyatvam, tathāpi kālāntaranirṇayanimittatvena samanantaranimittanirṇayebhyo dhaṭādibhyo bhedena vyapadeśaḥ smṛtiṣu brāhmaṇaparivrājakanyāyena draṣṭavya iti | atra viśeṣam āha hārītaḥ |
kośataṇḍuladharmāṃs tu dharmasaṃbhavam eva ca |
putradārādiśapathān sarvakālaṃ prayojayet ||
tatra viśeṣam āha prajāpatiḥ |
kujavāre ‘rkavāre ca caṇḍikārudrasaṃnidhau |
pūrvāhṇe sarvadivyānāṃ pradānaṃ parikīrtitam || iti |
yat tu pitāmahaḥ,
sthāvareṣu vivādeṣu divyāni parivarjayet |
iti, tad api likhitasākṣyādisaṃbhave divyāni parivarjayed iti vyākhyeyam | nanu vivādāntareṣv api likhitasākṣyādisadbhāve divyānām anavakāśa eva | satyam | ṛṇādiṣu vivādeṣūktalakṣaṇasākṣyupanyāse ‘rthinā krte ‘pi pratyarthī daṇḍam abhyupagamyāvaṣṭambhena divyam ālambate, tadā divyam api bhavati | sākṣiṇām āśaye doṣasaṃbhavād divyasya nirdoṣatvāt | sthāvareṣu vivādeṣu punaḥ pratyarthinā daṇḍāvaṣṭambhena divyālambane kṛte lekhyasākṣyādidṛṣṭapramāṇasaṃbhave na divyaṃ grāhyam iti pitāmahavacanasyārthaḥ | na punar ātyantikadivyanirākaraṇārthaṃ tadvacanam | tathātve likhitasākṣyabhāve sthāvaravivāde ‘py anirṇayaprasaṅgāt |
[iti divyāni]
yathāha nāradaḥ |
tatra satye sthito dharmo vyavahāras tu sākṣiṇī |
daivasādhye pauruṣeyīṃ na lekhyaṃ vā prayojayet || iti |
sthāvareṣu ca vivādeṣu pratyarthinā daṇḍāvaṣṭambhena divyāvalambane kṛte ‘pi sāmantādidṛṣṭapramāṇasadbhāve na divyaṃ grāhyam iti vikalpanirākaraṇārtham “sthāvareṣu vivādeṣu” ityādipitāmahavacanaṃ nātyantikadivyanirākaraṇārtham | likhitasāmantādyabhāve sthāvaravivādeṣv anirṇayaprasaṅgāt | atha dravyasya alpatvam ahatvānurūpeṇa divyādeḥ pravartatvāt tannirṇayāya vicāraḥ kriyate | suvarṇādidravyaparimāṇam āha manuḥ |
lokasaṃvyavahārārthaṃ yāḥ saṃjñāḥ prathitā bhuvi |
tāmrarūpyasuvarṇānāṃ tāḥ pravakṣyāmy aśeṣataḥ ||
jālāntaragate bhānau yat sūkṣmaṃ dṛśyate rajaḥ |
prathamaṃ tat pramāṇānāṃ trasareṇuṃ pracakṣate ||
reṇavo ‘ṣṭau tu vijñeyā likṣaikā parimāṇataḥ |
tā rājasarṣapās tisraḥ te trayo gaurasarṣapaḥ ||
sarṣapāḥ ṣaḍyavo madhyaḥ trayaḥ pañcaiva kṛṣṇalam |
pañcakṛṣṇaliko māṣaḥ te suvarṇas tu ṣoḍaśaḥ ||
palaṃ suvarṇāś catvāraḥ palāni dharaṇaṃ daśa |
dve kṛṣṇale samaghṛte vijñeyo rūpyamāṣakaḥ ||
te ṣoḍaśa syād dharaṇaṃ purāṇaṃ caiva rājatam |
kārṣāpaṇas tu vijñeyaḥ kārṣikas tāmrikaḥ paṇaḥ ||
dharaṇāni daśa jñeyaḥ śatamānas tu rājataḥ |
catuḥsuvarṇako niṣko vijñeyas tu pramāṇataḥ || iti |
yājñvalkyaḥ |
jālasūryamarīcisthaṃ trasareṇu rajaḥ smṛtam |
te ‘ṣṭau likṣā tu tās tisro rājasarṣapa ucyate ||
gauras tu te trayaḥ ṣaṭ te yavo madhyas tu te trayaḥ |
kṛṣṇalaṃ pañca māṣās tu te suvarṇas tu ṣoḍaśa ||
palaṃ suvarṇāś catvāraḥ pañca vāpi prakīrtitam |
aṇubhyaḥ ṣaḍbhyaḥ pratyakṣaḥ trasareṇuḥ | trasareṇavo ‘ṣṭau likṣā bhavanti | tāsāṃ likṣāṇāṃ trayaṃ rājasarṣapaḥ iti saṃjñaṃ bhavati | te rājasarṣapās trayo gaurasarṣapaḥ | gaurasarṣapaḥ ṣaṇmadhyayavaḥ | te trayaḥ kṛṣṇalaḥ | te kṛṣṇalāḥ pañcaiko māṣaḥ | te māṣāḥ ṣoḍaśaikaḥ suvarṇaḥ | te suvarṇāś catvāraḥ palam, yājñavalkyamate |
nāradādimate tu pañca suvarṇāḥ palam | tatra laukikavyavahāre mānacatuṣṭayaṃ kṛṣṇalam ity ucyate | ekaṃ vrīhiparimitaṃ mānam iti lokaprasiddham | ekataṇḍulaparimitaṃ mānam iti śāstraprasiddham, “mānataṇḍulamātraḥ syāt daśamānaḥ paṇaḥ smrtaḥ” ity āgamavacanāt, tathā vaikhānasa-ātreyasaṃhitādarśanāc ca |
tatra mānava-yājñavalkyayoḥ kākaṇidvayanyūnamānasaptakaṃ tu kṛṣṇalam ity ucyate | evaṃ trividheṣu kṛṣṇaleṣu satsu dūrvādiśapathaviṣaye vaiṣṇavadharmaśāstroktakṛṣṇalam evāśrayitum ucitam iti kākaṇikatrayanyūnaṣaṇmānaṃ kṛṣṇalam evātra svata āśrīyate | mānavaṃ kṛṣṇalaṃ tu kartṛguṇādibhir āśrīyate | tatra yenāpahṛtena prayojanam asti, tata ārabhya kṛṣṇalaparipūrte upoṣitaḥ snāta ārdrapaṭaḥ śūdro devabrāhmaṇasaṃnidhau dūrvākaraḥ śapathaṃ kuryāt | sa evaṃbhūtaḥ | dvitīyakṛṣṇalakād ārabhya ā tṛtīyāt pūrvavat tilakaraḥ śapathaṃ kuryāt | tṛtīyakṛṣṇalād ārabhya ā caturthāt sa eva pūrvavad eva rajatapāṇiḥ śapathaṃ kuryāt | kṛṣṇalacatuṣkād ārabhya ā pañcamād dhiraṇyapāniḥ śapathaṃ kuryāt | kṛṣṇalapañcakād ārabhya ā ṣaṣṭhāt lāṅgaloddhṛtamahīkaraḥ śūdraḥ śapathaṃ kuryāt | ṣaṣṭhakṛṣṇalād ārabhya daśamāntānāṃ daśakād arvāk śūdro nikṣṇalaś cet putreṇa bhāryayā vā śapathaṃ kuryāt | sacchūdras tasminn evārthe sarvaiḥ pātakaiḥ śapathaṃ kuryāt, “śūdraṃ sarvais tu pātakaiḥ” iti manuvacanāt | yady apy atra,
................. śūdraṃ sarvais tu pātakaiḥ |
putradārasya vāpy enaṃ śirasi sparśayet pṛthak ||
iti dravyaparimāṇaviśeṣo noktaḥ, tathāpy aucityena kṛṣṇalapañcakaparisamāpter ārabhya daśamānapaṇadaśakād arvāg etau bhavitum arhataḥ | katham iti cet, ucyate | tatra suvarṇārdhone “kośo deyaḥ śūdrasya” iti vaiṣṇavavacanāt sāmānyena kośe prāpte,
triśate taṇḍulā deyāḥ kośaś caiva tadardhake |
śate hṛte ‘pahnave ca datavyaṃ dharmaśodhanam ||
iti bṛhaspativacane ‘smin śatādīnāṃ saṃkhyeyatayā,
mānaṃ taṇḍulamātraṃ syāt daśamānaṃ paṇaṃ smṛtam |
iti vacanāt, taṇḍulatulitamānātmakam eva suvarṇaṃ grāhyam iti | kośapānaṃ tadardhakam iti pañcāśadadhikaśatamāne kośapāne vihite, pañcāśadadhikaśatasya pañcadaśapaṇātmakatvena tata ūrdhvam eva viṣṇuvacanaprāptasya kośasyopasaṃhārāt, paṇadaśakād ūrdhvaṃ dharmaśodhanaṃ syād iti | paṇadaśakād arvāk pañcakṛṣṇalād ūrdhvaṃ pratyayākāṅkṣaṃ bhavati | tathātra “śūdraṃ sarvais tu pātakaiḥ” iti etad api dravyaparimāṇākāṅkṣam ity ubhayākāṅkṣayā saṃbadhyata iti | kiṃ ca,
satyena śāpayed vipraṃ kṣatriyaṃ vāhanāyudhaiḥ |
gobījakāñcanair vaiśyaṃ śūdraṃ sarvais tu pātakaiḥ ||
iti manūktaśapathaviṣayaṃ smṛtam | bṛhaspatinā viṣādīni dharmajāntāni divyāny uktvoktam |
satyaṃ vāhanaśastrāṇi gobījaṃ kāñcanāni ca |
devabrāhmaṇapādāṃś ca putradāraśirāṃsi ca ||
ete tu śapathāḥ proktāḥ svalpe ‘rthe sukarāḥ sadā || iti |
atra śapathānāṃ dharmajaṃ praty alpārthaviṣayād vā dharmasya svarūpaparāmarśenaiva sīroddhṛtamahīṃ prati gurvarthaviṣayatvāvagamāc ca dharmajaviṣayapaṇadaśakād arvāk sīroddhṛtamahīviṣayakṛṣṇalapañcakād ūrdhvam eva mānāvā bārhaspatyāś ca śapathā veditavyāḥ |
agniṃ vāhārayec chūdram apsu caiva nimajjayet |
iti dharmajasya tātkālikaphalatvena dṛṣṭapratyayatvāt kālāntaraphaleṣu śapatheṣv anuktir iti draṣṭavyam | daśamānānāṃ paṇānāṃ daśakād ārabhya pañcadaśakād arvāk śūdrasya dharmapratyayo bhavati | tasminn evārthe phālopajīvināṃ kṛṣīvalānāṃ phālaṃ leḍhavyam | bṛhaspatiḥ |
śate hṛte ‘pahnute ca dātavyaṃ dharmaśodhanam |
gocarasya pradātavyaṃ sabhyaiḥ phālaṃ prayatnataḥ ||
mānānāṃ “śate hṛte ‘pahnute ca” śūdrasya dharmaśodhanaṃ bhavati | mānaśatakasya daśapaṇātmakatvāt paṇadaśakād ārabhya paṇapañcadaśakād arvāk dharmapratyayo bhavatīty arthaḥ | tasminn evārthe gocarasya kṛṣīvalasya śūdrasya phālaṃ datavyam iti | paṇapañcadaśakād ārabhya triṃśatpaṇād arvāk śūdrasya kośapānaṃ syāt | triṃśatpaṇād ārabhya catvāriṃśatpaṇād arvāk śūdrasya taṇḍulabhakṣaṇaṃ bhavati | catvāriṃśatpaṇād ārabhya pañcāśatpaṇād arvāk śūdrasya taptamāṣo bhavati | tathā ca bṛhaspatiḥ |
catuḥśatābhiyoge tu dātavyas taptamāṣakaḥ |
triṃśate taṇḍulā deyā kośaś caiva tadardhake ||
catuḥśatābhiyoge śatacatuṣṭayamānasuvarṇaviṣayābhiyoge taptamāṣo dātavyaḥ | teṣāṃ catvāriṃśatpaṇātmakatvāt, catvāriṃśatpaṇābhiyoge śūdrasya taptamāṣo dātavya ity arthaḥ | “triṃśate taṇḍulā deyā” ity etad api śatatrayamānasuvarṇe triṃśatpaṇātmake śūdrasya taṇḍulā deyā iti pratipādayati | pañcāśatpaṇād ārabhya saptādhikaṣaṣṭipaṇād arvāk śūdrasya tulādhāraṇaṃ bhavati | saptādhikaṣaṣṭipaṇād ārabhya pañcādhikasaptatipaṇād arvāk śūdrasya jalapratyayo bhavati | pañcādhikasaptatipaṇād ārabhya śatapaṇād arvāk śūdrasya paraśur bhavati | śatapaṇād ārabhya sarvatra śūdrasya viṣabhakṣaṇam eva | tathā ca bṛhaspatiḥ |
śate hṛte ‘pahnute ca dātvayaṃ dharmaśōdhanam |
viṣaṃ sahasre ‘prahṛte pādone ca hutāśanaḥ ||
tribhāgone ca salilam ardhe deyā tulā sadā |
paṇaśatātmake sahasramānasuvarṇe śūdrasya viṣabhakṣaṇaṃ bhavati | mānasahasracaturbhāgahīne pañcasaptatipaṇātmake pañcāśadadhike śataspatakamāne śūdrasya taptaparaśudhāraṇaṃ bhavati | tathā sahasramānasuvarṇasya tṛtīyabhāgahīne saptaṣaṣṭipaṇe śūdrasya jalapatyayo bhavati | pañcāśatpaṇātmake sahasramāṇārdhe śūdrasya tulārohaṇaṃ bhavatīty arthaḥ | nanu atra divyaprakaraṇe sahasrādisaṃkyānāṃ saṃkhyeyatvena taṇḍulatulitamānasuvarṇam eva gṛhyata iti katham avasīyate iti | ucyate | kātyāyanas tāvad āha |
sarvadravyapramāṇaṃ tu jñātvā hema prakalpayet |
hemapramāṇayuktaṃ tu tadā divyaṃ niyojayet ||
śate viṣaṃ tu pādone hutabhuk tatra dīyate |
āpas tribhāgahīne tu śatārdhe tu tulā smṛtā ||
kośapānaṃ tadardhe vā daśapañcakasapta vā |
tadardhe taṇḍulā deyāḥ tadardhe taptamāṣakaḥ || iti |
kātyāyanaḥ |
jñātvā saṃkhyā suvarṇasya śatanāśe viṣaṃ smṛtam |
aśītas tu vināśe tu hutabhuk pratyayaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
hutabhuk paraśuḥ |
ṣaṣṭyānāśe jalaṃ jñeyaṃ syāc catvāriṃśake dhaṭaḥ |
triṃśaddaśavināśe vā kośapānaṃ vidhīyate ||
iti bṛhaspatiḥ |
pañcārdhe tasya vā nāśe tadardhasya ca taṇḍulāḥ |
iti vṛddhamanuḥ | anayoḥ kātyāyana-vṛddhamanuvacanayoḥ śatanāśe viṣam uktam | bṛhaspativacane tu viṣaṃ sahasre ‘pahṛte iti sahasranāśe viṣam uktam ity anena tābhyāṃ mahāvirodho ‘stīty eva pratīyate | satyam | eteṣām avirodhe kārye bhūyasā nyāyena dvayor vacanānusāreṇa bṛhaspativacanaṃ yojanīyam | evaṃ sati dharmaśāstreṣu daṇḍavidhānādau saṃkhyāmātre nirdiṣṭe paṇānāṃ vyavahārārthatayā kḷptatvāt saṃkhyeyatayā prāyeṇa tatra paṇā eva gṛhyanta iti | tatra bṛhaspativacane kākaṇikacatuṣṭaye ‘pi loke paṇavyavahāradarśanāt kākaṇikaḥ paṇacaturbhāga iti vaiyākaraṇaprasiddheḥ catuṣkākaṇikasya taṇḍulaparimitasya sahasrādisaṃkhyāsaṃkhyeyatvena grahaṇam | kātyāyanavacane tu,
mānaṃ taṇḍulamātraṃ syāt daśamānaṃ paṇaḥ smṛtaḥ |
iti uktadaśamānasya paṇasya śatādisaṃkhyāsaṃkhyeyatvena grahaṇam ity avirodhaḥ | ihāpi kātyāyana-vṛddhamanuvacanagataśatādisaṃkhyānāṃ saṃkhyeyatvena paṇā eva gṛhyante | evaṃ sati bṛhaspativacanagasahasrasaṃkhyādīnāṃ saṃkhyeyatayā taṇḍulatulitasuvarṇaṃ gṛhyate |
mānaṃ taṇḍulamātraṃ syāt daśamānaṃ paṇaḥ smṛtaḥ |
ityādivacanāt śatasahasrayor api avirodhaḥ | yad apīdaṃ yājñavalkyavacanam,
nāsahasrād dharet phālaṃ na tulāṃ na viṣaṃ tathā |
iti, atra sahasraśabdaḥ pādonārdhonayoḥ sahasrayor upalakṣaṇārtha ity avirodhaḥ | atra phālaśabdaḥ paraśuparaḥ mukhyaphālasyālpārthaviṣayatvasya darśitatvāt | yat tv atra vṛddhamanunoktam,
aśītes tu vināśe tu hutabhuk pratyayaḥ smṛtaḥ |
ṣaṣṭyā nāśe jalaṃ jñeyaṃ syāc catvāriṃśake dhaṭaḥ ||
iti, yac ca kātyāyanenoktam,
śate viṣaṃ tu pādone hutabhuk tatra dīyate |
āpas tribhāgahīne tu śatārdhena tulā smṛtā ||
ity etābhyām eva vacanābhyām evaṃ pratīyate | pañcasaptatyā ārabhyāśīter arvāg agnir udakaṃ ca prāptam | evaṃ ṣaṣṭhyā ārabhya saptaṣaṣṭipradeśād arvāk jaladhaṭau prāptau | sarvatrotkṛṣṭapuruṣasya laghupratyayo bhavati | apakṛṣṭasya tu gurupratyayo bhavati iti vyavasthā draṣṭavyā, prākdṛṣṭadoṣam ityādivaiṣṇavavacanadarśanāt |
tadardhe taṇḍulā deyāḥ tadardhe taptamāṣakaḥ |
ity alpaviṣaye taptamāṣakavidhānam api cauryādiśaṅkāviṣayam ity alpadravye ‘pi prāptam ity avirodhaḥ,
dattasyāpahvano yatra pramāṇaṃ tatra kalpyate |
steyasāhasayor divyaṃ svalpe ‘py arthe pradāpayet ||
iti kātyāyanavacanadarśanāt | brāhmaṇasyāgāmikāla iti bhaviṣyatkālakāryaviṣaye tu brāhmaṇasyāpi kośo bhavatīty arthaḥ | pūrvoktāni dravyaparimāṇāni śūdraviṣayāṇi | pūrvoktasarvaviṣayeṣu dviguṇe ‘rthe vaiśyasya | triguṇe ‘rthe rājanyasya | caturguṇe ‘rthe brāhmaṇasya iti draṣṭavyam | yad idaṃ bārhaspatyavacanam,
eṣā saṃkhyā nikṛṣṭānāṃ madhyānāṃ dviguṇā smṛtā |
caturguṇottamānāṃ tu kalpanīyā parīkṣakaiḥ ||
atra “madhyānāṃ dviguṇā smṛtā” iti triguṇasyāpy upalakṣaṇārtham iti vyākhyeyam | śūdrād arvācīnānāṃ śūdravad eva divyāni bhavanti,
śūdrāṇāṃ tu sadharmāṇaḥ sarve ‘padhvaṃsajāḥ smṛtāḥ |
iti manuvacanāt | brāhmaṇānām api keṣāṃcit śūdravad eva divyaṃ deyam | tathā ca kātyāyanaḥ |
gorakṣakān vānijakān śilpinaś ca kuśīlavān |
preṣyān vārdhuṣikāṃś caiva viprāñ chūdravad ācaret ||
śūdrasya putradārādiśapathasthāne vaiśyasya gobījakāñcanaiḥ śapatho bhavati | kṣatriyasya vāhanaśastraiḥ, brāhmaṇasya satyavākyena śapatho bhavati,
satyena śāpayed vipraṃ kṣatriyaṃ vāhanāyudhaiḥ |
gobījakāñcanair vaiśyaṃ śūdraṃ sarvais tu pātakaiḥ ||
putradārasya vāpy enaṃ śirāṃsi sparśayet pṛthak |
iti manuvacanāt | atra brahmāṇḍapurāṇe viśeṣaḥ |
sthāvaraṃ jaṅgamaṃ vāpi yad dravyam abhiyujyate |
kriyāprayogakāle tu yan mūlyaṃ dharmato ‘rhati ||
tenaiva kanakīkṛtya tatra divyaṃ prayojayet | iti |
atra kecit divyeṣu paṇānāṃ saṃkhyeyatve ‘pi kākaṇicatuṣṭaye loke paṇavyavahārāt kākaṇikaḥ paṇacaturbhāga iti vaiyākaraṇaprasiddheś ca kākaṇicatuṣṭayātmakaṃ suvarṇam eva sahasraśatādisaṃkhyāsaṃkhyeyam iti manyante | anye tu tāmrikakārṣikābhiprāyāt sarvadivyeṣu saṃkhyeti manyante | apare tu,
mānaṃ taṇḍulamātraṃ syāt daśamānaḥ paṇaḥ smṛtaḥ |
ity āgamavacanāt daśamānātmaka eva paṇa iti manyante | apare tu punaḥ,
māṣo viṃśatimo bhāgo jñeyaḥ kārṣāpaṇasya tu |
kākaṇī tu caturbhāgo māṣasyaiva prakīrtitaḥ ||
ityādyauśanasavacanāt, tathā,
māṣakaṃ tu budhāḥ prāhuḥ kākaṇīnāṃ catuṣṭayam |
viṃśatiḥ syāt punas teṣāṃ sa mukhyaḥ paṇa ucyate ||
iti smṛtyantaradarśanāt, tathā,
māṣo viṃśatimo bhāgaḥ paṇasya parikīrtitaḥ |
iti smṛteś ca, viṃśatimāṣāḥ paṇaḥ smṛta iti tadviṣayā divyeṣu saṃkhyeti manyante | evaṃ catuṣṭayeṣu paṇeṣu caramaḥ paṇatvaṃ na gṛhyate,
paṇo viṃśatimāṣas tu divyād anyatra kīrtyate |
iti divyeṣu viṃśatimāṣasya niṣedhasmaraṇāt | tathā “jñātvā saṃkhyāṃ suvarṇasya” iti |
sarvadravyapramāṇaṃ tu jñātvā hema prakalpayet | iti |
tathā “sarveṣv arthajāteṣu mūlyaṃ kanakaṃ kalpayet” ity evamādibhir vacanaiḥ suvarṇasyaiva saṃkhyeyatvāvagamāt tāmrikakārṣāpaṇasya divyeṣu saṃkhyeyatvapratipādakavacanābhāvāc ca suvarṇapaṇānām eva divyeṣu saṃkhyeyatvaṃ yuktam | sahasrādiṣu saṃkhyeyeṣu catuṣkākaṇikasya taṇḍulaparimitasya paṇasya saṃkhyeyatvaṃ śatānāśa ityādiṣu daśamānātmakatvaṃ paṇasya saṃkhyeyatvam iti | nanu kākaṇikānām aṣṭakaṃ taṇḍulaśabdenocyate | māyaśabdenāpi tad evocyate | tatra daṇḍaprakaraṇe,
māyān aṣṭau tu mahiṣī sasyaghātasya kāriṇī |
iti vacanāt,
dāpayet paṇapādaṃ tu gāṃ pādau mahiṣīṃ tathā |
iti nāradavacanāt,
māṣaḥ pādo dvipādo vā yatra daṇḍaḥ prakalpitaḥ |
anirdiṣṭaṃ tu sauvarṇaṃ māṣakaṃ tu prakalpayet ||
iti kātyāyanavacanāc ca | paṇapādadvayāṣṭamāṣaśabdayor ekārthatvāvagamāt, ṣaḍaśamānātmakaḥ paṇa iti gamyate | tathā ca keṣām api vacanam |
utthitas tv arkatāpena trasareṇuka ucyate |
tryaṣṭakaṃ trasareṇūnāṃ likṣeti parikīrtyate ||
tās tisraḥ sarṣapaḥ proktaḥ kākaṇyā tulitas tu saḥ |
aṣṭakaṃ kākaṇīnāṃ tu taṇḍulaḥ parikīrtitaḥ ||
taṇḍulānāṃ dvayaṃ dhānyaṃ māṣakaṃ paripaṭhyate |
taddvayaṃ kṛṣṇalaṃ proktam andhikā kṛṣṇaladvayam ||
andhikāpaṇaśabdau tu paryāyeṇa prakīrtitau | iti |
atrāpi ṣoḍaśamānātmakaḥ paṇa iti manyante | tathā vaikhānasa-ātreyasaṃhitāyām api |
mānaṃ taṇḍulamātraṃ syāt daśamānaḥ paṇaḥ smṛtaḥ | iti |
atrāpy aṣṭakam eva kākaṇikānāṃ mānaśabdena taṇḍulaśabdena cocyate | tena ṣoḍaśamānātmakaḥ paṇa iti divyeṣv api tasya saṃkhyeyatvaṃ prāptam | satyam, etad gṛhyate | ubhayoḥ śāstrasiddhatvāt ubhayam api gṛhyate | ten ṣoḍamānātmakaṃ vā daśamānātmakaṃ vā paṇaḥ saṃkhyeyatvena gṛhyate | tatra śodhyasya guṇavattve ṣoḍaśamānātmakasya grahaṇam, nirguṇasya śodhyatve daśamānātmakasya paṇasya saṃkhyeyatvam iti vyavasthā | tatra viṣapānaṃ kaliyuge ghaṭikāvasthāyāṃ niṣiddham | pūrvācāryā evam atra vyavasthāṃ kurvanti, mātṛjātitulyam anulomānāṃ divyam, pitṛjātitulyaṃ pratilomānām iti | atra taiḥ paṭhitāḥ saṃgrahaślokāḥ |
viṣaṃ śate ‘gniḥ pādone jalaṃ ṣaṣṭau vidhīyate |
tulā ca taptamāṣaś ca catvāriṃśaty udīritau ||
adhyardhapañcasu steye taptataṇḍulayor vidhiḥ |
anuttamadravyacaurye taṇḍulāśanam ucyate ||
kośaṃ pañcadaśasyāhuḥ āstikyaṃ cen mataṃ nṛṣu |
ṣaṭsu ca kṣudraśaṅkāyāṃ dveṣṭiko dharmasaṃbhavaḥ ||
daśakena paṇānāṃ syāt vaiguṇye kṣatraviśyayoḥ |
tato vaiśyasya viṣaye smṛtyuktā śapathakriyā ||
viṭkṣatrabrāhmaṇārthe tat śūdrād dvitricaturguṇā ||
iti |
cāturguṇye tu viprāṇāṃ sarvasyeti vikalpyate |
sāhasādiṣu sarveṣāṃ pañcaite syur dhaṭādayaḥ ||
iti |
niṣke tu satyavacanaṃ dviniṣke pādalambhanam |
trikād arvāk viṣaṃ syāt tu kośapānam ataḥ param ||
idaṃ ca daiṣṭikasya pūrvoktavyavasthayā vikalpaḥ ||
atha śapathaḥ
dhaṭādayo dharmajāntā yatra noktā mahātmabhiḥ |
tatra śodhyas tu śapathair abhiyuktas tu mānavaḥ ||
atra śapathaviṣaye viśeṣam āha hārītaḥ |
dhane pramuṣite coraiḥ prabhūtaiś ca dhanī svayam |
parimāṇaṃ svarūpaṃ ca śapathenaiva bhāvayet ||
kasyacid dhane “coraiḥ pramuṣite” apahnute vā dhanī svayaṃ pramuṣitadhanasya vā apahṛtadhanasya vā guruparimāṇam alpaparimāṇaṃ cānādṛtya taddhanasvarūpaṃ pramāṇaṃ ca pālakānāṃ śapathenaiva sādhayet iti | smṛtyantare |
muṣitasya pramāṇaś ca svajanair ity avañcitam |
phālakānām adṛṣṭena prabhūte ‘pi dhane śapet || iti |
anyatra |
svabandhuputrabhṛtyādyair agṛhītaṃ dhanī svayam |
pramāṇaṃ ca prabhūte ‘pi śapathenaiva bhāvayet || iti |
sa evāha |
caurair apahṛte svāmī svayaṃ dravye mahaty api |
na dṛṣṭaśapathaṃ kuryāt dṛṣṭenaiva viśodhayet ||
coirair apahṛtadravyasya pramuṣitadravyasya vā alpamahatve ‘nādṛtya dravyasvarūpaṃ parimāṇaṃ ca dravyavān svayaṃ pālādīnāṃ mahaty api dravye anādṛtya adṛṣṭena śapathenaiva sādhayet | na tu dṛṣṭaśapathaṃ kuryād iti | divyair jayaparājayāvadhāraṇe daṇḍaviśeṣāḥ kātyāyanena darśitāḥ |
śatārdhaṃ dāpayec chuddham aśuddho daṇḍabhāg bhavet |
daṇḍaṃ ca sa evāha |
viṣe toye hutāśe ca tulākośe ca taṇḍule |
taptamāṣakadivye ca kramād daṇḍaṃ prakalpayet ||
sahasraṃ ṣaṭchataṃ caiva tathā pañcaśatāni tu |
catustridvyekam ekaṃ ca hīnaṃ hīneṣu kalpayet ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
arvāk caturdaśād ahno yasya no rājadaivikam |
vyasanaṃ jāyate ghoraṃ sa śuddhaḥ syān na saṃśayaḥ ||
śapathe doṣādoṣāḥ tatparīkṣākālaviśeṣāś ca kathyante | tatra kaṇvaḥ |
tripakṣe vā trisaptāhe saptāhābhyantare ‘pi vā |
rogāgnijñātimaraṇaṃ paśudhānyadhanakṣayaḥ ||
evamādīni dṛśyante yasya ghorāṇi sarvaśaḥ |
taṃ pāpaṃ vinayed rājā tasya ghorāparādhataḥ || iti |
svalpe ‘parādhe saptāhaṃ madhyame dvisaptāhaṃ mahāparādhe trisaptāhaṃ pakṣatrayaṃ veti kalpaḥ | viṣṇuḥ |
yasya paśyet dvisaptāhāt trisaptāhād athāpi vā |
rogo ‘gnir jñātimaraṇaṃ rājātaṅkam athāpi vā ||
tam aśuddhaṃ vijānīyāt viśuddhaṃ tadviparyaye | iti |
śātātapaḥ |
saptāhābhyantare yasya dvisaptāhāntare ‘pi vā |
trisaptāhe ‘pi vā rogo vahnijñātimṛtis tathā ||
rājadaivikam ātaṅkaṃ paśudhānyadhanakṣayaḥ |
yasyaivamādiko ‘narthaḥ tasyānarthaṃ vinirdiśet ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
sīmācaṅkramaṇe kośe padasparśe tathaiva ca |
tripakṣapakṣasaptāhāt daivarājakam iṣyate ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
saptāhe vā dvisaptāhe na vipad rājadaivikī |
bāndhaveṣu sapiṇḍeṣu dhaneṣu śapathaiḥ śuciḥ ||
mātṛpakṣe pañcapuruṣāvadhir anarthāḥ pitṛpakṣe saptapuruṣāvadhiḥ anarthaḥ | yasya bandhuviṣaye jñātiviṣaye vā vipattau svavipad iva kleśo bhavati, tādṛśavipad veditavyety ācāryāḥ | nāradaḥ |
saptāhābhyantare yasya dvisaptāhe ‘pi vā punaḥ |
rogo ‘gnir jñātimaraṇaṃ paśudhānyadhanakṣayaḥ ||
pratyātmikaṃ bhaved yasya vidyāt tasya na śuddhatām |
ūrdhvaṃ yasya dvisaptāhāt vaikṛtaṃ sumahad bhavet ||
nābhiyojyaḥ sa viduṣā kṛtakālavyatikramāt || iti |
alpasya dehināṃ paribhāvyatvānmahad bhayam ity uktam | etan mahābhiyogaviṣayam, “mahābhiyogeṣv etāni” iti prastutyābhidhānāt, iti | manuḥ |
ā caturdaśamād ahno yasya no rājadaivikam |
vyasanaṃ jāyate ghoraṃ sa jñeyaḥ śapathaiḥ śuciḥ || iti |
avadhyantarāṇi pitāmahenoktāni alpadhanaviṣayāṇi “kośam alpe ‘pi dāpayet” iti vacanāt |
trirātrāt saptarātrād vā dvādaśāhād dvisaptakāt |
vaikṛtaṃ yasya dṛśyeta pāpakṛt sa udāhṛtaḥ || iti |
mahābhiyogoktadravyād arvācīnaṃ dravyaṃ tridhā vibhajya trirātrādipakṣatrayaṃ vyavasthāpyam iti |
iti varadarājīye vyavahāranirṇaye
dviyapramāṇanirṇayakāṇḍaṃ samāptam
6. mānasaṃgrahaḥ
atha mānasaṃgrahaḥ
bhāvasādhyatvabhāvo ‘pi kecit syāt saṃśayeṣu ca |
sākṣyādīnāṃ abhāve ‘tra divyābhāve ca kutracit ||
divye tu mūrddhadaṇḍābhyāṃ sādhyardhaṃ hema kalpayet |
tatrārabhyārdhakākaṇyāḥ śūdro dūrvākaraḥ śapet ||
trikākaṇyūnaṣaṇmānāt kṛṣṇalāt tilahastakaḥ |
dvigunād rūpyapāṇis tu triguṇāt kāñcanaṃ dadhat ||
caturguṇāt kare kṛtvā mṛttikāṃ lāṅgaloddhṛtām |
nīcaḥ pañcaguṇāt putradārair anyatra pātakaiḥ ||
pāṇānāṃ daśamānānāṃ daśakād dharmaśodhanam |
tatraivārtheṣu leḍhavyaṃ phālaṃ phālopajīvinā ||
syāt pañcadaśakāt kośaḥ triṃśatas taṇḍulāḥ smṛtāḥ |
catvāriṃśat tāvattā tu taptamāṣasamuddhṛtiḥ ||
pañcāśatas tulā jñeyāḥ saptaṣaṣṭhe jalaṃ bhavet |
pañcasaptatikād agniḥ śātāt syāt viṣabhakṣaṇam ||
etāni śūdrasyoktāni dviguṇe ‘rthe viśaḥ smṛtāḥ |
catvāriṃśad dhānyamāṣaḥ tau yavaḥ tau ca kṛṣṇalam ||
kṛṣṇaladvitayaṃ proktaṃ aṇvikāpy atra paṇo ‘py asau |
tisro ‘ṇvikāḥ kṛṣṇalaṃ ca hemamāṣa itīryate ||
śāṇoṇvikāś catasras tu dīnārāsthā (-khyaḥ?) caturdaśa |
aṇvikā viṃśatiḥ niṣkaṃ śāṇaṃ sāṣṭāṇvikaṃ tu tat ||
aṣṭāṇvikādhikaṃ niṣkadvitayaṃ citrako bhavet |
saṣoḍaśāṇvikaṃ niṣkadvayaṃ karṣaka ucyate ||
daśabhir vā paṇaṃ caikā daśabhis tu catuṣpaṇaiḥ |
niṣkair dvādaśabhir veti laukiko mānasaṃgrahaḥ ||
yo rājasarṣapasyārdho laukikaḥ kākaṇis tu saḥ |
kākaṇis tritayaṃ gauro māṣaḥ syād ardhataṇḍulaḥ ||
sa kākaṇidvayaṃ mānadvitayaṃ madhyamo yavaḥ |
kākaṇidvitayaṃ nyūnasapatamāṣas tu kṛṣṇalaḥ ||
kārṣāpaṇas tu aṣṭamāno daśamānapaṇaḥ paraḥ |
śāṇoṇvikā catuṣkā syāt laukike ‘py evam eva saḥ ||
ṣaṭkākaṇikamānādyo māṣo ‘nyaś caturaṇvikaḥ |
kārṣāpaṇo ‘nyo niṣkā mā pūrvadeśe pravartate ||
suvarṇo ‘ṣṭāṇvikāyuktaṃ niṣkadvayam ihocyate |
suvarṇa eva dīnāraḥ citrakaś ceti kathyate ||
akṣārdhaṃ dvyaṇukādyardhamānayuktaṃ dviniṣkakam |
atha rajatamānavidhiḥ
rūpyamānena (tu) māṣaḥ syāt mānair ardhacaturdaśaiḥ |
saptāṇvikādhikaṃ niṣkaṃ purāṇ(ṃ?) mānaṃ (mānavaṃ?) ca tat ||
śatamānaṃ palaṃ ca syāt niṣkair ardhacaturdaśaiḥ |
tair eva rājataṃ niṣkam atha tāntrikam ucyate ||
pāñcarātravaikhānasānusāri niṣkapramāṇam ucyate |
syād aṇvikācatuṣkeṇa triniṣkaḥ karṣakaḥ paṇaḥ |
pūrṇo ‘rdhapādapādonaniṣkaṣoḍaśako bhavet ||
sāhaso. dviguṇo madhya uttamaḥ syāc caturguṇaḥ |
ardhapādonaviṣko dviniṣkaṣoḍaśako ‘tha vā ||
pūrvas taddviguṇo madhye madhyād dviguṇa uttamaḥ |
yavaś ca kṛṣṇalaṃ māṣaḥ suvarṇo rūpyaniṣkakaḥ ||
mānavoktayavādibhyaḥ ṣaṣṭhāṃśo nāsti vaiṣṇave ||
iti varadarājīye mānasaṃgrahaḥ samāptaḥ
7. ṛṇādānakāṇḍam
athāṣṭādaśapadeṣu prathamaprāptam ṛṇādānam ucyate | atra nāradaḥ |
ṛṇaṃ deyam adeyaṃ ca yena yatra yathā ca yat |
dānagrahaṇadharmaś ca ṛṇādānam iti smṛtam ||
īdṛśaṃ ṛṇaṃ deyam, īdṛśam ṛṇaṃ adeyam, īdṛśam ṛṇam anenādhikāriṇā deyam, asmin samaye deyam, anena prakāreṇa deyam — ity adharmarṇe pañcavidham | uttamarṇe dānavidhir ādānavidhiś ceti dvividham iti saptavidham | tatrarṇapradānapūrvakatvād itareṣāṃ prathamaṃ tatpradānaprakāraṃ yājñavalkya āha |
aśītibhāgo vṛddhiḥ syāt māsi māsi sabandhake |
varṇakramāc chataṃ dvitricatuḥpañcakam anyathā ||
viśvāsārtham adhamarṇena uttamarṇe yad ādhīyate tad ādhiḥ atra bandhaka ity ucyate | tasmin sabandhake prayoge prayuktasya pratimāsam aśītibhāgo vṛddhir dharmyā bhavati | tena paṇaviṃśatyāḥ paṇapādo vṛddhir bhavati | tathā ca manuḥ |
vasiṣṭhavacanaproktāṃ vṛddhiṃ vārdhuṣike śṛṇu |
pañca māṣās tu viṃśatyā vṛddhir dharmo na hīyate ||
tathā ca gautamaḥ: “kusīdavṛddhir dharmyā viṃśatiḥ pañcamāṣikī māsam” | daśamānasya paṇatve paṇaviṃśatitamo bhāgo māṣo ‘rdhataṇḍulo bhavati | viṃśatimānasya paṇatve taṇḍulaparimito bhavati | ṣoḍaśamānapaṇatve paṇasya tadanuguṇaṃ kalpyam | evaṃ sabandhake prayoge aśītibhāgo vṛddhir dharmyā bhavati | anyathā abandhake prayoge varṇānāṃ brāhmaṇādīnāṃ caturṇāṃ krameṇa dvitricatuḥpañcakaṃ śataṃ dharmyaṃ bhavati | brāhmaṇasya brāhmane ‘dhamarṇe dvikaṃ śatam | kṣatriye ‘dhamarṇe trikaṃ śatam | vaiśye ‘dharmarṇe catuṣkaṃ śatam | śūdre ‘dharmarṇe pañcakaṃ śataṃ dharmyaṃ māsi māsi bhavati | dvitricatuḥpañcāsmin śate vṛddhir dīyata iti dvitricatuḥpañcakaṃ śatam | “tad asmin vṛddhyāyalābhaśulkopadādīyate” (Pāṇ 5.1.47) iti kan | kṣatriyasya kṣatriye ‘dharmarṇe dvikaṃ śatam, vaiśye ‘dhamarṇe trikaṃ śatam, śūdre ‘dharmarṇe catuṣkaṃ śatam, tathā vaiśyasya vaiśye ‘dhamarṇe dvikaṃ śatam, śūdre ‘dhamarṇe trikaṃ śatam, śūdrasya śūdre ‘dhamarṇe dvikaṃ śatam iti | tena sajātīye sarvatra dvikaṃ śataṃ dharmyam, anantare trikaṃ śatam, ekāntare catuṣkaṃ śatam, dvyantare pañcakaṃ śataṃ dharmayaṃ bhavati | tathā ca manuḥ |
dvikaṃ trikaṃ catuṣkaṃ ca pañcakaṃ ca śataṃ smṛtam |
māsasya vṛddhiṃ gṛhṇīyāt varṇānām ānupūrvaśaḥ ||
atra vṛddhiś caturvidhety āha nāradaḥ |
kāyikā kālikā caiva kāritā ca tathā smṛtā |
cakravṛddhiś ca śāstreṣu tasya vṛddhiś caturvidhā ||
atra viśeṣam āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
vṛddhiś caturvidhā proktā pañcadhānyaiḥ prakīrtitā |
ṣaḍvidhānyaiḥ samākhyātā tattvatas tāṃ nibodhata ||
nāradaḥ |
kāyikā kālikā caiva cakravṛddhir athāparā |
kāritā ca śikhāvṛddhiḥ bhogalābhas tathaiva ca ||
kāyikā karmasaṃyuktā māsagrāhyā tu kālikā |
vṛddher vṛddhiś cakravṛddhiḥ kāritā ṛṇinā kṛtā ||
pratyahaṃ gṛhyate yā tu śikhāvṛddhis tu sā smṛtā |
śikheva vardhate nityaṃ śiraśchedān nivartate ||
grahāt stomaḥ śadaḥ kṣetrāt bhogalābhaḥ prakīrtitaḥ |
tatra vyāsaḥ |
dohyavāhyakriyāyuktā kāyikā samudāhṛtā |
kāyāvirodhinī śaśvat paṇapādādi kāyikā ||
ity aśītibhāgavṛddhiḥ kāyiketi | nāradaḥ |
pratimāsaṃ sravantī yā vṛddhiḥ sā kālikā smṛtā |
vṛddhiḥ sā kāritā nāma dhanikena svayaṃkṛtā ||
vṛddher api punar vṛddhiḥ cakravṛddhir udāhṛtā |
pratyahaṃ gṛhyamāṇā dhānyaviṣayā vṛddhiḥ śikhāvṛddhiḥ | vyāsaḥ |
gṛhopabhogabhogyas tu stoma ity abhidhīyate |
kṣetrotpannaṃ dhānyādikaṃ śadaḥ | kātyāyanaḥ |
grāhyaṃ syāt dviguṇaṃ dravyaṃ prayuktaṃ dhaninā sadā |
labheta cen na dviguṇaṃ purā vṛddhiṃ prakalpayet || iti |
tatra viśeṣam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
kāntāragās tu daśakaṃ sāmudrā viṃśakaṃ śatam |
dadyur vā svakāṃ vṛddhiṃ sarve sarvāsu jātiṣu ||
kāntāram araṇyaṃ gacchantīti kāntāragāḥ | samudraṃ gacchantīti samudragāḥ | tebhyo daśakaṃ sataṃ viṃśakaṃ śataṃ ca dharmyaṃ bhavati | māsi māsīty atrāpy anuṣajyate | kāritāyāṃ vṛddhau sarve brāhmaṇādayaḥ kāryave(va?)śena svakṛtāṃ svābhyupagatāṃ vṛddhiṃ abandhake sabandhake vā sarvāsu jātiṣu dadyur iti |
atha dravyaviśeṣād vṛddher avadhiviśeṣa ucyate | tatra kātyāyanaḥ |
maṇimuktāpravālānāṃ suvarṇarajatasya ca |
triṣu tridviguṇā vṛddhiḥ phalasyaidhodakasya ca ||
manuḥ |
kusīdavṛddhir dvaiguṇyaṃ nātyeti sakṛd āhṛtā |
dhānye śade lave vāhye nātikrāmati pañcatām ||
śadaḥ kṣetraphalaṃ puṣpādi | lavaḥ ūrṇācamarakeśādi | vāhyo balīvardaturagādiḥ | bṛhaspatiḥ |
hiraṇye dviguṇā vṛddhiḥ triguṇā vastrarūpyake |
dhānye caturguṇā proktā śadavāhyalaveṣu ca ||
nāradaḥ |
hiraṇyadhānyavastrāṇāṃ vṛddhir dvitricaturguṇā |
rasasyāṣṭau guṇāḥ proktāḥ strīpaśūnāṃ tu santatiḥ ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
uktā pañcaguṇā śāke bīje caiva caturguṇā |
lavaṇasnehamadyeṣu vṛddhir aṣṭaguṇā matā ||
guḍe madhuni caivoktā prayukte cirakālike ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
tailānāṃ caiva sarveṣāṃ madyānām atha sarpiṣām |
vṛddhir aṣṭaguṇā jñeyā guḍasya lavaṇasya ca ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
tṛṇakāṣṭheṣṭakāsūtrakīlacarmāsthipakṣmaṇām |
hetipuṣpaphalānāṃ ca vṛddhis tu na nivartate ||
