atra kāṣṭhādīnāṃ sāhacaryād aṣṭaguṇād arvāṅ na nivartata ity arthaḥ | viṣṇuḥ: “kiṇvakārpāsa-sūtracarmavarmāyudheṣṭakāṅgārāṇām akṣayā, anuktānāṃ dviguṇā” | bharadvājaḥ |
hiraṇyadhānyavastrāṇāṃ vṛddhir dvitricaturguṇā |
ghṛtasyāṣṭaguṇā vṛddhiḥ tāmrādīnāṃ caturguṇā ||
tailānāṃ ṣaḍguṇā vṛddhiḥ strīpaśūnāṃ tu santatiḥ |
kośānāṃ syāt pañcaguṇā kārpāsasya caturguṇā ||
kāṣṭhānāṃ candanādīnāṃ vṛddhir aṣṭaguṇā bhavet |
evaṃ vṛddhividhiḥ prokto nāsti vṛddhir ataḥ parā ||
na vṛddher vṛddhir astīti dharmakārasya śāsanam |
na cāpy asaṃśrutā vṛddhir ativṛddhiś ca karhicit ||
tāmraśabdena suvarṇarajatavyatiriktānāṃ trapusīsādīnāṃ grahaṇam | kośānāṃ kośadhānyānām ity arthaḥ | kārpāsagrahaṇena paṭaprabhṛtīnāṃ grahaṇam | sugandhadravyopalakṣaṇaṃ candanagrahaṇam ity asahāyenoktam | atra viṣṇuḥ |
steye brahmasvabhūteṣu suvarṇaharaṇe tathā |
paścāt taptena dātavyaṃ tasmād ekādaśādhikam ||
brāhmaṇasaṃbandhisuvarṇavyatiriktadravyāpahāre kṣatriyavaiśyasaṃbandhisuvarṇāpahāre ca, sadya eva dvādaśaguṇaṃ bhavati | tathā ca pitāmahaḥ |
sadyaḥ syāt dvādaśaguṇaṃ coditaṃ ratnahāṭakam |
brāhmaṇasvaṃ ca rūpyādi sadyo hy ekādaśādhikam ||
rūpyaṃ pañcaguṇaṃ bhūmiḥ tathaivāṣṭaguṇā matā |
sadya eva iti vacanād avṛddhyabhāvaḥ | atra nāradaḥ |
na vṛddhiḥ prītidattānāṃ syād anākāritā kvacit |
anākāritam apy ūrdhvaṃ vatsarārdhād vivardhate ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
kṛtvoddhāram adatvā yo yācitas tu diśaṃ vrajet |
ūrdhvaṃ māsatrayāt tasya tad dhanaṃ vṛddhim āpnuyāt ||
paṇyaṃ gṛhītvā yo mūlyam adatvaiva diśaṃ vrajet |
svadeśastho ‘pi vā yas tu na dadyād yācito ‘sakṛt ||
sa tatra kāritāṃ vṛddhim anicchann api cāharet |
yatnaiḥ prāptaṃ rakṣitaṃ vā gaṇārthe vā ṛṇaṃ kṛtam ||
ṛtutrayasyopariṣṭāt dhanaṃ vṛddhim avāpnuyāt |
evamādiṣv aśītibhāgavṛddhir vivakṣitā | kātyāyanaḥ |
prītidattaṃ tu yat kiṃcid vardate na tv ayācitam |
yācyamānam adattaṃ cet vardate pañcakaṃ śatam ||
pratimāsaṃ pañcakaṃ śataṃ vardhata ity arthaḥ |
carmasasyāsavadyūtapaṇyamūleṣu sarvadā |
strīśulke ca na vṛddhiḥ syāt pratibhāvyakṛteṣu ca ||
yadi svayaṃ kṛtā ca syāt kāritāṃ vṛddhim āpnuyāt |
saṃvartaḥ |
na vṛddhiḥ strīdhane lābhe nikṣepe ca tatharṇike |
saṃdighe prātibhāvye ca yadi na syāt svayaṃ kṛtā ||
atra yājñavalkyaḥ |
dīyamānaṃ na gṛhṇāti prayuktaṃ yat svakaṃ dhanam |
madhyasthasthāpitaṃ tat syāt vardhate na tataḥ param ||
atra viṣṇu-gautamau: “bhuktādhir na vardhate ditsato ‘varuddhasya ca” iti | vyāsaḥ |
pratibhāvyaṃ bhuktabandham agṛhītaṃ ca ditsataḥ |
na vardhate prapannasya damaḥ śuklaṃ pratiśrutam ||
athādhividhir ucyate
tatra ṛṇapradānaṃ nāma pratipradānopādhikaṃ svasya paraṃ pratipradānam | tad anekavidham, sabandhakaṃ savṛddhikam abandhakam avṛddhikaṃ ceti | tatra sabandhakasya savṛddheś ca prayoga ucyate | tatra bṛhaspatiḥ |
ādhir bandhaḥ samākhyātaḥ sa ca proktaś caturvidhaḥ |
jaṅgamaḥ sthāvaraś caiva gopyo bhogyas tathaiva ca ||
hārītaḥ |
ādhiś caturvidhaḥ prokto gopyo bhogyas tathaiva ca |
arthapratyayahetuś ca caturthas tv ājñayā kṛtaḥ ||
teṣāṃ svarūpam āha |
śrāvaṇā pūrvalikhito bhogyādhiḥ śreṣṭha ucyate |
gopyādhis tu parebhyaḥ svaṃ datvā yo gopyate gṛhe ||
arthapratyayahetur yaḥ pratyayādhiḥ sa ucyate |
ājñādhir nāma yo rājñā saṃsadā vājñayā kṛtaḥ ||
śrāvaṇā saṃsadi prakāśanam | vyāsaḥ |
ājñādhis tatkrayaś caiva krūradaṇḍo vidhīyate |
ubhāv anyatra na syātām iti dharmasya niścayaḥ ||
ādhis tu dvividhaḥ prokto sthāvaro jaṅgamas tathā |
siddhir asyobhayasyāpi bhogo yady asti nānyathā ||
ādheḥ praveśane rodhaḥ ṛṇikena kṛto yati |
dāpyaḥ sa bhogam atyarthaṃ daṇḍayitvādhiṣaḍguṇam ||
bhogyādhiviṣayam idam | gopyādiṃ praty āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
gopyādhibhoge no vṛddhiḥ sopakāre ‘tha hāpite |
niṣṭo deyo vinaṣṭaś ca daivarājakṛtād ṛte ||
sopakārabhāvanaṃ kāryayogyatvakāraṇam | nāradaḥ |
yasyādher mūlahāniḥ syāt daivarājakṛtāt kvacit |
ādhyarthas tvarṇināṃ deyaḥ tv aśakto bhogado bhavet ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
gopyādhir dviguṇād ūrdhvaṃ kṛtakālas tathāvadheḥ |
śrāvayitvā tv ṛṇī kāle bhoktavyaḥ samanantaram ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
ādhātā yatra na syāt tu dhanī bandhaṃ nivedayet |
rājñas tataḥ sa vikhyāto vikreya iti dhāraṇā ||
vyāsaḥ |
gṛhītṛdoṣān naṣṭaś cet bandho hemādiko bhavet |
ṛṇaṃ salābhaṃ saṃśodhya tanmūlaṃ dāpayed dhanī ||
ādhau praveśane rodha ṛṇikena kṛto yadi |
dāpyaḥ sa bhogam adhyardhaṃ daṇḍaṃ dāpyaś ca ṣaḍguṇam ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
pracchādyādhim ṛṇī kuryāt krayādīn balataś ca yaḥ |
daṇḍaṃ sa triguṇaṃ datvā punar ādhyarthdo bhavet ||
tathā ca manuḥ |
yaḥ svāminānanujñātam ādhiṃ bhuṅkte ‘vicakṣaṇaḥ |
tenāsya vṛddhir moktavyā tasya bhogasya niṣkṛtiḥ ||
atra kātyāyanaḥ |
ākāmam ananujñātam ādhiṃ yaḥ karma kārayet |
bhogakarmaphalaṃ dāpyo vṛddhiṃ vā na labheta saḥ ||
nāradaḥ |
na bhoktavyo balād ādhiḥ (?) bhuñjāno vṛddhim utsṛjet |
mūlyena toṣayec cainam ādhis tenānyathā bhavet ||
idaṃ dvipadacatuṣpadajaṅgamādhiviṣayam | atra bhāradvājaḥ |
ādheḥ praveśane kāle bhogaṃ necchati tad dhanī |
bhogo naśyaty ataś cordhvaṃ na vardhayati tad dhanam ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
ādhīkṛtaṃ tu yat kiṃcid vinaṣṭaṃ daivarājataḥ |
tatrarṇaṃ sodayaṃ dāpyo dhaninām adhamarṇikaḥ ||
bhogādhikyaṃ ca bhogyādheḥ hrāsaṃ ca na vicālayet |
lekhye tu likhitaṃ yāvat tāvad bhoktavyam eva tu ||
atra nāradaḥ |
rakṣyamāṇo ‘pi yatrādhiḥ kāle neyād asāratām |
ādhir anyo ‘dhikartavyo deyaṃ vā dhanino dhanam ||
idam api jaṅgamādhiviṣayam iti, sthāvarakṣetrādāv asāratve ‘py anyādhipratiṣedhāt | yad vā yatrāhitaṃ vastu kālena niḥsāraṃ bhavati | na hetvantareṇa | yathā vahanakarṣaṇamahimayogyadaśāyā pratidāta iti mahiṣaturaṅgabalīvardādi kālavaśenaiva vahanādyayogyatayā niḥsāraṃ yathā vā candanakuṅkumaghṛtaguḷādi kālavaśenaiva nairgandhyadaurgandhyādinā niḥsāraṃ bhavati tadviṣayam | kātyāyanenedaṃ spaṣṭam uktam |
na ced dhanikadoṣeṇa niṣpated vā mriyeta vā |
ādhir anyas tu kāryaḥ syāt ṛṇān mucyeta narṇikaḥ ||
“niṣpated vā mriyeta vā” iti jaṅgamaviṣaye ādhyantarakaraṇavacanāt | sthāvare tu viśeṣam āha |
srotasāpahṛte kṣetre rājñā caivāpahārite |
ādhir anyo ‘dhikartavyo deyaṃ vā dhanine dhanam ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
upasthitasya moktavya ādhistenānyathā bhavet |
prayojake ‘sati dhanaṃ kule ‘nyasyādhim āpnuyāt ||
tatkālakṛtamūlyo vā tatra tiṣṭhed avṛddhikam |
vinā dhāraṇakād vāpi vikrīṇīta sasākṣikam ||
yadā tu dviguṇībhūtam ṛṇam ādhau tadā khalu |
mocya ādhis tadutpanne praviṣṭe dviguṇe dhane ||
sapratyayabhogyādhiviṣayam idam |
ādhiḥ praṇśyet dviguṇe dhane yadi na mokṣyate |
kāle kālakṛto naśyet phalabhogyo na naśyati ||
ādhipraṇāśo nāmādhamarṇasaṃbandhaṃ parityajya dhanikasaṃbandhāpattiḥ | hiraṇyādi ādhiviṣayam idam | kṛtakālaprayoge ‘py evam eva | phalabhogyaḥ kṣetrārāmādi | sa kadācid api na naśyati | ādhipraṇāśasyāpavādam āha |
caritrabandhakṛtaṃ savṛddhyā dāpayed dhanam |
satyaṃkārakṛtaṃ dravyaṃ dviguṇaṃ pratipādayet ||
caritreṇa dhanina svacchāśayatvena bahumūlyam ādhīkṛtyādhamarṇena svalpam eva dravyam ātmasāt kṛtam | evaṃbhūtaṃ dravyaṃ dviguṇībūte ‘pi na naśyati | kiṃ tu dviguṇaṃ gṛhītvā dātavyam iti | bandhakakalpanayā dviguṇībhūtā | dviguṇībhūte ‘pi dviguṇaṃ dātavyam | nādhināśa iti | satyaṃkārety asyāyam arthaḥ | vaṇijo hi bahumūlyaṃ kramukādiphalaṃ svīcikīrṣantaḥ vikretur haste dvitrāṇi nistulāni datvā krayavikrayavyavahāravyavasthāṃ ca niścitya paṇyaṃ svīkurvanti | tatra yadi dravyaprayoktā saṃvidam atilaṅghayet, mūlyahātim avāpnuyāt | gṛhītrā tadvilaṅghane tadgṛhītadravyaṃ dviguṇam eva deyam | prayukte dviguṇībhūte uttamarṇasya bandhakaviṣayam āha |
satyaṃkāreṇāpi dviguṇam eva dātavyam |
bṛhaspatiḥ |
pūrṇāvadhau śāntalābhe bandhe svāmī dhanī bhavet |
anirgate daśāhe tu ṛṇī bhokṣitum arhati ||
na visravati yat tat syāt dhaniko mūlabhāg bhavet |
dviguṇād api cotkarṣe kālikā yasya cādinām ||
vivādanyāyatattvajñaiḥ tadā rājā vinirṇayet |
vyāsaḥ |
ādhānakālikād arvāk dviguṇaṃ yadi kālikam |
mūlamātraṃ tu pādonaṃ tadardhaṃ pādam eva cā ||
dviguṇātikramān naiva dhaniko labhate dhanam | iti |
bṛhaspatiḥ |
hiraṇye dviguṇībhūte mṛte naṣṭe ‘dhamarṇike |
dravyaṃ tadīnaṃ saṃgṛhya vikrīnīta sasākṣikam ||
rakṣed vā kṛtamūlyaṃ tu daśāhaṃ janasaṃsadi |
ṛṇānurūpaṃ parataḥ gṛhītvālpaṃ ca vartayet ||
sadhanaṃ tu sthirīkṛtya gaṇanākuśalair naraiḥ |
tadbandhujātividitaṃ pragṛhṇan nāparādhnuyāt ||
atra vyāsaḥ |
dravye tu dviguṇībhūte pūrṇe kāle kṛtāvadhau |
bandhakasya dhanī svāmī dvisaptāhaṃ parīkṣya tu ||
tato ‘ntarā dhanaṃ datvā ṛṇī bandham avāpnuyāt |
phalabhogyaṃ pūrṇakālyaṃ dadyād dravyaṃ tu sāmakam ||
pūrṇakālam api phalabhogyaṃ kṣetrādikaṃ na kadācid api naśyati | sāmakaṃ samayasiddham ṛṇaṃ savṛddhikaṃ datvā kṣetram āpnuyād iti | tatra bhogyādhir dvividhaḥ sapratyayabhogyādir apratyayabhogyādhiś ceti | savṛddhikamūlāpākaraṇārtha ekaḥ | sa sapratyayabhogyādhir ity ucyate | vṛddhimātrāpākaraṇārtho ‘paraḥ | so ‘pratyayabhogyādhir ity ucyate | tatra sapratyayabhogyādhim āha vyāsaḥ |
kāṃcid vṛddhiṃ samābhāṣya dravyam ādāya tattvataḥ |
tat kṣetraṃ bhuṅkṣva vṛddhyartham adhikaṃ mūlanāśanam ||
ityādhiḥ pratyayādhiḥ syāt vaiguṇyena kṣayo bhavet |
savṛddhikamūlyāpākaraṇārtho bhogyādhiḥ sapratyayabhogyādhir ity padārthaḥ | apratyayabhogyādhim āha kātyāyanaḥ |
dravyaṃ gṛhītvā vṛddhyarthaṃ bhogayogyaṃ dadāti cet |
jaṅgamaṃ sthāvaraṃ vāpi bhogyādhiḥ sa tu kathyate ||
mūlyaṃ tad akhilaṃ datvā svaṃ kṣetrādikam āpnuyāt |
vṛddhimātrāpākaraṇārthe bhogyādhau adhamarṇa uttamarṇāt prāptaṃ mūlyaṃ datvā svakṣetrādikam āpnuyāt | eṣo ‘pratyayabhogyādhir iti vacanābhiprāyaḥ | bharadvājaḥ |
pratyayādher uktakāle vṛddhyabhāve svayaṃ dhanī |
ādhiṃ praviśya tatkāle bhuñjīta likhitaṃ sthitam ||
pratyayādhau tu bhoktavyā vṛddhir yā pūrvalekhitā |
tāvad eva tu bhoktavyam iti śāstraviniścayaḥ ||
yat tu tatrādhikaṃ vṛddheḥ deyaṃ tad ṛṇine punaḥ |
dadyād yāvat tu tad vṛddheḥ tāvat saṃpūrayed ṛṇī ||
sapratyayabhogyādhau niṣkrayakāle savṛddhikaṃ mūlyasyāparyāptaṃ pūrayet | adhikaṃ cādadīta iti vacanasya tātparyārthaḥ | bharadvājaḥ |
vaiśākhyāṃ yasya bhogyādher āṣāḍhyāṃ niṣkrayo bhavet |
hīnaṃ tad dhanino doṣād etat pūraṇam arhati ||
hīnasyāpūraṇe vṛddhiḥ cakravṛddhyā vivardhate |
sarvādhīnāndhavāk bhogāt niṣkrayo nāsti dharmataḥ |
balād bhukte kṛte kāle niṣkraye dviguṇo damaḥ ||
bharadvājaḥ |
yaḥ svāminānanujñāta ādher ādhi karoti cet |
svadhanāt sa tu hīnaḥ syāt karoty āpadi pūrvavat ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
kṛpavaprakhalaprāyā ādhigrāheṇa nāśitāḥ |
vināśakā bhavanty ādher evaṃrūpās tathā pare ||
ekam eva ādhiṃ dvayor dadhator daṇḍam āha vyāsaḥ |
ādhim ekaṃ dvayor yatra kuryāt kā pratipad bhavet |
tayoḥ pūrvataraṃ grāhyam ādhātā coradaṇḍabhāk ||
ādhau pratigrahe krīte pūrvā tu balavattarā |
iti vacanāt pūrvataraṃ grāhyam | bṛhaspatiḥ |
kṣetram ekaṃ dvayor bandhe dattaṃ yat samakālikam |
yena bhuktaṃ tayoḥ pūrvaṃ tasya tat siddhim āpnuyāt ||
tulyakālopasthitayor dvayor api samaṃ bhavet |
pradāne vikraye caiṣa vidhiḥ saṃparikīrtitaḥ ||
ṛṇādāne bharadvājaḥ kramaviśeṣam āha |
ādhyarthaḥ kevalārthaś ca dhanino yatra dṛśyate |
kevalaḥ prathamo deyaḥ paścāt syād ādhiniṣkrayaḥ ||
sabandhakābandhakayoḥ prathamam abandhakaṃ deyaṃ paścāt sabandhakam ity arthaḥ |
ṛṇikasyārthasadbhāva uttamarṇo bhaved yadi |
tasminn ṛṇe hi tatkāle tatkrameṇa vihīyate ||
tatkrameṇa tatkārārdheṇa tadṛṇaṃ hīyata ity arthaḥ | ṛṇaikadeśagrahaṇe prakāram āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
lekhyasy pṛṣṭhe ‘bhilikhet datvā datvarṇiko dhanam |
dhanī vopagataṃ dadyāt svahastaparicihnitam ||
nāradaḥ |
gṛhītvopagataṃ dadyāt ṛṇikāya dhanaṃ dhanī |
na dadyād yācyamānaś cet śeṣahānim avāpnuyāt ||
akhaṇḍagrahaṇaṃ praty āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
datvarṇaṃ pāṭayel lekhyaṃ śuddhyaivānyat tu kārayet |
sākṣimac ca bhaved yad dhi tad dātavyaṃ sasākṣikam ||
nāradaḥ |
brāhmaṇasya tu yad deyaṃ sānvayaḥ sa ca nāsti cet |
sakulyeṣv asya nivapet tadabhāve ‘sya bandhuṣu ||
yadā tu na sakulyāḥ syuḥ na ca saṃbandhibāndhavāḥ |
tadā dadyād dvijātibhyaḥ teṣv asatsv apsu nikṣipet || iti |
athārghavaśād ṛṇaprakārapradānam
kātyāyana āha |
mūlyagrahaṇakālārghād vaiguṇyaṃ kālato bhavet |
mūlyam eva ṛṇī dadyāt na vṛddhiṃ dātum arhati ||
dviguṇe kālike ‘rghe tu mūlyaṃ taddviguṇaṃ bhavet |
na niḥsravati yas tasmād dhaniko mūlabhāg bhavet ||
dviguṇād api cotkarṣe kālikārghasya vādinām |
vivādaṃ nyāyatattvajñaiḥ tadā rājā vinirṇayet ||
vyāsaḥ |
ādhānakālikād arvāk dviguṇaṃ yadi kālikam |
mūlamātraṃ tu vā dānaṃ tadardhaṃ pādam eva vā ||
dviguṇāṃ niṣprabhāṃ caiva dhaniko labhate dhanam || iti
atha pratibhūvidhir ucyate
atra bṛhaspatiḥ |
darśane pratyaye dāne ṛṇidravyārpaṇe tathā |
cuatuṣprakāraḥ pratibhūḥ śāstre dṛṣṭo manīṣibhiḥ ||
āhaiko darśayāmīti sādhur eṣo ‘paro ‘bravīt |
dātāham etad draviṇam arpayāmi paro vadet ||
ādau tu vitathe dāpyau tatkālāveditaṃ dhanam |
uttarau tu visaṃvāde tau vinā tatsutau tathā ||
atha pratibhūtve niṣiddhā ucyate | viṣṇuḥ: “svāmiripuniruddhādhikṛtadaṇḍitasaṃśayastha-rikthimitrātyantavāsirājakāryapravṛttavītarāgavratidaridrabālavṛddhastrīrugnā na pratibhuvaḥ | rikthimitrayoḥ kvacid anujñānād anyābhāve kāryai (?)” | kātyāyanaḥ |
na svāmī na ca vai śatruḥ svāminādhikṛtas tathā |
niruddho daṇḍitaś caiva saṃdigdhaś caiva na kvacit ||
naiva rikthī na mitraṃ ca na caivātyantavāsinaḥ |
rājakāryaniyuktaś ca ye ca pravrajitā narāḥ ||
nāśako dhanine dātuṃ daṇḍaṃ rājñe ca tatsamam |
nāvijñāto gṛhītavyaḥ pratibhūtvakriyāṃ prati ||
saṃdigdhaḥ abhiśastaḥ | atyantavāsinaḥ naiṣṭhikabrahmacāriṇaḥ | atra yājñavalkyaḥ |
bhrātṝṇām atha dampatyoḥ pituḥ putrasya caiva hi |
prātibhāvyam ṛṇaṃ sākṣyam avibhakte na tu smṛtam ||
kecit pratibhūtraividhyam āhuḥ | enaṃ bhavadapekṣite kāle deśe vā darśayāmīti vadan darśanapratibhūḥ | tathā kīdṛśo ‘yam ity ukte sādhur eṣa iti viśvāsavacanakṛt pratyayapratibhūḥ | ubhau darśanābhāve viśvāsābhāve ca dāpyau | dātāham etat draviṇam iti dānapratibhūḥ | tatrādyau mṛtau cen na tatputrā dadyuḥ | dānapratibhuvi mṛte putrair api deyam iti viśeṣaḥ | tathā ca yājñavalkyenoktam |
darśanapratibhūr yatra mṛtaḥ prātyayiko ‘pi vā |
na tatputrā ṛṇaṃ dadyur dānāya yaḥ sthitaḥ ||
prajāpatiḥ |
durbhikṣe rāṣṭrasaṃbādhe dharmakārye tathāpadi |
ṛṇaṃ deyaṃ mūlyam eva na vṛddhir iti niścayaḥ ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
prātibhāvyena yo dadyāt pīḍito dhanikādibhiḥ |
tripakṣāt paratas tasmāt dviguṇaṃ labdhum arhati ||
dviguṇam iti sarvadraveṣu paravṛddhyupalakṣaṇam | atra pīḍāgrahaṇāt dvaiguṇyādilobhena dattaṃ cen na vardheta | yathodayaṃ ca deyam iti gamyate | yājñavalkyaḥ |
pratibhūr dāpito yatra prakāśaṃ dhanināṃ dhanam |
dviguṇaṃ pratidātavyam ṛṇikais tasya tad dhanam ||
viṣṇuḥ |
yaṃ cārthaṃ pratibhūr dadyād dhanikenopapīḍitaḥ |
dviguṇaṃ pratidātavyam ṛṇikais tasya tad dhanam ||
atra viśeṣam āha prajāpatiḥ |
dhādako vittahīnaḥ syāt lagnako vittavān yadi |
mūlyaṃ tasya bhavaddeyaṃ na vṛddhiṃ dātum arhati ||
khādakaḥ adhamarṇaḥ | lagnakaḥ pratibhūḥ | vittahīno ‘dhamarṇo lagnakena pratibhuvā dattam eva dadyāt | dattamātradāne pratibhuvaś chedobhāvāt | durbhikṣe rāṣṭrasaṃbādhae ityādinā saṅkaṭe mūlyamātrārpaṇasyoktatvāt tulyanyāyatvād asyeti | bṛhaspatiḥ |
naṣṭasyānveṣaṇaṃ kālaṃ dadyāt pratibhuve dhanī |
deśānurūpataḥ pakṣaṃ māsam abdam athāpi vā ||
idānīṃ prayuktasya dhanasya grahaṇadharmā ucyante | tatra yājñavalkyaḥ |
prapannaṃ sādhayann arthaṃ na vācyo nṛpater bhavet |
sādhyamāno nṛpaṃ gacchet daṇḍyo dāpyaś ca tad dhanam ||
prapannam adharmarṇenābhyupagataṃ sādhayan pratyāharan uttamarṇo ‘dhamarṇena nṛpater naiva vācyaḥ | athādhamarṇaḥ sādhyamāno nṛpaṃ gacchati rājānam abhigamya sādhayan tam abhiyuṅkte, caturbhāgaṃ sa daṇḍyaḥ | uttamarṇasya ca tad dhanaṃ dāpyaḥ | etat smṛtyācāravyapetena ity asya pratyudāharaṇam anyāyyatvāt | tathā ca manuḥ |
ya sādhayantaṃ chandena vedayed dhanikaṃ nṛpe |
sa rājñā taccaturbhāgaṃ dāpyas tasya ca tad dhanam ||
dharmeṇa vyavahāreṇa chalenācaritena ca |
prayuktaṃ sādhayed arthaṃ pañcamena balena ca || iti |
dharmeṇa satyānusāreṇa | vyavahāreṇa sākṣilekhyādinā | chalena pramādābhihitena | ācaritena abhojanādinā | balena nigaḍabandhādinā sādhayed iti | tatra bahuṣūttamarṇeṣu prāpteṣu kramam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
gṛhītānukramād dāpyo dhaninām ādhamarṇikaḥ |
datvā tu brāhmaṇāyaiva nṛpates tadanantaram ||
samajātīyeṣu yena krameṇa gṛhītam ṛṇaṃ tenaiva krameṇa dāpyam | bhinnajātīyeṣu jātyutkarṣakrameṇa dadyād iti | svayaṃ sādhayitum asamarthaṃ praty āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
rājñādhamarṇiko dāpyaḥ sādhitād daśakaṃ śatam |
pañcakaṃ ca śataṃ dāpyaḥ prāptārtho hy uttamarṇikaḥ ||
sādhitasyārthasya daśamabhāgaṃ daṇḍarūpeṇādhamarṇo dāpyaḥ | bhṛtitvena viṃśatitamaṃ bhāgam uttamarṇo dāpya iti | nirdhanam adharmaṇaṃ praty āha |
hīnajātiṃ parikṣīṇam ṛṇārthaṃ karma kārayet |
brāhmaṇas tu parikṣīṇaḥ śanair dāpyo yathodayam ||
brāhmaṇavyatiriktam adhamarṇaṃ jātyādyabhijanaśaktyapekṣayā karma kāreyet | dhanarahito brāhmaṇo yathāsaṃbhavaṃ dāpyaḥ | atra hīnagrahaṇam utkarṣābhāvapratipādanadvāreṇa samasyāpy upalakṣaṇam | tathā brāhmaṇagrahaṇam api śreṣṭhopalakṣaṇārtham iti brahmavitpravareṇoktam | yathāha manuḥ |
karmaṇāpi samaṃ kuryāt dhanikenādhamarṇikaḥ |
samo ‘pakṛṣṭajātiś ca dadyāc chreyāṃś ca tac chanaiḥ ||
iti brāhmaṇavyatiriktaparikṣīṇaṃ karma kārayet | brāhmaṇas tu yathodayaṃ dadyād iti | atra bharadvājaḥ |
ṛṇikasya dhanābhāve deyo ‘nyasya tu tatkramāt |
dhānyaṃ hiraṇyaṃ lohaṃ ca gomahiṣyādikaṃ tathā ||
vastraṃ bhūr dāsavargaś ca vāhanādi yathākramam |
dhanikasya tu vijñeyo deyāś caivānupūrvaśaḥ ||
kṣetrābhāve tathārāmaḥ tasyābhāve gṛhakrayaḥ |
dvijānāṃ grahaṇābhāve kālahāro vidhīyate ||
atha ṛṇakṣayaparivṛttir ucyate
ṛṇaṃ dātum aśaktasya kiṃcid datvā parvayuktānāṃ vṛddhiṃ ca mūlīkṛtya vṛddhaye prayogāntarakḷptim āha manuḥ |
ṛṇaṃ dātum aśakto yaḥ kartum icchet punaḥ kriyām |
sa datvā nirjitāṃ vṛddhiṃ karaṇaṃ parivartayet ||
adarśayitvā tac caiva hiraṇyaṃ parivartayet |
yāvatī saṃbhaved vṛddhiḥ tāvatīṃ dātum arhati ||
nirjitāṃ tadānīṃ deyatayā prāptām | karaṇam ṛṇasvīkaraṇam | parivṛttiḥ punaḥkriyā, na avaśyaṃ hiraṇyaṃ darśayitavyam | samayamātreṇa parivṛttiḥ kāryety arthaḥ | atra viśeṣam āha prajāpatiḥ |
vṛddhiṃ vṛddhyekadeśaṃ vā mūle ‘py aṃśam athāpi vā |
yathāśakti pradāyaiva karaṇaṃ parivartayet ||
atra viśeṣam āha kātyāyanaḥ |
vidyamāne ‘pi rogārte svadeśāt proṣite tathā |
viṃśāt saṃvatsarād deyam ṛṇaṃ pitṛkṛtaṃ sutaiḥ ||
jīvatā pitrā kṛtasya ṛṇasya tasya aśaktau putrair deyam iti | smṛtyantare |
vyādhitonmattavṛddhānāṃ tathā dīrghapravāsinām |
ṛṇam evaṃvidhaṃ putrā jīvatām api dāpayet ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
sānnidhye ‘pi pituḥ putraiḥ ṛṇaṃ deyaṃ vibhāvitam |
jātyandhapatitonmattakṣayaśvitrādirogiṇaḥ ||
ebhir vacanaiḥ jīvati pitari putrair eva deyam ṛṇaṃ na pautraiḥ | putrābhāve pautrair api deyam | atra kātyāyanaḥ |
yad deyaṃ pitṛbhir nityaṃ tadabhāve tu taddhanāt |
tad dhanaṃ putrapautrair vā deyaṃ tatsvāmine tadā ||
pitur dhanasadbhāve tasmād eva ṛṇaṃ deyam | tad dhanābhāve putrārthāt pautrārthāc ca deyam ity arthaḥ |
pūrvaṃ paitāmahaṃ deyaṃ pitryaṃ deyaṃ tataḥ param |
paścād deyaṃ tathātmīyam evaṃ deyam ṛṇaṃ sutaiḥ ||
putrapaurair ity arthaḥ | nāradaḥ |
ṛṇam ātmīyavat pitryaṃ putrair deyaṃ vibhāvitam |
paitāmahaṃ samaṃ deyam adeyaṃ tatsutasya tu ||
vibhāvitaṃ sādhitam ātmīyavat savṛddhikaṃ putrair deyam | pautraiḥ samaṃ vṛddhirahitaṃ deyam | pitāmahadhanasadbhāve savṛddhikam eva deyam | naptrādibhiḥ mūlyam api na deyaṃ svadhanāt | tasya dhanasadbhāve savṛddhikam eva deyam |
kramād abhyāgataṃ prāptaṃ putrair yan na samuddhṛtam |
dadyuḥ paitāmahaṃ pautrāḥ tac caturthān nivartate ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
pitari proṣite prete vyasanābhiplute ‘pi vā |
putrapautrair ṛṇaṃ deyaṃ nihnave sākṣibhāvitam ||
proṣite vyasanābhiplute vā putrair eva deyam | mṛte pautrair api | kātyāyanaḥ |
anekārthābhiyoge tu yāvat svaṃ sādhayed dhanī |
sākṣibhis tāvad evāsau labhate sādhitaṃ dhanam ||
nātra vibhāvitaikadeśanyāyaḥ | pitṛkṛtasyājñānena nihnavābhāvāt |
athaikacchāyapraviṣṭam ṛṇādānam ucyate
bṛhaspatiḥ |
ekachāyāpraviṣṭānāṃ dāpyo yas tatra dṛśyate |
proṣitasya sutaḥ sarvaṃ putryam aṃśaṃ mṛtasya tu ||
grāmādhyartham ṛṇādāne bahubhiḥ puruṣair yatra prātibhāvyaṃ kṛtam, te hi ekachāyāpraviṣṭāḥ | tatas teṣāṃ yady eko dṛśyate sa eva sarvaṃ dadyāt | proṣitasya suto dṛṣṭaḥ sarvaṃ dadyāt | pitari mṛte sutaḥ svāṃśam eva dadyād iti | atra manuḥ |
dāpyaḥ pararṇam eko ‘pi jīvann api sutaiḥ krtam |
mṛteṣu tu na tatputraḥ pararṇaṃ dātum arhati ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
ekachāyākṛtaṃ sarvaṃ dadyāt tu proṣite sutaḥ |
mṛte pitari pitraṃśaṃ pararṇaṃ na kadācana ||
atra viśeṣam āha pitāmahaḥ |
avibhaktā ṛṇaṃ dadyuḥ pitryaṃ mātṛkam eva vā |
tadabhāve vibhaktāś ca na tu tābhyāṃ pratiśrutam ||
mātāpitṛsaṃbandhidravyābhāve tābhyāṃ pratiśrutaṃ na deyam | tatsadbhāve deyam eva | vyāsaḥ |
ṛṇaṃ paitāmahaṃ pautrāḥ prātibhāvyaṃ gataṃ sutāḥ |
samaṃ dadyus tatsutau tu na dāpyāv iti niścayaḥ ||
ṛṇe pautro na vṛddhiṃ dadyāt | putro ‘pi prātibhāvayate na vṛddhiṃ dadyat | tayoḥ sutau tatsutau ubhāv api na dāpyāv iti | yājñavalkyaḥ |
avibhaktaiḥ kuṭumbārthe yad ṛṇaṃ tu kṛtaṃ bhavet |
dadyus tad rikthinaḥ prete proṣite vā kuṭumbini ||
manuḥ |
gṛhīto yadi naṣṭaḥ syāt kuṭumbe ca kṛto vyayaḥ |
dātavyaṃ bāndhavais tat syād avibhaktair api smṛtam ||
nāradaḥ |
pitṛvyeṇāvibhaktena bhrātrā vā yad ṛṇaṃ kṛtam |
mātrā vātha kuṭumbārthe dadyus tad rikthino ‘khilam ||
nāprāptavyavahārais tu pitary uparate kvacit |
kāle tu vidhinā deyaṃ pateyur narake ‘nyathā ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
pitṛvyabhrātṛputrastrīdāsaśiṣyānujīvibhiḥ |
yad gṛhītaṃ kuṭumbārthe tad gṛhī dātum arhati ||
ya svāminā niyuktas tu dhanāyatyayapālane |
kusīdakṛṣivāṇijye nisṛṣṭārthas tu sa smṛtaḥ ||
pramāṇaṃ tatkṛtaṃ sarvaṃ lābhālābhavyayodayam |
svadeśe vānyadeśe vā svāmī na tu visaṃvadet ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
kanyāvaivāhike caiva pretakārye ‘pi yat kṛtam |
etat sarvaṃ pradātavyaṃ kuṭumbena kṛtaṃ prabhoḥ ||
uśaṇāḥ |
daṇḍaṃ daṇḍavaśeṣaṃ vā śulkaṃ taccheṣam eva ca |
na dātavyaṃ tu putreṇa yac ca nāvyavahārikam ||
svadhanān na deyam, pitur dhane sati deyam iti | yājñavalkyaḥ |
na yoṣit patiputrābhyāṃ na putreṇa kṛtaṃ pitā |
dadyād ṛte kuṭumbārthān na patiḥ strīkṛtaṃ tathā ||
gopaśauṇḍikaśailūṣarajakavyādhayoṣitām |
ṛṇaṃ dadyāt patis tāsāṃ yasmād vṛttis tadāśrayā ||
hetuvacanād anyāsām apy ayaṃ samānaḥ | nāradaḥ |
dadyād aputrā vidhavā niyuktā yā mumūrṣuṇā |
yāvad rikthaṃ samādadyāt yato riktham ṛṇaṃ tataḥ ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
pratipannaṃ striyā deyaṃ patyā vā saha yat kṛtam |
svayaṃ kṛtaṃ vā yad ṛṇaṃ nānyat strī dātum arhati ||
yā hi bhartṛsnehena bhartur maraṇasamaye tenānunītā madīyād dhanād aham eva dāsyāmīti saṃpratipadyate sā patikṛtam api dadyāt | svayaṃ kṛtaṃ vā yad ṛṇaṃ iti dṛṣṭāntartham | kātyāyanaḥ |
bhartukāmena vā bhartrādyuktā deyam ṛṇaṃ tvayā |
aprapannāpi sā dāpyā dhanaṃ yady āśritaṃ striyā ||
viṣṇuḥ: “saputrasya vāpy aputrasya rikthagrāhī ṛṇaṃ dadyāt | nirdhanasya strīgrāhī” | nāradaḥ |
antimā svairiṇīnāṃ yā punarbhūḥ prathamā ca yā |
ṛṇaṃ tayoḥ patikṛtaṃ dadyād yas te upāśnute ||
parapūrvāḥ striyāḥ sarvāḥ sapta proktā yathākramam |
punarbhūs trividhā tāsāṃ svairiṇī tu caturvidhā ||
kanyā vākṣatayonir yā pāṇigrahaṇadūṣitā |
punarbhūḥ prathamā proktā punaḥ saṃskārakarmaṇā ||
deśadharmān apekṣya strī gurubhir yā pradīyate |
sotpannasāhasāny asmai sā dvitīyā prakīrtitā ||
asatsu devareṣu strī bāndhavair yā pradīyate |
savarṇāya sapiṇḍāya sā tṛtīyā prakīrtitā ||
tisṝṇāṃ punaḥpāṇigrahaṇasaṃskārāt punarbhūtvam ucyata iti |
strī prasūtāprasūtā vā patyau svasthe tu jīvati |
kāmāt samāśrayed anyaṃ svairiṇī prathamā tu sā ||
kaumāraṃ patim utkramya yānyaṃ puruṣam āśritā |
punaḥ patyur gṛham iyat sā dvitīyā parikīrtitā ||
mṛte bhartari yā nārī devarādīn apāsya tu |
upagacchet paraṃ kāmāt sā tṛtīyā prakīrtitā ||
prāptadeśād dhanakrītā kṣutpipāsāturā tu yā |
tavāham ity upagatā sā caturthī prakīrtitā ||
iti catasṝṇāṃ svairiṇīnāṃ yā antimā, tisṝṇāṃ punarbhuvāṃ yā prathamā, tayoḥ patikṛtam ṛṇaṃ tayoḥ pravartamāno dadyāt |
adhanasya hy aputrasya mṛtasyopeti yaḥ striyam |
ṛṇaṃ voḍhuḥ sa bhajate tad evāsya dhanam smṛtam ||
sa eva antyatra āha |
dhanastrīhāriputrāṇām ṛṇabhāg yo dhanaṃ haret |
putro ‘satoḥ strīdhaninoḥ strīhārī dhaniputrayoḥ ||
iti vacanāt |
nanu yoṣidgrāhābhāve putro dāpyaḥ, putrābhāve yoṣidgrāha iti viruddham ucyate | ubhayasaṃbhave na kiṃcid dātavyam iti na virodhaḥ | te ‘ntimasvairiṇīgrāhasya prathamapunarbhūgrāhasya cābhāve putro dāpyaḥ, putrābhāve na nirdhananirapatyayoṣidgrāho dāpya iti | yad vā dhanastrīhārītyādivacane madhyamaputraśabdo gauṇaputraparaḥ, antimaputraśabdo mukhyaputrapara ity avirodhaḥ | ubhayasaṃbhave putrayoṣidgrāhāsaṃbhave 'nyatareṇāpy ṛṇaṃ deyam, anyatarābhāve anyatarasya ṛṇādānokteḥ | tathā hi “putro ‘sato strīdhaninoḥ” iti vacanāt |
strīhāryabhāve ṛṇaṃ putreṇa dātavyam iti pratīyate, tathā “strīhārī dhaniputrayoḥ” iti vacanāt | putrābhāve strīhāriṇā ṛṇaṃ dātavyam iti | ubhayasaṃbhave anyatareṇāpi dātavyam | yadā “asato strīdhanino” ity atra strītiśabdena svairiṇīnām antimā  punarbhuvā prathamā gṛhyate, tathā strīhārī dhaniputrayoḥ ity atra strīśabdena svairiṇīnām antimā vyatiriktā punarbhuvā prathamā vyatiriktā vā gṛhyanta ity avirodhaḥ | ata eva mukhyaputrapraśaṃsām āha nāradaḥ |
icchanti pitaraḥ putraṃ svārthahetor yataḥ sutam |
uttamarṇādhamarṇebhyo mām ayaṃ mokṣayiṣyati ||
ataḥ putreṇa jātena svārtham utsṛjya yatnataḥ |
ṛṇāt pitā mocanīyo yathā na narakaṃ vrajet ||
tapasvī cāgnihotrī ca ṛṇavān mriyate yadi |
tapaś caivāgnihotraṃ ca tat sarvaṃ dhanināṃ dhanam ||
uttamam ṛṇam uttamarṇaṃ devarṣisaṃbandhi yāgabrahmacaryam, adhamam ṛṇaṃ adharmarṇaṃ manuṣyasaṃbandhi | bṛhaspatiḥ |
uddāmād ṛṇam ādāya svāmine na dadāti yaḥ |
so ‘sya dāso ‘rthaṃ bhṛtyastrī paśur vā jāyate gṛhe ||
tasmād avaśyaṃ putrapautrapatnyādibhiḥ ṛṇāpākaraṇaṃ kartavyam |
[iti varadarājīye vyavahāranirṇaye ṛṇādānaprakaraṇaṃ samāptam]
8. nikṣepakāṇḍam
idānīṃ nikṣepākhyaṃ vivādapadam ucyate | atra nāradaḥ |
svadravyaṃ yatra visrambhān nikṣipatyaviśaṅkitaḥ |
nikṣepo nāma tat proktaṃ vyavahārapadaṃ budhaiḥ ||
anyadravyavyavahitaṃ dravyam avyāhṛtaṃ ca yat |
nikṣipyate paragṛhe tad aupanidhikaṃ smṛtam ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
bhājanastham anājñāya haste nyasya yad arpyate |
dravyaṃ tad aupanidhikaṃ pratideyaṃ tathaiva tat ||
upanidhir api nikṣepabheda eva | savrūpaṃ saṃkhyāṃ cānāvedya karaṇḍakādinihitaṃ mudritaṃ nikṣiptam upanidhiḥ | tat tathaiva deyam | svarūpasaṃkhyām āvedyārpitaṃ nikṣepaḥ | bṛhaspatiḥ |
anākhyātaṃ vyavahitam asṃkhyātam adarśitam |
mudrāṅkitaṃ ca yad dattaṃ tad aupanidhikaṃ smṛtam ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
yac ca prokṣitanikṣiptaṃ yac cānvāhitavācitam |
anyapratyarpitaṃ caiva so ‘rthas tūpanidhiḥ smṛtaḥ ||
manuḥ |
kulaje vṛttasaṃpanne dharmajñaṃ satyavādini |
mahāpakṣe dhaniny ārye nikṣepaṃ nikṣiped budhaḥ ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
jñānaṃ gṛhaṃ gṛhasthaṃ ca tatphalaṃ vibhavaṃ guṇān |
satyaṃ śaucaṃ bandhujanaṃ nirīkṣya sthāpayen nidhim ||
manuḥ |
yo yathā nikṣiped dhaste yam arthaṃ yasya mānavaḥ |
sa tathaiva gṛhītavyo yathā dāyas tathā grahaḥ ||
svayam eva tu yo dadyāt mṛtasya pratyantare |
na sa rājñābhiyoktavyo na nikṣeptuś ca bandhubhiḥ ||
mitho dāyaḥ kṛto yena gṛhīto mitha eva vā |
mitha eva pradātavyo yathā dāyas tathā grahaḥ ||
asākṣiman nikṣiptam asākṣikam eva pratidāpayet, sasākṣikaṃ sasākṣikam eveti |
sākṣyabhāve praṇidhibhir vayorūpasamanvitaiḥ |
apadeśais tu saṃnyasya hiraṇyaṃ tasya tattvataḥ ||
yācyaḥ iti śeṣaḥ | yājñavalkyaḥ |
na dāpyo ‘pahṛtaṃ tat tu rājadaivikataskaraiḥ |
bhreṣaś cen mārgite ‘datte dāpyo daṇḍaṃ ca tatsamam ||
rājadaivikataskaraiḥ bhreśaś cet na dāpyaḥ | bhreṣābhāve svāminā mārgite yācite yadi na dadāti tadā tatsamaṃ daṇḍaṃ tac ca dāpya iti | kātyāyanaḥ |
upekṣaṇād virodhād vā nikṣiptasya vināśane |
nikṣeptre tat pradeyaṃ syāt na daivād rājatas tathā ||
ṣaṭtriṃśanmate |
paropanihitaṃ dravyaṃ saṃrakṣet svādaraṃ svavat |
naṣṭe daivena rājñā vā tad adeyaṃ yamo ‘bravīt ||
bhoktāraṃ praty āha |
ājīvan svecchayā daṇḍyo dāpyas taṃ cāpi sodayam |
yācitānvāhitanyāsanikṣepādiṣv ayaṃ vidhiḥ || iti |
yācitānvāhitayoḥ svarūpaṃ kātyāyana āha |
yac ca svakāryasiddhyarthaṃ pratideyaṃ yathā tathā |
yācitvā pragṛhītaṃ tu tad yācitakam ucyate ||
anumārgeṇa kāryeṣu amuṣya vacanān mayā |
deyaṃ tvayeti yad dattaṃ tad anvāhitam ucyate ||
nyāsasvarūpam āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
rājacorādikabhayād dāyādānāṃ ca vañcanāt |
sthāpyate ‘nyagṛhe dravyam nyāsas tu parikīrtitam ||
ādigrahaṇāt suvarṇakārahaste kaṭakādyābharaṇanirmāṇāya nyastasya suvarṇasya tathā dhanavato viśvāsārthaṃ suvarṇakāreṇa pratidattasyāpi grahaṇam | tathā ca nāradaḥ |
nikṣepaharaṇe daṇḍaṃ tat prayatnena pālayet |
iti |
eṣa eva vidhir dṛṣṭo yācitānvāhitādiṣu |
śilpiṣūpanidhau nyāse pratinyāse tathaiva ca ||
upanidheḥ yaḥ pratidānavidhiḥ sa eva yācitādiṣu veditavyaḥ | eṣu viṣayeṣu vṛddhipramāṇaṃ cāha kātyāyanaḥ |
nikṣepaṃ vṛddhiśeṣaṃ ca krayaṃ vikrayam eva ca |
yācyamānaṃ na cet dadyāt vardhate pañcakaṃ śatam ||
ājīvan svecchayā daṇḍya iti daṇḍavidhānaṃ śrīmantaṃ pratyucyate na nirdhanam | tasya sodayadānam eva | tathā ca kātyāyanaḥ |
bhakṣitaṃ sodayaṃ dāpyaḥ samaṃ dāpya upekṣitam |
kiṃcin nyūnaṃ pradāpyaḥ syāt dravyam ajñānanāśitam ||
upekṣayā nāśe samaṃ dāpyaḥ | ajñānanāśe caturthāṃśanyūnam iti brahmavitpravaraḥ | bṛhaspatiḥ |
devarājopaghātena yadi tannāśam āpnuyāt |
gṛhītadravyasahitaṃ tatra doṣo na vidyate ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
nikṣiptaṃ yasya yat kiṃcit tat prayatnena pālayet |
devarājakṛte nāśe gṛhītaṃ dātym arhati ||
manuḥ |
corair hṛtaṃ jale naṣṭam agninā dagdham eva ca |
na dadyād yadi tasmāt sa na saṃharati kiṃcana ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
bhedenopekṣayā nyāsaṃ grahītā yadi nāśayet |
yācyamāno na dadyād vā dāpyaṃ tat sodayaṃ bhavet ||
nyāsadravyeṇa yat kiṃcit sādhayet kāryam ātmanaḥ |
daṇḍyaḥ sa rājñā bhavati dāpyaṃ tac cāpi sodayam ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
yaiś ca saṃskriyate nyāso divasaiḥ parikīrtitaiḥ |
tadūrdhvaṃ sthāpayec chilpī dāpyo daivahato ‘pi tam ||
nikṣepādirakṣaṇe grahītur aṃśam āha | nāradaḥ |
daivataskararājāgnivyasane samupasthite |
yat tat svaśaktyā saṃrakṣet tasyāṃśo daśamaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
corataḥ salilād agneḥ dhanaṃ yat svayam āharet |
tasyāṃśo daśamo dayaḥ sarvadravyeṣv ayaṃ vidhiḥ ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
dadato yad bhavet puṇayaṃ hemarūpyāmbarādikam |
tat syāt pālayato ‘nyasya tathā ca śaraṇāgatam ||
bhartṛdrohe yathā nāryāḥ puṃsaḥ putrasuhṛdvadhe |
doṣo bhavet tathā nyāse bhakṣitopekṣite nṛṇām ||
manuḥ |
upādhibhiḥ sa yaḥ kaścit paradravyaṃ haren naraḥ |
sasahāyaḥ sa hantavyaḥ prakāśaṃ vividhair vadhaiḥ ||
nīcaviṣayam idam | viṣṇuḥ: “nikṣepāpahārī vṛddhisahitaṃ dhanaṃ rājñā dāpyaḥ” | tatsamaṃ daṇḍaṃ ca kātyāyanaḥ |
yo yācitakam ādāya na dadyāt pratiyācitaḥ |
sa nigṛhya balād dāpyo daṇḍyaś ca na dadāti yaḥ ||
vṛddhiprakāraṃ sa evāha |
krayamūlyaṃ karārthaṃ ca nikṣepāpahnave tathā |
cakravṛddhyā vivardheta yāvat pañcaguṇaṃ bhavet ||
tripakṣāt parato ‘rthaṃ syāt trimāsāt triguṇaṃ bhavet |
ata ūrdhvaṃ vivardheta cakravṛddhivyavasthayā ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
raho datte nidhau yatra visaṃvādaḥ prajāyate |
vibhāvakaṃ tatra divyam ubhayor api ca smṛtam ||
iti śrīmadvaradarājīye nikṣepakāṇḍaṃ samāptam
9. asvāmivikrayakāṇḍam
atha asvāmivikrayaḥ | nāradaḥ |
nikṣiptaṃ vā paradravyaṃ naṣṭaṃ labdhvāpahṛtya vā |
vikrīyate ‘samakṣaṃ yat sa jñeyo ‘svāmivikrayaḥ ||
manuḥ |
asvāminā kṛto yas tu krayo vikraya eva vā |
akṛtaḥ sa tu vijñeyo vyavahāre yathā sthitiḥ ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
vikretur darśanāc chuddhiḥ svāmī dravyaṃ nṛpo damam |
kretā mūlyam avāpnoti tasmād yas tasya vikrayī ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
asvāmivikrayaṃ dānam ādhiṃ ca vinivartayet |
manuḥ |
vikrīṇīte parasya svaṃ yo ‘svāmī svāmyasaṃmataḥ |
na taṃ nayeta sākṣyaṃ tu stenam astenamāninam ||
dravyam asvāmivikrītaṃ prāpya svāmī tad āpnuyāt |
prakāśakrayataḥ śuddhiḥ kretuḥ steyaṃ rahaḥkrayāt ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
svaṃ labhetānyavikrītaṃ kretuḥ doṣe prakāśite |
hīnadrohe hīnamūlye velāhīne ca taskaraḥ ||
smṛtyantare |
prakāśaṃ vā krayaṃ kuryān mūlyaṃ vāpi samarpayet |
mūlyānayanakālaś ca deyas tatrārthasaṃkhyayā ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
asamāhāryamūlas tu krayam eva viśodhayet |
viśodhite kraye rājñā vaktavyaḥ sa na kiṃcana ||
prakāśaṃ ca krayaṃ kuryāt sādhubhiḥ mantribhiḥ svakaiḥ |
na tatrānyā kriyā proktā daivikī na ca mānuṣī ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
antargṛhe bahir grāmād upāṃśu śatayojanāt |
hīnamūlyaṃ bhayakrītaṃ vijñeyo ‘svāmivikrayaḥ ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
avijñātatayā krītaṃ vikretā yata vā mṛtaḥ |
svāmī tadardhaṃ mūlyaṃ tu pragṛhṇīta svakaṃ dhanam ||
marīciḥ |
avijñātaviśeṣatvāt yatra mūlyaṃ na labhyate |
hānis tatra samā kalpyā kretṛnāṣṭikayor dvayoḥ ||
smṛtyantare |
anupasthāpayan mūlyaṃ krayaṃ vāpy anuśodhayan |
yathābhiyogaṃ dhanine dhanaṃ dāpyo damaṃ ca saḥ ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
hṛtaṃ praṇaṣṭaṃ yo dravyaṃ parahastād avāpnuyāt |
anivedya nṛpe daṇḍyaḥ sa tu ṣaṇṇavitiṃ paṇān ||
manuḥ |
apahāryo bhavec caiṣa sānvayaḥ ṣaṭchataṃ dhanam |
niranvayo nāpahāraprāptaḥ syāc corakilbiṣam ||
anena vidhinā śasyaḥ kurvann asvāmivikrayam |
ajñānāj jñānapūrvaṃ tu coravad daṇḍam arhati ||
nāradaḥ |
dravyam asvāmivikrītaṃ prāpya svāmī tad āpnuyāt |
asvāmyanumatād dāsād asataś ca janād rahaḥ ||
hīnamūlyam avelāyāṃ krīṇaṃs taddoṣabhāg bhavet |
bṛhaspatiḥ |
vikrīyorvīṃ (?) tu yaḥ kretuḥ bhuktiṃ yogyāṃ na sādhayet |
sa tasmai tad dhanaṃ dadyād anyathā coradaṇḍabhāk ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
dattakrītābhidhānāṃ tu virodhe niṣkraye bhavet |
kṣetraṃ tatsadṛśaṃ dadyād aśaktaḥ tuṣṭim āvahet ||
śaṅkhaḥ |
anātmīyasya vikretā gṛhakṣetrādikasya tu |
sa tasmai tad dhanaṃ dadyāt mūlyaṃ vā kretur icchayā ||
kāmakāre tatsamaṃ daṇḍaṃ tatsamaṃ vā kṣetrādikaṃ dadyāt | akāmakāre na, tatkālavyavahriyamāṇamūlyaṃ vā tatsadṛśaṃ vā dadyāt, na daṇḍam | kṛṣṇīye tu krayaparivartanayor ayaṃ viśeṣaḥ – kraye mūlyaṃ tatsadṛśaṃ kṣetraṃ vā dadyāt | parivartanāyāṃ tu svaṃ gṛhṇīyād ity uktam |
asvāmivikrayakāṇḍaṃ samāptam
10. saṃbhūyasamuttānādidaśapadakāṇḍam
saṃbhūyasamutthānam
atha saṃbhūyasamutthānākhyaṃ vivādapadam ucyate | atra nāradaḥ |
vaṇikprabhṛtayo yatra karma saṃbhūya kurvate |
tat saṃbhūyasamutthānaṃ vyavahārapadaṃ smṛtam ||
phalahetor upāyena karma saṃbhūya kurvatām |
ādhāradhārabhūtaḥ prakṣepas tenottiṣṭheyur aṃśataḥ ||
ekaikapuruṣavyāpāraprayojanād adhikalābhārthaṃ karma saṃbhūya kurvatā ādhārabhūta upāyabhūto dravyaprakṣepaḥ | tatra yena yāvad adravyam upanītaṃ tadanuguṇaṃ vyāpāraṃ kuryāt | lābham api tathā gṛhṇīyād ity arthaḥ |
samo ‘natirikto hīno vā yasyāṃśo yatra yādṛśaḥ |
kṣayavyayau tathā vṛddhiḥ tasya tatra tathāvidhā ||
alpāṃśasyālpo mahāṃśasya mahān ity arthaḥ | bṛhaspatiḥ |
aśaktālasarogārtamandabhāgyanirāśrayaiḥ |
vaṇijyā ca sahaitais tu kartavyā budhaiḥ kriyā ||
kulīnadakṣānalasaiḥ prājñaiḥ kaṇṭakavedibhiḥ |
āyavyayajñaiḥ śucibhiḥ śūraiḥ kāryā saha kriyā ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
samavāyena vaṇijāṃ lābhārthaṃ karma kurvatām |
lābhālābhau yathādravyam arthaṃ saṃvidvaśād bhavet ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
samavetās tu ye kecit śilpino vaṇijo ‘pi vā |
yathāṃśaṃ karma kurvāṇaiḥ prāptaṃ tatra phalaṃ samam ||
parīkṣaṅāḥ sākṣiṇaś ca ta evoktāḥ parasparam |
saṃdighe ‘rthe vañcanāyāṃ na ca vidveṣasaṃyutāḥ ||
yaḥ kaścid vañcakas teṣāṃ vijñātaḥ krayavikraye |
śapathaiḥ sa tu śodhyaḥ syāt sarvavādeṣv ayaṃ vidhiḥ ||
krayahānir yadā tatra daivarājakṛtā bhavet |
sarveṣām eva sā proktā kalpanīyā yathāṃśataḥ ||
anirdiṣṭo vāryamāṇaḥ pramādād yas tu nāśayet |
tasyāṃśaṃ daśamaṃ datvā gṛhṇīyus te ‘ṃśato ‘pare ||
nāradaḥ |
ekasya cet syād vyasanaṃ dāyādo ‘syaitad āpnuyāt |
anyo vāsati dāyāde śaktāś cet sarva eva vā ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
rājādadīta ṣaḍbhāgaṃ navamaṃ dvādaśaṃ tathā |
śūdraviṭkṣatrajātānāṃ viprād gṛhṇīta viṃśakam ||
abdād ūrdhvaṃ tu nāgacchet yatra svāmī kathaṃcana |
tadā gṛhṇīta tad rājā brahmasvaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ śrayet ||
manuḥ |
praṇaṣṭasvāmikaṃ rikthaṃ rājā tryabdaṃ nidhāpayet |
arvād tryabdāl labhet svāmī pareṇa nṛpatir haret ||
nāradaḥ |
asvāmikam adāyādaṃ daśavarṣasthitaṃ tataḥ |
rājā tad ātmasāt kuryād evaṃ dharmo na hīyate ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
ardhaprakṣepaṇād viṃśaṃ bhāgaṃ śulkaṃ nṛtpo haret |
vyāsiddhaṃ rājayogyaṃ ca vikrītaṃ rājagāmi tat ||
deśāntaragate prete dravyam ādāya bāndhavāḥ |
jñātayo vā hareyus tam āgatās tair vinā nṛpaḥ ||
jihmaṃ tyajeyur nirlābham aśakto ‘nyena kārayet |
anena vidhir ākhyāta ṛtvikkarṣakakarmiṇām ||
nāradaḥ |
ṛtvik tu trividho dṛṣṭaḥ pūrvair juṣṭaḥ svayaṃ vṛtaḥ |
yadṛcchayā ca yaḥ kuryād ārtvijyaṃ prītipūrvakam ||
manuḥ |
ṛtvig yadi vṛto yajñe svakarma parihāpayet |
tasya karmānurūpeṇa deyo ‘ṃśaḥ saha kartṛbhiḥ ||
ardhakaraṇe ardhaṃ deyam | pādakaraṇe pādaṃ deyam ityādi |
saṃbhūya svāni karmāṇi kurvadbhir iha mānavaiḥ |
anena kramayogena kartyāṃśaprakalpanā ||
yasmin karmaṇi yās tu syur uktāḥ pratyaṅgadakṣiṇāḥ |
sa eva tā ādadīta bhajeran sarva eva vā ||
manuḥ |
rathaṃ haret cādhvaryuḥ brahmādhāne ca vājinam |
hotā cāpi hared aśvaṃ udgātā cāpy anaḥ kraye ||
ādhāne agnyādhāne, kraye somakraye | atha sādhāraṇānāṃ dakṣiṇānāṃ vibhāgam āha |
sarveṣām ardhino mukhyāḥ tadardhenārdhino ‘pare |
tṛtīyinas tṛtīyāṃśāś caturthāṃśās tu pādinaḥ ||
ayam arthaḥ | ṛtvijo hotrādayaḥ ṣoḍaśa | teṣāṃ madhye ye mukhyāś catvāro hotā adhvaryuḥ brahmā udgātā ceti | te gośatasyārdhinaḥ sarveṣāṃ bhāgaparipūraṇopapattivaśāt aṣṭācatvāriṃśadrūpeṇa ardho ‘ṃśabhājaḥ, apare maitrāvaruṇaḥ pratipasthātā brāhmaṇācchāṃsī prastotā ceti catvāraḥ | tadardhena tasya mukhāṃśasyārdhena caturviṃśatirūpeṇa arthabhājaḥ | ye tu tṛtīyinaḥ acchāvākaneṣṭragnīdhrapratihartāraḥ te mukhyāṃśasya ṣoḍaśāṃśabhāgarūpatṛtīyāṃśabhājaḥ, ye tu pādinaḥ grāvastut-unnetṛ-protṛ-subrahmaṇyāḥ te mukhyasya bhāgasya caturthāṃśāḥ dvādaśagorūpacaturtho ‘ṃśabhājaḥ vājapeyādiṣu samadakṣiṇāḥ | atra manuḥ |
ṛtvijaṃ yas tyajed yājyo yājyaṃ ṛtvik tyajed yadi |
śaktaṃ karmaṇy aduṣṭaṃ ca tayor daṇḍaḥ śataṃ śatam ||
nāradaḥ |
kramāgateṣv eṣu dharmo vrateṣv ṛtvikṣu ca svayam |
yādṛcchakeṣu saṃyājyaṃ tyakte nāsty eva kulbiṣam ||
atra viśeṣam āha kauṭilyaḥ: “agniṣṭomādiṣu ca kratuṣu dīkṣaṇīyāyā ūrdhvaṃ yājako ‘vasannaḥ pañcamam aṃśaṃ labhate | somakrayād ūrdhvaṃ caturtham, madhyamopasada pravargyodvāsanād ūrdhvaṃ tṛtīyam, agniṣomīyād ūrdhvaṃ dvitīyam, prataḥsavanād ūrdhvaṃ pādonam, mādhyandinād ūrdhvaṃ samagram, nītāsu dakṣiṇāsu bhavatīti |
bṛhaspatiḥ |
śrūyatāṃ karṣakādīnāṃ vidhānam idam ucyate |
bāhyakarṣakabījādyaiḥ kṣetropakaraṇena ca ||
ye samarthās tu taiḥ sārdhaṃ kṛṣiḥ kāryā vijānatā |
vipine nagarābhyāśe tathā rājapathasya ca ||
ūṣaraṃ kaṇṭakavyāptaṃ kṣetraṃ yatnena varjayet |
gartānūpaṃ susekaṃ ca samantāt kṣetrasaṃyutak ||
sukṛṣṭaṃ ca kṛtaṃ kāle vāpayan phalam aśnute |
kṛśātivṛddhaṃ kruddhaṃ ca rogiṇaṃ prapalāyinam ||
kāṇaṃ khañjaṃ ca nādadyād dhuryaṃ prājñaḥ kṛṣīvalaḥ |
bījavāhyād yayā hanyā kṣetrahāniḥ prajāyate ||
yasyaitat tasya sā proktā nānyeṣām iti niścayaḥ |
kṣetrahāniḥ bhogahāniḥ |
eṣa dhamaḥ samākhyātaḥ kīṇāśānāṃ purātanaḥ ||
hiraṇyarūpyasūtrāṇāṃ kāṣṭhapāṣāṇacarmaṇām |
saṃskartā tatkriyābhijñaḥ śilpī cokto manīṣibhiḥ ||
hemakārādayo yatra śilpaṃ saṃbhūya kurvate |
karmānurūpaṃ nirveśaṃ labheraṃs te yathāṃśataḥ ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
śikṣakāḥ karmakuśalāḥ ācāryāś ceti śilpinaḥ |
ekadvitricaturbhāgān hareyus te yathottaram ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
harmyaṃ devagṛhaṃ vāpī cārmikopaskarāṇi ca |
saṃbhūya karvatāṃ teṣāṃ pramukhyo ‘rdho ‘ṃāsam arhati ||
nartakānām eṣa eva dharmaḥ sadbhir udāhṛtaḥ |
tālajño labhate ‘dhyardhaṃ gāyakās tu samāṃśinaḥ ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
pararāṣṭrād dhanaṃ yat syāt coraiḥ svāmyājñayā hṛtam |
rājñe daśāṃśam utsṛjya vibhajeran yathāvidhi ||
corāṇāṃ mukhyabhūtas tu caturo ‘ṃśāṃs tato haret |
śūro ‘ṃśāṃs trīn samartho dvau coras tv ekaikam eva tu ||
teṣāṃ cet prasṛtānāṃ yo grahaṇaṃ samavāpnuyāt |
tan mokṣārthaṃ yad dattaṃ tasya kāryā samakriyā ||
iti saṃbhūyasamutthānaṃ nāma tṛtīyaṃ vivādapadaṃ samāptam
dattāpradānikam
atha dattāpradānikam ucyate | tatra nāradaḥ |
datvā drayam asamyag yaḥ punar ādātum icchati |
dattāpradānikaṃ nāma tad vivādapadaṃ smṛtam ||
adeyam atha deyaṃ ca dattaṃ cādattam eva ca |
vyavahāre ca vijñeyo dānamārgaś caturvidhaḥ ||
adeyaṃ dānānarham, deyaṃ dānārham, dattaṃ sthiram, adattaṃ asthiraṃ nivartanīyam ity evaṃ caturvidho dānamargaḥ |
tatrehāṣṭāv adeyāni deyam ekavidhaṃ smṛtam |
dattaṃ saptavidhaṃ vidyād adattaṃ ṣoḍaśātmakam ||
aṣṭau adeyāny āha nāradaḥ |
anvāhitaṃ yācitakam ādhiḥ sādhāraṇaṃ ca yat |
nikṣepaḥ putradārāś ca sarvasvaṃ cānvaye sati ||
āpatsv api hi kaṣṭāsu vartamānena dehinā |
adeyāny āhur ācāryā yac cānyasmai pratiśrutam || iti |
atra viśeṣam āha dakṣaḥ |
sāmānyaṃ yācitanyāsa ādhir dārāś ca taddhanam |
anvāhitaṃ ca nikṣepaḥ sarvasvaṃ cānvaye sati ||
āpatsv api na deyāni nava vastūni paṇḍitaiḥ |
yo dadāti sa mūḍhātmā prāyaścittīyate naraḥ ||
atra viśeṣam āha kātyāyanaḥ |
vikrayaṃ caiva dānaṃ ca na kāryāḥ syur anicchavaḥ |
dārāś ca putrāḥ sarvasvam ātmany eva tu yojayet ||
āpatkāle tu kartavyaṃ dānaṃ vikrayam eva vā |
anyathā na pravarteta iti śāstrasya niścayaḥ ||
āpattāratamyena dānādānavikalpaḥ | atra vasiṣṭhaḥ: “śuklaśoṇitasaṃbhavaḥ puruṣo mātāpitṛnimittakaḥ, tasya pradānavikrayaparityāgeṣu mātāpitarau prabhavataḥ | na tv ekaṃ putraṃ dadyāt pratigṛhṇīyād vā | sa hi santānāya pūrveṣām | na tu strī putraṃ dadyāt pratigṛhṇīyāt | anyatrānujñānād bhartuḥ” | kātyāyanaḥ |
kramāgataṃ gṛhakṣetraṃ pitryaṃ paitāmahaṃ tathā |
putrapautrasamṛddhasya na deyam ananujñayā ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
vaivāhike kramāyāte sarvadānaṃ na vidyate | iti |
tathā ca manuḥ |
vibhaktā vāvibhaktā vā dāyādāḥ sthāvare samāḥ |
eko hy anīśaḥ sarvatra dānādhamanavikraye ||
manuḥ |
dharmārthaṃ yena dattaṃ syāt kasmaicid yācate dhanam |
paścāc ca na tathā tat syāt na deyaṃ tena tad bhavet ||
dānārham ekavidham āha nāradaḥ |
kuṭumbabharaṇād dravyaṃ yat kiṃcid atiricyate |
tad deyam uparuddhyānyad dadad āga samāpnuyāt ||
atra viśeṣam āha kātyāyanaḥ |
sarvasvaṃ gṛhavarjaṃ tu kuṭumbabharaṇādikam |
yad dravyaṃ tat svakaṃ deyam adeyaṃ syād ato ‘nyathā || iti |
svakaṃ svayam ārjitam |
na putreṣu na dāreṣu na ca bandhuṣv apekṣiṇaḥ |
sarvakāryeṣu puruṣāḥ svadravye prabhaviṣṇavaḥ ||
svadravye svayam ārjite prabhaviṣṇava ity arthaḥ | atha dāne pātraviśeṣāt phalaviśeṣa ucyate |
vyāsaḥ |
andhādiṣu śataṃ dānam anantaṃ duhitur bhavet |
pitre śataguṇaṃ dānaṃ sahasraṃ mātur ucyate ||
bhaginyāḥ śatasāhasraṃ sodarye dattam akṣayam |
dakṣaḥ |
mātāpitror gurau mitre vinīte copakāriṇi |
dīnānāthaviśiṣṭeṣu dattaṃ tu saphalaṃ bhavet ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
śūdre samaguṇaṃ dānaṃ vaiśye dviguṇam eva ca |
kṣatriya triguṇaṃ prāhuḥ brāhmaṇe ṣaḍguṇaṃ smṛtam ||
śrotriye tac ca sāhasram upādhyāye tu taddvayam |
ācārye triguṇaṃ jñeyam āhitāgniṣu taddvayam ||
ātmajñe śatasāhasram anantaṃ tv agnihotriṇi |
somape śatasāhastram anantaṃ brahmavādine ||
bharadvājaḥ |
dānam ekātmakaṃ prāhuḥ tad bhavet toyapūrvakam |
vinā toyapradānena dharmadānaṃ na vidyate ||
atra āpastambaḥ: “sarvāṇy udakapūrvāṇi dānāny adṛṣṭārthāni” | gautamaḥ: “svastivācya bhikṣādānam appūrvam, dadātiṣu caiva dharmyeṣu” | bṛhaspatiḥ |
strīdhanaṃ strī svakulyebhyaḥ prayacchet tantuvarjitā |
kulyābhāve tu bandhubhyas tadabhāve dvijātiṣu ||
saptavidhaṃ dattam āha nāradaḥ |
paṇyamūlyaṃ bhṛtis tuṣṭyā snehāt pratyupakārataḥ |
strīśulkānugrahārthaṃ ca dattaṃ saptavidhaṃ smṛtam ||
paṇyamūlyam paṇyasya krītasya mūlyatvena datta, kṛtakarmaṇe bhṛtitvena, tūṣṭya cāraṇādīnām, snehād duhitṛbhyaḥ putrādibhyaḥ, upakṛtavate pratyupakārarūpeṇa, pariṇayanārthaṃ kanyājñātibhyaḥ śulkatvena, yac cādṛṣṭārthaṃ anugrahārthaṃ ca dattaṃ tad etat saptavidham api dattam eva na pratyāharaṇīyam | bṛhaspatiḥ |
bhṛtis tuṣṭyā paṇyamūlyaṃ strīśulkam upakāriṇe |
śraddhānugrahasaṃprītyā dattam aṣṭavidhaṃ smṛtam ||
manuḥ |
mātāpitror gurau mitre vinīte copakāriṇi |
dīnānāthaviśiṣṭebhyo dattaṃ tu saphalaṃ bhavet ||
saudāyikaṃ kramāyātaṃ śauraprāptaṃ ca yad bhavet |
strījñātisvāmyanujñātaṃ dattaṃ siddham avāpnuyāt ||
saudāyike bhāryānujñā, kramātāyāte putrādyanujñā, śauryaprāpte svāmyanujñeti |
kātyāyanaḥ |
bhayatrāṇāya rakṣārthaṃ tathā kāryaprasādhanāt |
anena vidhinā labdhaṃ vidyāt pratyupakāritam ||
prāṇasaṃśayam āpannaṃ yo mām uttārayed itaḥ |
sarvasvaṃ tasya dāsyāmīty ukte ‘pi na tathā bhavet ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
pratigrahaḥ prakāśaḥ syāt sthāvarasya viśeṣataḥ |
deyaṃ pratiśrutaṃ caiva datvā nāpaharet punaḥ ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
svecchayā yaḥ pratiśrutya brāhmaṇāya pratigraham |
na dadyād ṛṇavad dāpyaḥ prāpnuyāt pūrvasāhasam ||
atra viṣṇuḥ: “pratiśrutasyāpradāyī tad dāpayitvā prathamasāhasaṃ daṇḍyaḥ” | hārītaḥ |
pratiśrutasyādānena dattasyocchedanena ca |
vividhān narakān yāti tiryagyonyāṃ ca jāyate ||
gautamaḥ: “pratiśrutyāpy adharmasaṃyuktāya na dadyāt” | bharadvājaḥ |
vacasā yat pratiśrutya karmaṇā nopapāditam |
ṛṇaṃ tad dhanasaṃyuktam iha loke paratra ca ||
ṛṇavat pratiśrutaṃ deyam ity arthaḥ | ṣoḍaśātmakam adattam āha nāradaḥ |
adattaṃ tu bhayakrodhaśokavegaruganvitaiḥ |
tathotkocaparīhāsavyatyāsachalayogataḥ ||
bālamūḍhāsvatantrārtamattonmattāpavarjitam |
kartā mamāyaṃ karmeti pratilobhecchayā ca yat ||
apātre pātram ity ukte kārye cādharmasaṃhite |
dattaṃ syād yad avijñānāt adattaṃ tad api smṛtam ||
bhayakrodhaśokaśabdaiḥ vegaśabdaḥ pratyekam abhisaṃbadhyate | kātyāyanaḥ |
kāmakrodhāsvatantrārtaklībonmattapramohitaiḥ |
vyatyāsaparihāsāc ca yad dattaṃ tat punar haret ||
hārītaḥ |
ākrośād arthahīnānāṃ pratikārāya tad bhayāt |
pradīyate tat kartṛbhyo bhayadānaṃ tad ucyate ||
utkocalakṣaṇaṃ nārada āha |
steyasāhasikodvṛttapāradārikaśaṃsanāt |
darśanād vṛttanaṣṭasya tathāsatyapravartanāt ||
prāptam etais tu yat kiṃcit tad utkocākyam ucyate ||
atra viśeṣam āha kātyāyanaḥ |
niyukto yas tu kāryeṣu sa ced utkocam āpnuyāt |
sa dāpyas tad dhanaṃ kṛtsnaṃ damaṃ caikādaśādhikam ||
aniyuktas tu kāryārtham utkocaṃ yady avāpnuyāt |
kṛtapratyupakārārthaṃ tasya doṣo na vidyate ||
yasya kāryasya siddhyarthaṃ utkocas tu pratiśrutaḥ |
tasya kāryasyāprasiddhau na deyaḥ sa kathaṃcana ||
tasya prāg eva dattaḥ syāt pratidāpyaḥ sa taṃ balāt ||
niyuktānām utkocopajīvane daṇḍaviśeṣam āha |
utkocajīvino martyān ghoṣayitvā svamaṇḍalāt |
sarvasvaharaṇaṃ kṛtvā rājā viprān vivāsayet ||
itarān api tat kṛtvā vadhādyair eva yojayet |
itarān brāhmaṇavyatiriktān | ādiśabdena aṅgachedādi gṛhyate | parihāsena līlayā vyatyāsena mūlyaparikalpanāyām anyad darśayitvā prayogakāle anyasya dānaṃ chalena asatyena yogena dattam, bālena aprāptaṣoḍaśavarṣeṇa dattam, mūḍhena vyādhinidrāmativibhramādinā lokavedānabhijñena dattam | asvatantrā nāredenoktāḥ |
asvatantrāḥ prajāḥ sarvāḥ svatantraḥ pṛthivīpatiḥ |
asvatantraḥ svataḥ śiṣya ācārye tu svatantratā ||
asvatantrāḥ striyaḥ putrā dāso yaś ca parigrahaḥ |
svatantras tasya tu gṛhī yasya syāt tat kramāgatam ||
garbhasthaiḥ sadṛśo jñeya āṣṭamād vatsarāc chiśuḥ |
bāla āṣoḍaśād varṣāt pogaṇḍaś ceti śabdyate ||
parato vyavahārajñaḥ svatatraḥ pitarau vinā |
jīvato hy asvatantraḥ syāt jarayāpi samanvitaḥ ||
tayor api pitā śreyān bījaprādhānyadarśanāt |
abhāve bījino mātā tadabhāve tu pūrvajaḥ ||
vyādhitena ārtena dattam, madanīyena śīdhupānādinā mattena dattam, vātapittādinā unmattena dattam | tathā coktaṃ nāradena |
pañconmādāḥ samākhyātāḥ vātapittakaphodbhavāḥ | iti |
kartā mamāyam upakārāyeti pratilābhecchayā ca dattam, apātre vṛṣalīpatyādau tapasvīti buddhyā dattam, yajñārthaṃ labdhaṃ dhanaṃ dyūtādau viniyujyamānāya dattam, ity evaṃ ṣoḍaśaprakāraṃ dattam apy adattam eva pratyāharaṇīyatvāt | ārtadattasyādattatvaṃ dharmakāryavyatiriktaviṣayam, kātyāyanavacanāt |
svasthenārtena vā dattaṃ śrāvitaṃ dharmakāraṇāt |
adatvā tu mṛte dāpyas tatsuto nātra saṃśayaḥ ||
tathedaṃ vacanaṃ saṃkṣiptārthavacanam |
yogādhamanavikrītaṃ yogadānapratigraham |
yatra vāpy upadhiṃ paśyet tat sarvaṃ vinivartayet ||
yoga upādhiḥ | yena yogādinā upādhiviśeṣeṇādhikrayavikrayadānapratigrahāḥ kṛtāḥ, tad upādhivigame teṣāṃ krayādīnāṃ nivṛttir iti |
dānaviśeṣaviṣayāṇi vacanāny ucyante | atra bharadvājaḥ |
prayojanam apekṣyaiva pātrebhyo yat pradīyate |
tad arthadānam ity āhur aihikaṃ phalam asya tu ||
dṛṣṭvā prayojanaṃ strīṇāṃ prasaṅgād yat pradīyate |
anarheṣu ca rāgeṇa kāmadānaṃ tad ucyate ||
saṃsadi vrīḍayā śrutvā parebhyo yat pradīyate |
vrīḍānām iti prāha tad dānaṃ tattvadarśinaḥ ||
dṛṣṭvā priyaṃ tathā śrutvā harṣād yac ca prayacchati |
harṣadānaṃ tad ity āhuḥ dṛṣṭam evāsya tat phalam ||
kalyāṇe ca vipattau ca yat priyeṣu pradīyate |
dānaṃ laukikam ity eva dṛṣṭārthaṃ tad punar bhavet ||
svatantro ‘pi hi yat kāryaṃ kuryād aprakṛtiṃ gataḥ |
tad apy akṛtam evāhur asvatantratvahetavaḥ ||
kāmakrodhābhiyuktoktibhayavyasanapīḍitāḥ |
rāgadveṣaparītāś ca jñeyāt svaprakṛtiṃ gatāḥ ||
tasmād evaṃbhūtāny adeyāni | bṛhaspatiḥ |
madūrdhvam iti yad dattaṃ na tat svatvāvahaṃ bhavet |
tenedānīm adattatvān mṛte rikthinam āpatet ||
nāradaḥ |
gṛhṇāti dattaṃ yo mohāt yaś cādeyaṃ prayacchati |
daṇḍanīyāv ubhāv etau dharmajñena mahīkṣitā ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
adattabhoktā daṇḍyaḥ syāt tathā deyapradāyakaḥ |
manuḥ |
adeyaṃ yaś ca gṛhṇāti yaś cādeyaṃ prayacchati |
tāv ubhau coravac chāsyau dāpyau tāvatsamaṃ damam ||
iti dattāpradānikaṃ [nāma caturthaṃ vivādapadam} samāptam
abhyupetyāśuśrūṣā
athābhyupetyāśuśrūṣocyate | bṛhaspatiḥ |
abhyupetya tu śuśrūṣāṃ yas tāṃ na pratipadyate |
aśuśrūṣābhyupetyaitat vivādapadam ucyate ||
śuśrūṣām ahaṃ karma tava kariṣyāmīty abhyupetya tat karma yo na karoti abhyupetya tu śuśrūṣety etad vivādapadaṃ vistāreṇocyata iti | nāradaḥ |
śuśrūṣakaḥ pañcavidhaḥ śāstre dṛṣṭo manīṣibhiḥ |
caturvidhaḥ karmakaraḥ teṣāṃ dāsās tripañcakāḥ ||
śuśrūṣāṃ pratipadyantaḥ pañcavidhā dṛṣṭā asmin śāstre śāstrāntare ca paṇḍitaiḥ | teṣām avalambya caturvidham icchataḥ karmapratipannaniyogatāsvatantrāḥ tān ubhayān āha |
śiṣyāntevāsibhṛtakāś caturthaś cādhikarmakṛt |
ete karmakarāḥ proktāḥ dāsās tu gṛhajādayaḥ ||
tatra śiṣyavṛttiṃ bṛhaspatir āha |
vidyā trayī samākhyātā ṛgyajuḥsāmalakṣaṇā |
tadarthaṃ guruśuśrūṣāṃ prakuryāc chāstracoditām ||
vidyāvijñānakāmārthanimittena caturvidhā |
ekaikaṃ punar eteṣāṃ kriyābhedāt prabhidyate ||
vijñānam ucyate śilpaṃ hemarūpyādisaṃskṛtiḥ |
nṛttādikaṃ ca tac chikṣan kuryāt karma guror gṛhe ||
nāradaḥ |
ādiyāgrahaṇāc chiṣyaḥ śuśrūṣet prayato gurum |
tadvṛttir gurudāreṣu guruputre tathaiva ca ||
vedatrayādhyāyināṃ trayāṇām ayam upadeśaḥ |
samāvṛttaś ca gurave pradāya gurudakṣiṇām |
pratīyāt svagṛhān eṣā śiṣyavṛttir udāhṛtā ||
svaṃ śilpam icchann āhartuṃ bāndhavānām anujñayā |
ācāryasya vased ante kālaṃ kṛtvā suniścitam ||
ācāryaḥ śikṣayed enaṃ svagṛhe dattabhojanam |
na cānyat kārayet karma putravac cainam ācaret ||
kātyāyana-bṛhaspatī |
yas tu na grāhayec chilpaṃ karmāṇy anyāni kārayet |
prāpnuyāt sāhasaṃ pūrvaṃ tasmāc chiṣyo nivartate ||
śikṣayantam aduṣṭaṃ sa yas tv ācāryaṃ parityajet |
balād vārayitavyaḥ syāt vadhabandhau ca so ‘rhati ||
śikṣito ‘pi kṛtaṃ kālam antevāsī samāpayet |
tatra karma ca yat kuryād ācāryasyaiva tat phalam ||
gṛhītaśilpaḥ samaye kṛtvācāryaṃ pradakṣiṇam |
śaktitaś cānumānyaivam antevāsī vinartate ||
nāradaḥ |
bhṛtakas trividho jñeya uttama madhyamo ‘dhamaḥ |
śaktibhaktyanurūpā syād eṣāṃ karmāśrayā bhṛtiḥ ||
uttamas tv āyudhīyo ‘tra madhyamas tu kṛṣīvalaḥ |
adhamo bhāravāhī syād ity evaṃ trividho bhṛtaḥ ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
āyudhīyottamas teṣāṃ madhyamaḥ sīravāhakaḥ |
bhāravāho ‘dhamaḥ proktaḥ tathā ca gṛhakarmakṛt ||
dviprakārā bhārabhṛtaḥ kṛṣigojīvinaḥ smṛtāḥ |
jātasasyāt tathā kṣīrāl labhate paribhāṣitam ||
nāradaḥ |
arthe ‘py adhikṛto yaḥ syāt kuṭumbasya tatopari |
so ‘dhikarmakaro jñeyaḥ sa ca kauṭumbikaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
śubhakarmakarā hy ete catvāraḥ samudāhṛtāḥ |
jaghanyakarmabhājas tu śeṣā dāsās tripañcakāḥ ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
dinamāsārdhaṣaṇmāsatrimāsābdabhṛtas tathā |
karma kuryāt pratijñātaṃ labhate paribhāṣitam ||
nāradaḥ |
karmāpi dvividhaṃ proktam aśubhaṃ śubhav eva ca |
aśubhaṃ dāsakarmoktaṃ śubham anyad udīritam ||
gṛhadvārāśucisthānarathāvaskaraśodhanam |
guhyāṅgasparśanocchiṣṭaviṇmūtragrahaṇojjhanam ||
iṣṭataḥ svāminaś cāṅgair upasthānam athāntataḥ |
aśubhaṃ karma vijñeyaṃ śubham anyad ataḥ param ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
viṇmūtrotsarjanaṃ caiva nagnatve parimardanam |
prāyo dāsīsutāḥ kuryuḥ śavādibharaṇaṃ ca yat ||
nāradaḥ |
gṛhajātas tathā krīto labdho dāyād upāgataḥ |
anākālabhṛtas tadvad āhitaḥ svāminā ca yaḥ ||
mokṣito mahataś carṇād yuddhe prāptaḥ paṇe jitaḥ |
tavāham ity upagataḥ pravrajyāvasitaḥ kṛtaḥ ||
bhaktadāsaś ca vijñeyaḥ tathaiva baḍavābhṛtaḥ |
vikretā cātmanaḥ śāstre dāsāḥ pañcadaśa smṛtāḥ || iti |
svagṛhe dāsyā jāto gṛhajātaḥ | svadravyeṇānyasmāt krītaḥ krītaḥ | labdhaḥ pratigrahādinā | pitrādikrameṇāgato dāyād upāgataḥ | anākālabhṛtaḥ durbhikṣe aśanavasanādibhir bhṛtaḥ | sop ‘pi pūrvavad eva svāminā dravyaṃ gṛhītvā vṛddhyarthaṃ karmakaraṇārthaṃ vā sthāpitaḥ | āhitaḥ svayam ṛṇaṃ dātum aśakto mahataś carṇāt mokṣitaḥ | rājñā yuddhe prāptaḥ | paṇe kasmiṃścit spardhayā jitaḥ | tavāhaṃ dāso ‘smi iti abhyupagataḥ | kenacin nimittena pravrajya atikrāntaḥ | iyantaṃ kālaṃ karma kariṣyāmīti pravṛttaḥ kṛtaḥ | yāvad bhaktaṃ dadāmi tāvad dāso ‘smīty upagato bhaktadāsaḥ | vaḍavā nāma gṛhadāsī aniyatā tayā saha vāsena nimittena dāsatvam upagato vaḍavābhṛtaḥ | bṛhaspatiḥ |
yo bhuṅkte paradāsīṃ tu sa jñeyo vaḍavābhṛtaḥ |
karma tat svāminaḥ kuryāt yathānyas tu bhṛto naraḥ ||
dravyaṃ gṛhītvā ya ātmano vikretā, ete pañcadaśāśubhakarmakarāḥ |
atra pūrvaś caturvargo dāsatvān na vimucyate |
prasādāt svāmino ‘nyatra dāsyam eṣāṃ kramāgatam ||
vikrīṇīte svatantro yaḥ svam ātmānaṃ narādhamaḥ |
sa jaghanyatamas tv eṣāṃ so ‘pi dāsyān na mucyate ||
atra teṣu pañadaśasu madhye gṛhjātakrītalabdhadāyāgatāś catvāro ‘nicchati svāmini dāsyān na mucyate | svāmī yadi prasanno muñcati, tadā mucyate | anyathā nityadāsā eva | tathā antimo ‘py ātmano vikretā yaḥ so ‘py evam eva | nāradaḥ |
yaś caiṣāṃ svāminaṃ kaścin mocayet prāṇasaṃśayāt |
dāsatvāt sa vimucyeta putrabhāgaṃ bhajeta ca ||
anākalabhṛto dāsyān mucyate goyugaṃ dadat |
bhakṣitaṃ cāpi durbhikṣe na tac chuddyeta karmaṇā ||
durbhikṣe mṛtaḥ pañcamogoyugaṃ datvā mucyate | durbhikṣe pramāṇarakṣārhaṃ bhakṣitaṃ tattatkarmaṇā nistīryata ity arthaḥ |
āhito ‘pi dhanaṃ datvā svāmī yady enam uddharet |
athopagamayed enaṃ so ‘pi krītād anantaraḥ ||
ṣaṣṭha āhito yathābhṛtaṃ dhanaṃ datvā prabhuḥ yady enaṃ mokṣayet tadā mucyeta | eṣa ca krītadharmā krītasya yā gatiḥ sā tasyety arthaḥ |
ṛṇaṃ tu sodayaṃ datvā ṛṇī dāsyād vimucyate |
saptama ṛṇān mokṣito ṛṇaṃ vṛddhiṃ ca datvā mucyate ||
kṛtakālavyuparamāt kṛtakālo vimucyate |
tavāham ity upagato yuddhaprāptaḥ paṇe jitaḥ ||
pratiśīrṣapradānena mucyeraṃs tulyakarmaṇā |
tavāham iti kālaniyamaṃ kṛtvā upagataḥ, yuddhe jitaḥ paṇe jitaś ca trayo ‘pi pratimānuṣyam ātmatulyakarmāṇaṃ datvā mucyante |
rājña eva tu dāsaḥ syāt pravrajyāvasito naraḥ |
na tasya pratimokṣo ‘sti na viśuddhiḥ kathaṃcana ||
pravrajyāvasito rājña eva dāsa uktaḥ | nityadāso nityāśuddhaḥ asaṃvyavahāryaś ca |
bhaktasyopekṣaṇāt sadyo bhaktadāsaḥ pramucyate |
nigrahād baḍavāyāś ca mucyate baḍavābhṛtaḥ ||
bhaktadāso bhaktasyādānān mucyate | baḍavābhṛto baḍvānigrahān mucyate | kātyāyanaḥ |
svadāsīṃ yas tu saṃgacchet prasūtā ca bhavet tataḥ |
avekṣya bījaṃ kāryā syād adāsī sānvayā tu sā ||
nāradaḥ |
svadāsam icched yaḥ kartum adāsaṃ prītamānasaḥ |
skandhād ādāya tasyāśu bhindyāt kumbhaṃ sahāmbhasā ||
sākṣatābhiḥ sapuṣpābhiḥ mūrdhni cainam avākiret |
adāsa iti coktvā triḥ prāṅmukhaṃ tam athotsṛjet ||
tataḥ prabhṛti vaktavyaḥ svāmyanugrahapālitaḥ |
bhojyānno ‘tha pratigrāhyo bhavaty abhimukhaḥ satām ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
dāsīsutāś ca dāsāḥ syuḥ jātaḥ patyāvareṇa vā |
utpādako yadi svāmī na dāsās tv asya sūnavaḥ ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
dāsenoḍhā tv adāsī yā sāpi dāsītvam āpnuyāt |
yasmād bhartā prabhus tasyāḥ svāmyadhīnaprabhur yataḥ ||
vyāsaḥ |
anyadīyā tu sā dāsī na dāsy anyasya sā bhavet |
śulkaṃ kṛtvā tu tāṃ gacchen na gantā dāsyam ṛcchati ||
na devadāsyāgamane śulkado dāsatāṃ vrajet |
na cec chulkaṃ tu bījārthaṃ tad bījaṃ kṣetriṇo bhavet ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
`        kāmāt tu saṃśritā yas tu dāsīṃ kuryāt balāt striyam |
saṃkrāmayeta vānyatra daṇḍyaḥ tac cākṛtaṃ bhavet ||
balād dhātrīm adāsīṃ ca dāsīm iva bhunakti yaḥ |
paricārakapatnīṃ ca prāpnuyāt pūrvasāhasam ||
vikrośamānāṃ yo bhaktāṃ dāsīṃ vikretum icchati |
anāpadisthāh śaktaḥ san prāpnuyād dviśataṃ damam ||
nāradaḥ |
yathā go’śvoṣṭradāsīṣu mahiṣyājāvikādiṣu |
notpādakaḥ prajābhāgī tathaivānyāṅganāsv api ||
upte bīje parakṣetre bījī na labhate phalam |
apatyam utpādayituḥ tāsāṃ yāḥ śulkato bhṛtā ||
aśulkopanatāyāṃ tu kṣetrikasyaiva tat phalam |
kṣetrikasya yad ajñānāt kṣetre bījaṃ prakīryate ||
na tatra bījino bhāgaḥ kṣetrikasyaiva tad bhavet |
oghavātāhṛtaṃ bījaṃ yasya kṣetre prarohati ||
phalabhāṅ niyatakṣetrī na bījī labhate phalam |
mahokṣo janayed vatsān yasya goṣu vrajec caran ||
tasya te yasya tā gāvo moghaṃ skanditam ārṣabham |
kṣetrikānumataṃ bījaṃ yasya kṣetre samupyate |
tad apatyaṃ dvayor eva bījikṣetrikayor matam ||
narte kṣetrād bhavet sasyaṃ na ca bījād vināsti tat |
ato ‘patyaṃ dvayor iṣṭaṃ pitur mātuś ca dharmataḥ ||
atha dāsyādhikāriṇa ucyante | kātyāyanaḥ |
triṣu varṇeṣu vijñeyaṃ dāsyaṃ viprasya na kvacit |
varṇānām ānulomyena dāsyaṃ na pratilomataḥ ||
rājanyavaiśyaśūdrāṇāṃ tyajatāṃ hi svatantratām |
samavarṇe tu vipraṃ tu dāsatvaṃ naiva kārayet ||
śīlādhyayanasaṃpanne na nyūnaṃ karma kāmataḥ |
tatrāpi nāśubhaṃ karma prakurvīta dvijottamaḥ ||
brāhmaṇasya hi dāsatvāt nṛpatejo vihanyate |
nāradaḥ |
varṇānām ānulomyena dāsyaṃ na pratilomataḥ |
svadharmatyāgino ‘nyatra dāravad dāsatā matā ||
svadharmatyāgī pravrajyāvasito vipraḥ kṣatriyāder dāso bhavati, yathā dāragrahaṇam ānulomyena na prātilomyena, evaṃ dāsyam api | uśanāḥ |
na gurur na sapiṇḍaś ca na vipro nāntyayonayaḥ |
dāsabhāvaṃ na gachanti na ca vidyādhiko dvijaḥ ||
na gurur na sapiṇḍa iti brāhmaṇavyatiriktajātyantaraviṣayam | brāhmaṇasyāntyayonir na dāsaḥ | kṣatriyādisamavarṇasya vidyādhiko na dāsaḥ | kaṇvaḥ |
ajñātayonayaḥ sarve naiva dāsā dvijanmanām |
vidhyādhikaḥ sajātaś ca sapiṇḍo guravo ‘pi ca ||
viṣṇuḥ: “yas tūttamavarṇaṃ dāsye niyojayet sa uttamasāhasaṃ daṇḍyaḥ” | kātyāyanaḥ |
śūdraṃ tu kārayed dāsyaṃ krītam akrītam eva vā |
dāsyāyaiva hi sṛṣṭo ‘sau svayam eva svayaṃbhuvā ||
nāradaḥ |
bhāryā putraś ca dāsaś ca traya evādhanāḥ smṛtāḥ |
yat te samadhigacchanti yasyaite tasya tat phalam ||
abhyupetyāśrśrūṣāpadaṃ samāptam
vetanasyānapakriyā
atha vetanasyānapakriyā | nāradaḥ |
bhṛtyānāṃ vetanasyokto dānādānavidhikramaḥ |
vetanasyānapākarma tad vivādapadaṃ smṛtam ||
bhṛtyāya vetanaṃ dadyāt karmasvāmī yathākramam |
ādau madhyāvasāne ca karmaṇo yad viniścitam ||
bhṛtāv aniścitāyāṃ tu daśabhāgam avāpnuyāt |
lābhaṃ gobījasasyānāṃ vaṇiggopakṛṣīvalāḥ ||
gobījaṃ gosantatiḥ | gograhaṇaṃ paśūpalakṣaṇam | tathā ca yājñavalkyaḥ |
dāpyas tu daśamaṃ bhāgaṃ vāṇijyapaśusasyataḥ |
aniścitya bhṛtiṃ yas tu kārayet sa mahīkṣitā ||
deśaṃ kālaṃ ca yo ‘tīyāt lābhaṃ kuryāc ca yo ‘nyathā |
tatra tu svāminaś chando ‘dhikaṃ deyaṃ kṛte ‘dhike ||
yo yāvat kurute karma tāvat tasya tu vetanam |
ubhayor apy asādhyaṃ cet sādhyaṃ kuryād yathāśrutam ||
yaḥ punar aniścityaiva bhṛtiṃ kārayati sa rājñā daśamaṃ bhāgaṃ dāpayitavyaḥ | vāṇijyapaśusasyato vāṇiggopakṛṣīvalāḥ lābhagobījasasyānāṃ yathākramaṃ daśamaṃ bhāgaṃ labhante | paśūnāṃ santitim | yo deśakālocitaṃ karma kurvan “deśaṃ kālaṃ cātīyāt” atikrāmati | tasminn eva vā deśe kāle ca karma yathā kāraṇalābham apy anyathā kuryāt, hīnaṃ karoti, tatra tasmin bhṛtye svāminaḥ, chanda icchā bhṛtidānaṃ prati, yāvad icchati tāvad dadyāt, na tu sarvo bhṛtim | yadā tu deśakālādyabhijñatayādhiko lābhaḥ kṛtaḥ, tadādhikam api vetanaṃ deyam | yadā tv ekam eva karma niyatavetanaṃ ubhābhyāṃ bahubhir eva kriyamāṇaṃ “asādhyam” cet vyādhyādyabhibhavāt na parisamāptim, tadā tatkṛtakarmānusāreṇa | sādhye tu ubhābhyāṃ bahubhir vā karmaṇi nirvartite yathāśrutaṃ kuryāt | yāvat paribhāṣitaṃ tāvad evobhābhyāṃ bahubhyo vā dadyāt | na tu pratyekaṃ kṛtsnam | nāpi karmānurūpam iti | bṛhaspatiḥ |
tribhāgaṃ pañcabhāgaṃ vā gṛhṇīyāt sīravāhakaḥ |
bhaktādibhṛtakaḥ sīrāt bhāgaṃ gṛhṇīta pañcamam ||
jātasasyāt tribhāgaṃ tu pragṛhṇītopadhā bhṛtaḥ |
nāradaḥ |
karmopakaraṇaṃ teṣāṃ kriyāṃ prati yad arpitam |
āptabhāvena tad rakṣyaṃ na jihmena kadācana ||
vṛddhamanuḥ |
samudrayānakuśalā deśakālārthadarśinaḥ |
niyaccheyur bhṛtiṃ yā tu sā syāt prāg akṛtā yadi ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
bhṛtakas tu na kurvīta svāminaḥ śāṭhyam aṇv api |
bhṛtihānim avāpnoti tato vādaḥ pravartate ||
gṛhītavetanaḥ karma na karoti yadā bhṛtaḥ |
samarthaś ced damaṃ dāpyo dviguṇaṃ tac ca vetanam ||
dāpya iti śeṣaḥ | manuḥ |
yathoktam ārtaḥ svastho vā yas tat karma na kārayet |
na tasya vetanaṃ deyam alponasyāpi karmaṇaḥ ||
nāradaḥ |
karmākurvan pratiśrutya kāryo datvā bhṛtiṃ balāt |
bhṛtiṃ gṛhītvākurvāṇo dviguṇāṃ bhṛtim āvahet ||
manuḥ |
bhṛto nārto na kuryād yo darpāt karma yathoditam |
sa daṇḍyaḥ kṛṣṇalān aṣṭau na deyaṃ cāsya vetanam ||
nāradaḥ |
kāle ‘pūrṇe tyajan karma bhṛter nāśam avāpnuyāt |
svāmidoṣād apakrāman yāvat kṛtam avāpnuyāt ||
anayannādayitvā tu bhāṇḍaṃ vā yānavāhane |
dāpyo bhṛticaturbhāgaṃ sarvām ardhapathe tyajan ||
viṣṇuḥ: “bhṛtakaś cāpūrṇakāle karma tyajan sakalam eva mūlyaṃ jahyat | rājñe paṇaśataṃ dadyāt | svadoṣeṇa ca yadi naśyet tat svāmine deyam, anyatra daivarājopaghātāt | svāmī cet bhṛtakam apūrṇe kāle jahyāt, tasya sarvam eva mūlyaṃ dadyāt | paṇaśataṃ ca rājani anyatra bhṛtakadoṣāt” | yājñavalkyaḥ |
arājadaivikaṃ naṣṭaṃ bhāṇḍaṃ dāpyas tu vāhakaḥ |
prasthānavighnakṛc caiva pradāpyo dviguṇāṃ bhṛtim ||
prakrānte saptamaṃ bhagaṃ caturthaṃ pathi saṃtyajan |
bhṛtim ardhapathe sarvāṃ pradāpyas tyājako ‘pi ca ||
yadā prakrānte gamane prāk prasthānāt tyajati tadā bhṛtyā saḥa saptamaṃ bhāgaṃ dāpyaḥ | nirgatamātre yadi parityajati tadā bhṛtyā saha caturthaṃ bhāgam, ardhapathe sarvām | yadā punaḥ svāmī bhṛtakameṣu sthāneṣu tyajati tadānenāpi bhṛtakāya pūrvoktaṃ deyam | vṛddhamanuḥ |
pathi vikrīya tad bhāṇḍaṃ vaṇig bhṛtyaṃ tyajed yadi |
atha tasyāpi deyaṃ syāt bhṛter ardhaṃ labheta saḥ ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
yadā ca pathi tad bhāṇḍam āsiddhyeta hriyet vā |
yāvān adhvā gatas tena prāpnuyāt tāvato bhṛtim ||
tyajet pathi sahāyaṃ yaḥ śrāntaṃ rogārtham eva vā |
prāpnuyāt sāhasaṃ pūrvaṃ grāme tryaham apālayan ||
paṇyastrīṣu nārada āha |
śulkaṃ gṛhītvā paṇyastrī necchantī dviguṇaṃ vahet |
anicchañ chulkadātāpi śulkahānim avāpnuyāt ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
vyādhitā aśramā vyagrā rājakarmaparāyaṇā |
āmantritā ca nāgacched avācyā baḍavā smṛtā ||
nāradaḥ |
aprayacchaṃs tathā śulkam anubhūya pumān striyam |
akrameṇa samāgacchan ghātadantanakhādibhiḥ ||
akrameṇa niṣiddhamārgeṇa, ghātaḥ tāḍanam |
ayonau yaḥ samākrāmet bāhubhir vā visarjayet |
śulkaṃ so ‘ṣṭaguṇaṃ dāpyo vinayaṃ tāvad eva tu ||
veśyāpradhānā ye tatra kāmukās tadgṛhe sthitāḥ |
tatsamuttheṣu kāryeṣu nirṇayas taiḥ prakīrtitaḥ ||
parakṣetre gṛhaṃ kṛtvā vartamānān praty āha nāradaḥ |
parabhūmau gṛhaṃ kṛtvā stomaṃ datvā vaset tu yaḥ |
sa tad gṛhītvā nirgacchet tṛṇakāṣṭhakādikam ||
stomād vinābhyuṣitvā tu parabhūmāv anicchataḥ |
nirgacchaṃs tṛṇakāṣṭhāni na gṛhṇīyāt kathaṃcana ||
yāny eva tṛṇākāṣṭhāni iṣṭakā vā niveśitāḥ |
vinirgacchatsu tat sarvaṃ bhūmisvāmī nivedayet ||
stomaśabdena paragṛhānubhavārthaṃ tebhyo dattā dakṣiṇocyate | vyāsaḥ |
snehena suciraṃ labdhvā mandiraṃ kurute tu yaḥ |
nirgacchatas tasya dārū dattastomasya nānyathā ||
anyathā kṣetravataḥ | kātyāyanaḥ |
gṛhavāryāpaṇādīni gṛhītvā bhāṭakena yaḥ |
svāmino nārpayed yāvat tāvad dāpyaḥ sabhāṭakam ||
hastyaśvagokharoṣṭrādīn gṛhītvā bhāṭakena yaḥ |
nārpayet kṛtakṛtyaḥ san tāvad dāpyaḥ sabhāṭakam ||
bhāṭakaśabdena dakṣiṇocyate iti | nāradaḥ |
stomavāhīni bhāṇḍāni pūrṇakālāny upānayet |
gṛhītur ābhaved bhagnaṃ naṣṭaṃ cānyatra saṃplavāt ||
nauyāyināṃ śulkaniyamam āha manuḥ |
paṇaṃ yānaṃ tare dāpyaṃ pauruṣe ‘rdhapaṇaṃ tare |
pādaṃ paśuś ca yoṣic ca pādārdhaṃ riktakaḥ pumān ||
pūrṇabhāṇḍāni yānāni tāryaṃ dāpyāni sārataḥ |
riktabhāṇḍāni yat kiṃcit pumāṃsaś cāparicchadāḥ ||
dīrghe ‘dhvani yathādeśaṃ yathā kālaṃ tare bhavet |
nadītīre tu tad vidyāt samudre nāsti lakṣaṇam ||
garbhiṇī tu dvimāsādiḥ tathā pravrajito muniḥ |
brāhmaṇā liṅginaś caiva na dāpyās tārikaṃ tare ||
yat tad dhi kiṃcid dāśānāṃ viśīryetāparādhataḥ |
tad dāśair eva dātavyaṃ samāgamya svato ‘ṃśataḥ || iti |
rajakahaste naṣṭasya vastrasya mūlyadānakḷptim āha nāradaḥ |
mūlyāṣṭabhāgo hīyeta sakṛd dhautasya vāsasaḥ |
dviḥpādaṃ tris tṛtīyāṃśaḥ caturdhaute ‘rdham eva ca ||
ardhakṣayāt tu parataḥ pādāṃśopacayaḥ kramāt |
yāvat kṣīṇadaśaṃ jīrṇaṃ jīrṇasyāniyamakṣaye ||
aṣṭapaṇakrītasya sakṛd dhautasya vastrasya nāśitasyāṣṭabhāgonaṃ paṇaṃ nyūnamūlyaṃ deyam \ dvir dhautasya pādonaṃ trir dhautasya tṛtīyāṃśanyūnam | catur dhautasyārdham | paṇacatuṣṭayaṃ deyam | tataḥ paraṃ pratinirṇejanaṃ pādāpacayo deyaḥ | yāvaj jīrṇaṃ jīrṇasya nāśitasya icchāto mūlyakalpanam | yājñavalkyaḥ |
vasānas trīn paṇān dāpyaḥ rajakas tu parāṃśukam |
vikrayāvakrayādhānayāciteṣu paṇān daśa ||
rajako vastradhāvakaḥ | sa yāti nirṇejanārthaṃ samarpitāni vāsāṃsi svayaṃ yady ācchādayati tadā paṇatrayaṃ daṇḍyaḥ | yas tu tāni vikrīṇīte ‘vakrīnīte vā “tavaitāvantaṃ kālaṃ mayopabhogārthaṃ vastraṃ deyam mamāpi tvayā etāvad dhanaṃ deyam” ity evaṃ saṃvādya paṭādikaṃ dadāti ādhiṃ vā nayati, svasuhṛdbhyo vā yācitaṃ dadāti, asau daśapaṇaṃ daṇḍya iti |
iti vetanasyānapakriyā samāptā
svāmipālavivādaḥ
atha svāmipālavivādaḥ | tatra manuḥ |
paśuṣu svāmināṃ caiva pālānāṃ ca vyatikrame |
vivādaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi yathāvad dharmatattvataḥ ||
gopaḥ kṣīrabhṛto yas tu sa duhyāt daśato ‘parām |
gosvāmyanumato bhṛtyaḥ sā syāt pāle ‘bhṛte bhṛtiḥ ||
nāradaḥ |
gavāṃ śatād vatsatarī dhenuḥ syāt dviśatād bhṛtiḥ |
pratisaṃvatsaraṃ gope sandoho vāṣṭame ‘hani ||
gopasya bhṛtiḥ gośatād vatsatarī dvihāyanī bhṛtiḥ | dviśatād ekā dhenuḥ pratisaṃvatsaraṃ deyā, sarvasandoho vāṣṭame ‘hani |
upānayed gā gopāya pratyahaṃ rajanīkṣaye |
cīrṇāḥ pītāś ca tā gopaḥ sāyāhne pratyupānayet ||
divase divase prātaḥ svāmī gopasamīpaṃ nītvā arpaet | sa gopaḥ cīrṇāḥ vane vihṛtāḥ tṛptāḥ prītodakāḥ sāyāhne pratyānīya svāmine samarpayet | tathā ca yājñavalkyaḥ |
yathārpitān paśūn gopaḥ sāyaṃ pratyarpayet tathā |
pramādamṛtanaṣṭāṃś ca pradāpyaḥ kṛtavetanaḥ ||
nāradaḥ |
syāc cet govyasanaṃ gopo vyāyacchet tatra śaktitaḥ |
aśaktāv ābhiparyārthaṃ svāmine tan nivedayet ||
vyasanasaṃbhave bheṣajādinā yathāśakti vyāyacchet | aśaktau śīghraṃ gatvā svāmine brūyāt |
avyāyacchann avikrośan svāmine vānivedayan |
voḍhum arhati gopas taṃ vinayaṃ caiva rājataḥ ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
pāladoṣād vināśe tu pāladaṇḍo vidhīyate |
ardhatrayodaśapaṇaṃ svāmino dravyaṃ eva vā ||
vyāsaḥ |
pālagrahe grāmayāne tathā rāṣṭrasya viśrame |
yat praṇaṣṭaṃ hṛtaṃ vā syān na pālas tatra kilbiṣī ||
nāradaḥ |
ajāvike tu saṃruddhe vṛkaiḥ pāle tv anāyati |
yāṃ prasahya vṛko hanyāt pāle tat kilbiṣaṃ bhavet ||
yāṃ vyaktam | nāradaḥ |
anena sarvapālānāṃ vivādaḥ samudāhṛtaḥ |
nṛpeṣu hi viśuddhiḥ syāt vālaśṛṅgādidarśanāt ||
anena gopālavivādena mahiṣājāvyādipālānāṃ vivādo vyākhyātaḥ | manuḥ |
karṇau carma ca vālāṃś ca vastiṃ snāyu ca rocanām |
paśusvāmiṣu dadyāt sa mṛteṣv aṅgāni darśayan ||
atha gopracārabhūminiyamaḥ
atra viśeṣaṃ yājñavalkya āha |
grāmecchayā gopracāro bhūmī rājavaśena ca |
dvijas tṛṇaidhaḥ puṣpāṇi sarvataḥ svavad āharet ||
dhanuḥ śataṃ parīṇāho grāmakṣetrāntaraṃ bhavet |
dve śate kharvaṭasya syāt nagarasya caturguṇam ||
manuḥ |
tatrāparivṛtaṃ dhānyaṃ vihiṃsyuḥ paśavo yadi |
na tatra praṇayed daṇḍaṃ nṛpatiḥ paśurakṣiṇām ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
pathi grāmavivītānte kṣetre doṣo na vidyate |
vivītaḥ kulutthaniṣpāvakakārpāsārthā bhūmiḥ |
akāmataḥ kāmacārī coravad daṇḍam arhati ||
nāradaḥ |
utkramya tu vṛtiṃ yat syāt sasyaghāto gavādibhiḥ |
pālaḥ śāsyo bhavet tatra na cec chaktyā nivārayet ||
atra viśeṣam āha kātyāyanaḥ |
gobhir vināśitaṃ dhānyaṃ yo naraḥ pratiyācate |
pitaras tasya nāśnanti nāśnanti ca divaukasaḥ ||
āḍhyasyedam |
pathi kṣetre vṛtiḥ kāryā yām uṣṭro nāvalokayet |
na laṅghayet paśur voṣṭro na bhidyātāṃ śvasūkarau ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
vṛtiṃ ca tatra kurvīta yām uṣṭro nāvalokayet |
chidraṃ ca vārayet sarvaṃ śvasūkaramukhānugam ||
ajādiṣv eva sarveṣu kuryād āvaraṇaṃ mahat |
duḥkhena na hi vāryante labdhasvādurasā mṛgāḥ ||
gobhir vināśitaṃ dhānyaṃ sāmantair anumoditam |
kṣetriṇe gominā deyaṃ dhānyaṃ yatnena sādhyate ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
yāvat sasyaṃ vinaśyet tu tāvad deyaṃ ca gominā |
palālaṃ gomine deyaṃ dhānyaṃ tatkarṣakāya tu ||
kāryasaṃpratipattyā vā svāmikarṣakagominām |
nāradaḥ |
māṣaṃ gā dāpayed daṇḍaṃ dvau māṣau mahiṣīṃ tathā |
tathājāvikavatsānāṃ daṇḍaḥ syād ardhamāṣakaḥ ||
māṣaśabdena mānamātram ucyate | idam ajñānapūrvaṃ punaḥ prarohānuguṇabhakṣaṇaviṣayam | kātyāyanaḥ |
paṇapādau tu daṇḍyā gauḥ mahiṣy ekapaṇaṃ tathā |
tathājāvikavatsānāṃ pādo daṇḍaḥ prakīrtitaḥ ||
atyantanāśaviṣayam idam | yājñavalkyaḥ |
samam eṣāṃ vivīte ‘pi kharoṣṭramahiṣīsamam |
yāvat sasyaṃ vinaśyeta tāvat kṣetrī phalaṃ labhet ||
yā naṣṭā pāladoṣeṇa gaus tu sasyāni nāśayet |
na tatra gomino daṇḍaḥ pālas taddaṇḍam arhati ||
māṣān aṣṭau tu mahiṣī sasyaghātasya kāriṇī |
daṇḍanīyā tadardhaṃ tu gaus tadardham ajāvikam ||
idaṃ mahiṣyājāviṣayam | viṣṇuḥ: “divā rātriṣu muhūrtaṃ grāse māṣam, grāsamātre tv adaṇḍyā gauḥ mahiṣyādayaś ca sarveṣāṃ vai vatso māṣam | mahiṣī daśa, kharoṣṭraṃ ṣaḍajāvikaṃ tu caturaḥ | nāradaḥ |
sannānāṃ dviguṇo daṇḍo vātānāṃ tu caturguṇaḥ |
pratyakṣacorakāṇāṃtu coradaṇḍaḥ smṛto budhaiḥ ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
bhakṣayitvopaviṣṭānāṃ yathoktād dviguṇo damaḥ |
yāvat sasyaṃ vinaśyet tu yāvat syāt kṣetriṇaḥ phalam ||
pālas tāḍyas tu gomī tu pūrvoktaṃ daṇḍam arhati |
na tatra svāmino doṣaḥ pāle sati catuṣpadām ||
apāle svāmino doṣaḥ sasyaghāte smṛto budhaiḥ |
nāradaḥ |
rājagrāhagṛhīto vā vajrāśanihato ‘pi vā |
atha sarpeṇa daṣṭo vā śvabhre tu patito ‘pi vā ||
vyāghrādibhir hato vāpi vyādhibhir vāpy upadrutaḥ |
na tatra doṣaḥ pālasya na ca doṣo ‘sti gominām ||
adaṇḍyā hastino hy aśvāḥ prajāpālā hi te smṛtāḥ ||
uśanāḥ |
adaṇḍyāḥ kāṇakūṭāś ca vṛṣaś ca kṛtalakṣaṇaḥ |
adaṇḍyāgantukī gauś ca sūtikā cātisāriṇī |
adaṇḍyāś cotsave gāvaḥ śrāddhādiṣu ca karmasu ||
manuḥ |
anirdaśāhāṃ gāṃ sūtāṃ vṛṣaṃ devapaśuṃ tathā |
sapālān vā vipālān vā adaṇḍyān manur abravīt ||
nāradaḥ |
gauḥ prasūtā daśāhāt tu mahokṣo vājikuñjarau |
nivāryāḥ syuḥ prayatnena teṣāṃ svāmī na doṣabhāk ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
mahokṣotsṛṣṭapaśavaḥ sūtikāgantukādayaḥ |
pāyo yeṣāṃ ca te mocyā daivarājapariplutāḥ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
adhamottamamadhyānāṃ paśūnāṃ caiva tāḍane |
svāmī tu vavaded yatra tatra daṇḍaṃ prakalpayet ||
gavāṃ nirgacchatāṃ grāmāt kācit pārśve pramādataḥ |
grasen nirgatya sasyāni taddoṣaḥ svāmipālayoḥ ||
iti svāmipālavivādaḥ
samayānapākarma
atha samayānapākarma | tatra nāradaḥ |
pāṣaṇḍanaigamādīnāṃ sthitiḥ samaya ucyate |
samayasyānapākarma tad vivādapadaṃ smṛtam ||
bahubhiḥ sādhubhiḥ mahājanarakṣaṇārthaṃ kṛtā saṃvit samayaḥ | tasyānyathākaraṇam samayasyānapākarmocyate | bṛhaspatiḥ |
grāmaśreṇigaṇānāṃ ca saṅketaḥ samayakriyā |
bādhakārye tu sa kāryā dharmakārye tathaiva ca ||
dvau trayaḥ pañca vā kāryāḥ samūhahitavādinaḥ |
kartavyaṃ vacanaṃ teṣāṃ grāmaśreṇigaṇḍādibhiḥ ||
sabhāprapādevagṛhataṭākārāmasaṃskṛtiḥ |
tathānāthadaridrāṇāṃ saṃskāro yajanakriyā ||
kulāyananirodhaṃ ca kāryam asmābhir aṃśataḥ |
yatraital likhitaṃ patre dharmyā sā samayakriyā ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
yuktiyuktaṃ ca yo hanyāt vaktur yo ‘navakāśadaḥ |
ayuktaṃ caiva yo brūyāt sa daṇḍyaḥ pūrvasāhasam ||
avirodhena dharmasya nirgataṃ rājaśāsanam |
tasyaivācaraṇaṃ pūrvaṃ kartavyaṃ tu nṛpājñayā ||
rājapravartitān dharmān yo naro nānupālayet |
garhyaḥ sa daṇḍyo bandhyaś ca garhayan rājaśāsanam ||
samayavyatikrame daṇḍam āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
pālanīyā samarthais tu yaḥ samartho visaṃvadet |
sarvasvaharaṇaṃ daṇḍaḥ tasya nirvāsanaṃ purāt ||
tatra bhedam upekṣāṃ vā yaḥ kaścit kurute naraḥ |
catuḥsuvarṇaṃ ṣaṇṇikṣān tasya daṇḍo vidhīyate ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
gaṇadravyaṃ hared yad tu saṃvidaṃ laṅghayec ca yaḥ |
sarvasvaharaṇaṃ kṛtvā taṃ rāṣṭrād vipravāsayet ||
smṛtyantare |
śavakeśair vījyamānaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ hy uptaśekharam |
digvastraṃ gamayed rājā duṣṭaṃ samayalaṅghinam ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
yas tu sādhāraṇaṃ hiṃsyāt kṣipet traividyam eva vā |
sākṣikriyāṃ vihanyāc ca sa nirvāsyas tataḥ purāt ||
aruṃtudaḥ sūcakaś ca bhedakṛt sāhasī tathā |
śreṇipūganṛpadviṭ ca kṣipraṃ nirvāsyate tataḥ ||
kulaśreṇigaṇādhyakṣāḥ purarāṣṭranivāsinaḥ |
vāgdhikkarmaparityāgaṃ prakuryuḥ pāpakāriṇām ||
taiḥ kṛtaṃ yat svadharmeṇa nigrahānugrahaṃ nṛṇām |
tad rājñāpy anumantavyaṃ nisṛṣṭārthā hi te smṛtāḥ ||
bādhāṃ kuryur yad ekasya saṃbhūtā dveṣasaṃyutāḥ |
rājñā te vinivāryāḥ syuḥ śāsyāś caivānubandhinaḥ ||
mukhyaiḥ saha samūhānāṃ visaṃvādo yadā bhavet |
tadā vicārayed rājā svamārge sthāpayec ca tān ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
ekapātre ‘tha paṅktyāṃ vā saṃbhoktā yena yo bhavet |
akurvaṃs tat tathā daṇḍyaḥ tasya doṣam adarśayan ||
gaṇadravyaharaṇe daṇḍaḥ
atha gaṇadravyaharaṇe daṇḍaḥ | bṛhaspatiḥ |
saṃbhūyaikamataṃ kṛtvā rājabhāvyaṃ haranti ye |
te tadaṣṭaguṇaṃ dāpyā vaṇijaś ca palāyinaḥ ||
samūhakāryasiddhyarthaṃ rājādīnāṃ ca darśane |
tato labheta yat kiṃcit sarveṣām eva tatsamam ||
ṣaṇmāsaṃ māsikaṃ vāpi vibhaktavyaṃ yathāṃśataḥ |
deyaṃ vā niḥsvavṛddhārtastrībālāturarogiṣu ||
sāntānikādiṣu tathā darma eṣa sanātanaḥ |
yat taiḥ prāptaṃ rakṣitaṃ vā gaṇārthe vā ṛṇaṃ kṛtam |
rājaprasādalabdhaṃ ca sarveṣām eva tatsamam ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
samūhakāryaprahito yal labheta tad arpayet |
ekādaśaguṇaṃ dāpyo yady asau nārpayet svayam ||
samūhakārya āyātān kṛtakāryān visarjayet |
sadānamānasatkāraiḥ pūjayitvā mahīpatiḥ ||
tato labheta yat kiṃcit sarveṣām eva tatsamam ||
mahīpatigrahaṇam amātyānām apy upalakṣaṇam | samūhākāraṇanagarādikaṃ praviśya tadākāravicchede sati prātisvikalabdham api sādhāraṇam avibhaktārjitadhanavad iti kecit | tad ayuktam | samūhāvināśe ‘pi dṛṣṭakāraṇābhāve ca labdhaṃ prātisvikam eva |
[samayānapakriyā samāptā]
vikrīyāsaṃpradānam
atha vikrīyāsaṃpradānākhyaṃ vivādapadam ucyate | tatra nāradaḥ |
vikrīya paṇyaṃ mūlyena kretur yan na pradīyate |
vikrīyāsaṃpradānaṃ tat vivādapadam ucyate ||
loke ‘smin dvividhaṃ dravyaṃ sthāvaraṃ jaṅgamaṃ tathā |
krayavikrayadharmeṣu sarvaṃ tat paṇyam ucyate ||
ṣaḍvidhas tasya tu budhaiḥ dānādānavidhiḥ smṛtaḥ |
gaṇimaṃ tulimaṃ meyaṃ kriyayā rūpataḥ sriyā ||
ṣaḍvidham eva gaṇimam, saṃkhyeyaṃ kramukaphalādi | tulimaṃ tulayā dhāryam, hiraṇyacandanakuṃkumādi | meyaṃ prasthādibhiḥ vrīhyādi | kriyayā karṣaṇādinā balīvardādi | rūpataḥ khaḍgādiḥ | śriyā maṇyādiḥ, tasya paṇyasya dānādānagrahaṇavidhir evaṃ ṣaḍvidhaḥ \
vikrīya paṇyaṃ mūlyena yaḥ kretur na prayacchati |
sthāvarasya kṣayaṃ dāpyo jaṅgamasya kriyāphalam ||
mūlyaṃ gṛhītvā kretur yaḥ paṇyaṃ na dadāti tāvat kālaṃ krītavastrādeḥ bhujyamānasya yaḥ kṣayaḥ tāvad dāpyaḥ | kṣayasya dātum aśakyatvāt tadanuguṇaṃ dravyaṃ dāpyaḥ | jaṅgamasya dvipadāṃ catuṣpadāṃ ca tatkarmanimittaṃ mūlyaṃ dāpyaḥ | tac ca mūlyam,
arghāc ced avahīyeta sodayaṃ paṇyam āvahet |
sthāninām eṣa niyamo diglābho digvicāriṇām ||
vikrītapaṇyāpradāne dravyaṃ tatkālārghād apahīyet | krayakālenārgheṇa dānakāle bhāvyavṛddhiyuktaṃ paṇyaṃ dāpyaḥ | digvicāriṇā kālātikrame ‘rghahānau taddhīnaṃ dravyaṃ diglābhena saha dāpyaḥ |
upahanyeta vā paṇyaṃ dahyetāpahriyeta vā |
sa kretur eva so ‘nartho vikrīyāsaṃprayacchataḥ ||
anyac ca |
nirdoṣaṃ darśayitvā tu yaḥ sa doṣaṃ prayacchati |
mūlyaṃ tu dviguṇaṃ dāpyo vinayaṃ tāvad eva ca ||
mūlyagrahaṇakāle nirdoṣaṃ darśayitvā sadoṣaṃ paṇyaṃ prayacchan vikretā kretus tanmūlyadviguṇaṃ dāpyaḥ | tāvan mūlyaṃ daṇḍyaś ca | tathā,
anyahasteṣu vikrīya yo ‘nyahaste prayacchati |
so ‘pi taddviguṇaṃ dāpyo vinayaṃ tāvad eva ca ||
dīyamānaṃ na gṛhṇāti paṇyaṃ krītaṃ hi yaḥ krayī |
vikrīṇānas tadanyatra vikretā nāparādhnuyāt ||
dattamūlyasya paṇyasya vidhir eṣaḥ prakīrtitaḥ |
adattamūlye vikrīte na vikretur atikramaḥ ||
pūrvoktam upapādayati |
lābhārthe vaṇijāṃ sarvapaṇyeṣu krayavikrayaḥ |
sa ca lābho ‘rgham āsādya mahān bhavati vā na vā ||
lābhaparimāṇaṃ yājñavalkya āha |
svadeśapaṇye tu vaṇik śataṃ gṛhṇīta pañcakam |
paradeśasya daśakaṃ yaḥ sadyaḥ krayavikrayī ||
tenānyena krayasaṃbhāṣaṇe kṛte ‘py adatte mūlye lābhe saty anena vikrayaḥ kartavyaḥ |
nāradaḥ |
tasmād deśe ca kāle ca vaṇig arthaṃ prakalpayet |
na jihmena pravarteta śreyān eva vaṇikpathaḥ ||
yasmāc chreyān vaṇikpathas tasmāt jihmena na pravartetey arthaḥ | yājñavalkyaḥ |
vikrītam api vikreyaṃ pūrvakretary agṛhṇati |
hāniś cet kretṛdoṣeṇa kretur eva hi sā bhavet ||
daivarājopaghātena paṇye doṣam upāgate |
hānir vikretur evāsau yācitasyāprayacchataḥ ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
krītvā prāptaṃ na gṛhṇīyāt yo na dadyād adūṣitam |
sa mūlyād daśamaṃ bhāgaṃ datvā svaṃ dravyam āpnuyāt ||
aprāpte ‘rthe kriyākāle kṛte naiva pradāpayet |
evaṃ dharmo daśāhāt tu parato ‘nuśayo na tu ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
jñātvā sadoṣaṃ yat paṇyaṃ vikrīṇīte ‘vicakṣaṇaḥ |
tad eva dviguṇaṃ dāpyaṃ tatsamaṃ vinayaṃ tathā ||
vyāsaḥ |
satyaṃkāraṃ tayor datvā yathākālena darśayet |
paṇyaṃ bhaven nisṛṣṭaṃ tat dīyamānam agṛhṇataḥ ||
kṣetraviṣaye viśeṣam āha bṛhanmanuḥ |
adatte kṣetramūlyāṃśe yadi krītaṃ vinaśyati |
rājñā vā hriyate mūlaśeśe dadyān na tu krayī ||
vṛddhayājñavalkyaḥ |
adatte krayamūlyāṃśe śeṣadānaṃ vidhīyate |
krayasya vinivṛttir vā yāvad dattaṃ krayo ‘pi vā ||
saṃvādakāle dānaṃ syād ūrdhvaṃ krayanivartanam |
vikretur draviṇābhāve yāvad dattaṃ krayo bhavet ||
krayadravyāvaśeṣaṃ cet vṛddhaṃ kretrā na dīyate |
tatkālocitamūlyena vikrītaṃ punar āpnuyāt ||
iti vikrīyāsaṃpradānaṃ nāmāṣṭamaṃ vivādapadam
krītvānuśayaḥ
atha krītvānuśayaḥ | tatra nāradaḥ |
krītvā mūlyena yat paṇyaṃ kretā na bahumanyate |
krītvānuśaya ity etat vivādapadam ucyate ||
tatrāvikreyāṇy āha manuḥ |
nānyad anyena saṃsṛṣṭaṃ rūpaṃ vikrayam arhati |
na sāvadyaṃ na vā nyūnaṃ na dūreṇa tirohitam ||
tathānyatra |
sādhāraṇaṃ tu yat kṣetraṃ naiko dadyān narādhamaḥ |
nādadyān na ca gṛhṇīyāt vikrīnīyān na caiva hi ||
prasaṅgāt vikreyāṇy aśeṣāṇy apy āha manuḥ |
ādau nidhiḥ sadā proktā nikṣepas tadanantaram |
jalapāṣāṇakau paścād akṣiṇyāgāminau tathā ||
siddhāḥ sādhyāś ca vikreyāḥ cāṣṭabogāḥ prakīrtitāḥ |
bhujyata iti bhogāḥ, bhogārhā ity arthaḥ | te cāṣṭāv udāhṛtā draṣṭavyā ity arthaḥ | teṣāṃ svarūpaṃ ca bṛhaspatir āha |
nidhiḥ surālayaḥ prokto nikṣepo dravyabhājanam |
jalaṃ vāpītaḍākādiḥ pāṣāṇāḥ khanijādayaḥ ||
akṣiṇyaḥ parvatāś caiva saritaś ca sarāṃsi ca |
āgāmino bhāvinaḥ syuḥ gṛhārāmavanādayaḥ ||
siddhāḥ kḷptāḥ svabhogāya pitṛpaitāmahādibhiḥ |
svārjitāś ca tathā sādhyāḥ sādhanārhāḥ prayatnataḥ ||
evaṃ bhogāḥ suvijñeyāḥ sāmānyena manīṣibhiḥ |
prayoktavyāś ca sarvatra dānādhamanavikraye || iti |
krītanivṛttir nārada āha |
krītvā mūlyena yaḥ paṇyaṃ duṣkrītaṃ manyate krayī |
vikretuḥ pratideyaṃ tat tasminn evāhny avikṣatam ||
dvitīye ‘hni dadat kretā mūlyāt triṃśāṃśam āharet |
dviguṇaṃ tat tṛtīye ‘hni parataḥ kretur eva tat ||
etat tad dravyānuśayakālottaraṃ draṣṭavyam | bṛhaspatiḥ |
mattonmattena vikrītaṃ hīnamūlyaṃ bhayena vā |
asvatantreṇa mūḍhena tyājyaṃ tasya punar bhavet ||
nivṛttikālaṃ vyāsa āha |
carmakāṣṭheṣṭakāsūtradhānyāsavarasasya ca |
vastrarūpyahiraṇyānāṃ sadya eva parīkṣaṇam ||
sadyaḥ taddivasa evety arthaḥ |
hemarūpyāmbararasā parīkṣyā divasena tu |
muktāvajrapravālānāṃ saptāhaṃ syāt parīkṣaṇam ||
dvipadām ardhamāsaṃ tu puṃsāṃ tat dviguṇaṃ striyāḥ |
daśāhaṃ sarvabījānām ekāhaṃ lohavāsasām ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
daśaikapañcasaptāhaṃ māsatryahārdhamāsikam |
bījāyovāhyaratnastrīdohyapuṃsāṃ parīkṣaṇam ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
aśvarūpyahiraṇyānāṃ dhānyalohavāsasām |
carmakāṣṭhavikārāṇām ekāhaṃ syāt parīkṣaṇam ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
krītvā nānuśayaṃ kuryāt vaṇik paṇyavicakṣaṇaḥ |
kṣayaṃ vṛddhiṃ ca jānānaḥ paṇyānāṃ yasya yādṛśam ||
prīkṣeta svayaṃ paṇyam anyeṣāṃ ca pradarśayet |
parīkṣitaṃ bahumataṃ gṛhītvā na punas tyajet ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
kṣayaṃ vṛddhiṃ ca vaṇijā paṇyānāṃ ca vijānatā |
krītvā nānuśayaḥ kāryaḥ kurvan ṣaḍbhāgadaṇḍabhāk ||
nāradaḥ |
paribhuktaṃ tu yad vāsaḥ parikliṣṭaṃ malīmasam |
sadoṣaṃ api vikrītaṃ vikretur na bhavet punaḥ ||
ataḥ parīkṣya pravṛtteṣu jñātadoṣeṣu na parāvṛttiḥ | prasaṅgāt vastrāṇām upabhogavaśāt mūlyasyālpatvaṃ mahattvaṃ cāha nāradaḥ |
mūlyāṣṭabhāgo hīyeta sakṛd dhautasya vāsasaḥ |
dvir dvipādas tris tripādaś caturdhaute ‘rdham eva ca ||
ardhakṣayāt tu parataḥ pādāṃśāpacayaḥ kramāt |
yāvat kṣīṇadaśaṃ jīrṇaṃ ṣaṣṭhasyāniyamaḥ kṣaye ||
dvis trir ity atra pādaśabdo ‘rdhapādābhiprāyaḥ | tataḥ sakṛd dhautasya nāśe ‘rdhapādahāniḥ | dvir dhautasya pādahāniḥ | trir dhautasyārdhapādahāniḥ | catur dhautasya mūlyaṃ pañcame sāṣṭabhāgam ardhaṃ hīyeta | ṣaṣṭhe tv icchāto niyamo na śāstrataḥ | evaṃ paṭāvayavanāśāt paṭanāśakrameṇa mūlyāṃśahāniḥ | dvīpāntaragatacandanakuṃkumādivāṇijyaviṣaye kātyāyanaḥ |
dravyaṃ svaṃ pañcadhā kṛtvā tribhāgo mūlyam ucyate |
lābhaś caturtho bhāgaḥ syāt pañcamo ‘satya ucyate ||
asminn eva viṣaye bṛhaspatiḥ |
tadarhadravyapañcāṃśatryaṃśaṃ na kramaśo vinā |
tribhiḥ siddhiḥ krayo dāpyaḥ caturtho ‘ṃśo na pañcamaḥ ||
vacanadvayam idaṃ dvīpādāgatakuṃkumādivāṇijyaviṣayam | ata eva caturtho bhāgo lābhaḥ pañcamo bhāgo ‘satya ucyate | atra vyāsaḥ |
tatrāpy asadṛśair anyaiḥ hīnamūlyair vimiśritaiḥ |
kurvanty aupādhikaṃ cānye paṇyānāṃ parivartanam ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
saṃvibhāge vinimaye kṣetrayor ubhayor api |
anusmṛtikṛtā tābhyāṃ kāryasiddhir bhaviṣyati ||
maṇībhāśvāśvatarīṇām āgamair mūlyakalpanā |
nṛpājñayāpaṇasthānāṃ gobhūmyor ubhayecchayā ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
na sāmantair na saṃgrāmais tasya mūlyaṃ niyamyate |
idam asyeti yat kretā vikretrā saha saṃvadet ||
etad eva vikreyasya kṣetramūlyam iti | atra viśeṣam āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
yaḥ kaścid vañcakas teṣāṃ vijñātaḥ krayavikraye |
śapathaiḥ sa viśodhyaḥ syāt sarvavādeṣv ayaṃ vidhiḥ ||
krayavikrayaparivṛttisvarūpaṃ parivartanāsvarūpaṃ ca sa evāha |
ātmīyasya vijītīyaṃ [vijātīyaṃ?] dravyam ādāya cānyataḥ |
krayotthasya parityāgaḥ sāmye tu parivartanā ||
vyāsaḥ |
ātmīyasya vijātīyaṃ dravyam ādāya cānyataḥ |
krayo mūlyasya saṃtyāgaḥ svatvahetuḥ parasparam ||
parivṛttiḥ sajātīyadravye vinimayaḥ smṛtaḥ |
vaiṣamye vikrayaḥ prokto miśre vinimayaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
