svatvahetuphalajanakā ete krayavikrayaparivartanavinimayā iti | tatra loke jihāsitaṃ suvarṇādimūlyam ity ucyate | upāditsitaṃ kṣetragṛhādipaṇyam ity ucyate | tatra mūlyatyāgapūrvakapaṇyasvīkāraḥ krayaḥ | paṇyatyāgapūrvako mūlye svatvajanako mūlyasvīkāro vikrayaḥ | sajātīyatyāgapūrvakaḥ sajātīyasya svīkāraḥ parivartanā | vaiṣamye sati parivartanaivāvakrayaśabdenocyate | vijātīyasajātīyamiśraparivartanāyāṃ vijātīyādhikye ‘vakrayo bhavati, sajātīyādhikye parivartanā bhavati | sajātīyavijātīyayoḥ sāmye vinimayo bhavati | paṇyamūlyayor mithaḥ svīkāre vinimayaḥ koṭyantaram ity apare | atha krayādisiddhim āha kātyāyanaḥ |
ardhādhike krayaḥ siddhyet dadyād dhīnaṃ krayī dhanam |
ā daśāhān nivartyaḥ syāt krayo vikraya eva ca ||
ardhadāne nyūnadāne ca krayavikrayau nivartete iti |
saṃdhiś ca parivṛttiś ca vibhāgaś ca samo yati |
ā daśāhān nivaryāḥ syuḥ vaiṣamye navavatsarāt ||
sāmyapakṣe sāmānyaprāptadaśāhān nivṛttiḥ | nordhvam | vaiṣamye sati navavatsarān nivṛttir bhavati | vṛddhakātyāyanaḥ |
saṃdhiś ca parivṛttiś ca viṣamā vā tribhāgaśaḥ |
ājñayā virayaś cāpi nivartyāḥ parikīrtitāḥ ||
uktālābhakrayaś cāpi daśābdaṃ vinivartate |
jaṅgameṣu dvādaśābdam uktālābhaḥ paraṃ smṛtam ||
adhādhikena dravyeṇa miśritā parivartanā |
kraya eva bhaved ūnā viparītā samāv ubhau ||
tatra koṭyantaraṃ vidyāt mṛgarūpamanuṣyavat |
triprakāraparivartanāyāṃ na jñātyādipraveśaḥ, śuddhakrayaviṣayatvāt jñātyādipraveśasya apekṣitavyaktisaṃpādanāyogāc ca | bṛhaspatiḥ |
vikrayeṣu tu sarveṣu kūpavṛkṣādi lekhayet |
jalamārgādi yat kiṃcid anyac caiva bṛhaspatiḥ ||
kṣīrād apetaṃ paripakvasasyaṃ vṛkṣaṃ phalaṃ vāpy upabhogayogyam |
kūpaṃ taḍākaṃ ca gṛhaṃ samunnataṃ krīte ‘pi vikretur idaṃ vadanti ||
eṣv alikhiteṣu kretur na bhavantīty arthaḥ | kātyāyanaḥ |
ardhādhike krayaḥ siddhyed uktālābhe daśābdikaḥ |
avakrayas tribhogena sadya eva ṛṇakriyā ||
atra avakrayaśabdena parivartanocyate |
siddhyate vāciko ‘py ādhiḥ sthāvareṣu daśābdikaḥ |
jaṅgameṣu dvādaśābdād uktālābho ‘pi siddhyati ||
athājñākrayaḥ
tatra yājñavalkyaḥ (kātyāyanaḥ) |
palāyite tu karade karapratibhuvā saha |
karārthaṃ karadakṣetraṃ vikrīṇīyuḥ sabhāsadaḥ ||
prajāpatiḥ |
mūlyasya pādam ardhaṃ vā mūlyam ājñākraye smṛtam |
mūlyaṃ tad dattam akhilaṃ datvā svaṃ kṣetram āpnuyāt ||
ā tribhogāt tataḥ kretuḥ parato dṛḍhatām iyāt |
nāradaḥ |
vikrīte tv ājñayā kṣetre kṣetriṇo ‘nuśayo yadi |
datvā samastaṃ tan mūlyaṃ ā tribhogāt samāpnuyāt ||
navasaṃvatsarād ūrdhvaṃ na labhetety arthaḥ | bhāradvājaḥ |
ājñādhis tatkrayaś caiva karo daṇḍe vidhīyate |
ubhāv anyatra na syātām iti dharmavido viduḥ ||
uktālābhakrayam āha katyāyanaḥ |
pratidāne vyavasthāpya kālaṃ pūrvaṃ savṛddhikam |
asyedaṃ krayyam ity uktvā yat tu nirdiśyate purā ||
dhanikasya tadā tat svam uktālābhe bhaviṣyati |
bharadvājaḥ |
kiṃcic ca dravyam ādāya kāle dāsyāmi te kvacit |
no cen mūlyam idaṃ tasya kedārasyeti yaḥ krayaḥ ||
sa uktālābha ity uktaḥ uktakāle tv anarpaṇāt |
uktālābhāt saṃgṛhītā ca bhūmiḥ uktālābhāt dvādaśābdopabhuktā |
svatvaṃ gacched āgatena krameṇa tulyaṃ sadbhis tad bhavet saṃmatena ||
tribhogenājñayā krīta uktālābho daśābdikaḥ |
avakrayas tribhāgena sadyaḥ siddhyaty ṛṇakrayaḥ ||
atra kṣetraviṣaye anucitamūlyam āha kātyāyanaḥ |
samavetais tu sāmantaiḥ abhijñaiḥ pāpabhīrubhiḥ |
kṣetrārāmagṛhādīnāṃ dvipadāṃ ca catuṣpadām ||
kalpitaṃ mūlyam ity āhuḥ bhāgaṃ kṛtvā tad aṣṭadhā |
ekabhāgātiriktaṃ vā nyūnaṃ vānucitaṃ smṛtam ||
samāśate vyatīte ‘pi sarvaṃ tad vinivartate |
vyāsaḥ |
krayavikrayaṇe krayyaṃ yan mūlyaṃ dharmato ‘rhati |
tat turye pañcame ṣaṣṭhe saptame ‘ṃśe ‘ṣṭame ‘pi vā ||
hīne yadi vinivṛtte krayavikrayaṇe sati |
hīnamūlyaṃ tu tat sarvaṃ kṛtam apy akṛtaṃ bhavet |\
dharmadroṇe paṭhitaṃ vacanam |
aṣṭamāṃśadhikanyūnān mūlyāt sāmantakalpitāt |
tasyaivānucitatvena krayasyānuśayo dvayoḥ ||
uktād alpatare hīne krayo naiva praduṣyati |
kenāpy aṃśena hīyeta mūlyataḥ krayavikraye ||
kṛtam apy akṛtaṃ prāhur anye dharmavido janāḥ |
bṛhaspatiḥ |
mūlyāt pādādhikaṃ nyūnaṃ mūlyasyānucitaṃ smṛtam |
krayasiddhis tu naiva syāt vatsarāṇāṃ śatair api ||
krayo yady aprakāśaḥ syāt hīne mūlye ‘dhike ‘pi vā |
tasyaivānucitatvaṃ syāt daṇḍyau tatkarmakāriṇau ||
tathā smṛtyantare |
aṣṭamāṃśādhikān nyūnān mūlyāt sāmantakalpitāt
krayasiddhis tu naiva syāt vatsarāṇāṃ śatair api ||
catuḥsāmantasāṃnidhye prācī digbalavattarā |
udīcī ca pratīcī ca sarvābhāve tu dakṣiṇā ||
samānasalilāḥ paścāt saṃsaktāś ca tataḥ param |
tato ‘pi bāndhavāḥ paścāt tatsaṃsaktās tataḥ param ||
na cet tad vyavadhīyeta nadīsrotaḥ pathādibhiḥ |
sāmantais tu sadā grāhyaṃ vyavadhau sodakādibhiḥ ||
mārgādyavyavadhānaṃ cet sāmantais tadanantaraiḥ |
pūrvakrameṇa gṛhyeta pūrvoktānām asaṃbhave ||
bhāradvājaḥ |
jñātisāmantadhanikāḥ kramād bhūmiparigrahāḥ |
tataḥ sakulyāḥ sarveṣām abhāve tv anyajātayaḥ ||
anyajātayaḥ bāndhavāḥ | bṛhaspatiḥ |
sodarāś ca sapiṇḍāś ca sodakāś ca sagotriṇaḥ |
sāmantā dhanikā grāmyāḥ saptaite bhūkraye matāḥ ||
vyāsaḥ |
bhūmer daśāho ‘nuśayaḥ kretur vikretur eva ca |
dvādaśāhaḥ sapiṇḍānām avicālyam ataḥ param ||
tātkālikās tu sāmantāḥ tātkālā dhanikāḥ smṛtāḥ |
tātkālikāḥ sapiṇḍāś ca vedanīyāḥ kraye matāḥ ||
pañcādhyāyyām |
jñātisāmantadhanikāḥ jñāte tātkālikāḥ smṛtāḥ |
daśāhādyās tu te sarve kretur vikretur eva ca ||
jñātyādigāmī tat kṣetraṃ vikretur mūlyakārpaṇāt |
anyatra |
kraye yogyā nigaditā vikraye purataḥ sthitāḥ |
nārhanti te pratikroṣṭuṃ saṃkrāntās tad aho ‘ntare ||
jñātisāmantadhanikā avagatyantaram evāpekṣām upekṣāṃ vā kuryuḥ | nāharantare | kretur vikretuś ca daśāho ‘nuśayaḥ kālaḥ iti |
jñātyādipratyayenaiva sthāvarakraya iṣyate |
anyathā cet krayo na syāt daṇḍaś cāpi tayor bhavet ||
jñātyādīnāṃ saṃnidhāne idaṃ vacanam |
jñātisāmantadhanikāḥ kraye grāmād bahirgatāḥ |
nārhanti te pratikroṣṭuṃ krāntaṃ pakṣatraye krayāt ||
asaṃnidhāna idaṃ vacanāt | kātyāyanaḥ |
svagrāme daśarātraṃ syād anyagrāme tripakṣakam |
rāṣṭrāntare tu ṣaṇmāsaṃ bhāṣābhede tu vatsaram ||
prajāpatiḥ |
jñārisāmantadhanikān anujñāpya samīpagān |
krayavikrayaṇe kuryāt śrāvayitvā ca sākṣiṇaḥ ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
jñātyādīn ananujñāpya samīpasthān aninditān (?) |
krayavikrayadharmo ‘pi bhūmer nāstīti niścayaḥ ||
prajāpatiḥ |
jñātyādīn ananujñāpya samīpasthān aninditān |
krayavikramakartārau tatsamaṃ daṇḍam arhataḥ ||
samīpasthān ity ekagrāmavāsināṃ grahaṇam | bṛhaspatiḥ |
praṣṭavyāḥ saṃnidhisthāś cet kretrā jñātyādayas tathā |
anyathā cet kṛtaṃ karma jñātvecchāṃ darśayet tataḥ ||
tripakṣād atha vā māsatritayāt tu tad āpnuyāt |
bharadvājaḥ |
anyagrāmotamarṇebhyo neṣyate bhūmivikrayaḥ |
svagrāmiṇāṃ dhanābhāve ṛṇe tūbhayam iṣyate ||
atra manuḥ |
krītvā vikrīya vā kiṃcid yasyehānuśayo bhavet |
so ‘ntardaśāhāt tad dravyaṃ dadyāc caivādadīta ca ||
tad dravyaṃ iti svam eva gṛhyate | daivarājakṛte nāśe tatsajātīyam api deyam iti gamyate | asmin śloke pāṭhāntaraṃ varṇayatā bhāṣyakāreṇāyam artha uktaḥ | dīyamānaṃ mūlyaṃ grahaṇe yogyaṃ yadā na gṛhṇāti yadi, tadā madhyasthasthāpitaṃ kartavyam | tathā ca vyāsaḥ,
parārthaṃ ditsitaṃ dravyaṃ tathaivādhikrayādiṣu |
madhyasthasthāpitaṃ kāryam anyathā na prasiddhyati ||
ādhyādiṣv api pūrvoktaṃ daśāham iti darśayati | manuḥ |
yasmin yasmin kṛte kārye yasyehānuśayo bhavet |
tam anena vidhānena dharmye pathi niyojayet ||
tathā ca smṛtyantare |
daśāhe samatikrānta ādhiḥ siddhyet sthirīkṛtaḥ |
krītaṃ kṣetrādikaṃ caiva svīkārasya prakāśanāt ||
atra viśeṣam āha kātyāyanaḥ |
jñātyādipratyayenaiva sthāvarakraya iṣyate |
parivṛttau kṛṣau dāne tathāsau neṣyate budhaiḥ ||
bhūmer daśāho ‘nuśayaḥ kretur vikretur eva ca |
dvādaśāhaṃ sapiṇḍānām avicālyam ataḥ param ||
bharadvājaḥ |
pūrvāhṇe ‘bhyudite sūrye grāmamadhye catuṣpathe |
brāhmaṇān śrāvayed vidvān jñātisāmantasaṃnidhau ||
mūlyaṃ yathāvad ādāya kraye sarvaṃ tu darśayet |
atra śuddhakraye kretur vikretuś ca daśāho ‘nuśayakālaḥ | uktālābhakraye ājñākraye ca kretuḥ daśāho ‘nuśayaḥ kālaḥ | svāminis tūktālābhe kraye sthāvare daśavatsaram anuśayaḥ kālaḥ | jaṅgame dvādaśavatsaraṃ anuśayaḥ kālaḥ | ājñākraye svāmino navatsaram anuśayakālaḥ | ājñākraye uktālābhakraye ca jñātyādīnām anavakāśaḥ nānupraveśaḥ | mūlyasaṃtyāgāt dānasya vikrayaniṣedhe parihārārthatvāt svagre (tve) jñātyādivacanāvirodhāya dānarūpe vikraye jñātyādīnām api avyāhato ‘vakāśaḥ | tatroktālābhe vikretuḥ tātkālikavyāpārābhāve ‘pi pūrvasaṃkalpād eva svatvanivṛttiḥ parasvatvāpādanaṃ ca uttarakālaṃ siddhyatīti krayatvam aviruddham | “ardhādhike krayaḥ siddhyet” iti vacanaṃ na sarvakrayaviṣayam, kiṃ tu anucitamūlyakrayaviṣayaṃ kātyāyanavacanāt |
mūlyasyālpapradāne ‘pi krayasiddhiḥ kṛtā bhavet |
cakravṛddhyā pradātavyaṃ śiṣṭaṃ tatsamayād ṛte ||
etāvatā kālena dāsyāmīti samayād ṛte |
yācyamānam adattaṃ cec cakravṛddhyā pravardhate ||
samayākaraṇe tasmin kāle pūrvadattaśeṣaṃ deyam | tasminn api kāle,
yācyamānam adattaṃ cec cakravṛddhyā pravardhate |
tatrāpi vṛddhiniyamam āha kātyāyanaḥ |
nikṣepaṃ vṛddhiśeṣaṃ ca krayaṃ vikrayam eva ca |
yācyamānam adattaṃ cet vardhate pañcakaṃ śatam ||
smṛtyantare |
krayadravyaṃ paradravyaṃ prasahyāpahṛtam ca yat |
cakravṛddhyā vivardheta yāvat pañcaguṇaṃ bhavet ||
śiṣṭamūlyasya viṃśatibhāgo māsi māsi yāvat pañcaguṇaṃ vardhata ity arthaḥ | tena svalpadravyapradāne ‘pi krayasiddhir asty eva | tathā ca smṛtyantaram |
māṣamātram api dravyaṃ kretur vikretari sthitam |
vyāpnoti sakalāṃ bhūmiṃ kāyam alpaviṣaṃ yathā ||
ardhadattasyādattasya ca avakrayatvavacanāt kathaṃ svalpadāne krayasiddhiḥ, ucyate | apakrayo ‘pi yadi saṃkalpitakāle mūlyaṃ dīyate | anyathā na siddhyati,
ardhadattam adattaṃ tu krayam āhur apakrayam |
apakrayo nivartera yadi kāle na dīyate ||
iti vacanāt |
kṣetrādikaṃ yathā bhuktam utpannam adhikaṃ tataḥ |
mūlyodayapraviṣṭaṃ cet tadādhiṃ prāpnuyāt ṛṇī ||
paribhāṣya yadā kṣetraṃ pradadyād dhanike ṛṇī |
nanu ardhadattasyāvakratvaṃ ca kṛtam,
rājaprasādād alabdhaṃ ca sarveṣām eva tatsamam |
yājñavalkyaḥ |
samūhakāryaprahito yal labheta tad arpayet |
ekādaśaguṇaṃ dāpyo yady asau nārpayet svayam ||
samūhakārya āsaktān kṛtakāryān visarjayet |
sadānamānasatkāraiḥ pūjayitvā mahīpatiḥ ||
tato labheta yat kiṃcit sarveṣām eva tatsamam |
mahīpatigrahaṇam amātyānām apy upalakṣaṇam | samūhakārye nagarādikaṃ praviśya tadā kāryavicchede sati prātisvikalabdham api sādhāraṇam avibhaktārjitadhanavad iti kecit | tad ayuktam | samūhāvināśe ‘pi dṛṣṭakāraṇabhāve ‘pi ca labdhaṃ prātisvikam eva |
tvayaitac chāntalābhe ‘rthe moktavyam iti niścitam |
praviṣṭe sodaye dravye pradātavyaṃ tvayā mama ||
iti krītvānuśayaḥ
sīmāvivādaḥ
atha sīmāvivādanirṇayaḥ | yājñavalkyaḥ |
sīmāvivāde kṣetrasya sāmantāḥ sthavirādayaḥ |
gopāḥ sīmākṛṣāṇāṃ ca sarve tad vanagocarāḥ ||
nayeyur ete sīmānaṃ sthalāṅgāratuṣadrumaiḥ |
setuvalmīkanimnāsthicaityādyair upalakṣitām ||
grāmanirmāṇasamaye prakāśāprakāśāni sīmācihnāni kuryāt | bṛhaspatiḥ |
kūpavāpītaḍāgāni caityārāmasurālayāḥ |
sthalanimnanadīsrotaḥ śaragulmāśmarāśayaḥ ||
prakāśacihnāny etāni sīmāyāṃ kārayet sadā |
nihitāni tathānyāni yāni bhūmir na bhakṣayet ||
manuḥ |
sīmāvṛkṣāṃś ca kurvīta nyagrodhāśvatthakiṃśukān |
śālmalīsālatālāṃś ca kṣīriṇaś caiva pādapān ||
aśmano ‘sthīni govālān tuṣabhasmakapālikāḥ |
karīṣam iṣṭakāṅgāraśarkarāvālukāṃs tathā ||
yāni caivaṃprakārāṇi kālād bhūmir na bhakṣayet |
tāni saṃdhiṣu sīmāyām aprakāśāni kārayet ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
anaśvarāṇi dravyāṇi prakṛtyā yāni tāni tu |
prakṣipya kumbheṣv etāni sīmānteṣu nidhāpayet ||
tataḥ pogaṇḍabālānāṃ prayatnena pradarśayet |
vārdhake tu śuśūnāṃ te darśayeyus tathaiva ca ||
evaṃ paraṃparājñāne sīmābhrāntir na jāyate ||
manuḥ |
yadi saṃśaya eva syāt liṅgānām api darśane |
sākṣipratyaya eva syāt vivāde sīmni niścayaḥ ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
samantabhāvāt sāmantaḥ kuryāt kṣetravinirṇayam |
grāmasīmādiṣu tathā tadvan nagaradeśayoḥ ||
kṣetrarūpataṭākānāṃ kedārārāmayor api |
gṛhaprasādāvasathanṛpadevagṛheṣu ca ||
svārthasiddhyai praduṣṭeṣu sāmanteṣv arthagauravam |
tatsaṃsaktais tu kartavyam uddhāro nātra saṃśayaḥ ||
saṃsaktasaktaduṣṭeṣu tatsaṃsaktāḥ prakīrtitāḥ |
kartavyā na praduṣṭās tu rājñā dharmaṃ vijānatā ||
tyaktvā duṣṭāṃs tu sāmantān anyair maulādibhiḥ saha |
saṃmantrya kārayet sīmām evaṃ dharmavido viduḥ ||
nāradaḥ |
dhvajinī matsinī caiva naidhānī bhayavarjitā |
rājaśāsananītā ca sīmā pañcavidhā smṛtā ||
vṛkṣādīnāṃ prāṃśutvena dhvajatulyatvāt dhvajinī | matsinī salilavatī, matyādhārajalalakṣaṇatvān matsyaśabdasya | naidhānī nidhānatuṣāṅgāramatī, teṣāṃ nikhātatvena nidhānatulyatvāt | bhayavarjitā arthipratyarthiparasparasaṃpratipattinimittā | rājaśāsananītā jñātṛcihnābhāve rājecchā nirmitā | manuḥ |
sāmantānām abhāve tu maulānāṃ sīmasākṣiṇām |
imān apy anuyuñjīta puruṣān vanagocarān ||
vyādhāñ chākunikān gopān kaivartān mūlakhānakān |
vyālagrāhān uñchavṛttīn anyāṃś ca vanagocarān ||
atra bṛhaspatiḥ |
śapathaiḥ śāpitāḥ svaiḥ svair brūyuḥ sīmāviniścayam |
darśayeyur nidhānāni tat pramāṇam iti sthitiḥ ||
nāradaḥ |
naikaḥ samunnayet sīmāṃ naraḥ pratyayavān api |
gurutvād asya kāryasya kriyaiṣā bahuṣu sthitā ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
raktasragvasanāḥ sīmāṃ nayayuḥ kṣitidhāriṇaḥ |
raktamālyāmbaradharaḥ mṛdam ādāya mūrdhani ||
satyavrataḥ sopavāsaḥ sīmāṃ saṃdarśayen naraḥ |
nāradaḥ |
atha ced anṛtaṃ brūyuḥ sāmantās tu vinirṇaye |
sarve pṛthak pṛthag daṇḍyā rājñā madhyamasāhasam ||
tripakṣād daivarājakānarthadarśanena sākṣiṇām anṛtoktir jñāyate,
sīmāvāde tathā kośe pādasparśe tathaiva ca |
trikṣapakṣasaptāhāt devarājakam iṣyate ||
iti vacanāt | kātyāyanaḥ |
grāmo grāmasya sāmantaḥ kṣetraṃ kṣetrasya kīrtitam |
gṛhaṃ gṛhasya nirdiṣṭaṃ samantāt parirabhya ca ||
ye tatra pūrvasāmantāḥ paścād deśāntaraṃ gatāḥ |
tanmūlatvāt tu te maulā ṛṣibhiḥ saṃprakīrtitāḥ ||
cihnānāṃ sākṣiṇāṃ cābhāve rājñā sīmā kāryety āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
abhāve jñātṛcihnānāṃ rājā sīmnaḥ pravartakaḥ |
atha nadīpravartakam | bṛhaspatiḥ |
anyagrāmāt samāhṛtya dattānyasya yadā mahī |
mahānadyātha vā rājñā kathaṃ tatra vicāraṇā ||
nadyutsṛṣṭā rājadattā yasya tasyaiva sā mahī |
anyathā na bhavel lābho narāṇāṃ rājadaivikaḥ ||
kṣayodayau jīvanaṃ tu rājadaivavaśān nṛṇām |
tasmāt sarveṣu kāryeṣu tatkṛtaṃ na vicālayet ||
ekatra kūpapātaṃ tu bhūmer anyatra saṃsthitam |
nadītīre prakurute tasya tāṃ na vicālayet ||
kṣetraṃ sasasyam ullaṅghya bhūmiḥ channā yadā bhavet |
nadīsrotaḥpravāheṇa bhūmisvāmī labheta tām ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
vṛṣṭipātaṃ praṇālaṃ ca na kuryāt paraveśmasu |
tathā,
viṇmūtrodakavapraṃ ca vahniśvabhraniveśanam |
aratnidvayam utsṛjya parakuḍyān niveśayet || iti |
tathā,
yā rājñā krodhalobhena chalān nyāyena vā hṛtā |
pradattānyasya tuṣṭena na sā siddhim avāpnuyāt ||
pramāṇarahitāṃ bhūmiṃ bhuñjato yasya yā hṛtā |
guṇādhikāya vā dattā tasya tāṃ na vicālayet ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
niveśakālād ārabhya gṛhavāryāpaṇādikam |
yena vāpy atha vā bhuktaṃ tasya tāṃ na vicālayet ||
nāradaḥ |
setus tu dvividho dṛṣṭaḥ khāto bandhyas tathaiva ca |
toyapravartane khāto bandhyaḥ syād vinivartane ||
parakṣetrasya madhye tu setur na pratiṣidhyate |
mahāguṇo ‘lpabādhaś cet vṛddhir iṣṭā kṣaye sati ||
alpakṣayam anādṛtya vṛddhir eveṣṭā | yājñavalkyaḥ |
na niṣedhyo ‘lpabādhaś ca setuḥ kalyāṇakārakaḥ |
parabhūmiṃ haran kūpaḥ svapakṣetro bahūdakaḥ ||
svāmine yo ‘nivedyaiva kṣetre setuṃ prakalpayet |
utpanne svāmino bhogaḥ tadabhāve mahīpateḥ ||
nāradaḥ |
pūrvapravṛttam utpannam apṛṣṭvā svāminaṃ tu yaḥ |
setuṃ pravartayet kaścit na sa tatphalabhāg bhavet ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
asvāmyanumatenaiva saṃskāraṃ kurute tu yaḥ |
gṛhodyānataṭākānāṃ saṃskartā labhate na tu ||
khilakarṣaṇe nārada āha |
aśaktapretanaṣṭeṣu kṣetrikeṣv anivāritaḥ |
kṣetraṃ ced vikṛṣet kaścid aśnuvīta sa tatphalam ||
vikṛṣyamāṇe kṣetre ca kṣetrikaḥ punar āvrajet |
khilopacāraṃ tat sarvaṃ datvā kṣetram avāpnuyāt ||
tadaṣṭabhāgāpacayāt yāvat kṛptagatāḥ samāḥ |
samāpte tv aṣṭame varṣe bhuktakṣetraṃ labheta saḥ ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
aśaktito na dadyād cet khilārthaṃ yaḥ kṛtaṃ vyayam |
tadaṣṭabhāgahīnaṃ tu karṣakaḥ phalam āpnuyāt ||
varṣāṇy aṣṭau sa bhoktā syāt parataḥ svāmine tu tat |
nāradaḥ |
saṃvatsareṇārdhakhilaṃ khilaṃ syād vatsarais tribhiḥ |
pañcavarṣāvasānaṃ tu kṣetraṃ syād aṭavīsamam ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
phālāhatam api kṣetraṃ na kuryād yo na kārayet |
sa pradāpyaḥ kṛṣṭaphalaṃ kṣetram anyena kārayet ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
kṣetraṃ gṛhītvā yaḥ kaścit na kuryān na ca kārayet |
svāmine sa śadaṃ dāpyo rājñe daṇḍaṃ ca tatsamam ||
śadaṃ kṣetraphalam |
cirāvasanne daśamaṃ kṛṣyamāṇe tathāṣṭamam |
susaṃskṛte tu ṣaṣṭhaṃ parikalpyā yathāsthitiḥ ||
bhāradvājaḥ |
bhasmodakādisaṃskāro yasmin kṣetre vidhīyate |
kṣetrād uddhṛtasasyaṃ tu praviṣṭaḥ svaṃ kṛṣer dhanī ||
praveśakālāt prāk kṛṣṭaṃ kṣetradhāraṇakena yaḥ |
phalaṃ vinā tad udbhūtaṃ dhanī tat kṣetram āpnuyāt ||
atra viṣṇuḥ: “grāmadvayamaryādābhede dviśatam | kṣetramaryādābhede aṣṭaśatam | sīmātikrame aṣṭasahasram | kṣetrāt kṣetrodakaharaṇe taduparodhe vā aṣṭaśataṃ daṇḍyaḥ” | śaṅkhaḥ : “sīmābhettāram uttamasāhasaṃ daṇḍayitvā punaḥ sīmāṃ kārayet” | kātyāyanaḥ |
sīmāmadhye tu jātānāṃ vṛkṣāṇāṃ kṣetrayor dvayoḥ |
phalaṃ puṣpaṃ ca sāmānyaṃ kṣetrasvāmiṣu darśayet ||
anyakṣetre tu jātānāṃ śākhāḥ kṣetrāntare yadi |
tāsāṃ svāmī sa eva syāt yasya kṣetre tu tāḥ sthitāḥ ||
prajāpatiḥ |
anyakṣetre tu jātānām agraśākhāḥ paratra cet |
sa eva tāsāṃ svāmī syāt yat kṣetropari tāḥ sthitāḥ ||
bījine tu tṛtīyāṃśaṃ dadyād rakṣākṛte śadam |
dvāv aṃśau kṣetriṇaḥ syātāṃ svāmibhāgaḥ praśasyate ||
yadi tenoparodhaḥ syāt mūlenāruhya śākhinām |
chettavyā kṣetriṇāṃ śākhā phalāpy enaṃ svasīmani ||
yatra svalpoparodhaḥ syāt śākhayānyaprarohiṇām |
kadalīkramukādīnāṃ tatra svāmy eva bhogabhāk ||
parakṣetre samutpanno ‘py anyakṣetre phalaṃ dadat |
śākhā ced yā tu tatrasthā phalaṃ vā vibhajet tataḥ ||
sīmāvivādapadaṃ samāptam
strīpuṃsyogaḥ
atha strīpuṃsayogākhyaṃ vivādapadam ucyate | tatra nāradaḥ |
vivāhādividhiḥ strīṇāṃ yatra puṃsāṃ prakīrtyate |
strīpuṃsayoga ity etad vivādapadam ucyate ||
śaṅkhaḥ: “anujñāto guruṇā mātāpitor anumato dārān āharet” | nāradaḥ |
brāhmaṇakṣatriyaviśāṃ śūdrāṇāṃ ca parigrahe |
sajātiḥ śreyasī bhāryā sajātiś ca patiḥ striyāḥ ||
ā saptamāt pañcamāc ca bandhubhyaḥ pitṛmātṛtaḥ |
avivāhyāḥ sagotrāś ca samānapravarās tathā ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
pañcamāt saptamād ūrdhvaṃ mātṛtaḥ pitṛtas tathā |
daśapūruṣavikhyātāt śrotriyāṇāṃ mahākulāt ||
vasiṣṭhaḥ: “pañcamīṃ mātṛbandhubhyaḥ, saptamīṃ pitṛbandhubhyaḥ” iti | kaṇvaḥ |
tṛtīyāt kṣatriyo mātuḥ pañcamāt pitṛtaḥ parām |
etatkaṇvavacanabalāt mātṛtas tṛtīyāṃ pitṛtaḥ pañcamīm iti paiṭhīnasivacanaṃ kṣatriyādiviṣayaṃ draṣṭavyam | sumantuḥ: “kanyā ā pañcamād ā saptamāc cāvivāhyā bhavanti | pitṛpatnyaḥ sarvā mātaraḥ, tadbhrātaro mātulāḥ | tatsutā mātulasutāḥ | tatsvasāraś ca mātṛṣavasāraḥ | tatsutā mātṛṣvasṛsutāḥ | tadduhitaraś ca bhaginyaḥ | tadapatyāni bhāgineyāni | ato mitha eṣām avivāhaḥ | anyathā saṃkarakāriṇas te sarve patitāḥ | teṣāṃ adhyāpayitur apy evam eva” | atra kāśyapaḥ |
sapta paunarbhavāḥ kanyāḥ varjanīyāḥ kulādhamāḥ |
vācā dattā manodattā kṛtakautukamaṅgalā |
agniṃ parigatā yā ca yā ca pāṇigṛhītikā |
udakasparśitā yā ca punarbhūprasavā ca yā ||
ity etāḥ kāśyapenoktāḥ dahanti kulam agnivat |
prarohed agninā dagdhaḥ pādapaḥ sucirād api ||
na ca paunarbhavādagdhaṃ kulaṃ tasya prarohati |
baudhāyanaḥ: “sagotrāṃ ced amatyopayacchet mātṛvad enāṃ bibhṛyāt | prajātā cet kṛcchrābdapādaṃ caritvā ‘yan ma ātmanaḥ. ‘punar agniś cakṣur adāt’ iti dvābhyām, ‘yad devā devaheḍanam’ iti kūṣmāṇḍair ājyaṃ juhuyāt” | nāradaḥ |
strīpuṃsayoś ca saṃbandhāt varaṇaṃ prāg vidhīyate |
varaṇād grahaṇaṃ pāṇeḥ saṃskāro ‘tha dvilakṣaṇaḥ ||
tayor aniyataṃ proktaṃ varaṇaṃ doṣadarśanāt |
pāṇigrahaṇamantrābhyāṃ niyataṃ dāralakṣaṇam ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
yatnāt parīkṣitaḥ puṃstve yuvā dhīmān janapriyaḥ |
tathā ca nāradaḥ |
parīkṣya puruṣaṃ puṃstve nijair evāṅgalakṣaṇaiḥ |
pumāṃś ced avikalpena sa kanyāṃ labdhum arhati ||
puṃstvalakṣaṇaṃ sa evāha |
subaddhajatrujānvasthisubaddhāṃsaśirodharam |
sthūlagāḍhas tanūrutvagavilagnagatisvaraḥ ||
bījaṃ viṣṭhā ca plavate hrādimūtraṃ ca phenilam |
pumān syāl lakṣaṇair etaiḥ viparītas tu ṣaṇḍakaḥ || iti |
avilagnagatisvaraḥ avicchinnāvilambagatisvaraḥ | śabdavat phenitvamūtraḥ | ṣaṇḍalakṣaṇam api sa evāha |
caturdaśavidhaḥ śāstre ṣaṇḍo dṛṣṭo manīṣibhiḥ |
cikitsyaś cācikitsyaś ca teṣām ukto vidhiḥ kramāt ||
dattātreyaprabhṛtibhiḥ vaidyaśāstre yaś cikitsyo ‘cikitsya ity uktaḥ | purāṇe |
nisargaṣaṇḍo vandhyaś ca pakṣaṣaṇḍas tathaiva ca |
abhiśāpāt guro rogād devakrodhāt tathaiva ca ||
īrṣyāṣaṇḍaś ca sevyaś ca vātaretā mukhe bhagaḥ |
ākṣiptamoghabījau ca śālīno ‘nyāpatis tathā ||
ṣaṇḍāś caturdaśaite syuḥ varjanīyāḥ prayatnataḥ |
pakṣaṣaṇḍaḥ pakṣaṃ gamanāsamarthaḥ | guruśāpād upahatapuṃstvaḥ | tathā rogād api tathā devatāparādhād api | īrṣyāṣaṇḍaḥ parapuruṣeṇa sevyamānāṃ striyaṃ dṛṣṭvā yasya puṃsaḥ puṃstvaṃ jāyate, sa īrṣyāṣaṇḍaḥ veditavyaḥ | sevyaḥ puruṣeṇa sevyamānasyopajāyate puṃstvaṃ na striyā | vātaretāḥ vātena vyādhinā retaḥsraṃsanam | yadā mukhe bhaga iti mukhagrahaṇaṃ pradarśanārthaṃ bhagād anyatra bhagakāryaṃ bhavati, na bhagamukhena kriyamāṇe puṃstvam upajāyate, sa mukhe bhaga iti kecit | ākṣiptabījaḥ ratir bhavati saṃyogāt pratikālaṃ śuklaṃ tu nāsti | moghabījaḥ yasya śuklaṃ bhavati nāpatyam | śālīnaḥ striyaṃ dṛṣṭvā lajjayā na prahṛṣyati | anyāpatiḥ anyasyāṃ bhavati puṃstvam, na svabhāryāyām | evaṃvidhāḥ ṣaṇḍaviśeṣā varjyāḥ | ṣaṇḍānāṃ parīkṣākālam apy āha nārdaḥ |
tatrādyāv apratikārau pakṣākhyo māsam ācaret |
anukramāt trayasyāsya kālaḥ saṃvatsaraḥ smṛtaḥ ||
ādau niṣiddhapakṣe ṣaṇḍe tu māsam ācaret | tato jñātvā pakṣād ūrdhvam apuṃstve tyājyaḥ | anukramād anantarasya trayasya gururogadevatānimittaṣaṇḍasya saṃvatsaram udīkṣyordhvaṃ tyāgaḥ | nātra daṇḍaḥ |
īrṣyāṣaṇḍādayo ye ‘nye catvāraḥ samudāhṛtāḥ |
saṃtyaktavyāḥ patitavat kṣatayonyā api striyā ||
kim utākṣatayonyā varaṇapāṇigrahaṇamātāyāḥ |
ākṣipte moghabīje ca patyāv apratikarmaṇi |
patir anyaḥ smṛto nāryā vatsaraṃ saṃpratīkṣya tu ||
śālīnasyāpi ghṛṣṭastrīdarśanāt patito dhvajaḥ |
taṃ hīnaveṣamattastrībālādibhir upakramet ||
śālīnasyāpi pragalbhastrīsaṃnidhau nottiṣṭhati dhvajaḥ, utthito vibhajyate, taṃ hīnaveṣābhir anujvalābhiḥ paribhavet na lajjā bhavati, tathā mattābhir bālābhir api tathaivānyābhir upakramaḥ | tathāvagataśālīnabhāvaḥ svabhāryāyāṃ pravartiṣyate | anyāpatiḥ –
anyasyā yo manuṣyaḥ syād amunuṣyaḥ svayoṣiti  |
labheta sānyaṃ bhartāram etat kāryaṃ prajāpateḥ ||
apatyārthe striyaḥ sṛṣṭāḥ strī kṣetraṃ bījavān pumān |
kṣetraṃ bījavete deyaṃ nābījī kṣetram arhati ||
pitā dadyāt svayaṃ kanyāṃ mātā vānumate pituḥ |
mātāmaho mātulaś ca sakulyā bāndhavās tathā ||
mātābhāve tu sarveṣāṃ prakṛtau yadi vartate |
tasyām aprakṛtisthāyāṃ dadyuḥ kanyāḥ sajātayaḥ ||
doṣān āha |
dīrghakutsitarogārtā vyaṅgāḥ saṃsṛṣṭamaithunāḥ |
dhṛṣṭānyagatabhāvāś ca kanyādoṣāḥ prakīrtitāḥ || iti |
dīrgharogāḥ mehārśaśūlādiyuktāḥ | ārtāḥ rājagrāhādidāridryādiyuktāḥ | vyaṅgāḥ vikalāṅgāḥ | saṃsṛṣṭamaithunāḥ bhuktapūrvāḥ | dhṛṣṭā nirlajjāḥ kāryākārye prasahya pravartante | anyagatabhāvāḥ anyakāmāḥ | yathā kanyādoṣā ete pradarśanārthāḥ tathānye ‘pi prasiddhā draṣṭavyāḥ | varadoṣān āha |
unmattapatitaklībadurbhagatyaktabāndhavāḥ |
kanyādoṣau ca yau pūrvāv eṣa doṣagaṇo vare ||
iti |
pañconmādāḥ samākhyātāḥ vātapittakaphodbhavāḥ |
caturtho dhananāśena sarve vā tena pañcamaḥ ||
ity unmādair yuktāḥ, patitāḥ, mahāpātakādikṛtaḥ | klībāḥ, upahatapuṃstvāḥ | durbhagāḥ sarvasya dveṣyāḥ | bāndhavais tyaktāḥ, pūrvau ca kanyādoṣau ca yau dīrgharogāḥ kutsitarogāś ca, eṣa doṣagaṇo varaviṣayaḥ |
aṣṭau vivāhā varṇānāṃ saṃskārākhyāḥ prakīrtitāḥ |
brāhmas tu prathamas teṣāṃ prājāpatyas tathaiva ca ||
ārṣaṃ caivātha daivaṃ ca gāndharmo ‘athāsuras tathā |
rākṣaso ‘nantaras tasmāt paiśācaś cāṣṭamo ‘dhamaḥ ||
satkṛtyāhūya kanyāṃ tu brāhmo dadyāt svalaṃkṛtām |
saha dharmaṃ carety uktvā prājāpatyo vidhīyate ||
vastraṃ gomithunaṃ datvā vivāhas tv ārṣa ucyate |
antarvedyāṃ tu daivaḥ syād ṛtvije karma kurvate ||
icchantīm icchataḥ prāhuḥ gāndharvo nāma pañcamaḥ |
vivāhas tv āsuro jñeyaḥ śulkasavyavahārataḥ ||
prasahya kāraṇād ukto vivāho rākṣasas tathā |
suptapramattopagamāt paiśācaś cāṣṭamo ‘dhamaḥ ||
eṣāṃ dharmās tu catvāro brāhmādyāḥ samudāhṛtāḥ |
sādhāraṇaḥ syāt gāndharvaḥ trayo ‘dharmās tataḥ pare ||
nāduṣṭāṃ dūṣayet kanyāṃ nāduṣṭaṃ dūṣayed varam |
doṣe tv asati nāśaḥ syāt anyonyaṃ tyajatos tayoḥ ||
datvā nyāyena yaḥ kanyāṃ varāya na dadāti tām |
aduṣṭaś cet varo rājñā sa daṇḍyas tatra coravat ||
yas tu doṣavatīṃ kanyām anākhyāya prayacchati |
tasya kuryān nṛpo daṇḍaṃ pūrvasāhasacoditam ||
akanyeti tu yaḥ kanyāṃ brūyād doṣeṇa mānavaḥ |
sa śataṃ prāpnuyād daṇḍaṃ tasyā doṣam adarśayan ||
pratigṛhya tu yaḥ kanyāṃ naro deśāntaraṃ vrajet |
vineyaḥ so ‘py akāmo ‘pi kanyāṃ tām eva codvahet ||
atra śūdrāniṣedhaṃ yama āha |
na brahmahā brahmahā syāt brahmahā vṛṣalīpatiḥ |
yas tatra jāyate garbhaḥ sa tena brahmahā bhavet ||
atra vṛṣalī caṇḍālī | tathā ca manuḥ |
vṛṣalīphenapītasya niśvāsopahatasya ca |
tasyāṃ caiva prasūtasya niṣkṛtir na vidhīyate ||
nāradaḥ |
pratigṛhya tu yaḥ kanyāṃ varo deśāntaraṃ vrajet |
trīn ṛtūn samatikramya kanyānyaṃ varayed varam ||
manuḥ |
trīṇi varṣāṇy upāsīta kumāry ṛtumatī satī |
ūrdhvaṃ tu kālād etasmād vindeta sadṛśaṃ patim ||
alaṅkāraṃ nādadīta pitryaṃ kanyā svayaṃvarā |
mātṛkaṃ bhrātṛdattaṃ vā steyaṃ syād yadi taṃ haret ||
pitre na dadyāc chulkaṃ tu kanyām ṛtumatīṃ haran |
sa hi svāmyād apakrāmed ṛtūnāṃ pratirodhanāt ||
nāradaḥ |
kanyāyāṃ prāptaśulkāyāṃ śreyāṃś ced vara āvrajet |
dharmārthakāmasaṃyuktaṃ vācyaṃ tatrānṛtaṃ bhavet ||
pūrvadattām api śreyase dātuṃ hared iti | tathā ca yājñavalkyaḥ |
dattām api haret pūrvāṃ śreyāṃś ced vara āvrajet |
datvā kanyāṃ haran daṇḍyo vyayaṃ dadyāc ca sodayam ||
mṛtāyāṃ dattam ādadyāt pariśodhyo bhayavyayam |
vilakṣaṇapuruṣābhāve haran daṇḍyaḥ | atra vasiṣṭhaḥ |
adbhir vācā ca dattāyāḥ mriyetordhvaṃ varo yadi |
na ca mantropanītā syāt kumārī pitur eva sā ||
manuḥ |
pāṇigrahaṇikā mantrāḥ niyataṃ dāralakṣaṇam |
teṣāṃ niṣṭhā tu vijñeyā vidvadbhiḥ saptame pade ||
vasiṣṭhaḥ |
balāc cet prahṛtā kanyā mantrair yadi na saṃskṛtā |
anyasmai vidhivad deyā yathā kanyā tathaiva sā ||
yamaḥ |
nodakena na vācā ca kanyāyāḥ paririṣyate |
pāṇigrahaṇasaṃskārāt patitvaṃ saptame pade ||
smṛtyantare |
sā ced akṣatayoniḥ syāt gatapratyāgatāpi vā |
paunarbhavena bhartrā sā punaḥ saṃskāram arhati ||
vasiṣṭhaḥ |
kanyāyāṃ prāptaśulkāyāṃ mriyeta yadi śulkadaḥ |
devarāya pradātavyā yadi kanyānumanyate ||
kanyāvisaṃvāde nivṛttir eva | śātātapaḥ |
udvāhitā tu yā kanyā saṃprāptā na ca maithunam |
bhartāraṃ punar abhyeti yathā kanyā tathaiva sā ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
sa ca yady anyajātīyaḥ patitaḥ klība eva vā |
vikarmasthaḥ sagotro vā dīrghatīvrāmayo ‘pi vā ||
klībo ‘nyo yadi vā bhartā visṛṣṭaḥ puṃstvakāraṇaiḥ |
ūḍhāpi deyā sānyasmai sarvābharaṇabhūṣaṇā ||
pradāya śulkaṃ gacched yaḥ kanyāyāḥ strīdhanaṃ tathā |
dhāryā sā varṣam ekaṃ tu deyānyasmai vidhānataḥ ||
atha pravṛttir āgacchet pratīkṣeta samātrayam |
ata ūrdhvaṃ pradātavyā kanyānyasmai yathecchayā ||
kanyāṃ ced darśayitvānyām anyā voḍhre pradīyate |
sa ubhe ekaśulkena vahed ity abravīn manuḥ ||
gūhayitvātmano doṣān vindate kanyakāṃ yadi |
varasya dattanāśaḥ syāt kanyā cāpi nivartate ||
nāradaḥ |
yadi doṣavatīṃ kanyām anāryāya (anākhyāya) prayacchati |
rājñā damaṃ sa dāpyaḥ syāt pūrvasāhasacoditam ||
viṣṇuḥ: “kārṣāpaṇaṃ daṇḍadravyānuvṛttaiḥ doṣaṃ anākhyāya kanyāṃ prayacchet | tāṃ ca bibhṛyāt” iti | manuḥ |
utkṛṣṭāyānurūpāya varāya sadṛśāya ca |
aprāptām api tāṃ tasmai kanyāṃ dadyād yathāvidhi ||
asyārthavādaṃ sa evāha |
kāmam ā maraṇāt tiṣṭhet gṛhe kanyartumaty api |
na caivaināṃ prayacchet tu guṇahīnāya karhicit || iti |
nāradaḥ |
kanyā nartum upekṣeta bāndhavebhyo nivedayet |
te can na dadyus tāṃ bhartre te syur bhrūṇahabhiḥ samāḥ ||
yāvantaś cartavas tasyāḥ samatīyuḥ patiṃ vinā |
tāvatyo bhrūṇahatyāḥ syuḥ tasya yo na dadāti tām ||
manuḥ |
kāle ‘dātā pitā vācyaḥ vācyaś cānupayan patiḥ |
mṛte bhartari putras tu yāpyo mātur arakṣitā ||
yāpyo daṇḍyaḥ |
aprayacchan pitā kāle patiś cānupayan ṛtau |
putraś cābhuktado mātuḥ garhyo daṇḍyaś ca dharmataḥ ||
strīṇāṃ dūṣaṇāny āha [manuḥ] |
pānaṃ durjanasaṃsargaḥ patyā ca viraho ‘ṭanam |
svapno ‘nyagehavāsaś ca nārī saṃdūṣaṇāni ṣaṭ ||
naitā rūpaṃ parīkṣante nāsāṃ vayasi saṃsthitiḥ |
surūpaṃ vā virūpaṃ vā pumān ity eva bhuñjate ||
nirindriyā hy amantrāś ca striyo ‘nṛtam iti sthitiḥ | iti |
strībhiḥ prayojanam āha [manuḥ] |
prajanārthaṃ mahābhāgāḥ prajāhī gṛhadīptayaḥ |
striyaḥ śriyaś ca geheṣu na viśeṣo ‘sti kaścana ||
utpādanam apatyasya jātasya paripālanam |
pratyahaṃ lokayātrā tu pratyakṣaṃ strīnibandhanam ||
apatyaṃ dharmakāryāṇi śuśrūṣā ratir uttamā |
dārādhīnas tathā svargaḥ pitṝṇām ātmanaś ca hi ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
bhartrā patnī samabhyarcyā vastrālaṃkāraśobhanaiḥ |
utsave ca pitṛbhrātṛśvaśurādyaiś ca bandhubhiḥ ||
yatra striyo ‘bhipūjyante ramante tatra devatāḥ |
saṃpadaś ca prajāḥ śuddhāḥ kriyā ca saphalā bhavet ||
patiṃ yā nāticarati manovākkāyasaṃyatā |
sā bhartṛlokān āpnoti sadbhiḥ sādhvīti cocyate ||
dakṣaḥ |
prahṛṣṭamānasā nityā sthānamānavicakṣaṇā |
bhartuḥ priyakārī yā tu sā bhāryā itarāntarā ||
pūrvotthānaparā dakṣā jaghanyāsanaśāyinī |
avāgduṣṭānukūlā strī bhaved vai puṇyakarmaṇā || iti |
nāradaḥ |
mṛte bhartary aputrāyāḥ patipakṣaḥ prabhuḥ striyāḥ |
viniyogātmarakṣāsu bharaṇe ca sa īśvaraḥ ||
parikṣīṇe patikule nirmanuṣye nirāśraye |
tatsapiṇḍeṣu cāsatsu pitṛpakṣaḥ prabhuḥ striyāḥ ||
pakṣadvayāvasāne tu rājā tasyāḥ prabhuḥ smṛtaḥ |
sa tasyā bharaṇaṃ kuryāt nigṛhṇīyāt pathaścyutām ||
vyāsaḥ |
patiḥ sutaḥ pitā bhrātā pitṛvyo mātulas tathā |
mātāmahas tathā strīṇāṃ saptaite prabhavaḥ smṛtāḥ ||
yatheṣṭaṃ prabhavaḥ proktāḥ strīṇāṃ bandhukule sati |
striyo mūrkhajaḍonmattabālavṛddhādiṣu smṛtāḥ ||
strīṇāṃ ciārohaṇam āha | atra aṅgirāḥ |
mṛte bhartari yā nārī samārohed dhutāśanam |
sārundhatīsamācārā svargaloke mahīyate ||
tisraḥ koṭayo ‘rdhakoṭī ca yāni romāṇi mānave |
tāvanty abdasahasrāṇi svargaloke mahīyate ||
vyālagrāhī yathā vyālaṃ balād uddharate bilāt |
tadvad bhartāram ādāya tenaiva saha gacchati ||
mātṛkaṃ paitṛkaṃ caiva yatra kanyā pradīyate |
kulatrayaṃ sā punāti bhartāraṃ yānugacchati ||
tatra sā bhartṛparamā mudā paramayānivitā |
patyā saṃkrīḍate sārdhaṃ yāvantīndrāś caturdaśa ||
brahmahā vā kṛtaghno vā mitraghno vāpi yo naraḥ |
tam vāi punāti sā nārī ity āṅgirasabhāṣitam ||
sādhvīnām eva nārīṇām agniprapatanād ṛte |
nānyo dharmo hi vijñeyo mṛte bhartari karhicit ||
yāvan nāgnau dahed aṅgaṃ mṛte patyau pativratā |
tāvan na mucyate sā strī strīśarīrāt kathaṃcana ||
sadvṛttabhāvārpitabhartṛkāṇāṃ strīṇāṃ viyoge kṣaṇakātarāṇām |
tāsāṃ patyāv astamite hi naikāt nāgnipraveśād aparo ‘sti mārgaḥ ||
śaṅkha-likhita-hārīta-uśanasa-bṛhaspatibhiḥ vyāsena ca sādhvīnāṃ strīṇāṃ anumaraṇam uktam | atra viṣṇuḥ: “mṛte bhartari brahmacaryaṃ tadanvārohaṇaṃ veti” | yat tv idaṃ vacanam,
yā vai brāhmaṇajātīyā mṛtaṃ patim anuvratā |
sā svargam ātmaghātena nātmānaṃ ghātayed iti ||
tat pṛthakcitāpraveśaviṣayam,
pṛthakcitiṃ samāruhya na viprā gantum arhati |
iti vacanāt | tena abālāpatyānām agarbhiṇīnām ācaṇḍālaṃ sādhāraṇo ‘yaṃ dharmaḥ | parityājyā āha manuḥ |
vyādhiktāṃ vipraduṣṭāṃ vā chadmanā copapāditām |
vidhivat pratigṛhyāpi tyajet kanyāṃ vigarhitām ||
yas tu doṣavatīṃ kanyām anākhyāya prayacchati |
tasya tad vitathaṃ kāryaṃ kanyādānaṃ durātmanaḥ ||
pravasataḥ kartavyam āha |
vṛttiṃ vidhāya bhāryāyāḥ pravaset kāryavān naraḥ |
avṛttikarhitā [-karṣitā?] hi strī saṃduṣyed dhṛtimaty api ||
devadattāṃ patir bhāryāṃ vindate necchayātmanaḥ |
tāṃ sādhvīṃ bibhṛyān nityaṃ devānāṃ priyam ācaran ||
prajanārthaṃ striyaḥ sṛṣṭāḥ santānārthaṃ ca mānavāḥ |
tasmāt sādhāraṇo dharmaḥ śrutau patyā sahoditaḥ ||
āpastambaḥ: “jāyāpatyor na vibhāgo vidyate | pāṇigrahaṇād dhi sahatvaṃ karmasu | tathā puṇyaphaleṣu dravyaparigraheṣu ca | tathā dharmaprajāsaṃpanne dāre nānyāṃ kurvīta, anyatarābhāve kāryā prāg agnyādheyād ādhāne hi satī karmabhiḥ saṃbadhyate” iti |
atha proṣitabhartṛkādharmān āha manuḥ |
vidhāya proṣite vṛttiṃ jīven niyamam āsthitā |
proṣite tv avidhāyaiva jīvec chilpair agarhitaiḥ ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
krīḍāṃ śarīrasaṃskāraṃ samājotsavadarśanam |
hāsaṃ paragṛhe yānaṃ tyajet proṣitabhartṛkā ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
prasādhanaṃ nṛttagītasamājotsavadarśanam |
māṃsamadhyābhiyogaṃ ca na kuryāt proṣite prabhau ||
hārītaḥ |
na proṣite tv alaṃkuryān na veṇī . . . . muñcet || iti |
proṣitasya uktaḥ pratīkṣaṇakālaḥ |
proṣito dharmahetos tu pratīkṣyo ‘ṣṭau naraḥ samāḥ |
vidyārthaṃ ṣaḍ yaśo’rthaṃ vā kāmārthaṃ trīṃs tu vatsarān ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
pitrā bhartrā na yojyā strī ṣaḍvarṣaṃ proṣite prabhau |
naṣṭe tṛtīyaṃ . . . . pravrajite ‘pi vā ||
gautamaḥ: “naṣṭe bhartari ṣaḍvārṣikaṃ kṣapaṇam | śrūyamāṇe ‘bhigamanam | pravrajite tu nivṛttiḥ prasaṅgāt | dvādaśavarṣāṇi brāhmaṇasya vidyāsambandhe” |
bhāryā kāṅkṣeta śūdrasya varṣān aṣṭāgataṃ patim |
vaiśyarājanyaviprāṇāṃ bhāryā dvitricaturguṇam ||
yamaḥ |
bhartā na bhajate yāṃ sā pratīkṣeta samā daśa |
dvādaśāṣṭau tathā viprā tathaiva manur abravīt ||
kṣatriyākramya māḥ (?) svaṣṭau ṣaṭsamā vaiśyakanyakāḥ |
na śūdrāyāḥ smṛtaḥ kālo na ca dharmavyatikriyā ||
nāradaḥ |
viśeṣato ‘prasūtāyāṃ saṃvatsaraparā sthitiḥ |
apravṛttau smṛtaḥ kāla eṣa proṣitayoṣitām ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
patyau naṣṭe prayuktāyāṃ tasyām anyatra bandhubhiḥ |
tasmāc caiva prasūtāyāṃ kathaṃ tatrāgate bhavet ||
patir labheta bhāryāṃ tāṃ sāpatyām api dharmataḥ |
kṣetriṇas tv eva tān putrān manur āha na bījinaḥ ||
pravrajyāvasito yatra punar dārān samāharet |
nāsau svāmī bhavet tatra dāso hy eṣa vigarhitaḥ || iti |
bhartur apy āpadi bhāryāparityāgam āha manuḥ |
saṃvatsaram udīkṣeta dviṣantīṃ yoṣitaṃ patiḥ |
ūrdhvaṃ saṃvatsarād enāṃ dāyaṃ hṛtvā na saṃvaset ||
dāyaṃ bhūṣaṇādikam | saṅghāḥ sahavāsaḥ jaṅgamo vā | adhivettavyā āha manuḥ |
vandhyāṣṭame ‘dhivettavyā daśame tu mṛtaprajā |
ekādaśe strījananī sadyas tv apriyavādinī ||
madyapā tv anyavṛttā ca pratikūlā ca yā bhavet |
vyādhitā cādhivettavyā hiṃsrārthavacanī sadā ||
śaṅkhalikhitau : “aślīlāṃ puruṣadveṣiṇīm ananukūlāṃ cādhivindet” | devalaḥ |
ākāṅkṣetāṣṭavarṣāni bhartā nispṛhavān striyam |
daśaiva mṛtaputrāṃ tu dvādaśa strīprasūtikām ||
tato vindeta vidhinā putrārthaṃ dharmataḥ striyam |
putralābhāt paraṃ loke nāsti nispṛhavādinām ||
yā rohiṇī syāt tu (va)nitā saṃpannā caiva śīlataḥ |
sānujñāpyādhivettavyā nāvamānyā ca karhicit ||
ekām utkramya kāmārtham anyāṃ labdhuṃ ya icchati |
samarthas toṣayitvārthaiḥ pūrvoḍhām aparāṃ vahet ||
ekā śūdrasya vaiśyasya dve tisraḥ kṣatriyasya tu |
catasro brāhmaṇasya syuḥ bhātyā rājño yatheṣṭataḥ || iti |
adhivinnastriyam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
adhivinnastriyai dadyād ādhivedanikaṃ samam |
na dattaṃ strīdhanaṃ yasyai datte tv arthaṃ prakalpayet || iti |
ānukūlyādiguṇayuktāṃ tyajato daṇḍam āha nāradaḥ |
anukūlām avāgduṣṭāṃ dakṣāṃ sādhvīṃ prajāvatīm |
tyajan bhāryām avasthāpyo rājñā daṇḍena bhūyasā ||
kuṣṭhinīṃ patitāṃ vandhyām unmattāṃ vigatārtavām |
aniṣṭāṃ prabhave tyaktuṃ tīrthān na tv eva karmaṇā || iti |
yamaḥ |
ṛtusnātā tu yā nārī martāraṃ naiva gacchati |
tāṃ grāmamadhye vikhyāpya bhrūṇaghnīṃ tu vivāsayet ||
bhartuḥ pratiniveśe tu yā vṛthā yāpayed ṛtum |
tāṃ tu vikhyāpya bandhūnāṃ bhrūṇaghnīṃ tu vivāsayet ||
bodhāyanaḥ |
adhivinnā tu yā nārī nirgacched dūṣitā gṛhāt |
sā sadyaḥ saṃniroddhavyā tyājyā vā kulasaṃnidhau || iti |
bahvīṣu bhāryāsu dharmakāryayogyāṃ viṣṇur āha: “savarṇāsu bahvīṣu bhāryāsu vidyamānāsu jyeṣṭhayaiva saha dharmakāryaṃ kuryāt | miśrāsu ca kaniṣṭhayā api samānavarṇayā | samānavarṇābhāve tv anantarayā | na tv eva dvijaḥ śūdrayā” iti |
duhitṛdāne śulkagrahaṇaṃ niṣadhati manuḥ |
ādadīta na śūdro ‘pi śulkaṃ duhitaraṃ dadat |
śulkaṃ hi gṛhṇan kurute channaṃ duhitṛvikrayam ||
krītā dravyeṇa yā kanyā na sā patnī vidhīyate |
na sā daive na sā pitrye dāsīṃ tāṃ kāśyapo ‘bravīt ||
yamaḥ |
krayakrītā tu yā kanyā na sā patnī vidhīyate |
tathā daive ca pitrye ca dāsī sā dārasaṃjñitā || iti |
strīsaṃgrahaṇam
atha strīsaṃgrahaṇam āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
pāpamūlaṃ saṃgrahaṇaṃ triprakāraṃ nibodhata |
balopadhikṛte dve tu tṛtīyam anurāgajam ||
tat punas trividhaṃ proktaṃ prathamaṃ madhyamottamam |
anicchantyā yat kriyate mattonmattapramattayā ||
pralapantyāpi rahasi balātkārakṛtaṃ tu tat |
chadmanā gṛham ānīya datvā ca madakāraṇam ||
saṃyogaḥ kriyate yasyāḥ tad upādhikṛtaṃ viduḥ |
anyonyacakṣūrāgeṇa dūtīsaṃpreṣaṇena vā ||
kṛtaṃ rūpārthalobhena jñeyaṃ tadanurāgajam |
apāṅgapreṣaṇaṃ hāsyaṃ dūtīsaṃpreṣaṇaṃ tathā ||
sparśo bhūṣaṇavastrāṇāṃ prathamaḥ saṃgrahaḥ smṛtaḥ |
preṣaṇaṃ gandhamālyānāṃ phalamadyānnavāsasām ||
saṃbhāṣaṇaṃ ca rahasi madhyamaṃ saṃgrahaṃ viduḥ |
ekaśayyāsanaṃ krīḍā cumbanāliṅganaṃ tathā ||
etat saṃgrahaṇaṃ proktam uttamaṃ śāstravedibhiḥ |
nāradaḥ |
nadīnāṃ saṃgame tīrthe ‘py ārāmeṣu vaneṣu ca |
strīpuṃsau yat sameyātāṃ tac ca saṃgrahaṇaṃ smṛtam ||
manuḥ |
upakārakriyā keliḥ sparśo bhūṣaṇavāsasām |
saha khaṭvāsanaṃ caiva sarvaṃ saṃgrahaṇaṃ smṛtam ||
dantapādanakhānāṃ ca darśanaṃ garbhadhāraṇam |
paravastrasya dharaṇam alaṃkārāyudhasya ca ||
ebhiś cihnaiḥ sadā jñeyā vyabhicāraratāḥ striyaḥ |
atra daṇḍam āha āpastambaḥ: “abuddhipūrvam alaṃkṛto yuvā paradāram anupraviśan kumārīṃ vā vācā bādhyaḥ, buddhipūrvaṃ tu duṣṭabhāvo daṇḍyaḥ” | atra manuḥ |
bhikṣukā bandinaś caiva dīkṣitāḥ kāravas tathā |
saṃbhāṣaṇaṃ saha strībhiḥ kuryur aprativāritāḥ ||
na saṃbhāṣāṃ saha strībhiḥ pratiṣiddhaḥ samācaret |
niṣiddho bhāṣamāṇas tu suvarṇaṃ daṇḍam arhati ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
strīniṣedhe śataṃ dadyāt dviśataṃ tu damaṃ pumān |
pratiṣedhe tayor daṇḍo yathā saṃgrahaṇe tathā ||
manuḥ |
paradārābhimarśeṣu pravṛttān nṝn mahīpatiḥ |
udvejanakarair daṇḍaiḥ cihnayitvā pravāsayet ||
abrāhmaṇaḥ saṃgrahaṇe prāṇāntaṃ daṇḍam arhati |
caturṇām api varṇānāṃ dārā rakṣyatamāḥ smṛtāḥ ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
sajātāv uttamo daṇḍa ānulomye tu madhyamaḥ |
prātilomye vadhaḥ puṃsāṃ strīṇāṃ nāsādikartanam ||
nāradaḥ |
mātā mātṛṣvasā śvaśrūḥ mātulānī pitṛṣvasā |
pitṛvyasakhiśiṣyastrī bhaginī tatsakhī snuṣā ||
duhitācāryabhāryā ca sagotrā śaraṇāgatā |
rājñī pravrajitā dhātrī sādhvī varṇottamā ca yā ||
āsām anyatamāṃ gatvā gurutalpaga ucyate |
śiśnasyotkṛntanāt tatra nānyo daṇḍo vidhīyate ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
mātuḥ sapatnīṃ bhaginīm ācāryatanayāṃ tathā |
ācāryapatnīṃ svasutāṃ gacchaṃs tu gurutalpagaḥ ||
chittvā liṅgaṃ vadhas tasya sakāmāyāḥ striyas tathā |
vyāsaḥ |
icchantyām āgatāyāṃ tu gacchato ‘rdhadamo mataḥ |
manuḥ |
sahasraṃ brāhmaṇo daṇḍyo viprāṃ guptāṃ balād vrajan |
śatāni pañca daṇḍyaḥ syād icchantyā saha saṃgataḥ ||
mauṇḍyaṃ prāṇāntiko daṇḍo brāhmaṇasya vidhīyate |
itareṣāṃ tu varṇānāṃ daṇḍaḥ prāṇāntiko bhavet ||
agupte vaiśyarājanye śūdrāṃ ca brāhmaṇo vrajan |
śatāni pañca daṇḍyaḥ syāt sahasraṃ tv antyajastriyam ||
saṃvatsarābhiśastasya duṣṭasya dviguṇo damaḥ |
vrātyayā saha saṃvāse caṇḍālyā tāvad eva tu ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
antyābhigamane ‘ṅkitvā kubandhena pravāsayet |
śūdras tathāntya eva syād antyasyāryāgame vadhaḥ ||
strīṇāṃ daṇḍaṃ manur āha |
vṛṣalaṃ sevate yā tu brāhmaṇī madamohitā |
tāṃ śvabhiḥ khādayed rājā saṃsthāne vadhyakhādinām ||
vaiśyaṃ vā kṣatriyaṃ vāpi brāhmaṇī sevate tu yā |
śiraso muṇḍanaṃ tasyāḥ prayāṇaṃ gardabhena tu ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
gṛham āgatya yā nārī pralobhya sparśanādinā |
kāmayet tatra sā daṇḍyā narasyārdhadamaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
chinnanāsoṣṭhakarṇī tu pariśrāmyāpsu majjayet |
khādayed vā sārameyaiḥ saṃsthāne bahusaṃsthite ||
anicchantī tu yā bhuktā guptāṃ tāṃ vāsayed gṛhe |
malināṅgīm adhaḥśayyāṃ piṇḍamātropajīvinīm ||
kārayen niṣkṛtiṃ kṛcchraṃ parākaṃ vā samāgatām |
hīnavarṇopabhuktā sā tyājyā vadhyātha vā bhavet ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
sakāmāsv anulomāsu na doṣas tv anyathā damaḥ |
anyathā prātilomyena gamane vadha iṣyate || iti |
kanyādūṣaṇe daṇḍam āha manuḥ |
abhiṣahya tu yaḥ kanyāṃ kuryād darpeṇa mānavaḥ |
chettavye aṅgulī tasya daṇḍaṃ cārhati ṣaṭchatam ||
sakāmāṃ dūṣayaṃs tulyo nāṅgulicchedam āpnuyāt |
dviśataṃ tu damaṃ dāpyaḥ prasaṅgavinivṛttaye ||
(nāradaḥ) |
sakāmāyāṃ tu kanyāyāṃ savarṇe nāsty atikramaḥ |
kiṃ tv alaṃkṛtya satkṛtya sa evaināṃ samudvahet ||
sakṛdgamane kṛte kṛtaprāyaścittaḥ kṛtaprāyaścittām alaṃkṛtāṃ mantravad udvahet | vyāsaḥ |
bahubhir bhuktapūrvāṃ tu gached yas tāṃ narādhamaḥ |
tasya veśyāvad icchanti daṇḍaṃ na paradāravat ||
yaṃ kāmārtā svairiṇī yā svayam eva prakāmayet |
rājñā deśād vimoktavyā vikhyāpya janasaṃnidhau ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
avaruddhāsu dāsīṣu bhujiṣyāsu tathaiva ca |
gamyāsv api pumān dāpyaḥ pañcāśatpaṇikaṃ damam ||
prasahya veśyāgamane daṇḍo daśapaṇaḥ smṛtaḥ |
bahūnāṃ yady akāmāsau caturviṃśatikaṃ pṛthak ||
ayonau gacchato yoṣāṃ puruṣaṃ vāpi mehataḥ |
caturviṃśatiko daṇḍaḥ tathā pravrajitāgame ||
nāradaḥ |
paśuyonāv atikrāman vineyaḥ sadaśaṃ śatam |
madhyamaṃ sāhasaṃ goṣu tad evāntāvasāyiṣu ||
yad apīdaṃ śūdrāvardhakīgamane sacelasnānaṃ kṛtvā gāyatryā aṣṭasahasraṃ juhuyād iti, tat kāmato ‘bhyāsaviṣayam prāyaścittābhimukhasyeti tatra daṇḍābhāvaḥ | atra manuḥ |
paṇānāṃ dve śate sārdhe prathamaḥ sāhasaḥ smṛtaḥ |
madhyamaḥ pañca vijñeyaḥ sahasraṃ tv eva tūttamaḥ ||
śaṅkhalikhitau: “caturviṃśatir ekanavatiḥ prathamaḥ sāhasaḥ smṛtaḥ | dviśataṃ pañcaśataṃ ceti madhyamaḥ | ṣaṭchataṃ sahasraṃ cety uttamaḥ” | yājñavalkyaḥ |
sāśītiḥ paṇasāhasro daṇḍa uttamasāhasaḥ |
tadardhaṃ madhyamaḥ proktaḥ tadardham adhamaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
sarvatra pāpagauravalāghavāpekṣayā saṃkhyāviśeṣā draṣṭavyāḥ | strīrakṣaṇe manuḥ |
na kaścid yoṣitaḥ śaktaḥ prasahya parirakṣitum |
arthasya saṃgrahe caināṃ vyaye caināṃ niyojayet ||
śauce satye ‘nnapaktyāṃ ca pāriṇāhyasya rakṣaṇe |
pāriṇāhyam āsanaśayanopayānādi | strīrakām āha dakṣaḥ |
jalūkāvat striyaḥ sarvāḥ bhūṣaṇāchādanāśanaiḥ |
suvṛtā vihṛtā nityaṃ puruṣaṃ hy ākṛṣanti tu ||
jalūkā raktam ādatte kevalaṃ sā tapasvinī |
itarā tu dhanaṃ cittaṃ māṃsaṃ vīryaṃ balaṃ sukham ||
sāśaṅkā bālabhāve tu yauvane viṣayonmukhī |
tṛṇavan manyate nārī vṛddhabhāve svakaṃ patim ||
svakāmye vartamānā sā snehān na ca nivāritā |
apathyā tu bhavet paścāt yathā vyādhir upekṣitā ||
yādṛśaṃ bhajate hi strī sutaṃ sūte tathāvidham |
ataḥ prajāviśuddhyarthaṃ striyo rakṣyāḥ prayatnataḥ ||
manuḥ |
sūkṣmebhyo ‘pi prasaṅgebhyaḥ striyo rakṣyā viśeṣataḥ |
dvayor hi kulayoḥ śokam āvaheyur arakṣitāḥ ||
idaṃ hi sarvavarṇānāṃ paśyanto dharmam uttamam |
yatante rakṣituṃ bhāryāṃ bhartāro durbalā api ||
svāṃ prasūtiṃ caritraṃ ca kulam ātmānam eva ca |
svaṃ ca dharmaṃ prayatnena bhāryāṃ rakṣaṇ hi rakṣati ||
anyonyasyāvyabhicāro bhaved āmaraṇāntikaḥ |
eṣa dharmaḥ samāsena jñeyaḥ strīpuṃsayoḥ paraḥ ||
iti śrīmadvaradarājīye vyavahāranirṇaye
saṃbhūyasamutthānādivivādapadakāṇḍaṃ samāptam
11. dāyavibhāgakāṇḍam
atha dāyavibhāgaḥ
atra nāradaḥ |
vibhāgo ‘rthasya pitryasya putrair yatra prakalpyate |
dāyabhāga iti proktaṃ tad vivādapaṃ budhaiḥ ||
viṣṇuḥ: “pitā cet putrān vibhajate, tasya svecchātaḥ svayam ārjite ‘rthe” iti | atra “putrān vibhajet” ity adhikāre nāradaḥ |
mātur nivṛtte rajasi prattāsu bhaginīṣu ca |
nivṛtte vāpi ramaṇe pitary uparataspṛhe ||
vyādhitaḥ kupitaś caiva viṣayāsaktamānasaḥ |
ayathāśāstrakārī ca na vibhāge pitā prabhuḥ ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
pitror abhāve bhrātṝṇāṃ vibhāgaḥ saṃpradarśitaḥ |
mātur nivṛtte rajasi jīvator api śasyate ||
atra śaṅkhalikhitau: “jīvati pitari rikthavibhāgo vṛddhe viparītacetasi dīrgharogiṇi vā jyeṣṭhenaiva” | gautamaḥ: “ūrdhvaṃ pituḥ putrā rikthaṃ bhajeran | nivṛtte rajasi mātur jīvati cecchati” | hārītaḥ: “jīvann eva vā pravibhajya vanam āśrayed vṛddhāśramaṃ vā gacchet | svalpena vā saṃvibhajya bhūyiṣṭham ādāya vased yady apy upadasyet punas tebhyo gṛhṇīyāt | kṣīṇāṃś ca vibhajet | athāpy atra grahopadānaupacāyikīṃ śrutim udāharanti | pitāgrayaṇaḥ putrā itare grahāḥ | yad yathāgrayaṇaḥ upadasyet skandet itarebhyo grahebhyo gṛhṇīyāt | yad itare grahāḥ skandeyuḥ āgrayaṇāt gṛhṇīyāt | skandet upavādasyet | itarebhyaḥ grahebhyaḥ” iti vyākhyātam | nāradaḥ |
pitaiva vā svayaṃ putrān vibhajed vayasi sthitaḥ |
evamādibhir vacanaiḥ pitṛkartṛko vibhāgaḥ kadācit putrakartṛkaś ca pratīyate | tathā vibhāgakālo ‘py anekavidhaḥ pratīyate | pitur vibhāgecchāyāṃ satyām ekaḥ kālaḥ | tathā jīvaty eva pitari dravyanispṛhe ca | mātari nivṛttarajaskāyāṃ pitur anicchāyām api putrecchayaiva vibhāgaḥ | tathā sarajaskāyām api mātary anicchaty api pitari prattāsu bhaginīṣu pitary adharmavartini dīrgharogagraste vā, putrāṇām icchayā bhavati vibhāgaḥ | tathā ūrdhvaṃ pitur aparaḥ kālaḥ dharmavṛddhyarthaṃ vibhāgaḥ kartavya ity āha prajāpatiḥ |
evaṃ saha saseyur vā pṛthag vā dharmakāmyayā |
pṛthag vivardhate dharmaḥ tasmād dharmyā pṛthakkriyā ||
tathāha bṛhaspatiḥ |
ekapākena vasatā pitṛdevadvijārcanam |
ekaṃ bhaved vibhaktānāṃ tad eva syāt gṛhe gṛhe ||
nāradaḥ |
bhrātṝṇām avibhaktānām eko dharmaḥ pravartate |
vibhāge sati dharmo ‘pi bhaved eṣāṃ pṛthak pṛthak ||
tathā vyāsaḥ |
bhrātṝṇāṃ jīvatoḥ pitroḥ saha vāso vidhīyate |
tadabhāve vibhaktānāṃ dharmas teṣāṃ vivardhate ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
vibhāgaṃ cet pitā kuryād icchayā vibhajet sutān |
jyeṣṭhaṃ vā śreṣṭhabhāgena sarve vā syuḥ samāṃśinaḥ ||
yadā vibhāgaṃ pitā cikīrṣati tadaivecchayā vibhajet samaṃ vā viṣaṃ vā | icchāyā niraṅkuśatvāt kalpāntaram āha jyeṣṭhaṃ veti |
jyeṣṭhasya viṃśa uddhāraḥ sarvadravyāc ca yad varam |
tato ‘rdhaṃ madhyamasya syāt turīyaṃ tu yavīyasaḥ ||
iti manunoktaprakāreṇa jyeṣṭhaṃ śreṣṭhabhāgena kaniṣṭhaṃ kaniṣṭhabhāgena madhyamaṃ madhyamabhāgena vibhāgaḥ kartavyaḥ | siddhāntam āha – “sarve vā syuḥ samāṃśinaḥ” iti | tatra icchayā viṣamavibhāgas tu svayam ārjitavittaviṣayaḥ | pitṛkramāyāte tu dhane svāmyasya putrapautrāṇāṃ samatvān necchayā viṣamavibhāgo yuktaḥ | atra yājñavalkyaḥ |
bhūr yā pitāmahopāttā nibandho dravyam eva vā |
tatra syāt sadṛśaṃ svāmyaṃ pituḥ putrasya cobhayoḥ ||
tathā ca viṣṇuḥ: “arthe paitāmahe pitṛputrapautrayoḥ tulyaṃ svāmyam” | kātyāyanaḥ |
paitāmahaṃ samānaṃ syāt pituḥ putrasya cobhayoḥ |
svayaṃ tūpārjite pitrā na putraḥ svāmyam arhati ||
tathā vyāsaḥ |
kramāgate gṛhakṣetre pitṛputrāḥ samāṃśinaḥ |
paitṛkena vibhāgārhāḥ sutāḥ pitur anicchataḥ ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
dravyaṃ pitāmahopātte jaṅgame sthāvare tathā |
samaṃ svāmitvam ākhyātaṃ pituḥ putrasya cobhayoḥ ||
vyāsaḥ |
paitāmahaṃ vinā pitrā svaśaktyā yad upārjitam |
vidyāśauryādināvāptaṃ tatra svāmyaṃ pituḥ smṛtam ||
pradānaṃ cecchayā kuryād vibhāgaṃ ca tato dhanāt |
tadabhāve tu ye putrāḥ samāṃśāḥ parikīrtitāḥ ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
sthāvaradvipadaṃ caiva yady api svayam ārjitam |
asaṃbhūya sutān sarvān na dānaṃ na ca vikrayaḥ ||
asyāpavādam āha vyāsaḥ |
eko ‘pi sthāvare kuryāt dānādhamanavikrayam |
āpatkāle kuṭumbārthe dharmārthe ca viśeṣataḥ ||
maṇimuktāpravālādeḥ sarvasyaiva pitā prabhuḥ |
sthāvarasya tu sarvasya na pitā na pitāmahaḥ ||
evamādibhir vacanaiḥ paitāmahe putrapautrayoḥ svāmyaṃ samam | pituḥ svayam ārjite svātantryam iti gamyate | yad apīdaṃ mānavaṃ vacanadvayam |
bhāryā putraś ca dāsaś ca traya evādhanāḥ smṛtāḥ |
yat te samadhigacchanti yadyaite tasya tad dhanam ||
tathā |
ūrdhvaṃ pituś ca mātuś ca sametya bhrātaraḥ saha |
bhajetan paitṛkaṃ riktham anīśās te hi jīvatoḥ ||
yad apīdaṃ devalavacanam,
pitary uparate putrā vibhajeran pitur dhanam |
asvāmyaṃ hi bhaved eṣāṃ nirdoṣe pitari sthite ||
iti evamdīny asvātantryaparāṇi, na svatvābhāvaparāṇīti liṅgaviśeṣanirdeśāt puṃyuktam aitiśāyana ity adhikaraṇe bhagavatā bhāṣyakāreṇoktam | tathā ca śrutiḥ: “patnī hi pārīṇahyasyeśe” iti | tathā ca hārītaḥ: “jīvati pitari putrāṇām arthādānavisargākṣepeṣu na svātantryam” iti | kiṃ ca pūrvam eva svatvam utpannam ity asmin pakṣe sarvasmṛtiṣu vibhajed iti yujyate | anyathā vibhāgāt pūrvaṃ svatvābhāve putrebhyo dadyād iti vaktavyam | tathā vibhāgāt svatvapakṣe ekaputrasya mātāpitror ūrdhvaṃ vibhāgābhāvāt svatvaṃ na syāt | tena kulapraveśād eva pitṛpaitāmahadravye ‘pi putrasya svāmyam asty eva | tathā ca bṛhaspatiḥ |
jātā janiṣyad garbhasthāḥ pitṛsthā ye ca mānavāḥ |
sarve kāṅkṣanti tāṃ vṛttim anācchedyā tatas tu sā ||
janiṣyamāṇānāṃ viśeṣaṇaṃ garbhasthā iti tilamāṣādirūpeṇa | jātā anuśayinaḥ tatpakṣakapuruṣasaṃbandhāḥ pitṛsthā ucyante | tathā hi – kiṃcit kālaṃ śleṣmarūpeṇa tato retorūpeṇa ca pitari pariṇatāḥ strīsaṃbandhena tasyāṃ praviṣṭāḥ garbhasthā ity ucyante | evaṃ pitṛmātṛsaṃbandhaprabhṛti taddhaneṣu teṣāṃ tathā svāmitvam astīti | svasya sato vibhāgaḥ | na vibhāgāt svatvam | tena svayam ārjite pitur icchayā vibhāgaḥ ārjakasya prādhānyāt | kramāgate tu pitur icchayā putrecchayā ca vibhāgaḥ | svayam ārjitavibhāge nārada āha |
pitraiva tu vibhaktā ye samanyūnādhikair dhanaiḥ |
teṣāṃ sa eva dharmaḥ syāt sarvasya hi pitā prabhuḥ ||
tathā ca bṛhaspatiḥ |
samanyūnādhikā bhāgāḥ pitrā yeṣāṃ prakalpitāḥ |
tathaiva te pālanīyā vineyās te syur anyathā ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
nyūnādhikavibhaktānāṃ dharmyaḥ pitṛkṛtaḥ smṛtaḥ |
evam ityādivacanaiḥ yady api śāstradṛṣṭo viṣamavibhāgaḥ tathāpi lokavidviṣṭatvāt nānuṣṭheyaḥ,
asvargyaṃ lokavidviṣṭaṃ dharmam apy ācaren na tu |
iti niṣedhāt | tathā ca prajāpatiḥ |
yathā niyogadharmo ‘pi nānubandhyā gavādikā |
tathoddhāravibhāgo ‘pi naiva saṃprati vartate ||
yathā mṛte bhartari niyogadharmo lokavidviṣṭatvān nānuṣṭhīyate, yathā vā gavālambhaḥ, tadvad uddhāradharmo ‘pi lokavidviṣṭatvāt kaliyuge nānuṣṭheyaḥ | jīvadvibhāge viśeṣam āha nāradaḥ |
dvāv aṃśau pratipadyeta vibhajann ātmanaḥ pitā |
tathā ca bṛhaspatiḥ|
jīvadvibhāge tu pitā gṛhṇītāṃśadvayaṃ svakam |
tathā ca śaṅkhalikhitau: “yady ekaputraḥ syāt dvau bhāgāv ātmanaḥ” | kuryāt | dvipadaś catuṣpadeṣu rūpam adhikaṃ vṛṣabho jyeṣṭhāyeti | tatra pitur bhāgadvayagrahaṇaṃ kramād āgatadravyaviṣayam | tatra patnī nirbhāgeti kecit | yājñavalkyaḥ |
yadi kuryāt samān aṃśān patnyaḥ kāryāḥ samāṃsikāḥ |
na dattaṃ strīdhanaṃ yāsāṃ bhartrā vā śvaśureṇa vā ||
yadā svecchayā pitā sarvān eva sutān samabhāginaḥ karoti, tadā sajātīyapatnyaś ca putrasaamāṃśabhājaḥ kartavyāḥ | yāsāṃ patnīnāṃ bhartrā śvaśureṇa vā strīdhanaṃ dattam, datte ca strīdhane tadapekṣayā bhāgaparipūraṇaṃ kartavyam, adhivedanaviṣayaṃ tathoktatvāt |
adhivinnaḥ striyai dadyād ādhivedanikaṃ samam |
na dattaṃ strīdhanaṃ yasyai datte tv ardhaṃ prakalpayet || iti |
atra ardhaśabda ekadeśaparaḥ | tena pūrvadattāpekṣayā bhāgaparipūraṇaṃ kartavyam ity arthaḥ | evaṃ jīvadvibhāge samāṃśabhāgitvaṃ mātṝṇām uktvā pitari mṛte ‘pi samāṃśabhājo bhavantīty āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
pitur ūrdhvaṃ vibhajatāṃ mātāpy aṃśaṃ samaṃ haret | iti |
tathā ca nāradaḥ |
samāṃśabhāginī mātā putrāṇāṃ syān mṛte patau |
atra bṛhaspatiḥ |
tadabhāve tu jananī tanayāṃśasamāṃśinī |
vyāsaḥ |
asutās tu pituḥ patnyaḥ samānāṃśāḥ prakīrtitāḥ |
pitāmahyaś ca sarvās tu mātṛtulyāḥ prakīrtitāḥ ||
pitāmahyā api mātṛvad bhāgakalpanaṃ yuktam iti | viṣṇuḥ: “mātaraḥ putrabhāgānusāribhāgahāriṇyaḥ anūḍhāś ca duhitaḥ” iti | tathā ca bṛhaspatiḥ |
samāṃśā mātaras teṣāṃ turīyāṃśā ca kanyakā |
kātyāyanaḥ |
kanyakānāṃ tv adattānāṃ caturtho bhāga iṣyate |
bhrātṝṇāṃ ca trayo bhāgaḥ samaṃ tv alpadhane smṛtam ||
atra manuḥ |
svebhyo ‘ṃśebhyas tu kanyābhyaḥ pradadyur bhrātaraḥ pṛthak |
svāt svād aṃśāc caturbhāgaṃ patitāḥ syur aditsavaḥ || iti |
śaṅkhalikhitau: “vibhajyamāni dadyād ye kanyālaṃkāraṃ vaivāhikaṃ strīdhanaṃ ca kanyā labheta” | kanyālaṃkāraṃ kanyāyogyabhūṣaṇam | tathā paiṭhīnasiḥ: “kanyā vaivāhikaṃ strīdhanaṃ ca labheta” | atra bodhāyanaḥ: “mātur alaṃkāraṃ duhitaraḥ sāpradāyikaṃ labheran anyad vā” | atra vyāsaḥ |
asaṃskṛtās tu ye tatra paitṛkād eva taddhanāt |
saṃskāryā bhrātṛbhir jyeṣṭhaiḥ kanyakāś ca yathāvidhi ||
nāradaḥ |
avidyamāne pitrye ‘rthe svāṃśād uddhṛtya vā punaḥ |
avaśyakāryaḥ saṃskāro bhrātṝṇāṃ pūrvasaṃskṛtaiḥ ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
asaṃskṛtās tu saṃskāryāḥ bhrātṛbhiḥ pūrvasaṃskṛtaiḥ |
bhaginyaś ca nijād aṃśād datvāṃśaṃ tu turīyakam ||
sarvasmṛtiṣu duhitṝṇāṃ pāṇigrahaṇasaṃskārasya kaṇṭhoktatvāt putrāṇām api pūrvasaṃskṛtair jyeṣṭhaiḥ vivāhaparyantaḥ saṃskāraḥ kartavya iti vṛddhāḥ | tatra saṃskāraparyāptaṃ dhanaṃ duhitṝṇāṃ deyaṃ nānyad ity eke | anye tu bahuṣu dhaneṣu putrabhāgāc caturthabhāgadānam, alpe dhane samāṃśadānam iti manyate |
athānīhasya kiṃcid datvā vibhāgam āha manuḥ |
bhrātṝṇāṃ yas tu neheta dhanaṃ śaktaḥ svakarmaṇā |
sa nirbhājyaḥ svakād aṃśāt kiṃcid datvopajīvanam ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
śaktasyānīhamānasya kiṃcid datvā pṛthakkriyā |
āpastambaḥ: “sarve hi dharmayuktā bhāgino yas tv adharmeṇa dravyāṇi pratipādayati, jyeṣṭho ‘pi tam abhāgaṃ kurvīta” | gautamaḥ: “savarṇāputro ‘py anyāyavṛtto na labhetaikeṣām” | evaṃbhūtānām antato gatvā tāmbūlādikam api datvā vibhāgaḥ kartavyaḥ |
dāyānarhān āha manuḥ |
sarva eva vikarmasthā nārhanti bhrātaro dhanam |
śaṅkhalikhitau: “apapātrīkṛtasya rikthapiṇḍodakāni vyāvartante” | bṛhaspatiḥ |
savarṇajo ‘py aguṇavān nārhaḥ syāt paitṛkaṃ dhane |
tatpiṇḍadāḥ śrotriyā ye teṣāṃ tad abhidhīyate ||
uttamarṇādhamarṇebhyaḥ pitaraṃ trāyate sutaḥ |
atas tu viparītena tena nāsti prayojanam ||
tayā gavā kiṃ kriyate yā na dogdhrī na garbhiṇī |
ko ‘rthaḥ putreṇa jātena yo na vidvān na dhārmikaḥ ||
śāstraśauryārtharahitaḥ tapojñānavivarjitaḥ |
ācārahīnaḥ putras tu mūtroccārasamaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
evaṃbhūtānāṃ duṣṭānāṃ na kiṃcid deyam | atha bhartavyān āha manuḥ |
anaṃśau klībapatitau jātyandhabadhirau tathā |
unmattajaḍamūkāś ca ye ca kecin nirindriyāḥ ||
sarveṣām api tu nyāyyaṃ dātuṃ śaktyā manīṣiṇā |
grāsācchādanam abhyaṅgaṃ patito hy adadad bhavet ||
yady arthitā tu dāraiḥ syāt klībādīnāṃ kathaṃcana |
teṣām utpannatantūnām apatyaṃ dāyam arhati ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
klībo ‘tha patitas tajjaḥ paṅgur unmattako jaḍaḥ |
andho ‘cikitsyarogī ca bhartavyās tu niraṃśakāḥ ||
aurasakṣetrajās teṣāṃ nirdoṣā bhāgahāriṇaḥ |
sutāś caiṣāṃ prabhartavyā yāvad vai bhartṛsātkṛtāḥ ||
aputrā yoṣitaś caiṣāṃ bhartavyāḥ sādhuvṛttayaḥ |
nirvāsyāḥ vyabhicāriṇyaḥ pratikūlās tathaiva ca ||
patito brahmahādiḥ, tajjaḥ patitotpannaḥ, unmattaḥ vātikapaittikaślaiṣmikasānnipātikagrahāveśa-lakṣaṇair unmādair abhibhūtaḥ, jaḍo vikalakaraṇo, hitāhitāvadhāraṇākṣamaḥ | eteṣāṃ vibhāgāt prāg eva doṣaprāptau anaṃśatvam | na punar vibhaktasyāpi | eteṣāṃ klībādīnām aurasāṃ kṣetrajā vā putrā aṃśagrahaṇavirodhidoṣarahitā bhāgahāriṇaḥ | klībasya kṣetrajaḥ saṃbhavati | anyeṣām aurasā api saṃbhavati | aurasakṣetrajayor grahaṇād itaraputrāṇāṃ vyudāsaḥ | eṣāṃ klībādīnāṃ sutā duhitaraḥ prabhartavyāḥ yāvad vivāhasaṃskārasaṃskṛtā bhavanti tāvad bharaṇīyāḥ | evaṃ klībādīnāṃ putrā yoṣitaḥ sādhuvṛttayo bhartavyāḥ | vyabhicāriṇyas tu nirvāsyāḥ | devalaḥ |
mṛte na pitari klībakuṣṭhyunmattajaḍāndhakāḥ |
patitaḥ patitāpatyaṃ liṅgī dāyāṃśabhāginaḥ ||
teṣāṃ patitavargebhyo bhaktakaṃ tu pradīyate |
tatputrāḥ pitṛdāyāṃśaṃ labheran doṣavarjitāḥ ||
patitotpannasya patitatvān na dāyasaṃbandhaḥ | viṣṇuḥ: “anaṃśās tv āśramāntaragatāḥ klībonmattapatitāś ca | bharaṇaṃ klībonmattānām” | gautamaḥ: “jaḍaklībau bhartavyau apatyaṃ jaḍasya bhāgārham” iti | vibhaktāvibhaktayor avibhaktasya dhanagrahaṇam āha manuḥ |
ūrdhvaṃ vibhāgāj jātas tu pitryam eva hared dhanam |
saṃsṛṣṭās tena vā ye syuḥ vibhajeran sahaiva te ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
pitrā saha vibhaktā ye sāpatnā vā sahodarāḥ |
jaghanyajās tu ye teṣāṃ pitṛdāyaharās tu te ||
anīśaḥ pūrvajaḥ pitrye bhrātur bhāge vibhaktajaḥ |
smṛtyantare |
putraiḥ saha vibhaktena pitrā yat svayam ārjitam |
vibhaktajasya tat sarvam anīśāḥ pūrvajāḥ smṛtāḥ ||
yathā dhane tathā carṇe dānādhānakrayeṣu ca |
parasparam anīśās te muktvā śaucodakariyā ||
vibhaktajasyāpi pitṛdhanābhāve svāṃśebhyo bhāgakalpaneti yājñavalkyaḥ |
vibhakteṣu suto jātaḥ savarṇāyāṃ vibhāgabhāk |
dṛśyād vā tadvibhāgaḥ syād āyavyayaviśodhanāt ||
viṣṇuḥ: “pitrā vibhaktā vibhāgānantaram utpannasya vibhāgaṃ dadyuḥ” | pautrasya putreṇa saha samavibhāgam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
bhūr yā pitāmahopāttā nibandho dravyam eva vā |
tatra syāt sadṛśaṃ svāmyaṃ pituḥ putrasya cobhayoḥ ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
ṛṇaṃ lekhyaṃ gṛhaṃ kṣetraṃ yasya paitāmahaṃ bhavet |
virakālaproṣito ‘pi tatra bhāgaharas tu saḥ ||
paitāmahe dhane pituḥ putrasya ca samāne sati yadi punar ekasya bahavaḥ putrāḥ te ‘pi viṣayān putrān utpādya sthitā mṛtā vā tatra itthaṃ dāyavibhāga ity āha | yājñavalkyaḥ |
anekapitṛkāṇāṃ tu pitṛto dāyakalpanā |
pitṛdvāreṇāṃśabhājaḥ amī pautrā ity arthaḥ | bṛhaspatiḥ |
samavetais tu yat prāptaṃ sarve tatra samāṃśinaḥ |
tatputrā viṣamasamāḥ pitṛbhāgaharāḥ smṛtāḥ ||
samavetaiḥ avibhaktaiḥ yal labdhaṃ kṛṣyādinā tad ekena labdham api sarveṣāṃ samānam | tatputrāḥ teṣāṃ putrāḥ | viṣamasamāḥ pitur eva bhāgaharāḥ | atra viśeṣam āha kātyāyanaḥ |
avibhakte ‘nuje prete tatsutaṃ rikthabhāginam |
kurvīta jīvanaṃ yena labdhaṃ naiva pitāmahāt ||
labhetāṃśaṃ tu pitryaṃ sa pitṛvyāt tasya vā sutān |
sa evāṃśas tu sarveṣāṃ bhrātṝṇāṃ nyāyato bhavet |
labheta tatsuto vāpi nivṛttiḥ parato bhavet ||
avibhaktaputre mṛte tatsutaṃ pitāmahād alabdhajīvanaṃ rikthabhājinaṃ kuryāt | tasya riktabhāgaḥ svapitryāṃśaḥ pitāmahe mṛte pitṛvyāt svāṃśasya vibhāgena lābhaḥ | pitṛvyo ‘pi mṛtaś cet tasya sutāt svāṃśasya vibhāgāl lābhaḥ | pitṛvyasutād vibhāge ‘pi sa evāṃśo bhavet yo bhrātṝṇāṃ nyāyato bhavet | etad uktaṃ bhavati | viṣamasamā bhrātṛputrāḥ svapitṛbhāgaharā iti | pūrveṣāṃ maraṇe tatsuto ‘pi caturtha eva bhāgaṃ labheta | pañcamaprabhṛti sāpiṇḍyābhāvān nivṛttiḥ | ā caturthān nivṛttiṃ devalo ‘py āha |
avibhaktavibhaktānāṃ kulyānāṃ vasatāṃ saha |
bhūyo dāyavibhāgaḥ syād ā caturthād iti sthitiḥ ||
nāsapiṇḍyāḥ sakulyāḥ syuḥ piṇḍabhedas tv ataḥ param |
samam icchanti piṇḍānāṃ dāyārthasya vibhājane ||
vidhir eṣa savarṇānāṃ bandhūnāṃ samudāhṛtaḥ |
eka eva savarṇasya dāyo ‘tra na vibhajyate ||
vibhaktānām api saṃsṛṣṭānāṃ sakulyānāṃ punar ā caturthāt dāyavibhāgaḥ syāt | sakulyāḥ asapiṇḍāḥ | ataḥ paraṃ dāyabhāgo nāsti | asapiṇḍāḥ iti hetoḥ | sāpiṇḍye saty eva dāyavibhāgaḥ | tena pañcamasya sāpiṇḍyābhāvāt dāyavibhāgo nāsti | ukto vidhiḥ savarṇāsu jātānām eva | na hi bhinnajātīyastrīṣu jātānāṃ bhrātṝṇāṃ savarṇāsu jātānām ekajātīyatvān na tatra dāyavaiṣamyam iti | kātyāyano ‘pi caturthāt parato dāyanivṛttim āha |
rikthaṃ pratipradānaṃ tu datvā śeṣaṃ vibhājayet |
ā caturthāt tu tad grāhyaṃ krameṇaiva tu tatsutaiḥ ||
pratipradānaśabdena ṛṇam ucyate | pūrvoktacaturthapuruṣaparyantaṃ sāpiṇḍyam | pitṛpitāmahaprapitāmaheṣu tu mṛteṣu draṣṭavyam | teṣu jīvatsu pūrveṣām api trayāṇāṃ sāpiṇḍyāt saptapuruṣāvadhi sāpiṇḍyaṃ bhavati | etadabhiprāyeṇa bṛhaspatiḥ |
tṛtīyaḥ pañcamo vāpi saptamo vāpi yo bhavet |
tadanvayasyāgatasya dātavyā gotrajair mahī ||
tatra ṛṇakriyāviśiṣṭe dāyādānāṃ vibhāga ity āha nāradaḥ |
yac chiṣṭaṃ pitṛdāyebhyo datvarṇaṃ paitṛkaṃ ca yat |
bhrātṛbhis tad vibhaktavyam ṛṇī na syād yathā pitā ||
pitṛdāyaśabdena pitrādīnām aurdhvadehikakriyocyate | tathā ca kātyāyanaḥ |
bhrātrā pitṛvyamātṛbhyāṃ kuṭumbārtham ṛṇaṃ kṛtam |
vibhāgakāle deyaṃ tat rikthibhiḥ sarvam eva tu ||
tena ṛṇakriyāviśiṣṭe dāyaprāptiḥ | prajāpatiḥ |
dṛśyamānaṃ vibhajyeta gṛhaṃ kṣetraṃ catuṣpadam |
gūḍhadravyābhiśaṅkāyāṃ pratyayas tatra kīrtitaḥ ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
gṛhopaskaravāhyādi bhogyābharaṇakarmiṇaḥ |
dṛśyamānā vibhajyante gūḍhe kośo vidhīyate ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
anyonyāpahṛtaṃ dravyaṃ vibhakte yatra dṛśyate |
tat punas taiḥ samair aṃśaiḥ vibhajerann iti sthitiḥ ||
manuḥ |
ṛṇe dhane ca sarvasmin pravibhakte tatas tataḥ |
paścāt dṛśyeta yat kiṃcit tat sarvaṃ samatāṃ nayet || iti |
gṛhādivibhāge jyeṣṭhādīnāṃ nimaya uktaḥ | tatra smṛtyantarād viśeṣaḥ gṛhavibhāge dakṣiṇato jyeṣṭhasya, tadanantaram anantarasya tathetareṣām iti | kātyāyanaḥ |
kṣetrārāmagṛhādīnāṃ vibhāge samupasthite |
jyeṣṭhasya dakṣiṇo bhāgaḥ pratīcī vā tathā bhavet || iti |
tatra viśeṣam āha devalaḥ |
niṣāda ekaputras tu viprasya dvyaṃśabhāg bhavet |
dvau sapiṇḍaḥ sakulyo vā svadhādātā tu saṃharet ||
niṣādaśabdena śūdrāyāṃ jāta ucyate | ardhagrahaṇaṃ tṛtīyāṃśagrahaṇaṃ ca atyantaśuśrūṣoḥ śūdrasya draṣṭavyam | atra viśeṣam āha manuḥ |
yady api syāt tu satputro ‘py asatputro ‘pi vā bhavet |
nādhikaṃ daśamād dadyāt śūdrāputrāya dharmataḥ ||
brāhmaṇakṣatriyaviśāṃ śūdrāputro na rikthabhāk |
yad evāsya pitā dadyāt tad evāsya dhanaṃ bhavet ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
anapatyasya śuśrūṣoḥ guṇavāñ chūdrayonijaḥ |
labheta jīvanaṃ śeṣaṃ sapiṇḍās tu samāpnuyuḥ ||
śūdrāputro na rikthabhāg ityādirikthaniṣedhavacanam aśuśrūṣuviṣayaṃ draṣṭavyam | pratilomānām api saṃvyavahāryāṇāṃ sutānāṃ śuśrūṣūṇāṃ janakena jivanaṃ deyam ity āha gautamaḥ: “śūdrāputravat pratilomās tu” iti | atra viśeṣam āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
na pratigrahabhūr deyā kṣatriyādisutāya vai |
yady apy asya pitā dadyāt mṛte viprāsuto haret ||
pitari mṛte brāhmaṇīputra eva haret | tathā brahmadāye viśeṣam āha vṛddhamanuḥ |
brahmadāyagatāṃ bhūmiṃ hareyur brāhmaṇīsutāḥ |
gṛhadvijātayaḥ sarve tathaiva kṣetrakardamam ||
dvijagrahaṇāt śūdrāputrasya nivṛttiḥ | bṛhaspatiḥ |
śūdryāṃ dvijātibhir jāto na bhūmer bhāgam arhati |
svajātāvāpnuyāt (?) sarvam iti dharmo vyavasthitaḥ ||
atra viśeṣam āha manuḥ |
dāsyāṃ vā dāsadāsyāṃ vā yac chūdrasya suto bhavet |
so ‘nujñāto hared aṃśam iti dharmo vyavasthitaḥ ||
tathā ca yājñavalkyaḥ |
jāto ‘pi dāsyāṃ śūdreṇa kāmato ‘ṃśaharo bhavet |
mṛte pitari kuryus taṃ bhrātaras tv ardhabhāginam ||
sarvatra dāsīputrasya dhanagrahaṇaṃ samānotkṛṣṭaputrapautradauhitrādyabhāve draṣṭavyam | “jāto ‘pi dāsyāṃ śūdreṇa” ity ārabhya “abhrātṛko haret sarvaṃ duhitṝṇāṃ sutād ṛte” ity uktatvāt |
dvādaśavidhaputrāṇāṃ dāyaṃ vaktum teṣāṃ lakṣaṇam āha manuḥ |
svakṣetre saṃskṛtāyāṃ tu svayam utpādayet tu yam |
tam aurasaṃ vijānīyāt putraṃ prathamakalpitam ||
yas talpajaḥ pramītasya klībasya vyādhitasya vā |
svadharmeṇa niyuktāyāṃ sa putraṃ kṣetrajaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
atra viśeṣam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
aputreṇa parakṣetre niyogotpāditaḥ sutaḥ |
ubhayor apy asau rakthī piṇḍadātā ca dharmataḥ ||
asya putradvayātmakatam, bījino bījajaḥ putraḥ kṣetriṇas tu kṣetrajaḥ, ity etāvatā vibhāgena putradvayātmakatvaṃ yuktam iti | dvyāmuṣyāyaṇo ‘yam bījajasya svāṃśasaṃbhavāt kṣetrajāt utkṛṣṭatvaṃ draṣṭavyam | atra bṛhaspatiḥ |
agniṃ prajāpatiṃ ceṣṭvā kriyate gautamoktavat |
anye tv āhur aputrasya cintitā putrikā bhavet ||
smṛtyantare : “abhisandhimātrāt putrikety eke” iti | atra putrikāvidhiḥ | vasiṣṭhaḥ |
abhrātṛkāṃ pradāsyāmi tubhyaṃ kanyām alaṃkṛtām |
asyāṃ yo jāyate putraḥ sa me putro bhaviṣyati ||
manuḥ |
aputro ‘nena vidhinā sutāṃ kurvīta putrikām |
yad apatyaṃ bhaved asyāṃ tan mama syāt svadhākaram ||
anena tu vidhānena purā cakre svaputrikām |
vṛddhyarthaṃ tu svavaṃśasya svayaṃ dakṣaḥ prajāpatiḥ ||
atrāpi putradvayam | putrikaiva putra ity eke | putrikāyāḥ putra ity apare | manuḥ |
mātā pitā vā dadyātāṃ yam adbhiḥ putram āpadi |
sadṛśaṃ prītisaṃyuktaṃ sa jñeyo datrimaḥ sutaḥ ||
upapanno guṇaiḥ sarvaiḥ putro yasya tu datrimaḥ |
sa haretaiva tad rikthaṃ saṃprāpto ‘py anyagotrataḥ ||
gotrarikthe janayituḥ na bhajed datrimaḥ sutaḥ ||
nāradaḥ |
gotrarikthānugaḥ piṇḍo vyapaiti dadataḥ svadhā |
akhaṇḍādarśīye sākṣiprakaraṇe | kātyāyanaḥ |
dattasya janakāpatye mṛte tasminn api svayam |
kṛtvā karma sapiṇḍāntam akhilaṃ riktham āpnuyāt ||
krīṇīyād yas tv apatyārthaṃ mātāpitror yam antikāt |
sa krītakaḥ sutas tasya sadṛśo ‘sadṛśo ‘pi vā ||
mātāpitṛvihīno yaḥ tyakto vā syād akāraṇāt |
ātmānaṃ sparśayed yasmai svayaṃ dattas tu sa smṛtaḥ ||
sparśayet dadyād ity arthaḥ |
sadṛśaṃ tu prakuryād yaṃ guṇadoṣavicakṣaṇam |
putraṃ putraguṇair yuktaṃ sa vijñeyas tu kṛtrimaḥ ||
utpadyate gṛhe yasya na ca jñāyet kasyacit |
svagṛhe gūḍha utpannaḥ tasya syād yasya talpajaḥ ||
mātāpitṛbhyām utsṛṣṭaṃ tayor anyatareṇa vā |
yaṃ putraṃ parigṛhṇīyād apaviddhaḥ sa ucyate ||
pitṛveśmani kanyā tu yaṃ putraṃ janayed rahaḥ |
taṃ kānīnaṃ vidur nāmnā voḍhuḥ kanyāsamudbhavam ||
sa voḍhur eva putro na mātāmahasya | yājñavalkyaḥ |
kānīnaḥ kanyakājāto mātāmahasuto mataḥ |
nāradaḥ |
ajñātapitṛko yas tu kānīno gūḍhamātṛjaḥ |
mātāmahāya dadyāt sa piṇḍaṃ rikthaṃ haret tataḥ ||
sati voḍhari kānīno voḍhuḥ putraḥ | tasminn asaty anujñāto vā mātāmahasya putraḥ | vasiṣṭhaḥ |
adattā duhitā yasya putraṃ vindeta tulyataḥ |
pautrī mātāmahas tena dadyāt piṇḍaṃ hared dhanam ||
manuḥ |
yā garbhiṇī saṃskriyate jñātjñātāpi vā satī |
voḍhuḥ sagarbho bhavati sahoḍha iti cocyate ||
yā patyā vā parityaktā vidhavā cecchayātmanaḥ |
utpādayet punar bhūtvā sa paunarbhava ucyate ||
yaṃ brāhmaṇas tu śūdrāyāṃ kāmād utpādayet sutam |
sa pārayann eva śavaḥ tasmāt pāraśavaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
pārayan jīvann eva śavaḥ |
kṣetrajādīn sutān etān ekādaśa yathoditān |
putrapratinidhīn āhuḥ kriyālopān manīṣiṇaḥ ||
putrān dvādaś yān āha nṛṇā svāyaṃbhuvo manuḥ |
teṣāṃ ṣaḍ bandhudāyādā ṣaḍ adāyādabāndhavāḥ ||
bodhāyanena pūrvaṣaṭkasya rikthabhāktvam uttaraṣaṭkasya gotrabhāktvam uktam | tatra sarvasmṛtiṣu putrāṇāṃ nānākrameṇa pāṭhe pā **kamam anādṛtyārthakrameṇa ṣaṭkavibhāgaḥ kartavyaḥ | ata evoktam |
śreyasaḥ śreyaso ‘bhāve pāpīyān riktham arhati |
utpattito variṣṭhaś ca śreyān aurasaputrakaḥ ||
aurasaṃ putrikāputraṃ kṣetrajaṃ dattakṛtrimau |
gūḍhajaṃ cāpaviddhaṃ ca rikthabhājaḥ pracakṣate ||
kānīnaṃ ca sahoḍhaṃ ca krītaṃ paunarbhavaṃ tathā |
svayaṃ dattaṃ niṣādaṃ ca gotrabhājaḥ pracakṣate ||
kvacid aurasakṣetrajayoḥ śreyastvam uktam |
aurasakṣetrajau putrau pitṛrikthasya bhāginau |
daśāpare tu kramaśo gotrarikthāṃśabhāginaḥ ||
riktāṃśaśabdena jīvanamātram abhipretam | kṣetrajasyāṃsaniyamam āha |
ṣaṣṭhaṃ tu kṣetrajasyāṃśaṃ pradadyāt paitṛkād dhanāt |
auraso ‘tha bhajed dāyaṃ pitryaṃ pañcamam eva (vā) ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
eka evaurasaḥ pitrye dhane svāmī prakīrtitaḥ |
tattulyā putrikā proktā bhartavyās tv apare smṛtāḥ ||
atra manuḥ |
kānīnaś ca sahoḍhaś ca gūḍhāyāṃ yatra jāyate |
teṣāṃ voḍhā pitā jñeyaḥ te ca bhāgaharāḥ pituḥ ||
etair vacanaiḥ ṣaṭ bandhudāyādāḥ | anye grāsācchādanabhājaḥ | atra viśeṣam āha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “tasmiṃś cet pratigṛhīta aurasa utpadyeta sa caturthabhāk” iti | dattaputrasvīkāre sati aurasa utpadyeta aurasāc caturthāṃśabhāk bahudhanaviṣayam idam | kātyāyanaḥ |
utpanne tv aurase putre tṛtīyāṃśaharāh sutāḥ |
savarṇā asavarṇās tu grāsācchādanabhāginaḥ ||
tathā ca bṛhaspatiḥ |
sarve hy anausasyaite putrā dāyaharāḥ smṛtāḥ |
aurase punar utpanne teṣu jyaiṣṭhyaṃ na tiṣṭhati ||
teṣāṃ savarṇā ye putrāḥ te tṛtīyāṃśabhāginaḥ |
hīnās tam upajīveyuḥ grāsācchādanasaṃbhṛtāḥ ||
kiṃ bahunā |
eka evaurasaḥ putraḥ pitryasya vasunaḥ prabhuḥ |
śeṣāṇām ānṛśaṃsyārthaṃ pradadyāt tu prajīvanam ||
idam aurasapraśaṃsāparam iti kecit | yat tu nāradavacanam,
kānīnaś ca sahoḍhaś ca ūḍhāyāṃ yaś ca jāyate |
teṣāṃ voḍhā pitā jñeyaḥ te ca bhāgaharāḥ pituḥ ||
iti, tat teṣāṃ ṣaḍbandhudāyādā ity uktabandhudāyādaṣaṭke kānīnādīnāṃ antarbhāvārtham | bṛhaspatiḥ |
putrās trayodaś proktā manunā ye ‘nupūrvaśaḥ |
santānakāraṇaṃ teṣām aurasaḥ putrikā tathā ||
ājyaṃ vinā yathā tailaṃ sadbhiḥ pratinidhiḥ smṛtaḥ |
tathaikādaśa putrās tu putrikaurasayor vinā ||
atha putrātideśaḥ | tatra manuḥ |
bhrātṝṇām ekajātānām ekaś cet putravān bhavet |
sarve te tena putreṇa putriṇo manur abravīt ||
hārītaḥ |
bahūnām ekajātānāṃ yady ekaḥ putravān bhavet |
sarve te tena putreṇa putravanto na saṃśayaḥ ||
bahvīnām ekapatnīnāṃ yady ekā putriṇī bhavet |
sarvās tās tena putreṇa labhante putriṇāṃ gatim ||
manunā pratinidhir uktaḥ, hārītena pratinidhyabhāva uktaḥ, “labhante putriṇāṃ gatim” iti vacanāt | bṛhaspatiḥ |
yady ekajātā bahavo bhrātaras tu sahodarāḥ |
ekasyāpi sute jāte sarve te putriṇaḥ smṛtāḥ ||
bahvīnām ekapatnīnām eṣa eva vidhiḥ smrtaḥ |
ekā cat putriṇī tv āsāṃ sarvāsāṃ piṇḍadas tu saḥ ||
putratvādideśavākyānām ebhir evordhvadaihikam ity asminn arthe tātparyam iti vṛddhāḥ | tatra saṃskartur dakṣiṇāprekṣāyām āha dakṣaḥ |
atha ced dakṣiṇārthī syāt saṃskartā mṛtavit tataḥ |
gosahastraṃ śataṃ vāpi vindate yadi pañca vā ||
prajāpatiḥ |
arthavantaṃ guruṃ vānyaṃ sahasraṃ vā śataṃ gavām |
yo dahet pratigṛhṇīyāt ity uvāca prajāpatiḥ ||
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
pramītasya hi gurvādeḥ ye kurvanti dvijātayaḥ |
saṃskārodakadānāni brahmalokaṃ prayānti te ||
bharadvājaḥ |
atha cet dakṣiṇārthī syāt saṃskartā mṛtavit tataḥ |
aṃśaṃ hareta daśamaṃ pañcamaṃ sarvam eva vā ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
anyo yadi dahet kaścit putrāc chiṣyāc ca sodarāt |
saṃskārodakakṛd vipraḥ pramītasya tu taddhanāt ||
daśamāṃśaṃ hared arthī pañcamaṃ sarvam eva vā |
bahurakṣyasya daśamam alparakṣyasya pañcamam ||
aputrapitṛbhāryasya sarvam eveti śaunakaḥ |
anyo yadi dahet kaścit putrāc chiṣyac ca taddhanāt |
gosahasraṃ śataṃ vārthād gṛhṇīyāt tasya dakṣiṇām ||
vṛddhavasiṣṭhaḥ |
sapiṇdam asapiṇḍaṃ vā dahed yo dhaninaṃ gurum | iti |
ṛṇaṃ datvaiva vibhājyam iti sa evāha |
bhrātṛmātṛpitṛvyair yat kuṭumbārtham ṛṇaṃ kṛtam |
athāvibhājyam
atra manuḥ |
vydyādhanaṃ tu yady asya tat tasyaiva dhanaṃ bhavet |
maitram audvāhikaṃ caiva mādhuparkikam eva ca ||
atra yājñavalkyaḥ |
kramād abhyāgataṃ dravyaṃ kṛtam abhyuddharet tu yaḥ |
dāyādebhyo na tad tadyād vidyayā labdham eva ca ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
śauryaprāptaṃ vidyayā ca strīdhanaṃ caiva yat smṛtam |
etat sarvaṃ vibhāge tu naiva vaibhājyam ṛkthinā ||
nāvidyānāṃ tu vaidyena deyam vidhādhanāt kvacit |
samavidyādhikānāṃ tu deyaṃ vaidyena taddhanam ||
nāradaḥ |
vaidyo ‘vaidyāya nākāmo dadyād aṃśaṃ svato dhanāt |
pitṛdravyaṃ samāśritya na cet tena tadā hṛtam ||
śauryabhāryādhane cobhe yac ca vidyādhanaṃ bhavet |
trīṇy etāny avibhājyāni prasādo yaś ca paitṛkaḥ ||
mātrā ca svadhanaṃ dattaṃ yasmai syāt prītipūrvakam |
tasyāpy eṣa vidhir dṛṣṭo mātāpīṣṭe yathā pitā ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
vastrālaṃkāraśayyādi pitur yad vāhanādikam |
gandhamālyaiḥ samabhyarcya śrāddhabhoktre nivedayet ||
strīṣu ca saṃyuktāsu |
kṣetraṃ pūrtaṃ yogam iṣṭam ity āhus tattvadarśinaḥ |
avibhājye ca te prokte śayanāsanam eva ca ||
pūrvanaṣṭāṃ tu yo bhūmiṃ ekaś ced uddharet pumān |
yathābhāgaṃ labhante ‘nye datvāṃśaṃ tu turīyakam ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
pitṛdravyāvirodhena yad anyat svayam ārjitam |
maitram audvāhikaṃ caiva dāyādānāṃ tu tad bhavet ||
tathā |
pitṛbhyāṃ yasya yad dattaṃ tat tasyaiva dhanaṃ bhavet |
bṛhaspatiḥ |
ptāmahapitṛbhyāṃ ca dattaṃ mātrā ca yad bhavet |
tasya tan nāpahartavyaṃ śauryabhāryādhanaṃ tathā ||
manu-viṣṇū |
anupaghnan pitṛdravyaṃ śrameṇa yad upārjayet |
svayam īhitalabdhaṃ tat nākāmo dātum arhati ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
parabhaktapradānena prāptavidyo yadānyataḥ |
tayā prāptaṃ tu vidhinā vidyāprāptaṃ tad ucyate ||
upanyaste tu yal labdhaṃ vidyayā paṇapūrvakam |
vidyādhanaṃ tu tad vidyāt vibhāge na niyojyate ||
śiṣyād ārtvijyataḥ praśnāt saṃdighapraśnanirṇayāt |
vijñānaśāsanād vādāt labdhaṃ pratyāyanāc ca tat ||
vidyādhanaṃ tu tat prāhuḥ vibhāge na vibhajyate |
śilpeṣv api hi dharmo ‘yaṃ mūlyād yac cādhikaṃ bhavet ||
vidyāpaṇakṛtaṃ caiva yājyataḥ śiṣyatas tathā |
etad vidhyādhanaṃ prāhuḥ sāmānyaṃ yad ato ‘nyathā ||
vaivāhikaṃ tu tad vidyāt bhāryayā yat samāgatam |
dhanam evaṃvidhaṃ sarvaṃ vijñeyaṃ dharmasādhakam ||
manu-viṣṇū |
vastraṃ pātram alaṃkāraṃ kṛtānnam udakaṃ striyaḥ |
yogakṣemapracāraṃ ca na vibhājyaṃ pracakṣate ||
kuṭumbaṃ bibhṛyād bhrātuḥ yo vidyām adhigacchataḥ |
bhāgaṃ vidyādhanāt tasmāt sa labhetāśruto ‘pi san ||
manuḥ |
yat kiṃcit patari prete dhanaṃ jyeṣṭho ‘dhigacchati |
bhāgo yavīyasāṃ tatra yadi vidyānpālitaḥ ||
avidyānāṃ tu sarveṣām īhātaś ced dhanaṃ bhavet |
samas tatra vibhāgaḥ syād apitrya iti dhāraṇā ||
svayam utpādite ārjakasyāṃśadvayam āha vasiṣṭhaḥ : “yena caiṣāṃ svayam utpāditaṃ syāt sa dvyaṃśam eva haret” | vyāsaḥ |
sādhāraṇaṃ samāśritya yat kiṃcid vāhanāyudham |
śauryādināpnoti dhanaṃ bhrātaras tatra bhāginaḥ ||
tasya bhāgadvayaṃ deyaṃ śeṣās tu samabhāginaḥ |
atha vibhājyam
kātyāyanaḥ |
paitāmahaṃ ca pitryaṃ ca yat kiṃcit svayam ārjitam |
dāyādānāṃ vibhāge tu sarvam etad vibhajyate ||
rikthaṃ pratipradānaṃ tu datvā śeṣaṃ vibhājayet |
ā caturthāt tu tad grāhyaṃ krameṇaiva tu tatsutaiḥ ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
gṛhopaskāravāhyādidohyābharaṇakarmiṇaḥ |
dṛśyamānā vibhajyante gūḍhe kośo vidhīyate ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
kule vinītavidyānāṃ bhrātṝṇāṃ pitṛto ‘tha vā |
tayā prāptaṃ tu tad vittaṃ vibhājyaṃ tad bṛhaspatiḥ ||
tasmāt putrapautrādibhir vibhāgaḥ kartavyaḥ | atra sa eva viśeṣam āha |
samutpannād dhanād ardhaṃ tadarthe sthāpayet pṛthak |
māsaṣāṇmāsike śrāddhe vārṣike ca prayatnataḥ ||
atra viṣṇuḥ: “yaḥ sa cārthaharaḥ sa piṇḍado yo yasya ādadīta sa tasmai śrāddhaṃ kuryāt | prāptau tripuruṣaṃ dadyāt” iti |
jaṅgamaṃ sthāvaraṃ hemarūpyadhānyarasāmbaram |
ādāya dāpayec chrāddhe māsaṣāṇmāsikābdike ||
śayyāsanavastrachatropanadaṅgulīyakāny api śrāddhe deyātni | anyasy adhanasya vibhāgaḥ kartavyaḥ |
putrābhāve dāyagrahaṇam
atra viṣṇuḥ: “anapatyasya pramītasya dhanaṃ patnyabhigāmi | tadabhāve duhitṛgāmi | tadabhāve pitṛgāmi | tadabhāve mātṛgāmi | tadabhāve bhrātṛgāmi | tadabhāve bhrātṛputragāmi | tadabhāve sakulyagāmi | tadabhāve bandhugāmi | tadabhāve sahādhyāyigāmi | tadabhāve brāhmaṇadhanavarjaṃ rājagāmi | brāhmaṇadhanaṃ brāhmaṇā eva gṛhṇīyuḥ” | bṛhaspatiḥ |
bhāryāsutavihīnasya puruṣasya mṛtasya tu |
mātā rikthaharā jñeyā bhrātā vā tadanujñayā ||
atra vṛddhamanuḥ |
aputrā śayanaṃ bhartuḥ pālayantī vrate sthitā |
patnyaiva dadyāt tatpiṇḍaṃ kṛtsnam aṃśaṃ labheta ca ||
prajāpatiḥ |
āmnāye smṛtitantre ca lokācāre ca sūribhiḥ |
śarīrārdhaṃ smṛtā jāyā puṇyāpuṇyaphale samā ||
yasya noparatā bhāryā dehārdhaṃ tasya jīvati |
jīvaty ardhaśarīre ‘rthaṃ katham anyaḥ samāpnuyāt ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
bhartur dhanaharī patnī tāṃ vinā duhitā smṛtā |
tadabhāve bhrātṛsutā sakulyā bāndhavās tathā ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
aputrasyātha kulajā patnī duhitaro ‘pi vā |
tadabhāve pitā mātā bhrātā putrāś ca kīrtitāḥ ||
pitāmahaḥ |
kulyeṣu vidyamāneṣu pitṛbhrātṛsanābhiṣu |
asutasya pramītasya patnī tadbhāgahāriṇī ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
pūrvaṃ mṛtā hared agnim anvārūḍhā hared agham |
tatsapiṇḍā bāndhavā vā ye tasyāḥ paripanthinaḥ ||
hiṃsyur dhanāni tān rājā coradaṇḍena daṇḍayet |
atra yājñavalkyaḥ |
patnī duhitaraś caiva pitarau bhrātaras tathā |
tatsutā gotrajā bandhuśiṣyasabrahmacāriṇaḥ ||
eṣām abhāve pūrvasya dhanabhāg uttarottaraḥ |
svaryātasya hy aputrasya sarvavarṇeṣv ayaṃ vidhiḥ ||
aputrasya svaryātasya vibhaktasya asaṃsṛṣṭino dhanaṃ pariṇītā saṃyatā yajñasaṃyuktā strī sakalam eva gṛhṇāti | bahvyaś cet sajātīyā vijātīyāśca yathāṃśaṃ vibhajya dhanaṃ gṛhṇanti | tatra viśeṣam āha vyāsaḥ |
dvisārasrāt paro dāyaḥ striyai deyo mṛtasya tu |
yac ca bhartrā dhanaṃ dattaṃ sā yathākāmam āpnuyāt ||
dvisāhasrāt param api bhartrā dattaṃ yathākāmam āpnuyāt | adatte tu dvisāhasram eva dhanaṃ patnyā grāhyam, nādhikam | patnyabhāve duhitānūḍhaiva gṛhṇāti | tathā ca katyāyanaḥ |
patnī bhartur dhanaharī yā syād avyabhicāriṇī |
tadabhāve tu duhitā yady anūḍhā bhavet tadā ||
tathā ca devalaḥ |
kanyābhyaś ca pitṛdravyaṃ deyaṃ vaivāhikaṃ vasu |
aputrakasya svaṃ kanyā dharmajā putravad dharet ||
anūḍhāyā eva sāpiṇḍyam, “anantaraḥ sapiṇḍo yaḥ tasya (tasya) dhanaṃ haret” iti manuvacanāt | “putrābhāve yaḥ pratyāsannaḥ sapiṇḍaḥ” ity āpastambavacanāc ca anantarasya sapiṇḍasyaiva dhanagrahaṇaṃ pratīyate | anūḍhāyā abhāve ‘pi asapiṇḍayāpy ūḍhayā grāhyam | tasyā apy ekaśarīrānvayalakṣaṇasya sāpiṇḍyasya sadbhāve pratyāsatteś ca | atra manu-nāradau |
yathaivātmā tathā putraḥ putreṇa duhitā samā |
tasyām ātmani tiṣṭhantyāṃ katham anyo dhanaṃ haret ||
nāradaḥ |
putrābhāve tu duhitā tulyasantānadarśanāt |
putraś ca duhitā cobhau pituḥ santānakārakau ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
bhartur dhanaharī patnī tāṃ vinā duhitā smṛtā |
aṅgād aṅgāt saṃbhavati putravad duhitā nṛṇām ||
tasyāḥ pitṛdhanaṃ tv anyaḥ kathaṃ gṛhṇīta mānavaḥ ||
duhitrabhāve dauhitrā gṛhṇīyuḥ | tathā ca viṣṇuḥ |
aputrapautrasantāno dauhitrā dhanam āpnuyuḥ |
pūrveṣāṃ tu svadhākārye pautrā dauhitrakā matāḥ ||
pautrābhāve pautrakāryaṃ dauhitrair eva kartavyam | tathā ca manuḥ |
pautradauhitrayoḥ kārye na viśeṣo ‘sti dharmataḥ |
tayor hi mātāpitarau saṃbhūtau tasya dehataḥ ||
tathā |
pautradauhitrayor loke viśeṣo nopapadyate |
dauhitre ‘pi hy amutrainaṃ saṃtārayati pautravat ||
smṛtiḥ |
yathaivātmā tathā putraḥ putreṇa duhitā samā |
pautradauhitrayor loke viśeṣo nāsti dharmataḥ ||
manuḥ |
akṛtā vā kṛtā vāpi yaṃ vindat sadṛśaṃ sutam |
pautrī mātāmahas tena dadyāt piṇḍaṃ hared dhanam ||
tathā |
dauhitra eva ca hared aputrasyākhilaṃ dhanam |
tadabhāve mātāpitarau dhanabhājau | tathā ca manuḥ |
anapatyasya putrasya mātā dāyam avāpnuyāt |
tadabhāve pitā, pitror abhāve bhrātaro dhanabhājaḥ | bhrātṛṣv api sodarāḥ prathamaṃ gṛhṇīyuḥ | devalaḥ |
tato dāyam aputrasya vibhajeran sahodarāḥ |
“mātā dāyam avāpnuyāt,” “vibhajeran sahodarāḥ” iti vacanadvayaṃ na kramaparam | kiṃ tu dhanagrahaṇe adhikāramātraparam ity avirodhaḥ sodarāṇām abhāve tatputrā dhanabhājaḥ | tayor abhāve bhinnodarāḥ tatputrāś ca | bhrātṛputrāṇām abhāve tatputrā dhanabhājaḥ | tataḥ paraṃ sāpiṇḍyābhāvāt samānodakāḥ | tadabhāve gotrajā dhanabhājaḥ | tathā ca bodhāyanaḥ: “prapitāmahaḥ pitāmahaḥ pitā svayaṃ sodaryāḥ bhrātaraḥ sarvarṇāyāḥ putraḥ pautraḥ prapautraḥ tān avibhaktadāyādān sapiṇḍān ācakṣate | vibhaktadāyān api sakulyān ācakṣate | tadvaṃśajeṣu tadgāmīty artho bhavati | sapiṇḍābhāve sakulyaḥ | pūrvoktaṃ sāpiṇḍyaṃ pitṛpitāmahaprapitāmaheṣu triṣu jīvatsu pūrveṣāṃ trayāṇām api sāpiṇḍyam iti | saptapuruṣāvadhi sāpiṇḍyam” | tathā ca bṛhaspatiḥ |
sapiṇḍatā tu puruṣe saptame vinivartate |
samānodakabhāvas tu nivartetācaturdaśāt ||
tenaiva kalpāntaram apy uktam |
janmanām asmṛter eke tatparaṃ gotram ucyate | iti |
samānodakānām abhāve sagotrā dhanabhājaḥ | sagotrāṇām abhāve bāndhavāḥ |
bāndhavās trividhā iti baudhāyanaḥ |
ātmapitṛṣvasuḥ putrā ātmamātṛṣvasuḥ sutāḥ |
ātmamātulaputrāś ca vijñeyā ātmabāndhavāḥ ||
pituḥ pitṛṣvasuḥ putrāḥ pitur mātṛṣvasuḥ sutāḥ |
pitur mātulaputrāś ca vijñeyāḥ pitṛbāndhavāḥ ||
mātuḥ pitṛṣvasuḥ putrā mātur bhrātṛṣvasuḥ sutāḥ |
mātur mātulaputrāś ca vijñeyā mātṛbāndhavāḥ ||
sarvabandhūnām abhāve śiṣyaḥ, śiṣyābhāve sabrahmacārī, tadabhāve yaḥ kaścic chrotriyo gṛhṇīyāt | kṣatriyādidhanaṃ sabrahmacāriparyantānām abhāve rājā gṛhṇīyāt | sarvavarṇeṣv ayam eva dāyakramaḥ |
udyotana-asahāyās tv evaṃ manyante |
na bhrātaro na pitaraḥ putrā rikthaharāḥ pituḥ |
pitā hared aputrasya rikthaṃ bhrātara eva vā ||
iti manuvacane “bhrātara eva vā” ity evakārāt putrābhāve bhrātṝṇām eva prathamaṃ dhanagrahaṇaṃ pratīyate | tathā hi – aputrasya svaryātasya bhrātṛgāmi dravyam | tadabhāve mātāpitarau hareyātām | tadabhāve patnī vā jyeṣṭheti śaṅkhalikhitayoḥ vacanāc ca prathamaṃ bhrātṛgāmitvaṃ pratīyate, tathā –
tato dāyam aputrasya vibhajeran sahodarāḥ |
kulyā duhitaro vāpi kriyamāṇaḥ pitaiva vā ||
savarṇā bhrātato mātā bhāryā ceti yathākramam |
teṣām abhāve gṛhṇīyuḥ kulyānāṃ sahavāsinaḥ ||
iti devalavacanāc ca | prathamam anapatyasya sodarabhrātā dhanaṃ gṛhṇīyād iti pratīyeta | sodarabhrātur abhāve mātāpitarau gṛhṇīyātām ity eke | saṃsṛṣṭīty anye | uktānām abhāve bhārety apare | “patnī duhitaraś ca” ityevamādīni yāni patnyāḥ prathamaṃ dhanāharaṇaparāṇi vākyāni tāni sarvāṇi niyuktapatnīviṣayāṇi | yāni punar duhitṝṇāṃ dhanapratipādakāni vākyāni tāni pitrikāviṣaṇīti | anye tu strīṇāṃ na dāyasaṃbandhaḥ, “tasmāt striyo nirindriyā adāyādīḥ” iti śruteḥ,
dhūmāvasānikaṃ grāhyaṃ sabhāyāṃ snānataḥ punaḥ |
purāṇe,
vasanāśanavāsāṃsi nigaṇayya dhave mṛte |
iti vyāsavacanāt, tathā –
anyatra brāhmaṇāt kiṃ tu rājā dharmaparāyaṇaḥ |
tat strīṇāṃ jīvanaṃ dadyād eṣa dāyavidhiḥ smṛtaḥ ||
iti nāradavacanāt,
vidhavā yauvanasthā cen nārī bhavati karkaśā |
āyuṣaḥ kṣapaṇārthaṃ tu dātavyaṃ jīvanaṃ tadā ||
iti bṛhaspativacanāt, tathā –
aputrā yoṣitaś caiṣāṃ bhartavyāḥ sādhuvṛttayaḥ |
iti manuvacanāt, tathā –
āḍhakaṃ bhartṛhīnāyā dātavyaṃ vidhavāśanam |
iti prajāpativacanāt, tathā –
annārthaṃ taṇḍulapratham aparāhṇe tu sendhanam |
iti vacanāc cāputrāyā vidhavāyāḥ jñātibharaṇamātram eva, na dāyaprāptiḥ | dāya prāptis tu jñātīnām eveti manyante |
etat sarvam ayuktam |
pitā hared aputrasya rikthaṃ bhrātara eva vā |
iti manuvacane ‘pi kramapratipādakaśabdābhāvān na prathame patnīvyudāsaḥ | evakārāt pitrapekṣayā bhrātuḥ prāthamyam | tathā –
anapatyasya putrasya mātā dāyam avāpnuyāt |
iti manuvacane ‘pi kramaparaśabdābhāvāt na patnyā vyudāsaḥ | śaṅkhalikhita-devalavacanayoḥ yady api sodarabhrātṝṇāṃ prathamaṃ dhanagrahaṇaṃ pratīyate, tathāpi
“anapatyasya pramītasya dhanaṃ patnyadbhigāmī” iti vaiṣṇavavacanāt,
“bhāryāsutavihīnasya” iti bṛhaspativacanāt,
“aputrā śayanaṃ bhartuḥ” iti vṛddhamanuvacanāt,
“āmnāye smṛtitantre ca” iti prājāpatyavacanāt,
“bhartur dhanaharī patnī” iti bṛhaspativacanāt,
“aputrasyātha kulajā” iti kātyāyanavacanāt,
“kulyeṣu vidyamāneṣu” iti pitāmahavacanāt,
“asutasya pramītasya” iti bṛhaspativacanāt,
“patnī duhtaraś ca” iti yājñavalkyavacanāc ca,
sādhvācārāyāḥ patnyāḥ sakaladhanagrahaṇaṃ prathamaṃ bahubhiḥ vacanaiḥ pratīyata iti, teṣām ānuguṇyena tayor vacanayoḥ vyākhyānaṃ kartavyam | “svaryātasya hy aputrasya bhrātṛgāmi dravyam” iti saṃsṛṣṭabhrātṛgāmīty arthaḥ |
yad vā pāṭhakramavacanenānekakramavacanāvirodhāya patnyā duhitur mātāpitroś ca pūrvānupraveśaḥ kartavyaḥ | patnīdāyaparāṇi sakalavacanāni niyuktapatnīviṣayāṇīti yad uktaṃ tad ayuktam, sarvatra niyogaprastāvābhāvāt | tathā sarvāṇi duhitṛdāyaparāṇi vākyāni putrikāviṣayāṇīty apy ayuktam, putrīkaraṇasyāpi sarvatrāprastutatvāt | yat punar arthavādaḥ “tasmāt striyo nirindriyā adāyādīḥ’ iti tat pātnīvatagrahe patnyāḥ striyā aṃśo nāstīty evaṃparam, “indriyaṃ vai somapīthaḥ” iti indriyaśabdasya some darśanāt | kiṃ ca nātrendriyaśabdo vīryaparaḥ –
pumān puṃso ‘dhke śukle strī bhavaty adhike striyāḥ |
iti strīṇām api vīryavatvadarśanāt | tasmāt striyo nirindiryā iti vaktuṃ na śakyata iti indriyaśabdaḥ somapara eva yuktaḥ | “dhūmāvasānikaṃ grāhyam” ity alpadhane bhartari mṛte vibhāge jīvanāparyāptau jīvanaparyāptaṃ dhūmāvasānikaṃ grāhyam ity ucyate | avibhaktabhartṛviṣayaṃ vā dhūmāvasānikavacanam |
