“yajñārthaṃ dravyam utpannam” ity atra yajñaśabdo dharmamātropalakṣaṇaparaḥ, yajñasyaiva vivakṣitatve dānahomārthatvavirodhāt | tataś ca strīṇām api pūrtādisaṃbhavān na virodhaḥ | “anyatra brāhmaṇāt kiṃcit” ity etad avaruddhastrīviṣayam iti prakaraṇāt gamyate | “vidhavā yaunavasthā cet” iti śaṅkitavyabhicārāyāḥ sakaladhanagrahaṇaniṣedhaparam | “aputrā yoṣitaś caiṣāṃ bhartavyāḥ” iti klībādibhāryāviṣayam iti prakaraṇād avagamyate, patidvāreṇa tāsām arthasaṃbandhābhāvāt bharaṇamātram ucyata ity avirodhaḥ | “āḍhakaṃ bhartṛhīnāyā dātavyam,” tathā “annārthaṃ taṇḍulaprastham” itītyevamādīna vacanāny api śaṅkitavyabhicārastrīviṣayāṇi | yad apīdaṃ nāradīyavacanam,
bharaṇaṃ cāsya kurvīran strīṇām ā jīvitakṣayāt |
iti etat,
saṃsṛṣṭināṃ tu yo bhāgaḥ teṣām eva sa iṣyate |
iti prastutasaṃsṛṣṭistrīṇām anapatyānāṃ bharaṇamātraparam | yad apīdaṃ kātyāyanavacanam,
vibhakte saṃsthite dravyaṃ putrābhāve pitā haret |
bhrātā vā jananī vātha mātā vā tatpituḥ kramāt ||
iti, tad api bhāryāduhitror abhāve draṣṭavyam | bhartari jīvati tena saha sakaladhanasya svāmini tasmin mṛte kathaṃ nirdhanā syāt | tasmāt sādhuvṛttayuktāyāḥ patnyāḥ sakaladhanagrahaṇaṃ yuktam eva |
kecit tu alpadhanaviṣayāṇi patnīdāyavacanāni, mahati dhane jñātidāya eveti manyante |
punarbhūjātau praty āha bhāradvājaḥ |
dvau tu yau vivadeyātāṃ dvābhyāṃ jātau striyā dhane |
tayor yady asya pitryaṃ syāt sa tad gṛhṇīta netaraḥ ||
svotpādakadhanam eva gṛhṇīyātām ity arthaḥ |
strīdhanavibhāgaḥ
atha strīdhanavibhāgaḥ | tatra manuḥ |
jananyā saṃsthitāyāṃ tu samaṃ sarve sahodarāḥ |
bhajeran mātṛkaṃ rikthaṃ bhaginyaś ca sanābhayaḥ || iti |
pitṛdhanavan noddhāraḥ viṣamavibhāgo vā kartavyaḥ | kiṃ tu sodarāṇāṃ puruṣāṇāṃ strīṇāṃ ca mātṛdhane samo vibhāgo itīdam aparaṃ kartavyam ity āha ¦
yas tāsāṃ syur duhitaraḥ tāsām api yathārhataḥ |
mātāmahyā dhanāt kiṃcit pradeyaṃ prītipūrvakam ||
atra viśeṣam āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
strīdhanaṃ tadapatyānāṃ duhitā ca tadaṃśinī |
aprattā cet samūḍhā tu labhate mānamātrakam ||
śaṅkhalikhitau : “samaṃ sarve sahodarā mātṛkaṃ riktham arhanti kumāryaś ca” | gautamaḥ: “strīdhanaṃ duhitṝṇām aprattānām apratiṣṭhitānāṃ ca” | vasiṣṭhaḥ: “mātuḥ pāriṇāhyaṃ striyo vibhajeran” | vibhajerann ūrdhvam ity anuvṛttau, yājñavalkyaḥ |
mātur dihitaraḥ śeṣam ṛṇāt tābhyāṃ matena yaḥ |
duhitṝṇām abhāve tadanvayāḥ putrāḥ gṛhṇīyur ity arthaḥ | nāradaḥ |
mātur duhitaro ‘bhāve duhitṝṇāṃ tadanvayaḥ |
putrāṇāṃ samṛddhatve duhitṝṇām abhāve tadanvayaḥ, mātṛdauhitrā gṛhṇīyur ity arthaḥ | kātyāyanaḥ |
duhitṝṇām abhāve tu rikthaṃ putreṣu tad bhavet |
manuḥ |
mātus tu yautakaṃ yat syāt kumārībhāga eva saḥ |
yautakaṃ mātṛkulalabdhaṃ strīdhanaṃ tat kumāryā eva | bhartṛkulalabdhastrīdhane putrāḥ kanyāś ca sāpiṇḍyāt tulyāḥ | kanyābhāve prattāpy pratiṣṭhitāḥ putraiḥ tulyāḥ apratiṣṭhitāḥ aprajāḥ, “prajā vai pratiṣṭhā” iti brāhmaṇavādāt nirdhanā vā | kramavibhāgastrīdhanaviśeṣam āha manuḥ |
striyās tu yad bhaved vittaṃ pitrā dattaṃ kathaṃcana |
brāhmaṇī tad dharet kanyā tad apatyasya vā bhavet ||
kramavivāhe brāhmaṇīvyatiriktāsu aprajāsu mṛtāsu tāsāṃ dhanaṃ brāhmaṇīprasūtā kanyakā haret | tatputro vā na tu bhartrādayaḥ | pitrā dattam iti strīdhanamātropalakṣaṇam | atra pāraskaraḥ |
aprattāyās tu duhituḥ strīdhanaṃ parikīrtitam |
putras tu naiva labhate prattāyāṃ tu samāṃśabhāk ||
strīdhanalakṣaṇam āha manuḥ |
adhyagnyadhyāhavanikaṃ dattaṃ ca prītikarmaṇi |
bhrātṛmātṛpitṛprāptaṃ ṣaḍvidhaṃ strīdhanaṃ smṛtam ||
atra viśeṣam āha nāradaḥ |
adhyagnyadhyāhavanikaṃ bhartṛdāyaṃ tathaiva ca |
bhrātṛdattaṃ pitṛbhyāṃ ca ṣaḍvidhaṃ strīdhanaṃ smṛtam ||
atra yājñavalkyaḥ |
pitṛmātṛpatibhrātṛdattam adhyagnyupāgatam |
ādhivedanikādyaṃ ca strīdhanaṃ parikīrtitam ||
atra viṣṇuḥ: “pitṛmātṛsutabhrātṛdattam adhyagnyupāgatam ādhivedanikaṃ bandhudattaṃ śulkam agnyādheyikam iti strīdhanam” | devalaḥ |
vṛttir ābharaṇaṃ śulkaṃ lābhaṃ ca strīdhanaṃ bhavet |
kātyāyanaḥ |
prāptaṃ śilpais tu yad vittaṃ prītyā caiva yad anvitam |
bhartuḥ svāmyaṃ dhane tatra śeṣaṃ tu strīdhanaṃ smṛtam ||
bhartur anyato bandhubhyaḥ prītyā yal labdham ity arthaḥ | svaśilpaiḥ prāpte, bhartṛvyatiriktabandhūnāṃ sakāśāt prītyā prāpte ca śeṣe cādhyagnyādilakṣaṇe dhane strīṇāṃ svāmyaṃ strīdhanam ity arthaḥ | adhyagnyādisvarūpaṃ sa evāha |
vivāhakāle yaḥ strībhyo dīyate hy agnisaṃnidhau |
tad adhyagnikṛtaṃ sadbhiḥ strīdhanaṃ parikīrtitam ||
yat punar labhate nārī nīyamānā pitur gṛhāt |
adhyāhavanikaṃ nāma strīdhanaṃ samudāhṛtam ||
prītyā pradattaṃ yat kiṃcit śvaśrvā vā śvaśureṇa vā |
pādavandanikaṃ yat tat lāvaṇyārjitam ucyate ||
vivāhāt parato yat tu labdhaṃ bandhukulāt striyā |
anvādheyaṃ tad uktaṃ tu labdhaṃ bandhukulāt tathā ||
gṛhopaskaravāhyānāṃ dohyābharaṇakarmiṇām |
mūlyaṃ labdhaṃ tu yat kiṃcit tac chulkaṃ parikīrtitam ||
aputrāyā api vivāhabhedena dāyādān āha manuḥ |
anvādheyaṃ ca yad vittaṃ patyā prītena caiva yat |
patyau jīvati vṛttāyāḥ prajāyās taddhanaṃ bhavet ||
brāhmadaivārṣagāndharvaprājāpatyeṣu yad dhanam |
aprajāyām atītāyāṃ bhartur eva tad iṣyate ||
ukteṣu pañcavidheṣu vivāheṣv aprajāyām atītāyāṃ bhartur eva strīdhanaṃ bhavatīty arthaḥ |
yasyai tu syād dhanaṃ dattaṃ vivāheṣv āsurādiṣu |
atītāyām aprajāyāṃ mātāpitros tad iṣyate ||
āsurapaiśśācarākṣaseṣu triṣu vivāheṣu aprayāyām atītāyāṃ mātāpitṛbhyāṃ dattaṃ mātāpitror eva bhavatīty arthaḥ | devalaḥ |
sāmānyaṃ putrakanyānāṃ mṛtāyāṃ strīdhanaṃ striyām |
aprajāyāṃ hared bhartā mātā bhrātā pitāpi vā ||
pūrvārdhena mātari mṛtāyām aṣṭavidheṣu vivāheṣu strīdhane putrakanyāyāṃ samagrahaṇam ucyate | uttarārdhenāsurādiṣu vivāheṣu taddhanaṃ pradātṛbhir evādeyam ucyate | bhartṛdattaṃ bhartā haret | mātrādibhir dattaṃ tair evādeyam iti | kātyāyanaḥ |
duhitṝṇām abhāve tu rikthaṃ putreṣu tad bhavet |
bandhudattaṃ tu bandhūnām abhāve bhartṛgāmi tat ||
bhaginyo bāndhavaiḥ sārdhaṃ vibhajeran sabhartṛkāḥ |
strīdhanasyeti dharmyo ‘yaṃ vibhāge tu prakalpitaḥ ||
duhitṝṇām abhāve ityādyaṣṭavidhavivāhaviṣayam | bandhudattam ityāsurādivivāhaviṣayam | putrāṇām abhāve bandhubhir mātulādibhir dattaṃ teṣām eva | bandhūnām abhāve tair dattaṃ bhartṛgāmi |
pakṣāntaram āha “bhaginyaḥ” iti ardhena | bandhuṣu satsu bandhudattaṃ “sabhartṛkā bhaginyo bāndhavaiḥsaha vibhajeran” iti | atra kātyāyanaḥ |
āsurādiṣu yal labdhaṃ paitṛkaṃ strīdhanaṃ striyā |
abhāve tadapatyānāṃ mātāpitros tad iṣyate ||
yamaḥ |
āsurādiṣu yad dravyaṃ vivāheṣu pradīyate |
aprajāyām atītāyāṃ pitaiva tu dhanaṃ haret ||
gautamaḥ: “bhaginīśulkaṃ sodaryāṇām ūrdhvaṃ mātuḥ | pūrvaṃ caike” | yājñavalkyaḥ |
bandhudattaṃ tathā śulkam anvādheyikam eva ca |
aprajāyām atītāyāṃ bāndhavās tad avāpnuyuḥ ||
tatra śulkaṃ dvividham | kanyāvikrayamūlyatvena kanyāvadbhyo dattam ekam | tan mātṛgāmi bhrātṛgāmi vā | kanyābhūṣaṇagṛhopakaraṇamūlyatvena dattam aparaṃ śūlkam | tad dātṛbhir evādeyam | atra bodhāyanaḥ |
aprajāyāṃ mṛtāyāṃ tu putrikāyāṃ kathaṃcana |
dhanaṃ tatputrikābhartā haretaivāvicārayan ||
tathā devala-bodhāyanau |
rikthaṃ mṛtāyāṃ kanyāyāṃ gṛhṇīyuḥ sodarāḥ samam |
tadabhāve bhaven mātuḥ pitur mātuś ca tatkramāt ||
manuḥ |
patyau jīvati yaḥ strībhir alaṃkāro dhṛto bhavet |
na taṃ bhajeran dāyādāḥ bhajamānāḥ patanti te ||
alaṅmārasyādattatvenāstrīdhanatvāt bhartṛdhanatvena mṛte bhartari putrāṇāṃ vibhāge prāpte, tadapavādo ‘nena kriyate | kātyāyanaḥ |
strīṇāṃ bhartṛkulāl labdhaṃ pituḥ kulata eva vā |
bhūṣaṇaṃ na vibhājyaṃ syāt jīvane ca na yojayet ||
āpastambaḥ: “alaṃkāro bhāryāyā jñātidhanaṃ cety eke” | prītidattasyālaṃkārasya svatve prāpte sthāvare ‘pavādam āha nāradaḥ |
bhartrā prītena yad dattaṃ striyai tasmin mṛte ‘pi tat |
sā yathākāmam aśnīyāt dadyād vā sthāvarād ṛte ||
“prītidattaṃ sthāvaram” dātari mṛte striyāḥ na svaṃ bhavatīty arthaḥ | kecit tu prītidattaṃ sthāvaram ap svam eva | anumantur abhāve dānayogyaṃ na bhavatīty ucyata iti vadanti | atra viśeṣam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
durbhikṣe dharmakārye ca vyādhau saṃpratirodhake |
gṛhītaṃ strīdhanaṃ bhartrā nākāmo dātum arhati ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
naiva bhartā naiva suto na pitā bhrātaro na ca |
ādāne vā visarge vā strīdhane prabhaviṣṇavaḥ ||
yadi hy anyataro hy eṣāṃ strīdhanaṃ bhakṣayed balat |
savṛddhikaṃ prakāpyaḥ syāt daṇḍaṃ caiva samāpnuyāt ||
tad eva yady anujñāpya bhakṣayet prītipūrvakam |
mūlyam eva sa dāpyaḥ syād yadāsau dhanavān bhavet ||
vyādhitaṃ vyasanārtaṃ ca dhanikair vopapīḍitam |
jñātvā nisṛṣṭaṃ yat prītyā dadyād ātmecchayā tu saḥ ||
devalaḥ |
vṛttir ābharaṇaṃ śūlkaṃ lābhaṃ ca strīdhanaṃ bhavet |
bhoktrī tat svayam evedaṃ patir nārhaty anāpadi ||
vṛthā mokṣe ca bhoge ca striyai dadyāt savṛddhikam |
putrārtiharaṇe cāpi strīdhanaṃ bhoktum arhati ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
apakārakriyāyuktā nirlajjā cārthanāśikā |
vyabhicāraratā yā ca strīdhanaṃ na ca sārhati ||
bhratrā pratiśrutaṃ deyam ṛṇavat strīdhanaṃ sutaiḥ |
tiṣṭheta bhartṛkule yā vā sā vā pitṛkule vaset ||
vṛddhamanuḥ |
svabhartṛlabdhaṃ rikthaṃ yat vinā śulkaṃ tadarhaṇam |
śeṣaṃ vibhāgaṃ kurvīta sā bhavaty aṃśinī khalu ||
kavaṣaḥ |
kanyāvikrayamūlyaṃ yad arhaṇaṃ śulkam eva vā |
tadvināśaṃ prakurvīta sā bhavaty aṃśinī khalu ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
ūḍhayā kanyayā vāpi bhartuḥ pitṛgṛhe ‘pi vā |
bhrātuḥ sakāśāt pitror vā labdhaṃ saudāyikaṃ smṛtam ||
saudāyikaṃ dhanaṃ prāpya trīṇāṃ svātantryam iṣyate |
yasmāt tad ānṛśaṃsyārthaṃ tair dattaṃ tat prajīvanam ||
saudāyike sadā strīṇāṃ svātantryaṃ praikīrtitam |
vikraye caive dāne ca yatheṣṭaṃ sthāvareṣv api ||
āpadi bhartṛputrādyanumatyabhāve ‘pi vikrayādiṣu na doṣa ity abhiprāyaḥ | atra yājñavalkyaḥ |
adhivinnaḥ striyai dadyād ādhivedanikaṃ samam |
na dattaṃ strīdhanaṃ yasyai datte tv ardhaṃ prakalpayet ||
atra ardhaśabdaḥ paripūrakāṃśaparaḥ | kātyāyanaḥ |
atha cet sa dvibhāryaḥ syāt na ca tāṃ bhajate punaḥ |
prītyā nisṛṣṭam api cet pratidāpyaḥ sa tadbalāt ||
grāsācchādanavāsānām ācchedo yatra dṛśyate |
tatra svāny ādadīta strī vibhāge rikthināṃ tathā ||
likhitasyaiva dharmo ‘yaṃ prāpte pitṛkule vaset |
vyādhitā pretakāle tu gacched bhartṛkulaṃ tathā ||
atra viśeṣam āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
mātṛṣvasā mātulānī pitṛvyastrī pitṛṣvasā |
śvaśrūḥ pūrvapatnī ca mātṛtulyāḥ prakīrtitāḥ ||
yady āsām auraso na syāt suto dauhitra eva vā |
tatsuto vā dhanaṃ tāsāṃ svasrīyādyāḥ samāpnuyuḥ ||
caturthaparyantasapiṇḍeṣu satsu idaṃ vacanaṃ na pravartate | pañcamaprabhṛtyasapiṇḍeṣu satsu idaṃ vacanaṃ pravartata iti vyākhyātṛbhir uktam |
anyair evaṃ vyavasthā kṛta | mātṛṣvasṛprabhṛtīnāṃ ṣaṇṇāṃ prati saṃbandhiṣu svasrīyādiṣu ṣaṭsu prāpteṣu yatrāprajastrīdhanaviṣaye bhartuḥ prāptiḥ tatra tadabhāve bhartṛdvāreṇa saṃbandhiṣu triṣu pratyāsattyatiśayavaśena prathamaṃ bhartur anujo ‘rhati pūrvajapatnīdhanam | tadabhāve tasyāḥ bhrātuḥ putro ‘rhati | tadabhāve jāmātārhati | svasrīyādyā ity apatyapratyayenāpatyamātrapratīteḥ strīṇāṃ puruṣāṇāṃ ca dāyasaṃbandho yuta iti kecit |
anye tu strīpuṃsasādhāraṇātideśe ‘pi puṃsy eva prathamaṃ pratītyudayāt, “tasmāt striyo nirindriyāḥ” ity arthavādadarśanāc ca puruṣāṇām eva dāyasaṃbandho na strīṇām iti manyante |
saṃsṛṣṭānāṃ dāyaḥ
atha saṃsṛṣṭināṃ dāyaḥ | tatra manuḥ |
vibhaktāḥ saha jīvanto vibhajeran punar yadi |
samas tatra vibhāgaḥ syāt jyaiṣṭyaṃ tatra na vidyate ||
saṃsṛṣṭināṃ vibhāge jyeṣṭhāṃśoddhāro na kartavya ity arthaḥ |
yeṣāṃ jyeṣṭhaḥ kaniṣṭho vā hīyetāṃśapradānataḥ |
mriyetānyataro vāpi tasya bhāgo na lupyate ||
mahāpātakino nāṃśapradāho iti vacanād yeṣāṃ puruṣāṇāṃ jyeṣṭhaḥ kaniṣṭho vā brahmahatyadinā aṃśapradānān nivṛttaḥ hīyeta vā tasya bhāgo na lupyate | na saṃsṛṣṭino gṛhṇīyuḥ | kathaṃ tarhi kartavyam ity apekṣāyām āha sa eva |
sodaryā vibhajeyus taṃ sametya sahitāḥ samam |
bhrātaro ye ca saṃsṛṣṭā bhaginyaś ca sanābhayaḥ ||
patitasya mṛtasya pravrajitasya vā bhāgaṃ sodaryāḥ saṃbhūya vibhajeyuḥ | bhrātaro ye ca saṃsṛṣṭā asodarāḥ te asaṃsṛṣṭaiḥ samānodaraiḥ saha vibhajeyuḥ | sodaryā asyārthasaṃsargābhāve ‘py udarasaṃsargād grahaṇam | bhinnodarasyodarasaṃsargābhāvenāpy arthasaṃsargād grahaṇam iti | tathā mṛtasya sanābhayo ‘pi bhaginyaḥ pūrvoktasodarasaṃsargibhyāṃ saha samaṃ vibhajeyur iti | bhrātaro ye ca saṃsṛṣṭā iti bhrātṛgraham anyena pitrā pitṛvyeṇa vā saṃsarge tatpatnyādiprāptyartham iti ācāryaviśvarūpair uktam | atha yājñavalkyaḥ |
saṃsṛṣṭinas tu saṃsṛṣṭī sodarasya tu sodaraḥ |
dadyāc cāpaharec cāṃśaṃ jātasya ca mṛtasya ca ||
vibhaktaṃ dhanaṃ punar miśrīkṛtaṃ saṃsṛṣṭam | tad asyāstīti saṃsṛṣṭī | tatra saṃsṛṣṭiṣu niyamaṃ bṛhaspatir āha |
vibhakto yaḥ punaḥ pitrā bhrātrā vaikatra saṃsthitaḥ |
pitṛvyeṇātha vā prītyā sa tu saṃsṛṣṭa ucyate ||
tasya saṃsṛṣṭino mṛtasyāṃśaṃ vibhāgakāle avijñātagarbhāyāṃ bhāryāyāṃ paścād utpannasya putrasya saṃsṛṣṭī dadyāt | tathāvidhaputrābhāve mṛtāṃśaṃ saṃsṛṣṭyevāharet na patnyādi | sodarasya saṃsṛṣṭino mṛtasyāśaṃ sodaraḥ saṃsṛṣṭijātasya dadyāt | putrābhāve svayam evāpahared iti | saṃsṛṣṭiny aputre svaryāte saṃsṛṣṭino bhinnodarasyāsaṃsṛṣṭinaḥ samānodarasya ca sadbhāve kathaṃ dhanasya haraṇam ity apekṣāyām āha |
anyodaryas tu saṃsṛṣṭī nānyodaryadhanaṃ haret |
asaṃsṛṣṭy api cādadyāt saṃsṛṣṭo nānyamātṛjaḥ ||
anyodaryaḥ sāpatno bhrātā saṃsṛṣṭidhanaṃ na kṛtsnaṃ haret, api tv ardham | asaṃsṛṣṭy ardhāntaram ādadīta | ko ‘sāv asaṃsṛṣṭī ity āśaṅkāyām āha, saṃsṛṣṭa iti | ekodaraḥ saṃsṛṣṭaḥ sodarya iti yāvat | nānyamātṛja ity atraiva śabdādhyāhāreṇa vyākhyānaṃ kartavyam | saṃsṛṣṭy anyamātṛja eva saṃsṛṣṭino dhanaṃ nādadīta | api tu asaṃsṛṣṭy api sodara ādadīta | atrāpi śabdaśravaṇāt nānyamātṛja evety avadhāraṇam niṣedhenāvagamyate | tenādhyāhāraḥ kartavyaḥ | saṃsṛṣṭiviṣaye patnīdāyanivṛtte tāsām ābharaṇāt bharaṇam kartavyam |
tathā saṃsṛṣṭinaṃ praty āha nāradaḥ |
bharaṇaṃ cāsya kurvīran strīṇām ā jīvitakṣayāt | iti |
yad idaṃ saṃsṛṣṭino dhanagrahaṇam uktaṃ tatputrapatnīduhitṝṇām abhāve iti kecit | tathā bṛhaspatiḥ |
yadā kaścit pramīyeta pravrajed vā kathaṃcana |
na lupyate tasya bhāgaḥ sodarasya vidhīyate ||
yā tasya bhaginī sā tu tato ‘ṃśaṃ labdhum arhati |
anapatyasya dharmo ‘yam abhāryāpitṛkasya ca ||
nanu,
bharaṇaṃ cāsya kurvīran strīṇām ā jīvitakṣayāt |
rakṣanti śayyāṃ bhartuś ced ācchindyur itarāsu ca ||
iti nāradavacanāt, bhāryāsadbhāva eva saṃsṛṣṭino dhanagrahaṇam iti gamyate | satyam, patnī dāyāyogyā strīṣu nāradavacanam ity avirodhaḥ | yāsu smṛtiṣu yoṣidvidhavānārīstrībhāryādiśabdaprayogaḥ, tāsu tāsāṃ bharaṇam eva | yāsu smṛtiṣu patnīśabdaprayogaḥ tāsu dāyagrahaṇam iti vṛddhāḥ | atra viśeṣam āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
saṃsṛṣṭināṃ tu yaḥ kaścit vidyāśauryādinā dhanam |
prāpnoti tasya dātavyau dvyaṃśau śeṣāḥ samāṃśinaḥ ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
saṃsṛṣṭānāṃ tu saṃsṛṣṭā pṛthaksthānāṃ pṛthaksthitāḥ |
abhāve ‘rthaharā jñeyā nirbījānyonyabhāginaḥ ||
saṃsṛṣṭānāṃ kasyacid aputrasya maraṇe saṃsṛṣṭina eva dhanam | tathā asaṃsṛṣṭānāṃ kasyacit aputrasya maraṇe asaṃsṛṣṭānām eva dhanam | teṣām abhāve itareṣām api | saṃsṛṣṭeṣu nibījeṣu niranvayamṛteṣu asaṃsṛṣṭās taddhanahāriṇaḥ | tathā asaṃsṛṣṭeṣu nirbījeṣu saṃsṛṣṭās taddhanahāriṇaḥ | tatra viśeṣam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
vānaprasthayatibrahmacāriṇāṃ rikthabhāginaḥ |
krameṇācāryasacchiṣyadharmabhrātraikatīrthinaḥ ||
atra bhagavān viṣṇuḥ: “vānaprasthadhanam ācāryo gṛhṇīyāt śiṣyo vā” |
iti śrīvaradarājīye vyavahāranirṇaye dāyavibhāgakāṇḍaḥ samāptaḥ
12. dyūtasamāhvayādiṣaṭpadakāṇḍam
dyūtasamāhvayam
athadyūtasamāhvayākhyam ucyate | tayor lakṣaṇam āha manuḥ |
aprāṇibhir yat kriyate tal loke dyūtam ucyate |
prāṇibhiḥ kriyate yat tu sa vijñeyaḥ samāhvayaḥ ||
nāradaḥ |
akṣavadhriśalākādyaiḥ devanaṃ jalpakāritam |
paṇakrīḍāvayobhiś ca padaṃ dyūtasamāhvayam ||
anyonyaṃ parigṛhṇītām pakṣimeṣamṛgādayaḥ |
praharante kṛtapaṇāḥ te vadanti samāhvayam ||
tatra aprāṇibhiḥ akṣādibhir yat devanaṃ paṇapūrvakaṃ kriyate tad dyūtam | prāṇibhiḥ mallameṣamahiṣakukkuṭādibhiḥ paṇapūrvaṃ kriyamāṇaḥ samāhvaya ity ucyata ity arthaḥ |
manuḥ |
dyūtaṃ samāhvayaṃ caiva rājā rāṣṭre nivārayet |
rājyasyāntakarāv etau davau doṣau pṛthivīkṣitām ||
dyūtāt purātane kalpe dṛṣṭaṃ vairataraṃ mahat |
tasmād dyūtaṃ na seveta hāsyārtham api buddhimān ||
dharmaputrasya dyūte mahad vairaṃ draṣṭavyamity arthaḥ |
dyūtaṃ samāhvayaṃ caiva yaḥ kuryād yaś ca kārayet |
tān sarvān ghātayed rājā śūdrāṃś ca dvijaliṅginaḥ ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
dhruvaṃ dyūtāt kalir yasmāt viṣaṃ sarpamukhād iva |
tasmād rājā nivarteta viṣayavyasanaṃ hi tat ||
vartate cet prakāśaṃ tad dvārabandhitatoraṇam |
asaṃmohārtham āryāṇāṃ kārayet tatkarapradam ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
dyūtaṃ niṣiddhaṃ manunā satyaśaucadhanāpaham |
tatpravartitam anyais tu rājabhāgasamanvitam ||
sabhikādhiṣṭitaṃ kāryaṃ taskarajñāpakaṃ hi tat |
akṣādinikhilakrīḍopakaraṇakalpanāyuktā sabhā yasya sa sabhikaḥ | yājñavalkyaḥ |
glahe śatikavṛddhes tu sabhikaḥ pañcakaṃ śatam |
gṛhṇīyur dhūrtakitavāḥ taskarā daśakaṃ śatam ||
parasparaṃ saṃpratipattyā kitavakalpitaḥ paṇaḥ glaha ity ucyate | tatra glahe tadāśrayā śātikā śataparimitā tadadhikaparimitā vā vṛddhir yasyāsau śatikavṛddhiḥ | tasmāt dhūrtakitaāt pañcakaṃ śatam ātmakavṛddhyarthaṃ sabhiko gṛhṇīyāt | jitaglahasya viṃśatitamaṃ bhāgaṃ gṛhṇīyād ity arthaḥ | itarasmāt paripūrṇaśatikavṛddheḥ kitavād daśakaṃ śataṃ jitadravyadaśamaṃ bhāgaṃ sabhāpatir gṛhṇīyāt |
dyūtam ekamukhaṃ kāryaṃ tatkarajñānakāraṇāt |
eṣa eva vidhir jñeyaḥ prāṇidyūtasamāhvaye ||
ekamukham ekapradhānaṃ kuryāt | kātyāyanaḥ |
sabhikaḥ kārayet dyūtaṃ deyaṃ dadyāc ca taṃ nṛpe |
daśakaṃ ca śataṃ vṛddhiṃ gṛhṇīyāt sa parājitāt ||
jetur dadyāt svakaṃ dravyaṃ jitaṃ grāhyaṃ tripakṣakam |
sadyo vā sabhikenaiva kitavāt tu na saṃśayaḥ ||
nāradaḥ |
sabhikaḥ kārayed dyūtaṃ deyaṃ dadyāc chataṃ nṛpe |
daśakaṃ ca śataṃ vṛddhiḥ tasya syāt dyūtakāritā ||
atha vā kitavo rājñe datvā bhāgaṃ yathoditam |
prakāśaṃ devanaṃ kuryad evaṃ doṣo na vidyate ||
dvir abhyastāḥ patanty akṣā glahe yasyākṣadevinaḥ |
jayas tasya parasyāhuḥ kiitavasya parājayam ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
ekarūpo dvirūpo vā dyūto yasyākṣadevinaḥ |
dṛśyate vijayas tasya yasminn akṣā vyavasthitāḥ ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
dvandvayuddhena yaḥ kaścid avasādam avāpnuyāt |
tat svāminā paṇo deyo yas tatra parikalpitaḥ ||
nāradaḥ |
anirdiṣṭas tu rājñā dyūtaṃ kurvīta mānavaḥ |
na sa taṃ prāpnuyāt kāmaṃ vinayaṃ caiva so ‘rhati ||
kūṭākṣadevinaḥ pāpān nirhared dyūtamaṇḍalāt |
kaṇṭhe ‘kṣamālām ābaddhya sarveṣāṃ vinayaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
rājñā sacihnaṃ nirvāsyāḥ kūṭākṣopadhidevinaḥ |
viṣṇuḥ: “dyūte ca kūṭākṣadevināṃ karacchedaḥ | upadhidevināṃ sandaṃśacchedaḥ |
kitaveṣv eva tiṣṭheran kitavāḥ saṃśayaṃ prati |
ya eva tatra draṣṭāraḥ ta evaiṣāṃ tu sākṣiṇaḥ ||
eṣa eva vidhir jñeyaḥ prāṇidyūtasamāhvaye |
dyūtasamāhvayaḥ samāptaḥ
vākpāruṣyam
atha vākpāruṣyaṃ nāma vivādapadam ucyate | nāradaḥ |
deśajātikulāsīnām ākrośanyaṅgasaṃjñitam |
yad vacaḥpratikūlārthaṃ vākpāruṣyaṃ tad ucyate ||
nyaṅgasaṃjñā śiraḥpāṇyādyaṅgacchedayuktaṃ vacanam | tathā ca kātyāyanaḥ |
huṃkāraṃ caiva tvaṃkāraṃ loke yac ca nigarhitam |
anukuryād atha brūyād vākpāruṣyaṃ tad ucyate ||
niṣṭhurāślīlatīvratvāt tad api trividhaṃ smṛtam |
gauravānukramād asya daṇḍo ‘py atra kramād guruḥ ||
nāradaḥ |
sākṣepaṃ niṣṭhuraṃ jñeyam aślīlaṃ nyaṅgasaṃjñitam |
patanīyair upakrośaiḥ tīvram āhur manīṣiṇaḥ ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
yat tv asatsaṃjñitair aṅgaiḥ param ākṣipatā kvacit |
abhūtair vātha bhūtair vā niṣṭhurā vāk smṛtā tu sā ||
nyaṅgāvagū(go)raṇaṃ vācā krodhāt tu kurute yadā |
vṛttair deśakulānāṃ ca aślīlā sā budhaiḥ smṛtā ||
mahāpātakayuktā ca jagaddveṣakarī ca yā |
jātibhraṃśakarīyātha tīvrā sā prathitā ca vāk ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
deśajātikulādīnāṃ kṣepaḥ pāpena yojanam |
dravyaṃ vinā tu prathamaṃ vākpāruṣyaṃ tad ucyate ||
bhaginīmātṛsaṃbandham upapātakaśaṃsanam |
pāruṣyaṃ madhyamaṃ proktaṃ vācikaṃ śāstravedibhiḥ ||
abhakṣyāpeyakathanaṃ mahāpātakaśaṃsanam |
pāruṣyam uttamaṃ proktaṃ tīvram āhur manīṣiṇaḥ ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
aduṣṭasyaiva yo doṣān kīrtayet kopakāraṇāt |
anyāpadeśavādī ca vāgduṣṭaṃ taṃ naraṃ viduḥ ||
manuḥ |
mātaraṃ pitaraṃ jāyāṃ bhrātaraṃ tanayaṃ gurūn |
ākṣārayañ chataṃ dāpyaḥ panthānaṃ cādadad guroḥ ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
mohāt pramohāt saṃgharṣāt prītyā coktaṃ mayeti yaḥ |
nāham eva punar vakṣye daṇḍārdhaṃ tasya kalpayet ||
atra bṛhaspatiḥ |
samānayoḥ samo daṇḍo nyūnasya dviguṇaḥ smṛtaḥ |
uttamasyārdhiko daṇḍo vākpāruṣye parasparam ||
atra viṣṇuḥ: “kāṇakhañjādīnāṃ tattvavādy api kārṣāpaṇadvayaṃ daṇḍaḥ” | yājñavalkyaḥ|
satyāsatyānyathā stotraiḥ nyūnāṅgendriyarogiṇām |
kṣepaṃ karoti ced daṇḍyaḥ paṇān ardhatrayodaśān ||
abhigantāsmi bhaginīṃ mātaraṃ vā taveti ha |
śapantaṃ dāpayed rājā pañcaviṃśatikaṃ damam ||
bāhugrīvānetrasakthivināśe vācike damaḥ |
śatyas tadardhake pādanāsākarṇakarādiṣu ||
aśaktas tu vadann evaṃ daṇḍanīyaḥ paṇān daśa |
tathā śaktaḥ pratubhuvaṃ dāpyaḥ kṣemāya tasya tu ||
kṣipan svasrādikaṃ dadyāt pañcāśatpaṇikaṃ damam |
manu-nāradau |
śataṃ brāhmaṇam ākruśya kṣatriyo daṇḍam arhati |
vaiśyo ‘dhyardhaṃ śataṃ caiva śūdras tu vadham arhati ||
viprāḥ pañcaśataṃ daṇḍyāḥ kṣatriyasyābhiśaṃsane |
vaiśyaṃ syād ardhapañcāśac chūdre dvādaśako damaḥ ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
sacchūdrasyāyam uddiṣṭo vinayo ‘naparādhinaḥ |
guṇahīnasya pāruṣye brāhmaṇo nāparādhnuyāt ||
tathā |
vaiśyas tu kṣatriyākrośe daṇḍanīyaḥ śataṃ bhavet |
tadardhaṃ kṣatriyo vaiśyaṃ kṣipan vinayam arhati ||
śūdrākrośe kṣatriyasya pañcaviṃśatiko damaḥ |
vaiśyasya caiva dviguṇaḥ śāstravidbhir udāhṛtaḥ ||
vaiśyam ākṣārayan śūdro dāpyaḥ syāt pathamaṃ damam |
tathā |
kṣatriyaṃ madhyamaṃ caiva vipram uttamasāhasam |
atra manu-nāradau |
ekajātir dvijātīṃs tu vācā dāruṇayā kṣipan |
jihvāyāḥ prāpnuyāc chedaṃ jaghanyaprabhavo hi saḥ ||
nāmajātigrahaṃ tv eṣām abhidroheṇa kurvataḥ |
nikheyo ‘yomayaḥ śaṅkur jvalann āsye daśāṅgulaḥ ||
śūdre prakṛte bṛhaspatiḥ |
dharmopadeśaṃ darpeṇa dvijānām asya kurvataḥ |
taptam āsecayel tailaṃ vaktre śrotre ca pārthivaḥ ||
śūdra ity anuvṛttau gautamaḥ: “athāsya vedam upaśṛṇavtas trapujatubhyāṃ śrotrapratipūraṇam udāharaṇe jihvācchedo dhāraṇe śarīrabhedaḥ” | atra yamaḥ |
khaṇḍaśaś chedayej jihvām ṛcaṃ cai yady udāharet |
manuḥ |
pāpopapāpavaktāro mahāpātakaśaṃsakāḥ |
ādyamadhyottamān daṇḍān dadyus te yathākramam ||
atra yājñavalkyaḥ |
traividyanṛpadevānāṃ kṣepa uttamasāhasaḥ |
madhyamo jātipūgānāṃ prathamo grāmadeśayoḥ ||
nāradaḥ |
upakruśya ca rājānaṃ vartmani sve vyavasthitam |
jihvācchedād bhavec chuddiḥ sarvasvaharaṇena vā ||
brāhmaṇasya sarvasvaharaṇam, jātyantarasya jihvācchedaḥ | yājñavalkyaḥ |
rājño ‘niṣṭapravaktāraṃ tasyaivākrośakaṃ tathā |
tanmantrasya ca bhettāraṃ chittvā jihvāṃ pravāsayet ||
nāradaḥ |
loke ‘smin dvāv avaktavyāv adaṇḍyau ca prakīrtitau |
brāhmaṇaś caiva rājā ca tau hīdaṃ bibhrato jagat ||
patitaṃ patitety uktvā coraṃ coreti vā punaḥ |
vacanāt tulayadoṣaḥ syāt mithyā dvirdoṣavān bhavet ||
brāhmaṇo ‘pi svajātīyam ṛṇādivyapadeśataḥ |
vākpāruṣyaṃ prakurvāṇaḥ sa bhaved upapātakī ||
daṇḍapāruṣyam
atha daṇḍapāruṣyākhyaṃ vivādapadam ucyate | atra bṛhaspatiḥ |
hastapāṣāṇalaguḍaiḥ bhasmakardamapāṃsubhiḥ |
āyudhaiś ca praharaṇaṃ daṇḍapāruṣyam ucyate ||
nāradaḥ |
tasyāpi dṛṣṭaṃ traividhyaṃ mṛdumadhyottamakramāt |
avagoraṇaniḥsaṅgapātanakṣatadarśanaiḥ ||
hīnamadhyottamānāṃ tu trayāṇāṃ samatikramāt |
trīṇy eva sāhasāny āhuḥ tatra kaṇṭakaśodhane ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
bhasmapaṅkarajaḥsparśe daṇḍo daśapaṇaḥ smṛtaḥ |
amedhyapārṣṇiniṣṭhyūtasparśane dviguṇaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
sameṣv evaṃ parastrīṣu dviguṇas tūttameṣu ca |
hīneṣv ardhadamo mohamadādibhir adaṇḍanam ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
chardimūtrapurīṣādyair adhonābheś caturguṇaḥ |
ṣaḍguṇaḥ kāyamadhye tu mūrdhni tv aṣṭaguṇo damaḥ ||
udgūraṇe tu hastasya kāryo dvādaśako damaḥ |
sa eva dviguṇaḥ proktaḥ pātane tu sajātiṣu ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
udyate tu śilākāṣṭhe kartavyaḥ prathamo damaḥ |
parasparaṃ hastapāde daśaviṃśatikas tathā ||
iṣṭakopakāṣṭhaiś ca tāḍane tu dvimāṣakaḥ |
dviguṇaḥ śoṇitodbhede daṇḍaḥ kāryo manīṣibhiḥ ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
śoṇitena vinā duḥkhaṃ kurvan kāṣṭhādibhir naraḥ |
dvātriṃśataṃ paṇaṃ dāpyo dviguṇaṃ darśane ‘sṛjaḥ ||
manuḥ |
tvagbhedakaḥ śataṃ daṇḍyo lohitasya ca darśakaḥ |
māṃsabhettā tu ṣaṇṇiṣkān pravāsyās tv asthibhedakaḥ ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
tvagbhede prathamo daṇḍo māṃsabhede tu madhyamaḥ |
uttamas tv asthibhede tu ghātane tu pramāpaṇam ||
karṇanāsākarachede dantabhedāṅgabhedane |
kartavyo madhyamo daṇḍo dviguṇaḥ pātite sati ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
karṇoṣṭhaghrāṇapādākṣijihvāśiśnakarasya ca |
chedane cottamo daṇḍo bhedane madhyamo bhṛguḥ ||
manuḥ |
manuṣyāṇāṃ paśūnāṃ ca duḥkhāya prahṛte sati |
yathā yathā mahad duḥkhaṃ daṇḍaṃ kuryāt tathā tathā ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
viprapīḍākaraṃ chedyam aṅgam abrāhmaṇasya tu |
udgūrṇe prathamo daṇḍaḥ sparśane tu tadardhakaḥ ||
nāradaḥ |
yena yenāvaro varṇo brāhmaṇasyāparādhnuyāt |
tadaṅgaṃ tasya chettavyam evaṃ śuddhim avāpnuyāt ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
dvayoḥ praharator daṇḍaḥ samayo ‘stu samaḥ smṛtaḥ |
ārambhako ‘nubandhī ca dāpyaḥ syād adhikaṃ damam ||
nāradaḥ |
pūrvam ākṣārayed yas tu niyataṃ syāt sa doṣabhāk |
paścād yaḥ so ‘py asatkārī pūrve tu vinayo guruḥ ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
ākruṣṭas tu samākrośaṃs tāḍitaḥ pratitāḍayan |
hatvātatāyinaṃ caiva nāparādhī bhaven naraḥ ||
atha vṛddhakātyāyanaḥ |
uktvā paruṣam uktas tu tāḍayitvā tu tāḍitaḥ |
yam uddiśya tyajet prāṇān tena na syāt sa kilbiṣī ||
manu-nāradau |
sahāsanam abhiprepsur utkṛṣṭasyāpakṛṣṭajaḥ |
kaṭyāṃ kṛtāṅko nirvāsyaḥ sphicaṃ vāsya nikṛntayet ||
avaniṣṭivato darpāt dvāv oṣṭhau chedayen nṛpaḥ |
avamūtrayato meḍhram avaśardayato gudam ||
keśeṣu gṛhṇato hastau chedayed avicārayan |
pādayor nāsikāyāṃ ca grīvāyāṃ vṛṣaṇe tathā ||
manuḥ |
adhastāt tu prahartavyā nottamāṅge kathaṃcana |
ato ‘nyathā pravṛttas tu yathoktaṃ daṇḍam arhati ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
vākpāruṣye yathaivoktāḥ pratilomānulomataḥ |
tathaiva daṇḍapāruṣye pātyā daṇḍā yathākramam ||
manuḥ |
aṅgāvapīḍanaṃ cāsya vraṇaśoṇitayos tathā |
samutthānavyayaṃ dāpyaḥ sarvadaṇḍam athāpi vā ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
aṅgāvapīḍane caiva bhedane chedane tathā |
samutthānavyayaṃ dāpyaḥ kalahāpahṛtaṃ ca yat ||
nāradaḥ |
putrāparādhe na pitā nāśvena śuni daṇḍabhāk |
na markaṭena tatsvāmī tenaiva preṣitā na cet ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
śakto ‘py amoṣayan svāmī daṃṣṭrināṃ śṛṅgiṇāṃ tathā |
prathamaṃ sāhasaṃ dadyāt vikṛṣṭe dviguṇaṃ tathā ||
asākṣikahate cihnaiḥ yuktibhiś cāgamena ca |
draṣṭavyo vyavahāras tu kūṭacihnakṛtād bhayāt ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
hataḥ sa dṛśyate yatra ghātakas tu na dṛśyate |
pūrvavairānumānena jñātavyaḥ sa mahībhujā ||
prātiveśyānuveśyāś ca tasya mitrāṇi bāndhavāḥ |
praṣṭavyā rājapuruṣaiḥ sāmādibhir upakramaiḥ ||
vijñeyaḥ sādhyasaṃsargāc cihnahoḍhena vā naraiḥ |
kātyāyanaḥ |
kūṭasya bahavo yatra praharanti ruṣānvitāḥ |
marmaprahārado yas tu ghātakaḥ sa udāhṛtaḥ ||
marmaghātī ca yas teṣāṃ yathoktaṃ dāpayed damam |
ārambhakaḥ sahāyāś ca doṣabhājas tadarthataḥ ||
āśrayaḥ śastradātā ca bharadātā ca karmaṇām |
yuktyopadeśakaś caiva tadvināśaḥ pradarśakaḥ ||
upekṣākāryayuktaś ca doṣayuktānumodakaḥ |
aniṣedhakṣamo yaḥ syāt sarve te kāryakāriṇaḥ ||
na śārīro brāhmaṇasya daṇḍo bhavati karhicit |
gupte tu bandhane badhvā rājā bhaktaṃ pradāpayet ||
yathāparādhaṃ viprās tu karmāṇy api ca kārayet ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
hetyādibhir na paśyec cet daṇḍapāruṣyakāraṇam |
tad asākṣīkṛtaṃ tatra divyaṃ vādhvani yojayet ||
atra yājñavalkyaḥ |
duḥkham utpādayed yas tu sa samutthānajaṃ vyayam |
kātyāyanaḥ |
samutthānavyayaṃ cāsau dadyād ā vraṇaropaṇāt |
manuḥ |
bhāryā putraś ca dāsaś ca dāsī bhṛtyaś ca pañcamaḥ |
prāptāparādhās tāḍyāḥ syuḥ rajvā veṇudalena vā ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
duḥkhe ca śoṇitotpāde śākhāṅgacchedane tathā |
daṇḍaḥ kṣudrapaśūnāṃ ca dvipaṇaprabhṛtiḥ kramāt ||
liṅgasya chedane mṛtyau madhyame mūlyam eva ca |
mahāpaśūnām eteṣu sthāneṣu dviguṇo damaḥ ||
kārṣāpaṇaśataṃ daṇḍya ity anuvṛttau viṣṇuḥ:
paśūnāṃ puṃstvaghātī tathā gajāśvoṣṭragoghātī hy ekapādaḥ kāryaḥ | māṃsavikrayī ca grāmyapaśughātī ca kārṣāpaṇaśataṃ paṇḍyaḥ | paśusvāminaś ca tanmūlyaṃ dadyāt | araṇyapaśughātī kārṣāpaṇāpañcāśatam | pakṣighātī matsyaghātī ca daśakārṣāpaṇān | krīḍopaghātī kārṣāpaṇam ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
tripaṇo dvādaśapaṇo vadhe tu mṛgapakṣiṇām |
sarpamārjārakulaśvasūkaravadhe nṛṇām ||
manuḥ |
gokumārīdevapaśam ukṣāṇam ṛṣabhaṃ tathā |
vāhayan sāhasaṃ pūrvaṃ prāpnuyād uttamaṃ vadhe ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
śrāntān kṣudhārtāṃs tṛṣitān nākāle vāheyan naraḥ |
kharagomahiṣoṣṭrādīn prāpnuyāt pūrvasāhasam ||
manuṣyamāraṇe kṣipraṃ coravat kilbiṣaṃ bhavet |
prāṇabhṛtsu mahatsv ardhaṃ gokaraoṣṭragavādiṣu ||
kṣudrakāṇāṃ paśūnāṃ ca hiṃsāyāṃ daśamo damaḥ |
pañcāśataṃ bhaved daṇḍaḥ śubheṣu mṛgapakṣiṣu ||
gardabhājāvikānāṃ tu daṇḍaḥ syāt pañcamāṣakaḥ |
māṣakas tu bhaved daṇḍaḥ śvasūkaranipātane ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
pramāpaṇe prāṇabhṛtāṃ pratirūpaṃ tu dāpayet |
paśvānurūpyaṃ mūlyaṃ vā dāpya ity abravīn manuḥ ||
atra manuḥ |
daśasthānāni daṇḍasya manuḥ svāyaṃbhuvo ‘bravīt |
triṣu varṇeṣu yāni syur akṣato brāhmaṇo vrajet ||
upastham udaraṃ jihvā hastau pādau ca pañcamaḥ |
cakṣur nāsā ca karṇau ca dhanaṃ dehas tathaiva ca ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
hastaś ca liṅganayanajihvākarṇau ca nāsikā |
grīvā pādas tu sandaṃśo lalāṭoṣṭhau gudaṃ kaṭī ||
sthānāny etāni daṇḍasya nirdiṣṭāni caturdaśa |
tīvradoṣaviṣayam idam |
lalāṭakarṇau brāhmaṇasya nānyo daṇḍo vidhīyate |
yamaḥ |
jagat sarvam idaṃ hanyāt brāhmaṇasya na tatsamam |
tasmāt tasya vadhaṃ rājā manasāpi na cintayet ||
avadhyān brāhmaṇān āhuḥ sarvapāpeṣv avasthitān |
yad yad vipreṣu kuśalaṃ tat tad rājā samācaret ||
na jātu brāhmaṇaṃ hanyāt sarvapāpeṣv avasthitam |
rāṣṭrād enaṃ bahiḥ kuryāt samagradhanam akṣatam ||
vadhye karmaṇi tiṣṭhantaṃ samagradhanasaṃyutam |
vivāsayed dvijaṃ rājā doṣaṃ vikhyāpya saṃsadi ||
bodhāyanaḥ: “brāhmaṇasya brahmahatyāgurutalpagamanasvarṇasteyasurāpāneṣu kabandhabhagaśvāpadasurādhvajān taptenāyasena lalāṭe ‘ṅkayitvā viṣayāntare nirvāsyaḥ” | manuḥ |
gurutalpe bhagaḥ kāryaḥ surāpe ca surādhvajaḥ |
steye ca śvapadaṃ kāryaṃ brahmahaṇy aśirāḥ pumān ||
asaṃbhojyā hy asaṃ yājyaḥ asaṃpāṭyāvivādinaḥ |
careyuḥ pṛthivīṃ dīnāḥ sarvadharmabahiṣkṛtāḥ ||
jñātisaṃbandhibhis tv ete tyaktavyāḥ kṛtalakṣaṇāḥ |
nirdayā nirnamaskārāḥ tan manor anuśāsanam ||
prāyaścittaṃ tu kurvāṇāḥ pūrvavarṇā yathoditm |
nāṅkyā rājñā lalāṭe syuḥ dāpyās tūttamasāhasam ||
āgamas tu brāhmaṇasyeva kāryo madhyamasāhasaḥ |
vivāsyo vā bhaved rāṣṭrāt sadravyaḥ saparicchadaḥ ||
itare kṛtavantas tu pāpāny etāny akāmataḥ |
sarvasvahāram arhanti kāmatas tu pravāsanam ||
yamaḥ |
brāhmaṇasyāparādheṣu caturṣv evaṃ vidhīyate |
śiraso muṇḍanaṃ daṇḍaḥ purān nirvāsanaṃ tataḥ ||
prakhyāpanārthaṃ pāpasya prayāṇaṃ gardabhena ca |
lalāṭe cihnakaraṇaṃ kuryād rājā yathāvidhi ||
gurutalpe surāpāne steye brāhmaṇahiṃsane |
yathākramaṃ pravakṣyāmi cihnāny āha yathā manuḥ ||
gurutalpe bhagaḥ kāryo vrataghnasya dvijanmanaḥ |
surādhvajaḥ surāpāne surāpasya ca kārayet ||
steye ca śvapadaṃ kṛtvā śikhivat tena kuṭṭayet |
viśirāḥ puruṣaḥ kāryaḥ lalāṭe brahmaghātinaḥ ||
etais tu patitaiḥ sārdhaṃ saṃyogaṃ kurute dvijaḥ |
so ‘saṃbhojyas tu kartavyaḥ manuḥ svāyaṃbhuvo ‘bravīt ||
parityājyāḥ kṛtaṅkās tu jñātisaṃbandhibāndhavaiḥ ||
manuḥ |
kārṣāpaṇaṃ bhaved daṇḍyo yatrānyaḥ prākṛto janaḥ |
tatra rājā bhaved daṇḍyaḥ sahasra iti dhāraṇā ||
brāhmaṇān bādhamānaṃ tu kāmād avaravarṇajam |
hanyāc citrair vadhopāyaiḥ udvejanakarair nṛpaḥ ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
pravrajyāvasitaṃ śūdraṃ japahomaparaṃ tathā |
tuthena śāsayed rājā daṇḍyo vā dviguṇaṃ damam ||
aspṛśyaśūdradāsānāṃ narāṇāṃ pāpakāriṇām |
prātilomyaprasūtānāṃ tāḍanaṃ nārthato damaḥ ||
paratantrās tu ye kecit dāsatve ye ca saṃsthitāḥ |
anāthās te nirdiṣṭāḥ teṣāṃ daṇḍas tu tāḍanam ||
bandhanaṃ caiva teṣāṃ hi tathaiva tu nipīḍanam |
eṣa daṇḍo hi dāsasya nārthadaṇḍo bṛhaspatiḥ ||
iti varadarājīye daṇḍapāruṣyam
steyam
atha steyasāhasapadam ucyate | atra manuḥ |
sāhasaṃ syād anvayavat prasabhaṃ karma yat kṛtam |
niranvayaṃ bhavet steyaṃ kṛtvāpavyayate ca yat ||
yac ca sānvayam api kṛtvāpavyayane ca yat |
anvayavat iti sarakṣam ucyate | tena rakṣakasahite prasabhaṃ cāviśaṅkitena yat kriyate tat sāhasam | niranvayaṃ tu yad anādhiṣṭhaṃ rakṣakena kṛtam api apacyavate |
nihnute tad api steyaṃ kṛtvāpavyayane ca yat |
dvividhāṃs taskarān vidyāt paradravyāpahāriṇaḥ ||
prakāśāṃś cāprakāśāṃś ca cāracakṣur mahīpatiḥ |
nāradaḥ |
kṣudramadhyottamānāṃ tu dravyānām apakarṣaṇāt |
mṛdbhāṇḍāsanakhaṭvāsthidārucarmatṛṇādi yat ||
śamīdhānyaṃ kṛtānnaṃ ca kṣudradravyam udāhṛtam |
vāsaḥ kauśeyavarjaṃ ca govarjaṃ paśavas tathā ||
hiraṇyavarjaṃ lohaṃ ca madhaṃ vrīhiyavā api |
hiraṇyaṃ ratnakauśeyaṃ strīpuṃgogajavājinaḥ ||
devabrāhmaṇarājñāṃ ca vijñeyaṃ dravyam uttamam |
upāyair vividhair eṣāṃ vañcayitvāpakarṣaṇam ||
suptamattapramattebhyāh steyam āhur manīṣiṇaḥ ||
manuḥ |
dravyāṇi hiṃsyād yo yasya jñānato ‘jñānato ‘pi vā |
sa tasyotpādayet tuṣṭiṃ rājñe dadyāc ca tatsamam ||
śaṅkhalikhitau: “kūṭaśāsanaprayoge rājaśāsanapratiṣedhe kūṭatulāmānapratimān vyavahāre śarīre aṅgacchede vā” | manuḥ |
kūṭaśāsanakartṝṃś ca parkṛtīnāṃ ca dūṣakān |
strībālabrāhmaṇaghnāṃś ca hanyād viṭsevinas tathā ||
dvividhān taskarān vidyāt paradravyāpahāriṇaḥ |
prakāśāṃś cāprakāśāṃś ca cāracakṣur mahīpatiḥ ||
nāradaḥ |
tad api trividhaṃ proktaṃ dravyāpekṣaṃ manīṣibhiḥ |
krayādiṣu svatvahetuṃ vinā yayā kayā ca vidhayā aprakāśaharaṇam eva steyam | nāradaḥ |
prakāśataskarās tatra kūṭamānatulāśritāḥ |
utkocakāḥ sopadhikāḥ kitavāḥ paṇyayoṣitaḥ ||
pratirūpakarāś caiva maṅgalādeśavṛttayaḥ |
ityevamādayo jñeyāḥ prakāśās taskarā bhuvi ||
aprakāśās tu vijñeyā bahirabhyantarāśritāḥ |
suptamattapramattāṃś ca muṣṇanty ākramya ye narāḥ ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
sandhicchedaḥ pānthamuṣo dvicatuṣpādahāriṇaḥ |
utkṣepakāḥ sasyaharā jñeyāh pracchannataskarāḥ ||
vyāsaḥ |
sādhanāṅgānvitā rātrau civaranty avibhāvitāḥ |
avijñātavivāsāś ca jñeyāḥ pracchannataskarāḥ ||
utkṣepakaḥ sandhibhettā pāntha udgranthibhedakaḥ |
strīpuṃyo’śvapaśusteyī coro navavidhaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
tatra trividhasya sāhasasya daṇḍaprakāram āha nāradaḥ |
tasya daṇḍakriyāpekṣaḥ prathamasya śatāvaraḥ |
madhyamasya tu śāstrajñaiḥ dṛṣṭaḥ pañcaśatāvaraḥ ||
vadhaḥ sarvasvaharaṇaṃ purān nirvāsanāṅkane |
tadaṅgaccheda ity uktaḥ daṇḍa uttamasāhase ||
syātāṃ saṃvyavahāryau tau dhṛtadaṇḍau tu pūrvayoḥ |
dhṛtadaṇḍo ‘py asaṃbhāṣyo jñeya uttamasāhase ||
śaṅkhalikhitau: “kūṭaśāsanaprayogarājaśāsanapratiṣedhakūṭatulāmānapratimāne vyavahāreṇa” | manuḥ |
kūṭaśāsanakartṝṃś ca prakṛtīnāṃ ca dūṣakān |
strībālabrāhmaṇaghnāṃś ca hanyād viṭsevinas tathā || iti |
tān praty āha manuḥ |
tān viditvā tu caritaiḥ gūḍhais tatkarmacāribhiḥ |
cāraiś cānekasaṃsthānaiḥ prasahya vaśam ānayet ||
teṣāṃ doṣān abhikhyāpya sve sve karmani tattvataḥ |
kurvīta śāsanaṃ rājā samyak sārāparādhataḥ ||
saraśabdenārtha ucyate | bṛhaspatiḥ |
saṃsargacihnaloptraiś ca vijñeyo rājapūruṣaiḥ |
pradāpyopahṛtaṃ rājñā damaiḥ śāstrapracoditaiḥ ||
saṃsargo duṣṭaiḥ saha vartanam | cihnaṃ śvāpadaṃ cauryopakaraṇaṃ ca | loptraṃ coritadravyam | teṣāṃ mārgasthānam āha manuḥ |
sabhāprapādyūtaśālā veśmamadhyānnavikrayāḥ |
catuṣpathaṃ caityavṛkṣaḥ samājaprakṣaṇādi ca ||
jīrṇodyānāny araṇyāni kārukāveśanāni ca |
śūnyāni cāpy agārāṇi vanāny upavanāni ca ||
evaṃvidhān nṛpo deśān gulamasthāvarajaṅgamaiḥ |
taskarapratiṣedhārthaṃ cāraiś cāpy anucārayet ||
tatsahāyaiḥ svānugataiḥ nānākarmapravedibhiḥ |
vidyād utsādayec caiva nipuṇaiḥ pūrvataskaraiḥ ||
bhakṣyabhojyopadeśaiś ca brāhmaṇānāṃ ca darśanaiḥ |
śauryakarmopadeśaiś ca kuryāt teṣāṃ samāgatam ||
ye tatra nopasarpanti mūlapraṇihitān janān |
tān pragṛhya nṛpo hanyāt samitrajñātibāndhavān ||
corānveṣaṇārthaṃ rājñā preritā ye yathoktakāriṇo na bhavanti teṣam ukto daṇḍaḥ | coranigrahe niyamam āha |
na hoḍhena vinā coraṃ ghātayed dhārmiko nṛpaḥ |
sahoḍhaṃ sopakaraṇaṃ ghātayed avicārayan ||
hoḍhaḥ apahṛtadravyaikadeśaḥ | upakaraṇaśabdena sahakāriṇa ucyante |
bhayopadhābhiś citrābhiḥ brūyus tathyaṃ yathā hi te |
deśaṃ grāmaṃ diśaṃ nāma jātiṃ vā saṃpratiśrayam ||
kṛtaṃ kāryaṃ sahāyāś ca praṣṭavyāḥ syur vigṛhya te |
varṇasvrākārabhedāt sasaṃdigdhanivedanāt ||
adeśakāladṛṣṭatvān nivāsasya viśodhanāt |
asadvyayāt pūrvacauryād asatsaṃsargakāraṇāt ||
etair apy avagantavyo na hoḍhair api kevalaiḥ ||
corādhikāre vyāsaḥ |
te padenānugantavyā vibhāvyā loptradarśanaiḥ |
dyūtastrīpānasaktyā ca nirāyavyayakarmabhiḥ ||
nāradaḥ |
yāṃs tatra corān gṛhṇīyāt tān vitāḍya vilupya ca |
avaghuṣya ca sarvatra hanyāc citravadhena tu ||
acorā api dṛśyante coraiḥ saha samāgatāḥ |
yadṛcchayā naiva tu tān nṛpo daṇḍena saṃspṛśet ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
coraṃ pradāpyāpahṛtaṃ ghātayed vividhair vadhaiḥ |
sacihnaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ kṛtvā svarāṣṭrād vipravāsayet ||
grāmeṣv api ca ye kecit corāṇāṃ bhaktadāyakāḥ |
bhāṇḍāvakāśadāṃś caiva sarvāṃs tān api ghātayet ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
iṣṭakāpūrṇasaṃyukto raktamālyavibhūṣitaḥ |
ghoṣitas tena cauryeṇa steno rājñā nihanyate ||
annapradātā stenānāṃ stena eva sa ucyate |
tasya hṛtvā tu sarvasvaṃ śvapadaṃ tu mukhe ‘ṅkayet ||
bhittvā gṛhaṃ gṛhītvā svaṃ kṛtvā saṃskāram eva ca |
rājā sāhasikaṃ rāṣṭrāt …… tyajet ||
corādhikāre kātyāyanaḥ |
kretāraś caiva bhāṇḍānāṃ pratigrāhiṇa eva ca |
samadaṇḍāḥ smṛtāḥ sarve ye ca pracchādayanti tān ||
prajñātaviṣayam idam | aprajñāteṣu na doṣaḥ | ata eva gautamaḥ: “corasamaḥ sacivo matipūrvaṃ pratigrahītāpi” | atra nāradaḥ |
utkrośatāṃ janānāṃ tu hriyamāṇe dhane tathā |
śrutvā ye nābhidhāvanti te ‘pi taddoṣabhāginaḥ ||
rājñaḥ kośāpahartṝṃś ca hanyād evāvicārayan |
vandigrahāṃs tathā vājikuñjarāṇāṃ ca hāriṇaḥ ||
prasahya ghātinaś caiva śūlam āropayen nṛpaḥ ||
manuḥ |
rājñaḥ koṣāpahartṝṃś ca prātikūlyeṣv avasthitān |
arīṇām upajaptṝṃś ca śātayed vividhair vadhaiḥ ||
saṃdhiṃ chittvā tu ye cauryaṃ rātrau kurvanti taskarāḥ |
teṣāṃ chittvā nṛpo hastau tīkṣṇe śūle niveśayet ||
saṃdhichedanaṃ kavāṭādiyantrabhedanam | nārada-kātyāyanau |
svadeśaghātino ye syuḥ tathā mārganirodhinaḥ |
teṣāṃ sarvasvam ādāya rājā śūle niveśayet ||
pūrvoktaṃ damaniyamam āha manuḥ |
aṅguli saṃdhibhedasya chedayet prathame grahe |
dvitīye hastacaraṇau tṛtīye vadham arhati ||
tathā manuḥ |
agnidān bhaktadāṃś caiva tathā śastrāvakāśadān |
saṃnidhātṝṃś ca moṣasya śiṣyāc corān iveśvaraḥ ||
taṭākabhedakaṃ hanyād apsu śuddhavadhena vā |
yad vāpi pratisaṃskuryād dadyāc cottamasāhasam ||
pratisaṃskāraḥ samādhānam | atra yājñavalkyaḥ |
viṣāgnidāṃ striyaṃ caiva puruṣaghnīm agarbhiṇīm |
setubhedakarīm apsu śilāṃ baddhvā praveśayet ||
viṣāgnidāṃ patigurunijāpatyapramāpiṇīm |
vikarṇakaranāsoṣṭhīṃ kṛtvā gobhiḥ pramāpayet ||
tathā |
kṣetraveśmagrāmavanavivītakhaladāhakāḥ |
rājapatnyabhigāmī ca dagdhavyāḥ syuḥ kaṭāgninā ||
tathā manuḥ |
goṣṭhāgārāyudhāgāradevatāgārabhedakān |
hastyaśvarathahantṝṃś ca hanyād evāvicārayan ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
bandigrāhāṃs tathā vājikuñjarāṇāṃ ca hāriṇaḥ |
prasahya ghātinaṃ caiva śūlam āropayen narān ||
tathā ca viṣṇuḥ: “go’śvoṣṭragajāpahāy ekakarapādaḥ kāryaḥ | ajāvyapahāre ekakaraś ca” | tatra nāradaḥ |
mahāpaśūn stenayato daṇḍa uttamasāhasaḥ |
madhyamo madhyamapaśūn pūrvaḥ kṣudrapaśor hṛtau ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
yas tu pūrvaṃ niviṣṭasya taṭākasyodakaṃ haret |
āgamaṃ vāpy apāṃ bhindyāt sa dāpyaḥ pūrvasāhasam ||
cikitsakānāṃ sarveṣāṃ mithyāpracaratāṃ damaḥ |
amānuṣeṣu prathamo mānuṣeṣu tu madhyamaḥ ||
ajñātauṣadhamantras tu yas tu vyādher atattvavit |
rogibhyo hy artham ādatte sa daṇḍyaś coravad bhiṣak ||
manuḥ |
saṃkramadhvajayaṣṭīnāṃ pratimānāṃca bhedakaḥ |
praikuryāc ca tat pūrvaṃ pañca dadyāc chatāni ca ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
haret bhindyāt dahed vāpi devānāṃ pratimāṃ yadi |
tadgṛhaṃ caiva yo bhindyāt prāpnuyāt pūrvasāhasam ||
tatra śaṅkhalikhitau: “pratimārāmakṛpasaṃkramadhvajasetunipānabhaṅgeṣu tatsamutthāpanaṃ pratisaṃskāro ‘ṣṭaśataṃ ca” | pratisaṃskāraḥ pratimāntaranirmāṇam | manuḥ |
adūṣitānāṃ dravyāṇāṃ dūṣaṇe bhedane tathā |
maṇīnām apy avedhe ca daṇḍaḥ prathamasāhasaḥ ||
adūṣitānāṃ candanahiṅgvādīnāṃ bhedanam | akāṃsyādīnāṃ maṇīnām api vedhavyatiriktabhedena gurudravyeṣv idaṃ draṣṭavyam | atra yājñavalkyaḥ |
bheṣajasnehalavaṇagandhadānyaguḍādiṣu |
paṇyeṣu hīnaṃ kṣipata(ḥ) paṇā daṇḍas tu ṣoḍaśa ||
tathā manuḥ |
prākārasya ca bhettāraṃ parikhānāṃ ca pūrakam |
dvārāṇāṃ caiva bhettāraṃ kṣipram eva pravāsayet ||
abhicāreṣu sarveṣu kartavyo dviśato damaḥ |
mūlakarmaṇi cānāpte kṛtyāsu vividhāsu ca ||
mūlakarmaṇi vaśīkārakarmaṇi | anāpte phala ity arthaḥ | kātyāyanaḥ |
pravrajyāvasitaṃ śūdraṃ japahomaparāyaṇam |
vadhena śāsayet pāpaṃ daṇḍyo vā dviguṇaṃ damam ||
aspṛśyadhūrtadāsānāṃ narāṇāṃ pāpakāriṇām |
pratilomaprasūtānāṃ tāḍanaṃ nārthato damaḥ ||
paratantrās tu ye kecid dāsatvaṃ ye ca saṃśritāḥ |
anāthās te tu nirdiṣṭāḥ teṣāṃ daṇḍas tu tāḍanam ||
yac ca yasyopakaraṇaṃ yena jīvanti kāravaḥ |
sarvasvaharaṇe ‘py eṣāṃ na rājā hartum arhati ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
suvarṇaśatam ekaṃ tu vadhārho daṇḍam arhati |
aṅgacchedī tadardhaṃ tu vivāsyaḥ pañcaviṃśakam ||
bahujanopakārasya sādhor maraṇaprāptāv idam |
sarvakaṇṭakapāpiṣṭhaṃ hemakāraṃ tu pārthivaḥ |
pravartamānam anyāye chedayel lavaśaḥ kṣuraiḥ ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
kūṭasvarṇavyavahārī kumāṃsasya ca vikrayī |
tryaṅgahīnas tu kartavyo dāpyaś cottamasāhasam ||
mṛccarmamaṇisūtrāṇāṃ kāṣṭhavalkalavāsasām |
ajāter jātikaraṇaṃ vikraye ‘ṣṭaguṇo damaḥ ||
sarvavastūnām ajñāne jātikaraṇe etad eva tad iti vācā anubhāṣya vikraye mūlyād aṣṭaguṇo damaḥ | bṛhaspatiḥ |
alpamūlyaṃ tu saṃskṛtya nayanti bahumūlyatām |
strībālakān vañcayanti daṇḍyās te ‘rthānusārataḥ ||
hemamuktāpravālānāṃ kṛtrimaṃ kurvate tu ye |
te tanmūlyaṃ pradāpyās tu rājñā taddviguṇaṃ damam ||
abhinnadoṣavyāmiśraṃ punaḥ saṃskṛtya vikrayī |
paṇyaṃ taddviguṇaṃ dāpyaḥ maṇigaṇḍaś ca tatsamaḥ ||
nāradaḥ |
lohānām ap sarveṣāṃ hetur agnikriyāvidhau |
kṣayaḥ saṃskriyamāṇānāṃ teṣām iṣṭo ‘gnisaṃgamāt ||
suvarṇasya kṣayo nāsti rajate dvipalaṃ śatam |
śate tv aṣṭapalaṃ jñeyaṃ kṣayaṃ tu trapusīsayoḥ ||
tāmre pañcapalaṃ vidyād vikārā ye ca tanmayā |
taddhātūnām anekatvāt ayaso niyamaṃ kṣaye ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
agnau suvarṇam akṣīṇaṃ dvipalaṃ rajate śatam |
aṣṭau trapuṇi sīse ca tāmre pañca daśāyasi ||
manuḥ |
tantuvāyo daśapalād dadyād ekapalādhikam |
ato ‘nyathā vartamāno dāpyo dvādaśakaṃ damam ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
śate daśapalā vṛddhiḥ aurṇe kārpāsike tathā |
madhye pañcapalā sūtre sūkṣme tu tripalā matā ||
nāradaḥ |
triṃśāṃśo romabandhasya kṣayaṃ carmakṛtasya ca |
kauśeyavalkalādīnāṃ naiva vṛddhir na ca kṣayaḥ ||
viśvāmitraḥ |
ṣāṇmāsikam adho vāsa uttaraṃ tv ābdikaṃ bhavet |
dvivarṣaṃ tu prāvaraṇaṃ yathā bhogaṃ yathā vayaḥ ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
deśaṃ kālaṃ ca bhogaṃ ca jñātvā naṣṭe balābalam |
dravyāṇāṃ kuśalā brūyuḥ yat tad dāpyam asaṃśayam ||
manuḥ |
aṣṭāpādyaṃ hi śūdrasya steye bhavati kilbiṣam |
ṣoḍaśaiva tu vaiśyasya dvātriṃśat kṣatriyasya tu ||
brāhmaṇasya catuḥṣaṣṭiḥ pūrṇaṃ vāpi śataṃ bhavet |
dviguṇā vā catuḥṣaṣṭiḥ taddoṣaguṇavid dhi saḥ ||
nāradaḥ |
steneṣv alabhyamāneṣu rājā dadyāt svato dhanāt |
upekṣyamāṇo hy enasvī dharmād arthāc ca hīyate ||
yamaḥ |
asteyam agnaye kāṣṭham asteyaṃca tṛṇaṃ bhavet |
kanyāharaṇam asteyaṃ yāvarā yānalaṃkṛtā ||
anyasmai dātum alaṃkṛtāṃ nāharet | yājñavalkyaḥ |
puṣpe śākodake kāṣṭhe tathā mūlaphale tṛṇe |
adattādānam eteṣām asteyaṃ tu yamo ‘bravīt ||
tṛṇaṃ kāṣṭhaṃ phalaṃ puṣpaṃ prakāśaṃ vai haran dvijaḥ |
gobrāhmaṇārthaṃ gṛhṇan vai na sa pāpena lipyate ||
manuḥ |
vānaspatyaṃ mūlaphalaṃ darvy agnyarthaṃ tathaiva ca |
tṛṇaṃ ca gobhyo grāsārtham asteyaṃ manur abravīt ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
dvijas tṛṇaidhapuṣpāṇi sarvataḥ samupāharet |
yamaḥ |
pathikaḥ kṣīṇavṛttis tu dvāv ikṣū dve ca mūlake |
ādadānaḥ parakṣetrāt na daṇḍaṃ dātum arhati ||
nāradaḥ |
vartamāno ‘dhvani śrānto gṛhṇann anaśanaṃ svayam |
brāhmaṇo nāparādhnoti dvāv ikṣū dve ca mūlake ||
manuḥ |
yo nāhitāgniḥ śatagur ayajvā ca sahasraguḥ |
tayor api kuṭumbābhyām āhared avicārayan ||
brāhmaṇasvaṃ na hartavyaṃ kṣatriyeṇa kadācana |
dasyuniṣkrayayos tu svam ajīvan hartum arhati ||
yo ‘sādhubhyo ‘rtham ādāya sādhubhyaḥ saṃprayacchati |
sa kṛtvā plavam ātmānaṃ saṃtārayati tāv ubhau ||
yad dhanaṃ yajñaśīlānāṃ devasvaṃ tad vidur budhāḥ |
ayajvanāṃ ca yad dattam asurasvaṃ tad ucyate ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
adhāritabrahmasūtraḥ vṛṣalāśanasevakaḥ |
pratyabdaṃ sarvam ādāya vedavidbhyo nivedayet ||
tyaktāgniḥ saṃdhyayā hīnaṃ nityam asnāyinaṃ dvijam |
arkopasthānahīnaṃ ca śūdrapreṣyakaraṃ tathā ||
akartā nityayajñānāṃ pratyahaṃ pāpam āpnuyāt |
manuḥ |
anena vidhinā rājā kurvāṇaḥ stenanigraham |
yaśo ‘smin prāpnuyāl loke pretya cānuttamaṃ sukham ||
sāhasam
atha sāhasam ucyate | atra bṛhaspatiḥ |
stenānām eta ākhyātaṃ sarveṣāṃ daṇḍanigraham |
sāhasasyādhunā samyak śrūyatāṃ vadhaśāsanam ||
nāradaḥ |
manuṣyamāraṇaṃ cauryaṃ paradārābhimarśnam |
pāruṣyam ubhayaṃ ceti sāhasaṃ pañcadhā smṛtam ||
sahasā kriyate karma yat kiṃcid baladarpitaiḥ |
tat sāhasam iti proktaṃ saho balam ihocyate ||
tat punas trividhaṃ jñeyaṃ prathamaṃ madhyamaṃ tathā |
uttamaṃ ceti śāstrajñaiḥ tasyoktaṃ lakṣaṇam pṛthak ||
phalamūlodakādīnāṃ kṣetropakaraṇasya ca |
bhaṅgākṣepopamardādyaiḥ prathamaṃ sāhasaṃ smṛtam ||
vāsaḥ paśvannadhānyānāṃ gṛhopakaraṇasya ca |
etenaiva prakāreṇa madhyamaṃ sāhasaṃ smṛtam ||
vyāpādo viṣaśastrādyaiḥ paradāraprakarṣaṇam |
prāṇoparodhi yac cānyad uttamaṃ sāhasaṃ smṛtam ||
tasyaiva bhedas tredhā syāt viśeṣas tatra tūcyate |
ādhiḥ sāhasam ākramya steyam ādhicchalena ca ||
sāhasam ity anuvṛttau nāradaḥ |
tasya daṇḍaḥ kriyāpekṣaḥ prathamasya śatāvaraḥ |
madhyamasya tu śāstrajñaiḥ dṛṣṭaḥ pañcaśatāvaraḥ ||
uttame sāhase daṇḍaḥ sahasrāvara iṣyate |
vadhaḥ sarvasvaharaṇaṃ purān nirvāsanāṅkane ||
tadaṅgaccheda ity ukto daṇḍa uttamasāhase |
syātāṃ saṃvyavahāryau tu ghṛtadaṇḍau tu pūrvayoḥ ||
dhṛtadaṇḍo ‘py asaṃbhāṣyo daṇḍya uttamasāhase |
manuḥ |
dravyāṇi hiṃsyād yo yasya jñānato ‘jñānato ‘pi ca |
sa tasyotpādayet tuṣṭiṃ rājño daṇḍaṃ ca tatsamam ||
carma cārmikabhāṇḍeṣu kāṣṭhaloṣṭamayeṣu ca |
mūlyāt pañcaguṇo daṇḍaḥ puṣpamūlaphaleṣu ca ||
śaṅkhalikhitau: “kūṭaśāsanaprayoge rājaśāsanapratikṣepe kūṭatulāmānapratimānavyavahāre śarīrāṅgacchedau” | manuḥ |
kūṭaśāsanakartṝṃś ca prakṛtīnāṃ ca dūṣakān |
strībālabrāhmaṇaghnāṃś ca hanyād viṭsevinas tathā ||
garbhadhvaṃsādhike vyāsaḥ |
parikleśena pūrvaḥ syād bhaiṣajyena tu madhyamaḥ |
prahāreṇa tu garbhasya pātane daṇḍa uttamaḥ ||
haṃsādihiṃsane bodhāyanaḥ: “haṃsabhāsabarhinacakravākabalākakākolūkamaṇḍūkaśva-nakulaḍairikababhrukokilādīnāṃ vadhe śūdravat” | nāradaḥ |
gṛhītaḥ śaṅkayā yas tu na tat kāryaṃ prapadyate |
śapathaiḥ sa viśoddhavyaḥ sarvapāpeṣv ayaṃ vidhiḥ ||
divyair viśuddho mocyaḥ syād aśuddho vadham arhati |
atra bṛhaspatiḥ |
ākruṣṭas tu samākrośaṃs tāḍitaḥ pratitāḍayan |
hatvātatāyinaṃ caiva nāparādhī bhaven naraḥ ||
nātatāyivadhe hantā kilbiṣaṃ prāpnuyāt kvacit |
vināśanārtham āyāntaṃ ghātayan nāparādhnuyāt ||
devalaḥ |
ātmatyāgah parityāgāt pāpīyān pātakād api |
ghātake niṣkṛtiḥ proktā katham ātmahaniṣkṛtiḥ ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
śikhināṃ śṛṅgiṇāṃ caiva daṃṣṭriṇāṃ cātatāyinām |
hastyaśvānāṃ tathānyeṣāṃ vadhe hantā na doṣabhāk ||
udyatānāṃ tu pāpānāṃ hantur doṣo na vidyate |
nivṛttis tu yad ārambhād grahaṇaṃ na vadhaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
atra bṛhaspatiḥ |
ātatāyinam utkṛṣṭaṃ vṛttasvādhyāyasaṃyutam |
yo na hanyād vadhaprāptaṃ so ‘śvamedhaphalaṃ labhet ||
sumantuḥ: “ātatāyiny adoṣaḥ anyatra gobrāhmaṇāt” |
udyatāsiṃ viṣāgniṃ ca cāpodyatakaraṃ tathā |
ātharvaṇena hantāraṃ piśunaṃ caiva rājasu |
bhāryātikramiṇaṃ caiva vidyāt saptātatāyinaḥ ||
ātharvaṇena ābhicāreṇa | sāhasasya daṇḍapāruṣyeṇaiva samānayogakṣematvāt tatroktam ihānusaṃdheyam | iti |
iti sāhasam
aṣṭādaśapadeṣu viśeṣam āha
nāradaḥ |
tīritaṃ cānuśiṣṭaṃ ca yo manyeta vidharmiṇaḥ |
dviguṇaṃ daṇḍam āsthāya tat kāryaṃ punar uddharet ||
tīritānuśiṣṭayoḥ svarūpam āha kātyāyanaḥ |
asat sad iti ya pakṣaḥ sabhyai rājñāvadhāryate |
tīritaḥ so ‘nuśiṣṭas tu sākṣidauṣṭyāt prakīrtitaḥ ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
asat sad iti yaḥ pakṣaḥ sabhyai rājñāvadhāryate |
tīritaḥ so ‘nuśiṣṭas tu sākṣidauṣṭyāt prakīrtitaḥ ||
nirūpakadoṣād anyathā nirvṛttiḥ tīritaḥ | sākṣidoṣād anyathā nirvṛtto ‘nuśiṣṭaḥ | yājñavalkyaḥ |
durdṛṣṭāṃs tu punar dṛṣṭvā vyavahārān nṛpeṇa tu |
sabhyāḥ sajayino daṇḍyā vivādād dviguṇaṃ damam ||
yo manyetājito ‘smīti nyāyenāpi parājitaḥ |
tam āyāntaṃ punar jitvā dāpayed dviguṇaṃ damam ||
manuḥ |
amātyāḥ prāḍvivāko vā ye kuryuḥ kāryam anyathā |
tat svayaṃ nṛpatiḥ kuryāt tān sahasraṃ tu daṇḍayet ||
yasmin vivāde vijayaḥ kūṭasākṣikṛto bhavet |
tat tat kāryaṃ nivarteta kṛtaṃ cāpy akṛtaṃ bhavet ||
yogādhamanavikrītaṃ yogadānapratigraham |
yatra cāpy upadhiṃ paśyet tat sarvaṃ vinivartayet ||
balād dattaṃ balād bhuktaṃ balād yac cāpi lekhitam |
sarvān balakṛtān arthān akṛtān manur abravīt ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
balopadhivinirvṛttān vyavahārān nivartayet |
strīnaktamantarāgāraṃ bahiḥ śaṅkukṛtaṃ tathā ||
nāradaḥ |
svatantro ‘pi hi yat kāryaṃ kuryān na prakṛtiṃ gataḥ |
tad apy akṛtam evāhur asvatantratvahetutaḥ ||
kāmakrodhābhiyuktārthabhayavyasanapīḍitāḥ |
rāgadveṣaparītāś ca jñeyās tv aprakṛtiṃ gatāḥ ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
na kṣetragṛhadāsānāṃ dānādhamanavikriyā |
striyādibhiḥ kṛtāḥ siddhiḥ prāpnuyur nānuvarṇitāḥ ||
gobhūhiraṇyaratnānām ātmīyānāṃ striyā vinā |
vikrayādhipradānaṃ tu kṛtaṃ siddhyen nṛbhiḥ sadā ||
strīkṛtāny apramāṇāni kāryāṇy āhur anāpadi |
viśeṣato gṛhakṣetradānādhamanavikriyāḥ ||
etāny eva pramāṇāni bhartrā yady anumanyate |
putraḥ patyur abhāve vā bhrātā vā patiputrayoḥ ||
iti śrīmadvaradarājīye dharmaśāstre vyavahāranirṇaye
dyūtasamāhvayādiṣaṭpadakāṇḍaṃ samāptam
13. prakīrṇakam
atha prakīrṇakākhyaṃ vivādapadam ucyate | tatra kātyāyanaḥ |
rājadharmāṃś ca daṇḍāṃś ca saṃdigdhānāṃ viśeṣataḥ |
prākuktārthasya śeṣaṃ ca vakṣyāmy etat prakīrṇake ||
nāradaḥ |
prakīrṇakaḥ punar jñeyo vyavahāro nṛpāśrayaḥ |
rājñām ājñāpratīghātaḥ tatkarmakaraṇaṃ tathā ||
purapradhānasaṃbhedaḥ prakṛtīnāṃ tathaiva ca |
pāṣaṇḍanaigamaśreṇigaṇadharmaviparyayaḥ ||
pitṛputravivādaś ca prāyaścittavyatikramaḥ |
pratigrahavilopaś ca kopa āśramiṇām api ||
varṇasaṃkarahetuś ca tadvṛttiniyamas tathā |
na dṛṣṭaṃ yac ca pūrveṣu tat sarvaṃ syāt prakīrṇake ||
ājñālaṅghanaṃ rājaikāntakarmānuṣṭhānena tadanukaraṇam, puravartināṃ pradhānapuruṣāṇāṃ ca sāṃkaryam, prakṛtīnāṃ rāṣṭravartināṃ puruṣāṇāṃ ca sāṃkaryam, pāṣaṇḍādisaṃghānāṃ dharmaviparyāyaḥ | pitāputravivādaś ca pūrvokteṣu yad apekṣitaṃ nokaṃ tat sarvaṃ prakīrṇake draṣṭavyam | yathaite doṣā na bhavanti tathā kartavyam ity arthaḥ |
aśāstrokteṣu mārgeṣu pāpayukteṣu karmasu |
samīkṣya daṇḍaṃ daṇḍyeṣu pātayed avicārayan ||
rājñā pravartitān dharmān yo naro nānupālayet |
garhyaḥ sa pāpo vadhyaś ca lopayan rājaśāsanam ||
āyudhāny āyudhīyānāṃ bījāni kṛṣijīvinām |
veśastrīṇām alaṃkārān ātodyāni ca tadvidām ||
yac ca yasyopakaraṇaṃ yena jīvanti kārukāḥ |
sarvasvaharaṇe ‘py etān na rājā hartum arhati ||
eṣā sarvasvaharaṇe ‘pi prāpte etān na rājā haret | kiṃ punar anyasminn aparādhe etiar vinā na te jīvanti iti vadhād arvāṅ na hartavyaḥ | iti nītiḥ | rājaśāsane sarveṣāṃ vartamānārthaṃ rājño vilakṣaṇasvarūpam āha nāradaḥ |
rājā tv avahitaḥ sarvān āśramān paripālayet |
upāyaiḥ śāstraviditaiḥ caturbhiḥ prakṛtais tathā ||
rājamūlam idaṃ rāṣṭraṃ brahmakṣatrapurogamam |
sthite rājani dharme tu durācāraṃ na kurvate ||
pitāmahaḥ |
pañcarūpāṇi rājāno dhārayanty amitaujasaḥ |
agner indrasya somasya yamasya dhanadasya ca ||
yathā dhārayanti tathā pratipādayanti |
akāraṇān nimittād vā yadā krodhavaśaṃ gataḥ |
prajā dahati bhūpālaḥ tadāgnir abhidhīyate ||
yadā tejaḥ samālambya vijigīṣur udāyudhaḥ |
abhiyāti parān rājā tathendraḥ samudāhṛtaḥ ||
vigatakrodhasantāpo hṛṣṭaḥ puṣṭo yadā nṛpaḥ |
prajānāṃ darśanaṃ yāti soma ity udito tadā ||
dharmāsanagataḥ śrīmān daṇḍaṃ dhatte yadā nṛpaḥ |
samaḥ sarveṣu bhūteṣu tadā vaivasvato yamaḥ ||
yadānāthaguruprājñajñātyādīn avanīpatiḥ |
anugṛhṇāti dānena tadāsau dhanadaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
tasmān nārtaṃ vijānīyāt nātikrāmed viśeṣataḥ |
ājñāyā cāsya tiṣṭheta mṛtyuḥ syāt tadvyatikrame ||
atha rājādhikāre manuḥ |
tadarthaṃ sarvabhūtānāṃ goptraṃ dharmam ātmajam |
brahmatejomayaṃ daṇḍam asṛjat pūrvam īśvaraḥ ||
tasya sarvāṇi bhūtāni sthāvarāṇi carāṇi ca |
bhayād bhogāya kalpante svadharmān na calanti ca ||
nāradaḥ |
tasya vṛttiḥ prajārakṣā vṛddhaprājñopasevanam |
darśanaṃ vyavahārāṇām ātmanaś cābhirakṣaṇam ||
vṛddhāḥ śāstreṇa kṛtabuddhayaḥ | prājñāḥ svābhāvikabuddhayaḥ | tatra sarveṣāṃ duṣṭānāṃ śāsitā rājeti | hārītaḥ |
gurur ātmavatāṃ śāstā śāstā rājā durātmanām |
antaḥpracchannapāpānāṃ śāstā vaivasvato yamaḥ ||
gurubhir ye na śāsyante rājñā vā gūḍhakilbiṣāḥ |
te narā yamadaṇḍena śāstrā yānty adhamāṃ gatim ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
yo daṇḍyān daṇḍayed rājā samyag vadhyāṃś ca ghātayet |
iṣṭaṃ syāt kratubhis tena sahasraśatadakṣiṇaiḥ ||
iti saṃcintya nṛpatiḥ kratutulyaphalaṃ pṛthak |
vyavahārān svayaṃ paśyet sabhyaiḥ parivṛto ‘nvaham ||
adaṇḍyadaṇḍanaṃ loke yaśoghnaṃ kīrtināśanam |
asvargyaṃ ca paratrāpi tasmāt tat parivarjayet ||
adaṇḍyān daṇḍayan rājā daṇḍyāṃś caivāpy adaṇḍayan |
ayaśo mahad āpnoti narakaṃ caiva gacchati ||
vasiṣṭhaḥ: “daṇḍyotsarge rājaikarātram upavaset | trirātraṃ purohitaḥ | kṛcchram adaṇḍyadaṇḍane purohitas trirātraṃ rājā” | atha daṇḍaprakāram āha manuḥ |
anubandhaṃ parijñāya deśakālau ca tattvataḥ |
sārāparādhāv ālocya daṇḍaṃ daṇḍyeṣu pātayet ||
atra viṣṇuḥ |
āgaḥsu ca tathānyeṣu jñātvā jñātidhanaṃ vayaḥ |
daṇḍaṃ prakalpayed rājā sāmantabrāhmaṇaiḥ saha ||
manuḥ |
pitācāryaḥ suhṛn mātā bhāryā putrāḥ purohitaḥ |
nādaṇḍyo nāma rājño ‘sti yas tv adharme ‘pi tiṣṭhati ||
atra śaṅkhalikhitau: “adaṇḍyau mātāpitarau snātakapurohitau parivrājakavānaprasthau, janmakarmaśrutaśīlācāravantaś ca | te ca dharme pratīkārā rājñā strībālavṛddhās tapasvinaḥ | tebhyaḥ krodhaṃ niyacchet” | bṛhaspatiḥ |
vāgdhigdhanaṃ vadhaś caiva caturdhā kalpito damaḥ |
puruṣe doṣavibhavaṃ jñātvā saṃparikalpayet ||
svalpe ‘parādhe vāgdaṇḍo dhigdaṇḍaḥ pūrvasāhase |
madhyamottame ‘rthadaṇḍas tu rājadrohe vadhaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
vāgdaṇḍaṃ prathamaṃ kuryād dhigdaṇḍaṃ tadanantaram |
tṛtīyaṃ dhanadaṇḍaṃ tu vadhadaṇḍam ataḥ param ||
vadhenāpi yadā tv etān nigṛhītuṃ na śaknuyāt |
tadaiṣu sarvam apy etat prayuñjīta catuṣṭayam ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
gurūn purohitān putrān vāgdaṇḍenaiva daṇḍayet |
vivādino naraṃś cānyān doṣiṇo ‘rthena daṇḍayet ||
mahāpātakayuktāṃś ca vadhadaṇḍena daṇḍayet |
kātyāyanaḥ |
mitrādiṣu prayuñjīta vāgdaṇḍaṃ dhik tapasvinām |
yathoktaṃ tasya tat kuryur anuktaṃ sādhu kalpitam ||
adhārmikāṃs tribhir nyāyair nigṛhṇīyāt prayatnataḥ |
nirodhanena bandhena vividhena bhayena ca ||
bandhanāni ca sarvāṇi rājamārge niveśayet |
duḥkhitā yatra dṛśyante vikṛtāḥ pāpakāriṇaḥ ||
daśa sthānāni daṇḍasya manuḥ svāyaṃbhuvo ‘bravīt |
triṣu varṇeṣu tāni syur akṣato brāhmaṇo vrajet ||
upastham udaraṃ jihvā hanstau pādau ca pañcamam |
cakṣur nāsā ca kaṇṭhau ca dhanaṃ dehas tathaiva ca ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
hastāsyaliṅgnayanaṃ jihvā karṇau ca nāsikā |
grīvā pādaṃ tu saṃdaṃśo lalāṭaṃ ca gudaṃ kaṭiḥ ||
sthānāny etāni daṇḍasya nirdiṣṭāni caturdaśa |
tīvradrohaviṣayam idam |
lalāṭāṅko brāhmaṇasya nānyo daṇḍo vidhīyate |
yamaḥ |
jagat sarvam idaṃ hanyāt brāhmaṇasya na tatsamam |
tasmāt tasya vadhaṃ rājā manasāpi na cintayet ||
avadhyān brāhmaṇān āhuḥ sarvapāpeṣv avasthitān |
yad yad vipreṣu kuśalaṃ tat tad rājā samācaret ||
bodhāyanaḥ |
na jātu brāhmaṇaṃ hanyāt sarvapāpeṣv avasthitam |
rāṣṭrāt tv enaṃ bahiṣkuryāt samagradhanam akṣatam ||
viṣnuḥ: “vadhye karmaṇi tiṣṭhantaṃ samagradhanam akṣatam vivāsayet” | bodhāyanaḥ: “brāhmaṇasya bhrūṇahatyāgurutalpasuvarṇasteyasurāpāneṣu kabandabhagaśvāpadasṛgāla-dhvajāṃs taptenāyasena lalāṭe ‘ṅkayitvā viṣayāntare nirvāsanam” | manuḥ |
gurutalpe bhagaḥ kāryaḥ surāpāne surādhvajaḥ |
steye tu śvapadaṃ kāryaṃ brahmahaṇy aśirāḥ pumān ||
asaṃbhojyā hy asaṃyājyā asaṃpāṭhyā vivāsinaḥ |
careyuḥ pṛthivīṃ dīnāḥ sarvakarmabahiṣkṛtāḥ ||
jñātisaṃbandhibhis tv etaiḥ tyaktavyāḥ kṛtalakṣaṇāḥ |
nirdayā nirnamaskārāḥ tan manor anuśāsanam ||
prāyaścittaṃ tu kurvāṇāḥ pūrve varṇāḥ yathoditam |
nāṅkyā rājñā lalāṭeṣu dāpyās tūttamasāhasam ||
āgaḥsu brāhmaṇas ev eṣu dāpyo madhyamasāhasam |
vivāsyo vā bhaved rāṣṭrāt sadravyaḥ saparicchadaḥ ||
itare kṛtavantas tu pāpāny etāny akāmataḥ |
sarvasvahāram arhanti kāmatas tu pravāsanam ||
yamaḥ |
brāhmaṇasyāparādheṣu caturṣv evaṃ vidhīyate |
śiraso muṇḍanaṃ daṇḍaḥ purān nirvāsanaṃ tathā ||
prakhyāpanārthaṃ pāpasya prayāṇaṃ gardabhena ca |
lalāṭe cihnakaraṇaṃ kuryād rājā yathāvidhi ||
gurutalpe surāpāne steye brāhmaṇahiṃsane |
yathākramaṃ pravakṣyāmi cihnāny āha yathā manuḥ ||
gurutalpe bhagaḥ kāryo vrataghnasya dvijanmanaḥ |
surādhvajaḥ surāpāne surāpasyāpi kārayet ||
steye tu śvapadaṃ kṛtvā śikhipiñchena kuṭṭayet |
viśirāḥ puruṣaḥ kāryo lalāṭe brahmaghātinaḥ ||
ya etaiḥ patitaiḥ sārdhaṃ saṃyogaṃ kurute dvijaḥ |
so ‘saṃbhojyaś ca kartavyo manuḥ svāyaṃbhuvo ‘bravīt ||
ete tyājyā kṛtāṅkāḥ syuḥ jñātisaṃbandhibāndhavaiḥ ||
manuḥ |
kārṣāpaṇaṃ bhaved daṇḍaṃ yatrānyaḥ prākṛto janaḥ |
tatra rājñā bhavet daṇḍyaḥ sahasram iti dhāraṇā ||
brāhmaṇān bādhamānaṃ tu kāmād avaravarṇajam |
hanyāc citrair vadhopāyaiḥ udvejanakarair nṛpaḥ ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
pravrajyāvasitaṃ śūdraṃ japahomaparaṃ tathā |
vadhena śāsayet pāpaṃ daṇḍyo vā dviguṇaṃ damam ||
aspṛśyadhūrtadāsānāṃ narāṇāṃ pāpakāriṇām |
prātilomyaprasūtānāṃ tāḍanaṃ nārthato damaḥ ||
paratantrās tu ye kecit dāsatve ye ca saṃsthitāḥ |
anīśās te tu nirdiṣṭāḥ teṣāṃ daṇḍas tu tāḍanam ||
tāḍanaṃ bandhanaṃ caiva tathaiva ca nipīḍanam |
eṣa daṇḍo hi dāsasya nārthadaṇḍo bṛhaspatiḥ ||
suvarṇaśatam ekaṃ tu vadhārho daṇḍam arhati |
aṅgacchede tadardhaṃ tu vivāsyaḥ pañcaviṃśatim ||
strībālonmattavṛddhānāṃ daridrāṇāṃ tu rogiṇām |
śiphāvidalarajjvādyaiḥ vidadhyān nṛpatir damam ||
manuḥ |
nādadīta nṛpaḥ sādhuḥ mahāpātakino dhanam |
ādadānas tu tallobhāt tenādharmeṇa lipyate ||
apsu praveśya tad daṇḍaṃ varuṇāyopapādayet |
śrutavṛttopapanne vā brāhmaṇe pratipādayet ||
īśo daṇḍasya varuṇo rājā daṇḍadharo hi saḥ |
īśaḥ sarvasya jagato brāhmaṇo vedapāragaḥ ||
yamaḥ |
patitasya dhanaṃ hṛtvā rājā parṣadi dāpayet |
sarvasvaṃ tu haret rājā caturthaṃ vāvaśeṣayet ||
bhṛtyebhyo ‘nusmaran dharmaṃ prājāpatyam iti śrutiḥ |
nāradaḥ |
deyaṃ corahṛtaṃ dravyaṃ rājñā janapadāya tu |
anyatra karād ucitāt bhūmeḥ ṣaḍbhāgasaṃsthitāt ||
baliḥ sa tasya vihitaḥ prajāpālanavetanam ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
rājā labdhvā nidhīn dadyād dvijebhyo ‘rdhaṃ dvijaḥ punaḥ |
vidvān aśeṣam ādadyāt sa sarvasya prabhur yataḥ ||
itareṇa nidhau labdhe rājā ṣaṣṭhāṃśam āharet |
aniveditavijñāto rājñas tad daṇḍam eva ca ||
viṣṇuḥ: “nidhiṃ labdhvā tadardhaṃ brāhmaṇebhyo dadyāt | dvitīyam ardhaṃ kośe praveśayet | nidhiṃ brāhmaṇo labdhvā sarvam ādadyāt | kṣatriyaś caturthāṃśaṃ rājñe dadyāt | caturthaṃ brāhmaṇebhyo ‘rdham ādadyāt | vaiśyaś caturtham aṃśaṃ rājñe dadyāt | brāhmanebhyo ‘rdham | caturtham aṃśam ādadyāt | śūdraś cāvāptanidhiṃ dvādaśadhā vibhajya, pañcāṃśān rājñe dadyāt, pañcāṃśān brāhmaṇebhyaḥ, svayam aṃśadvayam ādadyāt | aniveditavijñātasya sarvasvam apaharet” | yamaḥ |
evaṃ dharmapravṛttasya rājño daṇḍadhārasya ca |
yajño ‘smin prathito loke svargavāsas tathākṣayaḥ ||
manuḥ |
evaṃ sarvān imān rājā vyavahārān samāpayet |
vyapohya kilbiṣaṃ sarvaṃ sa yāti paramāṃ gatim ||
iti śrīmadvaradarājīye vyavahāranirṇaye prakīrṇapadaṃ samāptam
vyavahāranirṇayaḥ samāptaḥ
